TL;DR: 针对现有仇恨模因检测基准的偏见，本文提出了FBHM数据集和LSV微调策略，有效解决了模型因依赖启发式规则而导致的跨域泛化失效问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的仇恨模因检测基准存在观测偏见，导致模型将修辞机制与目标群体特征混淆，未能有效评估模型的鲁棒性与泛化能力。

Method: 1. 构建FBHM基准：基于25种修辞功能和10个目标群体，人工构建5000个样本；2. 提出LSV（Learnable Steering Vectors）：一种基于因果干预目标的低数据量微调策略。

Result: 现有SOTA模型在FBHM上性能出现灾难性下降（接近随机水平）；利用仅500个样本的LSV策略，FBHM的Macro-F1性能提升了约30个百分点，且在不降低原始领域性能的前提下优于In-context learning和PEFT。

Conclusion: 现有的多模态模型在仇恨模因检测中依赖数据集特定的启发式规则而非鲁棒的推理。提出的LSV方法通过因果干预证明了在极低数据样本下能够显著提升模型泛化能力和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Hateful meme detection remains a formidable challenge for vision-language models, as existing benchmarks are structurally observational - confounding rhetorical hate mechanisms with target community features and preventing causal evaluation of model vulnerabilities. To address this, we introduce FBHM, a systematically curated benchmark of Functionality Based Hateful Memes constructed along two orthogonal axes: 25 distinct rhetorical functionalities and 10 target communities (5,000 memes total). Benchmarking state-of-the-art VLMs reveals a severe generalization gap: models highly accurate on standard datasets catastrophically drop to near-random performance on FBHM, proving they exploit dataset-specific heuristics rather than robust multimodal reasoning. To efficiently close this gap, we propose LSV (learnable steering vectors), an ultra-low data regime strategy that applies a causal intervention objective on as few as 500 steering samples (50 unique base memes), boosting FBHM performance by ~30 Macro-F1 points while outperforming in-context learning and PEFT without degrading source-domain performance.

</details>


### [77] [A Visually Impaired Assistance Benchmark for VLM-as-a-Judge Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31351)
*Yi Zhao,Siqi Wang,Zhe Hu,Yushi Li,Jing Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对 AI 视障辅助（VIA）评估成本高的问题，提出了评估 VLM 系统能力的基准 VIABLE。研究发现现有 VLM 作为裁判模型极不可靠，为此提出了 VIA-Judge-Agent 架构，显著提升了诊断准确率和用户偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视障辅助（VIA）领域的 AI 评估成本高昂，VLM-as-a-Judge  paradigm 在通用领域虽有应用，但在 VIA 领域的有效性和可靠性尚不明确，缺乏系统性的评估基准。

Method: 1. 构建了 VIABLE 基准：包含 300K+ 样本，涵盖三个场景，并配套 Effectiveness-Impartiality-Stability 评估框架及 12 种失效分类法。
2. 实验分析：评估 7 种不同规模的模型，揭示了模型在可靠性、偏见和脆弱性方面的重大缺陷。
3. 提出 VIA-Judge-Agent：一种模型无关的推理时架构，通过视觉证据提取和分类导向流程来优化评估效果。

Result: 现有最强模型 GPT-4o 在单一故障诊断方面准确率仅 52.6%，且存在 94.2% 的自我偏好倾向；开源模型则表现出极强的偏见和对抗脆弱性。VIA-Judge-Agent 被证明能提升诊断准确率，并获得更多 BLV 用户的偏好。

Conclusion: 当前的 VLM-as-a-Judge 在视障辅助（VIA）领域普遍表现不可靠（存在高偏见、低诊断准确率等问题）。通过提出的 VIA-Judge-Agent，可以有效增强现有模型的诊断能力，并提升符合视障及低视力（BLV）用户偏好的能力。

Abstract: AI-based Visually Impaired Assistance (VIA) remains challenging, largely due to the high cost of human evaluation. The VLM-as-a-Judge paradigm may offer a promising alternative, although it has mostly been studied in general domains. We therefore ask whether such judges can be trusted for VIA tasks. To investigate this question, we introduce VIABLE (Visually Impaired Assistance Benchmark for VLM-as-a-Judge Evaluation), the first benchmark for VLM-as-a-Judge evaluation in VIA. VIABLE contains over 300K judgment samples across three scenarios and introduces an Effectiveness--Impartiality--Stability framework with a 12-mode failure taxonomy. Based on VIABLE, our systematic study of seven judges across different model scales shows that existing models are largely unreliable across all evaluation axes. The strongest judge, GPT-5.4, achieves only 52.6% single-failure diagnostic accuracy, yet exhibits the highest self-preference rate at 94.2%; while open-source judges are strongly biased and adversarially fragile. To address these issues, we propose VIA-Judge-Agent, a model-agnostic inference-time harness that augments judges with visual evidence extraction and a taxonomy-guided workflow. It enables positive improvements in diagnostic accuracy and downstream VIA responses more preferred by BLV users. Data and code are available at: https://github.com/YiyiyiZhao/VIABLE

</details>


### [78] [The Latin Substrate: How Language Models Represent and Mediate Script Choice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31363)
*Daniil Gurgurov,Alan Saji,Katharina Trinley,Josef van Genabith,Simon Ostermann*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过机制分析揭示了LLM内部处理多脚本转换的原理，发现模型利用跨语言通用的注意力头来控制脚本，且隐含了对拉丁字母的系统性偏向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在揭示大语言模型（LLM）如何在内部处理多脚本语言中的正字法转换（转写），以及模型是如何在保持语义不变的情况下切换书写脚本的。

Method: 结合了逻辑镜头（Logit Lens）分析各层的输出分布、表征分析（构建线性定向向量 steering direction）、机械可解释性分析（定位负责脚本选择的注意力头并进行因果干预）。

Result: 研究发现：1. LLM在转写过程中存在潜在的“罗马化”过程；2. 不同脚本在深层越来越可分，线性向量可成功控制脚本切换；3. 存在一组跨语言通用的特定后期注意力头负责脚本路由；4. 存在明显的非对称性：非拉丁脚本由特定机制触发，而拉丁脚本输出则由全网协同产生。

Conclusion: 大语言模型围绕共享的潜在语义空间组织脚本变化，但表现出对拉丁字母的显著偏向性（Privileged Substrate）。脚本路由由语言不可知的机制控制，且非拉丁脚本的生成路径比拉丁脚本更为集中。

Abstract: Many languages are written in multiple scripts, requiring large language models (LLMs) to generate equivalent linguistic content in distinct orthographic forms. While prior work suggests that LLMs route information through shared latent representations, how they internally mediate script variation remains poorly understood.
  We study this question by first examining per-layer output distributions with the logit lens, which reveals consistent latent romanization during transliteration, and then through representational and mechanistic analyses of script generation. At the representational level, we show that scripts of the same language become increasingly separable across layers and that a simple linear steering direction can flip a model's output script while largely maintaining semantic content. The vector generalizes asymmetrically to writing systems unseen during construction, flipping non-Latin output to Latin reliably, but mapping Latin output into varied non-Latin scripts. At the mechanistic level, we localize a small set of late-layer attention heads that causally mediate script choice. These heads transfer across unrelated languages and writing systems, suggesting that script routing is implemented by language-agnostic components. Across both analyses, we observe a consistent directional asymmetry: non-Latin output is produced by a compact, identifiable gate, while Latin-script output emerges from diffuse contributions across the network. Collectively, our findings hint that LLMs organize script variation around shared latent representations while exhibiting a privileged substrate toward Latin script.

</details>


### [79] [Unlocking Fine-Grained Translation Quality Estimation in LRMs through Synergistically Evolving Implicit and Explicit Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31378)
*Renfei Dang,Xinye Wang,Zhejian Lai,Weilu Xu,Shimin Tao,Daimeng Wei,Min Zhang,Shujian Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对LRM在细粒度翻译质量评估（QE）任务上的短板，本文提出了RIEQE两阶段训练框架（NonThinking-SFT与Thinking-RLVR），实现了隐式与显式推理的协同进化，显著提升了QE任务的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型（LRM）在细粒度翻译质量评估（QE）任务中表现不佳，研究认为这是由于QE任务本身的难度，而非模型缺乏多语言能力，旨在通过协同优化隐式和显式推理来解决这一问题。

Method: 提出了RIEQE训练框架，包含两个阶段：1. NonThinking-SFT：在没有推理链的情况下进行监督微调，增强隐式推理倾向；2. Thinking-RLVR：利用带有可验证奖励的强化学习，强化显式推理。通过将复杂的QE任务分解为简单的子任务来促进这种协同。

Result: 基于Qwen3-4B构建的RIEQE在WMT测试集上显式推理性能超越了所有基线模型，且其隐式推理能力与当前最优的编码器（encoder-based）模型相当。

Conclusion: RIEQE框架成功实现了隐式（层级）和显式（token级）推理能力的协同进化，证明了这两种推理模式可以相互增强，从而有效提升了大型推理模型（LRM）在细粒度翻译质量评估（QE）任务中的表现。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) still struggle with fine-grained translation quality estimation (QE), even with long reasoning chains. We argue that LRMs already possess strong multilingual capabilities, while the core challenge stems from the intrinsic difficulty of learning the fine-grained QE task. In this paper, we propose RIEQE (Reasoning both Implicitly and Explicitly for QE), a simple two-stage training framework that enables the co-evolution of implicit (layer-wise) and explicit (token-wise) reasoning capabilities. To make implicit reasoning feasible, we first decompose the complex QE task into straightforward subtasks. Based on this, our two-stage approach applies: (1) NonThinking-SFT, Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) without reasoning chains to directly boost the model's implicit reasoning tendency and capability; and (2) Thinking-RLVR, standard Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Reward (RLVR) to subsequently strengthen explicit reasoning. Results demonstrate that implicit and explicit reasoning synergistically co-evolve under our framework. On the WMT test sets, RIEQE based on Qwen3-4B-Thinking-2507 surpasses all baselines in explicit reasoning performance, while its implicit reasoning capability is also comparable to the best current encoder-based models. We further provide evidence for the synergistic collaboration between implicit and explicit reasoning, showing how they mutually benefit each other.

</details>


### [80] [LLM Judges Inconsistently Disagree Across Safety Criteria and Harm Categories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31381)
*Krishnapriya Vishnubhotla,Soumya Vajjala,Akriti Vij,Isar Nejadgholi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究揭示了大语言模型作为“自动评判者”在安全性评估中存在严重的不一致性和不可靠性，特别是在专业领域；不同模型间的评估结果存在显著分歧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型常被用作评估其他模型输出的“自动化评判者”，但其在该角色下的表现（特别是在复杂或受监管领域）缺乏系统性研究，尚不明确其是否具备足够的评估一致性和准确性。

Method: 在无参考（reference-free）的设置下，对大语言模型作为自动化评判者在多维度安全性评估中的一致性和可靠性进行了评估，涵盖了不同领域、安全指标、语言和语言风格。

Result: 大语言模型在受监管领域（如金融）识别复杂安全性问题的可靠性较低，但在识别直观的有害内容（如暴力）方面表现相对较好；模型的评估一致性受安全标准、语言类型及风格影响显著；不同评判模型之间针对同一输出存在高度的分歧。

Conclusion: 大语言模型作为评估者在处理多维安全性评估时存在显著的不一致性和可靠性挑战。研究建议从业者在实际应用中审慎使用自动评估，不能完全依赖单一的自动裁判，且应针对特定的领域和指标进行针对性校准。

Abstract: We evaluate the consistency of automated judges in conducting a multi-dimensional safety evaluation in a reference-free setup. Our results indicate that Large Language Models are unreliable judges in identifying safety issues related to machine-generated advice in regulated domains such as finance, although they are more reliable at identifying more overt forms of unsafe/harmful content such as violence. The degree of inconsistency in a model's judgments can vary significantly by the chosen safety criteria and can be impacted by the language of the content and its linguistic style as well. Finally, there is high disagreement among different judges for the same output, across domains, safety criteria, and languages. These findings provide new insights on the practice of using LLMs as evaluators and offer several recommendations for practitioners on how to use automated judges in practical scenarios.

</details>


### [81] [Target-Side Paraphrase Augmentation for Sign Language Translation with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31393)
*Pedro Dal Bianco,Jean Paul Nunes Reinhold,Oscar Stanchi,Facundo Quiroga,Franco Ronchetti,Ulisses Brisolara Corrêa*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种利用GPT-4o生成目标端改写文本对手语翻译进行数据增强的技术，并通过两阶段训练和LLM-as-a-Judge评估，有效提升了手语翻译的质量与语义忠实度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的手语翻译（SLT）受限于配对的手语视频/文本语料匮乏以及长尾词汇分布问题，需要探索新的数据增强方法以提升模型性能。

Method: 1. 利用GPT-4o生成参考句的受控改写版本，对手语翻译语料库进行目标端数据增强；
2. 采用Signformer架构，分两阶段训练：先在增强语料库上预训练，后在原始参考集上微调；
3. 引入“LLM作为裁判”（LLM-as-a-Judge）的评估方法进行语义忠实度度量。

Result: 在PHOENIX14T数据集上，BLEU-4分数从9.56提升至10.33；在GSL和LSA-T数据集上探索了该方法的适用边界；证实了语义评估比传统词汇重叠度指标（如BLEU）更能捕捉翻译的提升。

Conclusion: 本研究验证了利用LLM生成的文本改写对缓解手语翻译（SLT）数据稀缺问题的有效性。实验结果显示，通过大模型增强的训练数据能提升翻译的忠实度，且语义评估指标能比传统的词汇重叠度指标更准确地反映翻译质量。

Abstract: Sign language translation (SLT) remains constrained by limited paired sign-video/text corpora and heavy-tailed target vocabularies. We study target-side augmentation in which GPT-4o generates controlled paraphrase variants of reference sentences while the sign input remains unchanged. A Signformer-style pose-based Transformer is trained under a two-stage schedule: pre-training on the augmented corpus followed by fine-tuning on the original references.
  We evaluate on three datasets spanning complementary challenges: PHOENIX14T (German Sign Language), with moderate lexical diversity; GSL (Greek Sign Language), with highly ontrolled, repetitive recordings; and LSA-T (Argentinian Sign Language), with severe long-tail sparsity. On PHOENIX14T, augmentation improves BLEU-4 from 9.56 to 10.33. The near-saturated GSL baseline and extremely sparse LSA-T setting reveal the limits of the approach. To our knowledge, this is the first study to apply LLM-generated target-side araphrases and LLM-as-a-Judge evaluation to SLT. The semantic evaluation reveals gains in fidelity that lexical overlap metrics understate.

</details>


### [82] ["Intelegi Româneşte?'' A Recipe for Romanian Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31401)
*Mihai Masala,Marius Leordeanu,Mihai Dascalu,Traian Rebedea*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文为罗马尼亚语开发了专用视觉语言模型，通过数据翻译、文化属性评估集（HoraVQA）构建及针对性消融实验，证明了通过语言适配的小型模型在特定语言表现上可优于大型通用模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉语言模型（VLM）主要遵循大语言模型（LLM）的路径，在英语任务上表现出色，但在低资源语言上性能严重下降，且缺乏相关的大规模语料和文化评估基准。

Method: 提出了一套完整的罗马尼亚语VLM构建流程：1.构建数据集：将主流英语VLM训练与评估数据翻译并适配为罗马尼亚语（包括图像内文本），并创建了“HoraVQA”文化原生评估集；2.实验与消融：对比不同规模和预训练背景的视觉骨干、不同类型的语言模型（多语言 vs. 罗马尼亚语适配）以及OCR风格数据的贡献。

Result: 实验结果表明，经过罗马尼亚语适配的VLM在所有评估指标上均稳定优于同等规模的通用模型，甚至能够超越规模更大的基线模型。

Conclusion: 针对低资源语言（如罗马尼亚语）构建专用视觉语言模型是可行的且高效的。通过针对性的语言模型适配与高质量的文化相关评估集，可以显著提升模型在特定语言任务上的表现，甚至超越更大规模的通用模型。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) largely follow the text-only LLM trajectory, excelling on English benchmarks but sharply degrading on low-resource languages, where neither large-scale image-text corpora nor culturally grounded evaluations exist. We present a systematic study of building a language-specific VLM for Romanian, covering the full pipeline from data construction to architectural choices. We translate established English VLM training and evaluation corpora into Romanian, applying machine translation to textual annotations and to in-image text, preserving visual grounding while adapting the textual content. Using this data, we train and ablate a series of VLMs to isolate the contribution of (i) vision backbones of varying scale and pretraining, (ii) language backbones from multilingual to Romanian-adapted LLMs, and (iii) OCR-style image-text data. We further curate HoraVQA, a culturally native evaluation set grounded in Romanian everyday scenes. Romanian-adapted VLMs consistently outperform their same-sized counterparts and, across all evaluated benchmarks, even surpass models from the next larger size category.

</details>


### [83] [The Sword, Shield, and Achilles' Heel: Characterizing the Linguistic Inductive Bias of Large Language Models for Spatial Reasoning in Navigation Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31404)
*Xudong Zhang,Jian Yang,Shengkai Wang,Jiangpeng Tian,Shaowen Chen,Xian Wei,Ke Li,Xiong You*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究揭示了LLM在导航任务中的语言归纳偏差，指出拓扑结构是核心，语义信息具有高风险，且导航文本表示的设计需平衡模型容量与压缩程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM导航系统通常将空间表示（如拓扑图、语义图）简单地转化为文本，忽略了语言结构和上下文特征的选择对LLM规划能力的直接影响及潜在交互作用。

Method: 提出了一种双重干预框架（Dual-Interventional Framework）：通过改变语言格式和压缩程度进行“表示干预”，结合上下文特征组合与冲突探测进行“上下文干预”，以此解耦语言结构与空间上下文特征，评估LLM的语言归纳偏差。

Result: 实验表明：拓扑信息是稳健规划的基础；语言格式的影响具有双重性，受限于模型规模与任务需求；而语义信息是脆弱环节，错误的语义线索会导致规划过程系统性崩溃。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的导航系统设计不应仅依赖单一表示，而应优先保持拓扑完整性，根据模型容量调整表示压缩率，并确保语义信息的正确性，以避免由于语义干扰导致的规划失效。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM)-based navigation systems commonly construct explicit spatial representations (e.g., topological graphs, semantic raster maps) and translate them into textual descriptions as LLMs' inputs. However, the linguistic structures of such text-based spatial representations and the choices of contextual features (e.g., topology, geometry) they contain are often treated as neutral engineering decisions rather than key factors that shape LLMs' behavior. To fill the gap, we propose a dual-interventional framework that disentangles linguistic structures from different contextual cues to evaluate the linguistic inductive bias of LLMs for navigation planning. In the framework, representation intervention varies the linguistic format and the degree of linguistic compression, clarifying when linguistic representations support or inhibit navigation planning. Context intervention, combined with contextual feature combination and conflict probing, explicitly clarifies the preferences and weaknesses of LLMs when processing different contextual cues. Experiments across diverse spatial reasoning tasks and multiple model scales reveal a consistent pattern: topological information is a sturdy shield and the backbone of robust planning; linguistic format is a double-edged sword whose effect depends on model size, task demands, and the compression level; and semantic information is a fatal Achilles' heel -- incorrect semantic cues can systematically derail the planning process. Overall, our study shows that effective text-based spatial representations in LLM-based navigation should preserve topological integrity, calibrate representational compression to model capacity, and ensure semantic correctness, rather than simply adopting a single representation. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/jonesdong150/LLM-Navigation-Inductive-Bias.

</details>


### [84] [SCOPE: Self-Play via Co-Evolving Policies for Open-Ended Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31433)
*Wai-Chung Kwan,Aryo Pradipta Gema,Joshua Ong Jun Leang,Pasquale Minervini*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SCOPE 是一种无需数据、基于 self-play 的框架，通过 Challenger 和 Solver 的协同进化以及利用初始模型作为自判官，实现了在开放式任务上的性能显著提升及良好的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自博弈（Self-play）方法通常依赖于可规则检查的答案，导致处理开放式任务时往往需要依赖人工策划的提示词或性能更强的商用模型进行评估，限制了其在大规模应用中的独立性。

Method: 提出 SCOPE 框架，包含两个协同进化的策略：Challenger（负责生成基于文档的开放式任务）和 Solver（负责通过多轮检索回答任务）。模型利用初始模型的冻结版本作为“自判官”，根据原始文档生成任务特定的评分标准，并对 Solver 的回复进行打分。

Result: 在 Qwen2.5、Qwen3 和 OLMo-3（7-8B 参数规模）上，SCOPE 在八个基准测试中提升最高达 10.4 分，且性能与使用约 9K 条策划数据的 GRPO_data 持平或更优。此外，该方法在七个未见过的短篇问答基准测试中提升最高达 13.8 分，全面超越 GRPO_data。

Conclusion: SCOPE 证明了在无需外部监督或人工标注数据的情况下，通过协同进化的自博弈框架显著提升语言模型在开放式任务中的表现，并具备良好的泛化能力至短篇问答任务。研究指出，自我评估评分标准的质量是当前性能提升的瓶颈。

Abstract: Self-play can train language models without external supervision. However, existing methods require rule-checkable answers, leaving open-ended tasks dependent on curated prompts or frontier-model judges. We introduce SCOPE, a data-free self-play framework for open-ended tasks that co-evolves two policies: a Challenger that generates document-grounded tasks, and a Solver that answers them through multi-turn retrieval. A frozen copy of the initial model serves as the self-judge, which writes task-specific rubrics from the source document and grades Solver responses against them. Across three 7-8B instruction-tuned models (Qwen2.5, Qwen3, OLMo-3), SCOPE improves open-ended performance by up to +10.4 points on eight benchmarks and matches or exceeds GRPO_data trained on ~9K curated prompts. Although trained only on open-ended tasks, SCOPE also improves held-out short-form QA by up to +13.8 points on seven held-out benchmarks, surpassing GRPO_data on all three models. Ablations show that co-evolving the Challenger is necessary to keep tasks near the Solver's frontier, that gains arise from improvements in both retrieval and synthesis with the relative contribution varying by task, and that rubric generation quality is the bottleneck for self-judging.

</details>


### [85] [Fine-grained Verification via Diagnostic Reasoning Supervision for Aspect Sentiment Triplet Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31446)
*Wenna Lai,Haoran Xie,Guandong Xu,Qing Li,S. Joe Qin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对 ASTE 任务提取结果不准确的问题，本文提出了 FiVeD 验证框架；通过辅助任务（诊断推理）和质量评分机制，对提取的三元组进行精细化过滤与重排序，显著提升了系统的整体性能与可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 ASTE 工作主要集中在端到端提取，缺乏事后验证机制，导致提取出的三元组可能存在局部合理但全局无效的问题；且候选三元组的失效往往是多方面的，急需一种能够过滤或重排序的细粒度验证框架。

Method: 提出了 FiVeD (Fine-grained Verification with Diagnostic reasoning) 框架。采用多重互补目标进行验证器训练，包含：1. 主任务：有效性分类与质量评分；2. 辅助任务：错误类型分类与诊断性推理生成；3. 数据增强：构建语义和句法约束下的逻辑错误三元组；4. 应用：通过 LLM 生成评分与论据，在推理阶段执行筛选或重排序。

Result: 实验表明 FiVeD 作为即插即用模块，能显著提升多个 ASTE 基线模型的效果，F1 值最高提升了 3.53 个点。

Conclusion: FiVeD 框架通过精细化的验证机制，有效解决了 ASTE 任务中提取结果“局部合理但全局无效”的问题，通过对多基线模型的性能提升证明了其作为即插即用验证模块的有效性。

Abstract: Aspect Sentiment Triplet Extraction (ASTE) aims to identify aspect terms, opinion terms, and sentiment polarities as structured triplets, providing essential inputs for downstream information system applications such as opinion mining, explainable recommendations, and review summarization. Prior work mainly focuses on end-to-end extraction, while post hoc verification of extracted triplets remains comparatively underexplored. This gap limits the reliability of ASTE systems, since predicted triplets may be locally plausible while being globally invalid. Moreover, candidate invalidity is multi-faceted and candidate usability is inherently graded, motivating a fine-grained verification mechanism that can filter or re-rank outputs from diverse extractors. In this paper, we propose FiVeD, a framework for Fine-grained Verification with Diagnostic reasoning supervision. Specifically, the verifier is trained with multiple complementary objectives, including validity classification and quality score estimation as primary tasks, with error type classification and rationale generation as auxiliary tasks. We define hierarchical error categories and construct plausible incorrect triplets under semantic and syntactic constraints, and leverage an off-the-shelf LLM with task-specific rubrics to produce quality scores and diagnostic rationales. During inference, the resulting quality scores are used to filter candidate outputs, supporting adjustable precision-recall tradeoffs. Experiments across multiple ASTE baselines demonstrate that FiVeD consistently improves extraction performance by up to 3.53 F1 points as a plug-and-play verification module.

</details>


### [86] [Translation Analytics for Freelancers II: Benchmarking Local LLMs for Confidential Translation Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31452)
*Yuri Balashov,Rex VanHorn,Mingxi Xu,Austin Downes*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文为隐私敏感型翻译需求提供了低门槛的本地大模型评估方法，并通过对比实验证明了本地LLM在特定场景下可作为专业NMT及商业云端接口的有效替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为自由翻译者和小型语言服务提供商提供实用且低门槛的翻译技术评估方法，特别是在必须满足高保密性要求、无法使用云端引擎或商业LLM的场景下。

Method: 1. 扩充语料库：将 Reeve Foundation Trilingual Corpus (RFTC) 扩展为支持德语和简体中文的 RFMC 多语言语料库；2. 评测设置：使用 Ollama 在本地运行多种LLM，选取1000+条句子进行测试；3. 对比实验：对比对象包括商业NMT（DeepL, Baidu）、前沿LLM (GPT-5.2) 及专业本地NMT（OPUS-CAT等）；4. 自动化评估：使用 MATEO 进行性能度量。

Result: 本地LLM的性能在不同语言方向和模型规模之间存在显著差异。最优秀的本地LLM表现与本地NMT系统相当甚至更好，在某些指标上甚至可与顶级前沿大模型抗衡，但总体仍略逊于商业NMT。

Conclusion: 精心选择的本地大语言模型（LLM）对于有隐私需求的翻译专业人士来说是可行的选择。虽然本地LLM目前仍不及顶级商业机器翻译（NMT），但已能达到或超越部分本地翻译系统及前沿大模型，这为未来模型扩展和多语言能力研究提供了参考。

Abstract: Building on our previous work, this paper develops practical, low-barrier methods for freelance translators and smaller language service providers to evaluate translation technologies using rigorous yet accessible analytic methods. Here we address a high-stakes, specialized need: offline translation for confidentiality-sensitive domains in which privacy constraints preclude the use of cloud-based engines and commercial LLMs. We expand the Reeve Foundation Trilingual Corpus (RFTC) used in our previous work into a multilingual corpus (RFMC) by adding sentence-aligned German and Simplified Chinese reference translations. We then benchmark several locally runnable language models (via Ollama) across four language directions on 1000+ sentences selected from this corpus. We use consistent single-prompt calls without fine-tuning or domain adaptation, comparing local LLM outputs against commercial NMTs (DeepL, Baidu), a frontier LLM (GPT-5.2), and professional-grade local NMT systems (OPUS-CAT, NeuralDesktop, Promt). Automatic evaluation is conducted with MATEO. Results reveal substantial variation in local LLM performance across language directions and model sizes. The best local LLMs match or surpass local NMT systems and a frontier LLM, though they remain behind top commercial NMTs. These findings underscore the viability of carefully selected local LLM translation for privacy-constrained professionals and inform future research on model scaling and multilingual capability.

</details>


### [87] [Scaling Conversational Hungarian ASR: The BEA-Dialogue+ Corpus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31469)
*Máté Gedeon,Piroska Zsófia Barta,Péter Mihajlik,Katalin Mády*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了 BEA-Dialogue+，通过优化说话人划分标准，将匈牙利语对话 ASR 语料库从 85 小时扩展至 200 小时，并验证了利用 SOT 技术在该扩展数据集上微调模型在多项指标上的一致性改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 匈牙利语对话式自动语音识别（ASR）受限于公开的对话风格训练数据匮乏；原有的 BEA-Dialogue 语料库因严格的“说话人分离”划分限制，导致有效数据量仅有 85 小时，难以支撑更深层模型的训练与评估。

Method: 1. 构建 BEA-Dialogue+ 语料库：在保持主要说话人完全分离的前提下，放宽了对话参与者和实验者的分离标准，将数据量从 85 小时扩展至 200 小时。
2. 实验设置：部署多种基于 Whisper 和 FastConformer 的模型。
3. 关键技术：采用基于序列输出训练（SOT）的方法进行对话语音转录微调。
4. 对比评估：对比在原始 BEA-Dialogue 和扩展版 BEA-Dialogue+ 上的性能表现。

Result: 1. 扩展后的语料库在未经微调的模型上表现出更高的难度。
2. 基于 SOT 的微调显著降低了 WER、CER、cpWER 和 cpCER 指标。
3. 验证了增加训练数据量对于提升对话 ASR 系统性能的价值及其实际可行性。

Conclusion: BEA-Dialogue+ 通过放宽说话人分离限制，提供了一个更大、更具挑战性的匈牙利语对话 ASR 基准数据集，并证明了 SOT（序列输出训练）微调在该任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Conversational automatic speech recognition in Hungarian is constrained by the limited amount of publicly available dialogue-style training data. The BEA-Dialogue corpus addresses this need, but its strictly speaker-disjoint train/dev/eval split reduces the usable material to only 85 hours. In this paper, we introduce BEA-Dialogue+, an expanded version of the corpus that relaxes the split criterion for experimenters and dialogue partners while preserving complete separation of the primary speakers. This results in 200 hours of transcribed natural conversations and enables a controlled study of the trade-off between additional training data and speaker overlap across the splits. We evaluate several Whisper- and FastConformer-based models on both corpus versions, including Serialized Output Training (SOT)-based fine-tuning for dialogue transcription. Our results show that the larger corpus is more challenging for models without fine-tuning, whereas SOT-based adaptation yields consistent improvements in WER, CER, cpWER, and cpCER. Overall, BEA-Dialogue+ provides a substantially larger yet still demanding benchmark for Hungarian dialogue ASR, and a practical resource for training and evaluating dialogue transcription systems.

</details>


### [88] [Language Models Can Resolve Reference Compositionally, But It's Not Their Native Strength: The Case of the Personal Relation Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31480)
*Bart Evelo,Meaghan Fowlie,Denis Paperno*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过“个人关系任务”对比发现，LLM在构建语言的结构化语义（内涵）方面表现优异，但在将语言关联至现实世界对象（外延）的能力上远逊于人类，揭示了LLM缺乏指称接地的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨大语言模型是否真正具备语言理解中的语义组合能力，区分语言模型在语义表达的“内涵”（意义结构）与“外延”（世界指代）两个层面的能力表现。

Method: 通过“个人关系任务”（Personal Relation Task）对比大语言模型（LLM）与人类在两种任务上的表现：1. 外延任务（Extensional）：识别短语在具体关系宇宙中对应的人（指代）；2. 内涵任务（Intensional）：将短语转化为结构化逻辑公式（语义合成）。

Result: 实验发现人类与LLM存在互补的强项：人类在外延任务上表现更好，而LLMs则在内涵任务（逻辑结构推导）上表现更佳。

Conclusion: LLM缺乏指称接地（referential grounding）是其未能像人类一样理解语言的关键缺失环节。模型在内涵表达构建上表现出色，但在外延推理（与现实世界关联）上弱于人类。

Abstract: Do neural models, such as Large Language Models, genuinely acquire compositional abilities for interpretation of natural language? When we talk about semantic interpretation, we can distinguish two complementary aspects: establishing what an expression refers to in the world (which we call the Extensional task) and representing its sense in a structured way (which we call the Intensional task). We evaluate LLMs and humans on both tasks in the setting of the Personal Relation Task (Paperno 2022) in which, given a universe of people and their relationships with each other, one is asked to interpret a noun phrase such as "Amber's parent's friend". Here, for the Intensional task, the answer is the formula "friend(parent(amber))", and for the Extensional task, the person. We find that humans and LLMs show opposite strengths: humans perform better on Extensional than Intensional tasks, and LLMs vice versa. Our methodology brings greater nuance to the understanding of compositional abilities in modern machine learning models. Our results support the notion that the lack of referential grounding in LLM training is a crucial missing component in mimicking human-like language understanding.

</details>


### [89] [BenHalluEval: A Multi-Task Hallucination Evaluation Framework for Large Language Models on Bengali](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31483)
*Shefayat E Shams Adib,Ahmed Alfey Sani,Ekramul Alam Esham,Ajwad Abrar,Ishmam Tashdeed,Md Taukir Azam Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对孟加拉语幻觉评估的缺失，本文提出了 BenHalluEval 工具集和 BenHalluScore 指标，通过双轨评估协议揭示了现有 LLM 在孟加拉语场景下存在显著且不均衡的幻觉问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管孟加拉语是全球第六大语言，但目前仍缺乏针对大语言模型（LLM）在该语言下幻觉现象的系统性评估。

Method: 1. 构建 BenHalluEval 框架，涵盖生成式问答、代码混杂问答、摘要和推理四类任务；2. 使用 GPT-4（原文标注 GPT-5.4，应为笔误）基于三大孟加拉语数据集生成 12,000 个幻觉候选样本；3. 采用双轨协议（Track A：真实样本误报率；Track B：幻觉检出率）；4. 提出 BenHalluScore 指标进行联合校准；5. 测试 7 款多语言及孟加拉语中心模型，并评估思维链（CoT）的缓解效果。

Result: 评估显示各模型及任务的 BenHalluScore 值在 7.72% 到 55.42% 之间波动，展现出显著的性能差异；思维链提示虽然改变了响应分布，但未能有效提升幻觉辨析能力。

Conclusion: BenHalluEval 是首个专门针对孟加拉语的幻觉评估基准。研究表明，现有的单轨评估和单纯依赖思维链（CoT）等提示工程方法在处理孟加拉语等低资源语言时表现不足，且各模型在幻觉校准方面存在巨大差异。

Abstract: Despite Bengali being the sixth most spoken language in the world, no prior work has systematically evaluated hallucination in large language models (LLMs) for Bengali. We introduce BenHalluEval, a fine-grained hallucination evaluation framework for Bengali covering four tasks: Generative Question Answering (GQA), Bangla-English Code-Mixed QA, Summarization, and Reasoning. We construct 12,000 hallucinated candidates using GPT-5.4 across twelve task-specific hallucination types, drawn from three existing Bengali datasets, and evaluate seven LLMs spanning reasoning-oriented, multilingual, and Bengali-centric categories under a dual-track protocol that independently measures false-positive rate on ground-truth instances (Track A) and hallucination detection rate on hallucinated candidates (Track B). To jointly penalise both failure modes and prevent inflated scores from uniform response bias, we propose BenHalluScore, a dual-track calibration metric that ranges from 7.72% to 55.42% across models and tasks, revealing substantial variation in hallucination calibration. Chain-of-thought prompting, applied as a mitigation strategy, shifts response distributions without consistently improving hallucination discrimination. BenHalluEval establishes the first dedicated hallucination benchmark for Bengali and highlights the inadequacy of single-track and prompting-only evaluation approaches for low-resource language settings. The dataset and code are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/BanglaHalluEval-EB77.

</details>


### [90] [Are Full Rollouts Necessary for On-Policy Distillation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31490)
*Yaocheng Zhang,Jiajun Chai,Songjun Tu,Yuqian Fu,Xiaohan Wang,Wei Lin,Guojun Yin,Qichao Zhang,Yuanheng Zhu,Dongbin Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究针对在线策略蒸馏中的长部署开销问题，提出了POPD和TOPD策略，通过控制部署轨迹长度，在不损失性能的前提下显著提升了长程推理模型的训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准在线策略蒸馏（OPD）在生成完整长轨迹时计算开销巨大，且早期训练阶段的长轨迹末端容易产生不可靠的教师反馈，导致效率低下。

Method: 提出了两种策略：1. 渐进式策略蒸馏（POPD）：在训练过程中动态扩展部署长度；2. 截断式策略蒸馏（TOPD）：仅在可靠的截断轨迹上进行固定长度的蒸馏。

Result: 在数学推理任务中，POPD将训练效率提升了3倍；TOPD仅使用10%的部署长度即可达到原始OPD的性能，大幅降低了计算成本和显存开销。

Conclusion: 通过控制“部署长度”（rollout horizon）可显著提升策略蒸馏的效率，POPD和TOPD证明了并非总是需要完整的轨迹来获得稳健的学习信号，这种机制是实现大规模推理任务高效训练的重要途径。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) provides dense teacher feedback along rollouts generated by the student and has emerged as a promising post-training paradigm for long-horizon reasoning. However, standard OPD typically generates full rollouts during training, which is computationally expensive and may expose the student to unreliable teacher feedback at late rollout positions, especially during early training. We identify the rollout horizon as a key bottleneck in OPD that substantially impacts training efficiency. Unlike Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR), OPD does not require a complete trajectory or a final answer reward to provide learning signals. This observation suggests that full rollouts may not always be necessary for effective OPD. Motivated by this insight, we propose two simple horizon-control strategies: Progressive OPD (POPD), which gradually expands the rollout horizon during training, and Truncated OPD (TOPD), which permanently performs distillation on reliable truncated rollouts. Experiments on mathematical reasoning show that POPD improves the training efficiency of OPD by up to 3$\times$, while TOPD matches OPD performance using only 10\% of the rollout horizon, leading to substantial wall-clock and memory reductions. These results demonstrate that controlling the rollout horizon offers a simple and practical path to more efficient OPD.

</details>


### [91] [Consolidating Rewarded Perturbations for LLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31494)
*Zheyu Zhang,Shuo Yang,Gjergji Kasneci*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CoRP 是一种通过奖励加权聚合微扰模型来高效微调大模型的方法，它在无需梯度的情况下，将群体优化转化为单一模型的权重更新，从而以极低的推理开销显著提升了基准模型的生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于群体微扰的后训练方法（如 RandOpt）虽然有效，但存在推理时需多次前向传播带来的高昂计算成本，且难以直接应用于自由文本生成任务。研究旨在探索如何将奖励群体合并为一个单一、高效的可部署模型，避免推理时的集成代价。

Method: 提出 CoRP (Consolidating Rewarded Perturbations) 算法，通过奖励加权聚合、兼容性感知重加权以及保留验证门控机制，将多个经过奖励筛选的微扰模型折叠为一个单一的部署模型，无需在训练过程中进行梯度更新。

Result: 在 5 个语言模型（0.5B-8B）和 5 个任务上进行了评估。相较于基准模型，CoRP 平均提升了 8.1 分；在仅使用 RandOpt 推理开销的 1/10 的情况下，取得了超过单次推理 RandOpt 6.5 分的性能，并达到了 50 次前向传播众数投票（majority-vote）集成策略一半以上的性能收益。

Conclusion: CoRP 成功地将群体随机微扰的优势整合进单一模型中，证明了在无需梯度反向传播的情况下，利用预训练模型的低秩扰动空间结构进行高效微调是可行的。它在实现高性能的同时，极大地降低了推理时的计算成本。

Abstract: Post-training of language models is commonly framed as a sample-score-update loop implemented by gradient descent. A recent line of work, exemplified by RandOpt, relocates this loop to weight space, sampling Gaussian perturbations around a pretrained model and ensembling the top-K rewarded specialists at inference. While competitive with PPO and GRPO under matched training compute, this prediction-level ensemble incurs K forward passes per test example and does not extend cleanly to free-form generation. We ask whether the rewarded population can instead be folded into a single deployable model, replacing the inference-time ensemble with one consolidated update. A split-half analysis over 25 model-task pairs reveals reproducible low-rank structure in every case. We turn this geometry into CoRP (Consolidating Rewarded Perturbations), a gradient-free operator that combines reward-weighted aggregation, compatibility-aware reweighting, and a held-out validation gate, with no gradient flowing through the language model. Across five language models from 0.5B to 8B and five tasks covering math, code, and creative writing, CoRP improves the base model by 8.1 points on average. Using one tenth of RandOpt's perturbation budget, CoRP exceeds single-inference RandOpt by 6.5 points and recovers more than half of the gain of the 50-pass majority-vote ensemble, at one forward pass per test example.

</details>


### [92] [Reliable Multilingual Orthopedic Decision Support from Clinical Narratives: Language-Aware Adaptation and Verification-Guided Deferral](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31512)
*Danish Ali,Li Xiaojian,Sundas Iqbal,Farrukh Zaidi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对低资源环境下的多语言骨科笔记分类，提出了一种基于 IndicBERT 的领域适配方法 (IndicBERT-HPA) 及确定性验证机制。研究表明，该方法在准确性与预测可靠性上均显著优于零样本 LLM 和基线模型，为临床智能决策提供了更稳健的路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源医疗环境下面临医疗叙述中专业术语多、混合脚本、证据不全、标签不平衡以及语言特定文档模式等挑战，目前的多语言骨科临床决策支持系统尚不成熟，亟需提高分类的准确性与系统的可靠性。

Method: 1. 提出了一种面向可靠性的多语言骨科决策支持框架；2. 对比了多种架构：任务对齐多语言Transformer、DistilBERT基线、零样本指令调优的LLM以及本文提出的 IndicBERT-HPA；3. 设计了基于 IndicBERT 的领域自适应适配头（Orthopedic adapter heads）；4. 引入了确定性选择性验证层（置信度门控、证据一致性检查、语言风险筛查）以实现明确的预测推迟（deferral）。

Result: 1. 在自然临床分布下，IndicBERT-HPA 表现最佳，Macro-F1 达到 0.8792，Macro-AUROC 为 0.894，AUPRC 为 0.902；2. 零样本 LLM 在封闭集分类任务中表现远逊于任务适配编码器且存在跨语言不稳定性；3. 选择性验证层将分类准确率从 71.5% 显著提升至 84.4%（72.3% 覆盖率条件下）。

Conclusion: 在低资源医疗环境中，针对骨科临床笔记的分类，基于领域适配的 IndicBERT-HPA 模型展现出最优的性能与稳定性。通过引入确定性的选择性验证层（selective-verification layer），结合置信度门控及一致性检查，能够显著提升模型在实际临床应用中的可靠性与安全性（支持在不确定情况下的主动拒绝）。

Abstract: Multilingual orthopedic decision support remains challenging in low-resource healthcare settings, where clinical narratives contain specialized terminology, mixed scripts, incomplete evidence, label imbalance and language-dependent documentation patterns. This article presents a reliability-oriented framework for classifying free-text orthopedic notes in English, Hindi and Punjabi. We compare task-aligned multilingual transformer encoders, a task-fine-tuned DistilBERT baseline, zero-shot instruction-tuned large language models (LLMs) and a domain-adaptive encoder, IndicBERT-HPA. IndicBERT-HPA augments IndicBERT with language-aware orthopedic adapter heads to support clinically relevant multilingual representation learning. Evaluation extends beyond aggregate accuracy to per-class performance, ROC-AUC, AUPRC, expected calibration error, cross-language stability and robustness under controlled balanced and natural-prevalence distributions. The evaluated zero-shot LLMs remain substantially less effective than task-adapted encoders for closed-set classification, with language-dependent instability. Under natural clinical prevalence, IndicBERT-HPA achieves the strongest overall performance, reaching an averaged Macro-F1 of 0.8792, Macro-AUROC of 0.894 and AUPRC of 0.902. We further implement a deterministic selective-verification layer combining confidence gating, evidence-consistency checking and language-risk screening. On a randomly selected held-out 5,000-record subset, it achieves 84.4% selective accuracy and 0.76 selective Macro-F1 at 72.3% coverage, compared with 71.5% accuracy and 0.65 Macro-F1 for accept-all prediction. These results support reliability-oriented multilingual clinical decision support with explicit deferral.

</details>


### [93] [Preference-Aware Rubric Learning for Personalized Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31545)
*Yilun Qiu,Xiaoyan Zhao,Yang Zhang,Yuxin Chen,Cilin Yan,Jiayin Cai,Xiaolong Jiang,Yao Hu,Yoko Yamakata,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 为了解决个性化大模型评估难的问题，该论文提出了 PARL 框架，将个性化评估转化为学习问题，通过从用户历史偏好中学习评估准则，实现了比传统方法更精准、稳定的个性化效果评价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估方法（如自动指标或 LLM-as-a-judge）无法有效捕捉用户长期交互历史中的主观个性化偏好，成为个性化大模型发展的瓶颈。

Method: 提出“个性化评估即学习”（Personalized Evaluation as Learning）范式，并开发了 PARL（Preference-Aware Rubric Learning）框架，通过学习用户历史数据诱导生成偏好感知评估准则，并引入自验证机制和对比强化学习目标来优化评估决策边界。

Result: 实验表明，PARL 能有效诱导出高保真度的评估准则，在识别用户对齐回复方面表现优异，且具有良好的跨用户、跨任务泛化能力，能稳定捕捉风格偏好和评估模式。

Conclusion: PARL 框架通过学习用户特定的评估指标，有效提升了对个性化模型输出的评估能力，能够捕捉细粒度的风格和偏好，为解决个性化对齐评估难题提供了新思路。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) evolve from general-purpose assistants to user-centric agents, personalization has become central to aligning model behavior with individual preferences, making the evaluation of personalized alignment a critical bottleneck. Existing evaluation methods-ranging from automatic metrics to LLM-as-a-judge approaches-fail to capture subjective, user-specific preferences embedded in long-term interaction histories. We identify three essential principles for reliable and effective personalized evaluation: Representativeness, User-Consistency, and Discriminativeness. To address these principles, we introduce Personalized Evaluation as Learning, a paradigm that formulates personalized evaluation as a learning problem rather than a static judgment. Under this paradigm, we propose PARL (Preference-Aware Rubric Learning for Personalized Evaluation), a framework that learns to induce preference-aware evaluation rubrics directly from raw user histories and performs a self-validation mechanism to ensure consistency with the user's preferences. PARL integrates rubric induction with a discriminative reinforcement learning objective that contrasts user-authored responses against competitive personalized model outputs, enabling the learned rubrics to capture precise, user-specific decision boundaries. Experiments on real-world personalized text generation tasks show that PARL consistently induces high-fidelity rubrics that reliably identify user-aligned responses and generalize across users and tasks, while capturing stable stylistic preferences and fine-grained evaluative patterns. To ensure reproducibility, our code is available at https://github.com/SnowCharmQ/PARL.

</details>


### [94] [Semantic Triplet Restoration: A Novel Protocol for Hierarchical Table Understanding in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31550)
*Yibin Zhao,Fangxin Shang,Dingrui Yang,Yuqi Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 为了解决传统表格序列化带来的标记冗余和结构解析困难，本文提出了基于“语义三元组”（STR）的表格表示方法，并通过TripletQL路由器动态筛选相关知识，有效提升了表格问答的效率与准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格问答主流方法依赖HTML/Markdown等标记语言，导致输入Token冗余且增加了大语言模型理解复杂行列映射（Row/Column spans）的难度。

Method: 1. 提出语义三元组恢复（STR）协议：将单元格重写为<项路径, 特征路径, 值>的原子事实，消除对隐式二维布局的依赖。
2. 开发TripletQL：一个轻量级的查询感知路由器，根据问题动态选择相关的三元组子集，降低上下文长度。

Result: 在四个中英文表格问答基准测试上，STR达到了与HTML基准相当或更优的性能，显著减少了输入Token，在处理长表和使用小参数量模型时效果更为突出。

Conclusion: 通过使用显式的语义三元组表示（STR），可以有效克服传统文档序列化（如HTML/Markdown）在表格结构解析上的局限性。该方法在减少输入Token消耗的同时，提升了模型处理复杂表格的性能，特别是在算力受限或针对小模型场景下优势更明显。

Abstract: Table question answering requires models to recover semantic relations encoded implicitly by two-dimensional layout, merged cells, and hierarchical headers. Current pipelines typically use HTML or Markdown as intermediate table representations, but these layout-oriented serializations introduce markup overhead and require large language models to infer header-cell alignments from row and column spans. We propose Semantic Triplet Restoration (STR), a protocol that rewrites each cell as an atomic fact <item path, feature path, value>, where the item path specifies the row-wise entity, the feature path specifies the hierarchical attribute, and the value contains the cell content. We also present TripletQL, a lightweight query-aware router that uses STR to select an appropriate rendering or filtered subset of triplets for each question. Across four Chinese and English table-QA benchmarks, STR matches or improves upon HTML-based baselines while reducing input tokens. The relative benefit grows for smaller language models and longer table contexts, suggesting that explicit semantic representations are especially useful under constrained inference budgets. Code and data are available at https://github.com/Phoenix-ni/STR.git .

</details>


### [95] [What Am I Missing? Question-Answering as Hidden State Probing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31561)
*Chu Fei Luo,Samuel Dahan,Xiaodan Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究提出通过“提问”作为模型推理时的干预手段来提高准确性。研究发现基于隐状态的诊断机制虽然能有效评估推理路径的好坏，但当前的干预策略在处理模型不确定性时，纠错与误伤的概率对等，暴露出大模型自我修正能力的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型在链式思维推理中表现出不稳定性（即便相同输入也会产生不同结果），现有的推理机制研究不足。旨在探究通过提问作为推理时干预手段，是否能挖掘模型隐状态中的有效信号以辅助推理。

Method: 提出了一种“学生-老师”框架，学生通过提问作为推理时干预手段。研究训练了一个探针（probe）分析学生询问前后的隐状态以预测最终准确性，并将其建模为序列决策问题，设计了一种最大化准确率概率的门控策略。

Result: 提出的探针能有效预测推理轨道的正确性；然而，实证结果揭示了诊断与恢复之间的差距，干预策略在尝试纠正错误时，具有同等概率损害原本正确的推理路径。

Conclusion: 研究表明，尽管基于提问的推理干预能有效识别模型状态，但模型在自我修正能力上存在“诊断与恢复”之间的瓶颈，干预措施在纠正错误的同时往往会损害已正确的路径，这对大模型在不确定性下的自我完善能力提出了挑战。

Abstract: Test-time reasoning has become a significant field of study since the introduction of chain-of-thought reasoning in large language models (LLMs). However, the mechanisms of this reasoning process are still under-explored -- from the same input prompt, and even the same partial solution, LLMs can produce varied answers if sampled multiple times. We propose to leverage question-asking as an inference-time intervention that articulates information about the model's hidden state. To achieve that, we present a student-teacher setting where a student asks questions to a teacher. We train a probe on the student's hidden state before and after asking a question and find it is predictive of the trajectory's final correctness, even before generating the teacher's answer. This suggests there is a meaningful signal from the self-diagnosis that occurs during question generation rather than information transfer from the teacher. We then frame question-asking as a sequential decision problem, using this probe as a quality score, and define a gating policy to ask questions that maximize likelihood of correctness. We find that the success of question-asking as an intervention is largely dependent on the model's self-consistency. Our empirical results show a gap between detection and recovery; while our gating policy captures model correctness and uncertainty, interventions are equally likely to harm correct trajectories as they are to recover incorrect ones. This gap between diagnosis and correction has broader implications on language models' capacity for self-refinement under uncertainty.

</details>


### [96] [Disagreeing Rationales: Rethinking Classification and Explainability Evaluation in Hate Speech Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31563)
*Benedetta Muscato,Beiduo Chen,Gizem Gezici,Barbara Plank,Fosca Giannotti*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对NLP主观任务中人类解释的差异性问题，本文提出了一个评估分类与可解释性的统一框架，发现软性标注和理由表征在捕捉人类推理多样性方面显著优于传统的硬性分权方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对于人类标注中的“解释差异性”（token级理由）关注不足，且缺乏对人类标签和理由进行可靠评估与聚合的标准方法，尤其是在主观性强的任务中，无法充分利用人类推理的多样性。

Method: 构建了一个统一的评估框架，重新整合了多种模型、训练策略及损失函数；从预测性、分布性两个维度定义分类指标，从合理性、忠实度、复杂度三个维度定义可解释性指标；并在多种硬/软标签和理由表征空间中进行了系统性实验评估。

Result: 实验结果显示，无论是针对分类性能的度量还是可解释性的度量，软性表征均优于硬性（离散）表征，能够更有效地反映人类认知的差异与丰富性。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一个统一的监督框架，证实了在处理主观NLP任务（如仇恨言论检测）时，软性标签和软性理由表征能更好地捕捉人类认知的多样性，并强调了重新审视现有评估指标必要性。

Abstract: Human disagreement is ubiquitous and well-known in labeling. However, variation in explanations, captured through token-level human rationales, remains far less explored. At the same time, it is unclear how to best evaluate human labels and rationales -- or even how to best aggregate rationales beyond majority vote -- in light of this variation. Yet, rationales may provide additional insights into the richness of human reasoning, that may differ in style, values and interpretations -- especially in subjective NLP tasks like hate speech detection. In this work, we unify diverse models, training strategies, loss functions, and existing evaluation metrics under a single protocol by systematically re-implementing them across different label and rationale representation spaces. Classification metrics are organized around two key properties -- predictive and distributional -- while explainability metrics through three complementary dimensions: plausibility, faithfulness, and complexity. In this unified supervision framework, we evaluate model behavior across classification and explainability metrics, as well as metric sensitivity to the choice of label (hard and soft) and rationale representation space (hard, intermediate and soft). Results show that both hard and soft metrics favor softer representations, highlighting their effectiveness in capturing variation and the need to rethink evaluation in subjective NLP.

</details>


### [97] [What Gets Unmasked First? Trajectory Analysis of Diffusion Models for Graph-to-Text Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31564)
*Qing Wang,Jacob Devasier,Chengkai Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了用于图转文本生成的掩码扩散语言模型（MDLM），发现SFT会干扰模型自然的解码轨迹，进而提出了无需训练的解码修正方案和结合图Transformer的Graph-LLaDA模型，显著提升了生成性能与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图转文本生成模型在处理复杂图结构时存在泛化不足及解码效率问题，作者旨在研究掩码扩散语言模型（MDLM）在这一领域的潜力及改进策略。

Method: 1. 对MDLM生成轨迹进行分析；2. 提出lambda缩放结构解码（lambda-scaled structural decoding）以修正SFT导致的结构性解码偏差；3. 提出Graph-LLaDA模型，将图Transformer编码器集成到LLaDA的解码过程。

Result: 1. 揭示了SFT（监督微调）会过早固定结构性标记导致生成内容偏差的缺陷；2. lambda缩放解码方案在不需训练的情况下提升了9.4 BLEU-4；3. Graph-LLaDA在LAGRANGE基准测试中展现出比传统基线更好的泛化效果。

Conclusion: MDLM在图转文本任务中优于自回归模型，且通过提出的lambda缩放结构解码和Graph-LLaDA模型，能有效解决SFT导致的结构性解码偏差，展现出更好的泛化性能。

Abstract: We present the first systematic study of masked diffusion language models (MDLMs) for graph-to-text generation. We analyze MDLM generation trajectories -- the order in which tokens are unmasked during iterative decoding -- and find that, unlike autoregressive LLMs which generate text linearly, MDLMs naturally prioritize entities first, followed by relational and function words, with structural tokens resolved last. We further identify a previously undocumented failure mode of supervised fine-tuning: SFT disrupts this strategy by prematurely anchoring structural sentence-ending tokens early in the decoding trajectory, effectively fixing the output length which can lead to omitted or hallucinated information. To address this, we propose lambda-scaled structural decoding, a training-free inference-time modification that downweights structural token confidence and recovers +9.4 BLEU-4. Finally, we introduce Graph-LLaDA, which integrates a Graph Transformer encoder into LLaDA's decoding process to explicitly incorporate relational graph structure. Cross-dataset evaluation on LAGRANGE reveals that previous baselines overfit to dataset-specific patterns, while LLM- and MDLM-based approaches generalize significantly better.

</details>


### [98] [LongTraceRL: Learning Long-Context Reasoning from Search Agent Trajectories with Rubric Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31584)
*Nianyi Lin,Jiajie Zhang,Lei Hou,Juanzi Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LongTraceRL 提出了一种通过构建高混淆性任务数据和细粒度实体级过程监督，提升大语言模型长上下文多跳推理能力的新框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的长上下文推理方法在面对高干扰内容时表现不佳，且传统的强化学习（RLVR）存在干扰项混淆性低和奖励信号稀疏（仅关注最终结果，缺乏过程监督）的问题。

Method: 1. 数据构建：利用知识图谱随机游动生成多跳问答，通过构建“层级干扰项”（区分已阅读但未引用与搜索结果中未点击的文档）来增加上下文难度；2. 奖励设计：引入“准则奖励”（rubric reward），利用推理链上的黄金实体对正确回答进行细粒度的过程反馈，并采用“仅正面策略”避免奖励破解。

Result: 在五个长上下文推理基准测试中，LongTraceRL 在 4B 到 30B 参数规模的模型上均显著优于现有强基线模型，成功提高了模型的证据利用率和推理质量。

Conclusion: LongTraceRL 通过引入基于层级干扰项的复杂数据构建和细粒度的实体级过程监督奖励机制，有效提升了大型语言模型在长上下文环境下的推理能力，证明了通过高质量过程监督引导模型进行证据导向推理的优越性。

Abstract: Long-context reasoning remains a central challenge for large language models, which often fail to locate and integrate key information in extensive distracting content. Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has shown promise for this task, yet existing methods are limited by low-confusability distractors and sparse, outcome-only reward signals that cannot supervise intermediate reasoning steps. To address these issues, we introduce \textsc{LongTraceRL}. For data construction, we generate multi-hop questions via knowledge graph random walks and leverage search agent trajectories to build \emph{tiered distractors}: documents the agent read but did not cite (high confusability) and documents that appeared in search results but were never opened (low confusability), producing training contexts that are far more challenging than those built by random sampling or one-shot search. For reward design, we propose a \emph{rubric reward} that uses the gold entities along each reasoning chain as fine-grained, entity-level process supervision. This rubric reward is applied only to responses with correct final answers (positive-only strategy), distinguishing the reasoning quality among correct responses and preventing reward hacking. Experiments on three reasoning LLMs (4B--30B) across five long-context benchmarks demonstrate that \textsc{LongTraceRL} consistently outperforms strong baselines and encourages comprehensive, evidence-grounded reasoning. Codes, datasets and models are available at \href{https://github.com/THU-KEG/LongTraceRL}{https://github.com/THU-KEG/LongTraceRL}.

</details>


### [99] [Language Models Learn Constructional Semantics, Not To Mention Syntax: Investigating LM Understanding of Paired-Focus Constructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31586)
*Wesley Scivetti,Ethan Wilcox,Nathan Schneider,Kanishka Misra,Leonie Weissweiler*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现中等规模的开源模型可以掌握稀有的“成对焦点”语言结构，且其语义理解的习得与世界知识的增长进程相关，晚于语法知识的习得。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨开源大模型是否具备对稀有语言结构（形式与意义的配对）的稳健理解能力，以及此类知识习得背后的学习动力学机制。

Method: 构建了一个用于测试“成对焦点”（如“let alone”）结构的新数据集，通过标量形容词语义和通用世界知识进行评估；对比了不同参数量、架构和预训练数据规模的模型，并分析了具有公开检查点的模型在训练过程中的表现变化。

Result: 中等规模模型已表现出对“成对焦点”结构形式与意义的敏感性，而使用人类规模数据训练的模型在所有意义评估中均表现失败；在训练动力学上，语义理解的习得晚于语法知识，且语义习得与世界知识的提升具有相关性。

Conclusion: 研究表明中等规模的开源模型能够掌握稀有的“成对焦点”结构；此外，该类结构语义的习得与世界知识的增长存在正相关性。

Abstract: Grasping the semantics of rare constructions (form-meaning pairings) has been shown to be a challenging problem that has currently only been solved by the largest LLMs. It remains an open question if open-source models have robust constructional understanding, and if so, what learning dynamics underlie the acquisition of this knowledge. Focusing on a set of rare Paired-Focus constructions in English (e.g. "let alone", "much less"), we construct a novel dataset to test their meanings using both scalar adjectival semantics and general world knowledge. Testing a wide range of models differing in parameter count, architecture, and pretraining dataset size, we find that several modestly sized models are sensitive to both the forms and the meanings of Paired-Focus constructions, though models trained on human-scale data fail at all meaning evaluations. Turning to training dynamics for a set of open-checkpoint models, we find that Paired-Focus understanding emerges later in training than Paired-Focus syntactic knowledge, and that learning of Paired-Focus semantics is correlated with gains in some domains of world knowledge. Overall, our empirical results support the conclusion that modestly sized open-source models can grasp the rare Paired-Focus constructions, and demonstrate a connection between knowledge of Paired-Focus constructions and other meaning domains.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [100] [Listing Even Cycles Faster than the Submodular-Width Barrier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30564)
*Vasileios Nakos,Hung Q. Ngo,Andreas Panayi*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文通过引入非对称超饱和技术，提出了一种改进的$2k$-环列举算法，刷新了保持30年的AYZ算法界的记录，并提供了更适合数据库系统实现的算法方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的AYZ界限（$\tilde{O}(m^{2-1/k}+t)$）在过去30年中一直是$2k$-环列举问题的天花板。随着近年来针对特定环长度（如$C_4, C_6$）的改进，针对一般$2k$-环的复杂性突破成为克服子模宽度（submodular-width）瓶颈的关键一步，而此前的算法方案在通用性上存在滞后。

Method: 提出了一种基于“非对称超饱和”（asymmetric supersaturation）结果的算法。与以往依赖于BFS的复杂图算法不同，该方法通过在多个树分解（tree-decomposition）计划上执行连接和投影操作，从而在数据库系统中更易于实现，同时规避了传统的复杂分析框架限制。

Result: 实现了对所有$k \ge 3$的一般性改进，将$2k$-环列举的时间复杂度下推至$\tilde{O}(m^{(2k^2-k+1)/(k^2+1)}+t)$，解决了该领域长达30年的公开问题。

Conclusion: 作者提出了一种针对$2k$-环（$k \ge 3$）的新列举算法，其时间复杂度为$\tilde{O}(m^{(2k^2-k+1)/(k^2+1)}+t)$，在所有$k \ge 3$的情况下均改进了经典的AYZ界限，打破了长期存在的复杂性瓶颈。此外，该算法由标准的数据库连接和投影操作组成，具有良好的实际可实现性。

Abstract: A classic result of Alon, Yuster, and Zwick (AYZ, Algorithmica 1997) shows that all $2k$-cycles in an $m$-edge graph can be listed in $\tilde O(m^{2-1/k}+t)$ time, where $t$ is the output size. This bound underlies the {\em submodular width} of Marx (JACM 2013) and the PANDA framework of Abo Khamis, Ngo, and Suciu (PODS 2017), which extend AYZ to arbitrary conjunctive queries with degree constraints. A central open question is whether combinatorial algorithms can beat the submodular-width barrier.
  Bringmann and Gorbachev (STOC 2025) gave lower-bound evidence that submodular width may be optimal for general conjunctive queries under combinatorial algorithms. The picture changes for $2k$-cycles on undirected graphs, whose queries have self-joins and symmetric EDBs: recent works improve on AYZ for even-cycle detection and listing. Pinning down the complexity of $C_{2k}$-detection and listing is thus a natural step toward overcoming the submodular-width barrier for such queries.
  For detection, Dahlgaard, Knudsen, and St{ö}ckel (STOC 2017) solved $C_{2k}$-detection in $\tilde O(m^{2k/(k+1)})$ time. Listing is harder. Jin and Xu (STOC 2023), and independently Abboud, Khoury, Leibowitz, and Safier (FSTTCS 2023), listed 4-cycles in $\tilde O(m^{4/3}+t)$ time; Vassilevska~Williams and Westover (ITCS 2025) listed 6-cycles in $\tilde O(m^{8/5}+t)$ time, improving the AYZ bounds of $\tilde O(m^{3/2})$ and $\tilde O(m^{5/3})$. The general case has remained open for 30 years.
  Building on these works, we list $2k$-cycles in $\tilde O(m^{(2k^2-k+1)/(k^2+1)}+t)$ time, improving AYZ for every $k\geq 3$. The key ingredient is an \emph{asymmetric supersaturation} result for even cycles. Our algorithms use only join and project operators over multiple tree-decomposition plans, making them naturally implementable in database systems, in contrast to prior BFS-based graph approaches.

</details>


### [101] [Modeling and Optimization for Massive Data Allocation in Database](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31002)
*Panpan Niu,Boxiang Ren,Hao Wu,Xin Yao*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文针对分布式数据库中的数据放置问题，提出了一种基于归一化割模型与Bregman近端梯度算法的优化方法，有效平衡了负载分布并大幅降低了通信开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分布式数据库场景下，为解决大数据量给在线事务处理（OLTP）系统带来的高通信开销问题，需要一种既能实现负载均衡又能最小化通信代价的数据放置策略。

Method: 受谱聚类中归一化割（Normalized Cut）启发，提出一种新的数据分配模型，将问题形式化为0-1优化问题；利用Bregman近端梯度法（Bregman Proximal Gradient Method）求解松弛后的优化问题。

Result: 实验结果表明，该算法不仅保证了收敛性，还通过平滑舍入策略获得了高质量的离散解，在最小化迁移成本和保持系统负载平衡方面均优于现有的简单分区及元启发式算法。

Conclusion: 该算法通过归一化割模型有效平衡了数据负载与通信开销，通过Bregman近端梯度法实现的松弛优化方案在收敛性上具有保证，且实验表明该方案所得解易于离散化，优于传统的简单分区或元启发式算法。

Abstract: In the era of big data, e-commerce and Internet platforms face the challenge of processing massive amounts of data. However, due to data being scattered across different machines in distributed database, extra communication costs are incurred in gathering relevant data to complete transactions. Without a carefully designed data placement scheme, this cost can severely impact the performance of Online Transaction Processing systems. To meet industry requirements, algorithms that output a data placement scheme that achieves i) data balance and ii) low communication overhead within a fixed period of time are eagerly investigated. Although some existing methods have been studied, they do not adequately meet the aforementioned requirements. In this paper, inspired by the normalized cut of spectral clustering, we introduce a novel model for data allocation problem. The normalized cut reconciles the inherent conflict between the two objectives. Taking into account the variable characteristics of the model, we formulate the problem as a 0-1 optimization problem, and solve the relaxed problem using the Bregman proximal gradient method with guaranteed convergence. The numerical experiments reveal that the convergent solutions can be smoothly rounded to discrete solutions. Furthermore, our algorithm surpasses both simple and meta-heuristic partitioning schemes by minimizing migration costs while maintaining a superior balance.

</details>


### [102] [SpecDB: LLM-Generated Customized Databases via Feature-Oriented Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31097)
*Yunkai Lou,Longbin Lai,Shunyang Li,Zhengping Qian,Ying Zhang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: SpecDB 是一个利用 LLM 自动合成定制化关系型数据库的系统，它通过分解生产系统特征并利用多智能体协作流水线，生成代码规模极小但性能足以媲美主流数据库的按需定制系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流关系型数据库通常提供庞大的通用功能集，而实际工作负载仅使用其中一小部分，导致系统过于臃肿。研究旨在探索是否可以根据特定工作负载的需求，按需生成精简且定制化的数据库。

Method: 1. 系统分解：将9个生产系统分解为10个功能模块及其实现变体；2. 模型建模：使用扩展的 FODA 特征模型（DBGraph）捕捉跨模块依赖；3. 多智能体架构：采用分层模块构建流水线，利用多个专用子智能体（Main、Tester、Architect）进行生成、验证与集成，并通过 Refining Agent 进行迭代修复；4. 端到端流水线：将自然语言工作负载描述转化为具体的实现变体组合。

Result: 生成的数据库（约 2.4 万行 Rust 代码）成功通过了 TPC-C 测试，在 10 个仓库配置下达到 130 tpmC，性能表现与 PostgreSQL 和 MySQL 持平，但代码体积仅为其 3% 左右。

Conclusion: SpecDB 证明了通过 LLM 生成按需定制的数据库是可行的，且在简化代码库的同时表现出与主流数据库（PostgreSQL/MySQL）相当的性能，这为未来构建高度专门化的数据库系统开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Mainstream relational databases ship a uniform feature set across deployments, although individual workloads exercise only a fraction of the available subsystems. We investigate whether a database can instead be generated on demand with a feature set matched to the target workload. We present SpecDB, a system that uses large language models (LLMs) to synthesize customized relational databases. We survey 9 production systems and decompose them into 10 functional modules, each further divided into implementation variants. To capture cross-module dependencies, including cases where implementations in disjoint subtrees must be co-designed, we adopt the FODA feature model and extend it with a cooperate edge, yielding a dependency graph DBGraph. SpecDB operationalizes DBGraph through a layered module-construction pipeline in which each module is generated, validated, and integrated by a dedicated subagent (driven by three inner agents: Main, Tester, Architect), and a Refining Agent that iteratively repairs and tunes the assembled database against a user-supplied refining harness with read-only access to existing database source code. A companion selection component translates a natural-language workload description into a set of implementation variants, providing an end-to-end pipeline from workload description to deployable database. We evaluate SpecDB on TPC-C with BenchmarkSQL. The generated database (23,779 lines of Rust) completes 60-minute TPC-C at 1 and 10 warehouses with zero errors. At 10 warehouses it reaches tpmC=130, compared to 128 for PostgreSQL and 127 for MySQL, with comparable latency at ~3% of their code size. Because the agent operates at module-specification level rather than product source, it can in principle combine techniques across system boundaries. Paired with falling LLM costs, generating a purpose-built database for a target workload is becoming straightforward.

</details>


### [103] [AIM: A practical approach to automated index management for SQL databases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31406)
*Ritwik Yadav,Satyanarayana R. Valluri,Mohamed Zaït*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: AIM 是一款用于 SQL 数据库的工业级自动化索引管理系统，通过指标驱动、可解释的优化策略，实现了索引的动态调整，确保生产工作负载的性能提升且无回归。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对大规模生产环境中的数据库，人工管理和优化二级索引成本高、效率低，且无法动态应对工作负载的变化，因此需要一套能够自动识别有效索引并优化 CPU、I/O 及存储资源使用的系统。

Method: 提出了一种可配置的索引管理系统（AIM），通过指标驱动的机制评估索引效果。其方法论重点包括：快速收敛算法（支持广泛的复合索引）、减少对查询优化器的依赖，以及内置的“无回归”保障机制。

Result: 实验结果显示，AIM 能够快速识别出最有效的索引，生成的物理设计方案接近最优，并成功在大规模生产数据库中实现了自动化运维，显著降低了人工干预。

Conclusion: AIM 是一种经过生产环境大规模验证的索引管理系统，能够实现自动化、快速且具可解释性的索引推荐，有效平衡了性能提升与资源消耗，是工业界实现数据库物理设计自动化的成功范例。

Abstract: This paper describes AIM (Automatic Index Manager), a configurable index management system, which identifies impactful secondary indexes for SQL databases to efficiently use available resources such as CPU, I/O and storage. It has been validated on thousands of databases which support production systems. With AIM, the physical design of the database adapts itself to the changes in the workload.We lay out the end to end design of AIM while calling out the guarantees and tradeoffs associated with our design choices. Some of the salient features of AIM include fast convergence even while recommending wide composite indexes, reduced reliance on the query optimizer and a "no regression" guarantee for production workloads. Each index recommendation from AIM is accompanied with a metrics driven explanation, making it easier to verify machine driven changes.AIM is one of the few industrial strength index recommendation engines that is deployed on production databases at a large scale. The experimental results show that AIM is quick in identifying the most effective indexes and the resulting physical design is close to optimal.

</details>


### [104] [A Theoretical Study of DBLog: Certified Virtual Cuts for a Snapshot-Equivalent Replay of Live Databases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31475)
*Andreas Andreakis*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文为DBLog的变更数据捕获（CDC）机制提供了理论形式化支撑，证明了其在无需物理全量快照的情况下，通过“虚拟切片”技术即可实现与物理快照一致的重放效果，并经Isabelle/HOL机器验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2019年DBLog论文提出了无需锁、无需暂停写入的同步机制（被Debezium和Flink CDC采用），但缺乏对该机制核心逻辑的正式形式化证明。

Method: 引入“认证虚拟切片（Certified Virtual Cut）”作为核心形式化对象，证明了DBLog运行在特定边界和键范围下的重放等价性，并使用Isabelle/HOL对所有证明进行了机器校验。

Result: 形式化证明了DBLog可以在没有单一物理快照读取的情况下，根据切片证据构建出等价的重放状态，并证明了该机制的持续推进（source-side continuation）能力。

Conclusion: 论文通过Isabelle/HOL形式化验证，证明了DBLog无需全物理快照即可构造出与快照等价的重放证书（虚拟切片），从理论上确立了其正确性基础。

Abstract: This paper is a theoretical follow-up to the 2019 DBLog paper, which introduced a change-data-capture (CDC) mechanism for both backfilling a downstream system from a live source database and streaming its ongoing changes, while the source keeps accepting writes. DBLog reads the table in primary-key range scans (chunks) interleaved with the source change log: watermarks locate each chunk in log order, chunk rows become refresh events, and CDC events repair stale chunk observations. The mechanism requires no table lock, no pause in writes, and no global read transaction, and has since been adopted by Debezium and Apache Flink CDC.
  The 2019 paper described the mechanism operationally but did not formalize its correctness object. This paper formalizes that object: DBLog constructs a snapshot-equivalent replay certificate without a single physical snapshot read.
  The central formal object is a certified virtual cut: a finite evidence bundle whose certified replay reaches the same per-key state as the source at a chosen frontier on a chosen key scope. A virtual cut is extensional: replay equality at a frontier, not a physical snapshot read, and does not assert one source timestamp across chunk rows. The paper proves per-key replay equality for every wellformed DBLog run at its frontier and scope, and that an accepted certificate, evaluated against faithful source observation, witnesses such a run and yields a virtual cut. It also proves a source-side continuation result: on the same scope, an established cut advances to later frontiers by appending the scope-filtered faithful CDC segment committed in between. Each result is Conditional on premises stated where the result appears. Whole-table correctness, exactly-once delivery, sink-state convergence, and transfer to named deployments are not consequences of certificate acceptance alone. All proofs are machine-checked in Isabelle/HOL.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [105] [FREESS: A Web-Based Educational Simulator for a RISC-V-Inspired Superscalar Processor with Tomasulo-Style Dynamic Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30377)
*Roberto Giorgi,Miquel Moretó Planas*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: FREESS 是一款开源且高度可视化的 RISC-V 超标量模拟器，专为教学设计，通过直观呈现 Tomasulo 机制及关键硬件结构的状态演变，帮助学生深入理解指令级并行技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决计算机体系结构教学中，由于超标量处理器流水线逻辑复杂、状态难以直观观察而导致学生难以理解指令级并行（ILP）机制的问题。

Method: 开发了一款基于 RISC-V 架构的超标量处理器模拟器，采用 Tomasulo 算法，通过文本化界面实时呈现寄存器重命名、指令窗口、重排序缓冲区（ROB）、加载/存储队列等关键硬件状态，并支持动态调整微架构参数（如发射宽度、队列大小、执行延迟）。

Result: 该工具已成功应用于高等计算机体系结构课程教学长达 15 年，能够直观地展示硬件状态的演变，支持多种组织架构的对比实验，且已在 GitHub 开源。

Conclusion: FREESS 通过提供一个开源、可配置且可视化程度高的 RISC-V 风格超标量处理器模拟环境，有效弥补了计算机体系结构教学中复杂硬件逻辑难以演示的弊端，已被证明是该领域长期有效的教学工具。

Abstract: FREESS (Free Educational Superscalar Simulator) is an open-source teaching environment for instruction-level parallelism in a RISC-V-inspired superscalar processor. It provides a compact, cycle-by-cycle view of register renaming, issue, execution, write-back, commit, and memory ordering in a Tomasulo-style machine. The simulator exposes the register map, free pool, instruction window, reorder buffer, and load/store queues in one textual representation, so the evolution of the hardware state can be followed on screen and reproduced on paper. Runtime parameters such as issue width, queue sizes, and functional-unit latencies can be changed easily, enabling direct comparison among alternative superscalar organizations. The tool has supported Advanced Computer Architecture teaching for about fifteen years and is publicly available on GitHub.

</details>


### [106] [Memory-Bound but Not Bandwidth-Limited: The Physical AI Inference Gap in Batch-1 LLM Decode](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30571)
*Josef Chen*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 针对物理AI的batch-1解码任务，本文研究发现高性能GPU不仅受内存带宽瓶颈限制，还深受启动侧开销影响；单纯的内存压缩（量化）若无高效Runtime支持，难以带来线性性能收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理AI系统（如机器人、自动驾驶）的batch-1解码任务常被视为内存带宽受限（memory-bandwidth-bound），但作者观察到高性能GPU在高带宽下并未获得预期的性能线性提升，旨在探索隐藏的性能瓶颈及其部署启示。

Method: 在四种不同的NVIDIA GPU（H100, A100, L40S, L4）上，针对7-8B参数的GQA变换器模型，在不同上下文长度（2048-16384）下进行batch-1解码测试；通过对比带宽理论上限与实际表现，并利用CUDA Graphs A/B测试分析延迟瓶颈；最后评估了多种量化方案（bnb-nf4, AutoAWQ+Marlin, GPTQ+ExLlamaV2）在实现内存压缩时的性能表现。

Result: 研究发现，带宽利用率随GPU峰值带宽上升而下降（H100仅达内存理论上限的27%，而L4达81%）。CUDA Graphs测试证实在H100上存在显著的启动侧开销，通过优化可改善约1.26倍延迟。实验还证明，单纯追求量化模型权重并不一定能等价提升速度，只有当运行时环境（如使用ExLlamaV2）能有效利用量化优势时，才能真正降低解码延迟。

Conclusion: 物理AI系统在batch-1解码任务中，不仅受限于内存带宽，还受到显著的“启动侧开销”（launch-side overhead）限制。在高性能GPU（如H100）上，这种计算与调度开销变得突出，导致高带宽内存的优势无法完全转化为延迟降低。因此，软件层面的运行时优化（如CUDA Graphs和高性能核函数）对于发挥硬件潜力至关重要。

Abstract: Physical AI systems, including robots, autonomous vehicles, embodied agents and edge copilots, often run a different inference workload from cloud LLM serving: single-stream, batch-1 autoregressive decode, where one robot, camera feed or user session waits on the next token. This workload is usually described as memory-bandwidth-bound. Each decode step streams model weights and the active KV cache, so latency should scale with peak HBM bandwidth. We show that this account is true but incomplete. We measure batch-1 decode for three 7 to 8B-class GQA transformers across four NVIDIA GPUs: H100 SXM5, A100-80GB SXM4, L40S and L4. We evaluate context lengths from 2048 to 16384, producing 44 valid cells under a controlled bf16 SDPA setup. The achieved fraction of peak HBM bandwidth falls as peak bandwidth rises. On the headline Qwen-2.5-7B ctx=2048 cell, an L4 reaches roughly 81 percent of its analytic memory floor, while an H100 reaches only 27 percent. Physical-AI decode is memory-dominated, but faster memory does not translate into proportional latency gains. We test the missing term with a CUDA Graphs A/B experiment. On H100 at ctx=2048, CUDA Graphs improves decode latency by 1.259x across N=10 fresh sessions, with a 95 percent bootstrap confidence interval of 1.253 to 1.267. On L4, the same intervention gives only 1.028x. This isolates a launch-side overhead that becomes visible on fast GPUs but remains mostly hidden on slower, bandwidth-bound GPUs. The deployment implication is that memory savings matter only when the runtime realises them. On L4, bf16 decode sits close to the memory floor, but common quantised paths do not recover the expected 4x weight-traffic reduction: bnb-nf4 reaches 59.36 ms/step and AutoAWQ+Marlin reaches 45.24 ms/step from a 62.32 ms bf16 baseline. GPTQ+ExLlamaV2, with Ada-tuned int4 kernels, reaches 17.36 ms/step.

</details>


### [107] [A Reconfigurable Computing In-Memory Macro with Charge-sharing-based Weighted Accumulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30814)
*Junyi Yang,Shuai Dong,Zhengnan Fu,Hongyang Shang,Arindam Basu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 针对SRAM存内计算的ADC开销、多位输入延迟及读位线电压限制，本文提出了一种支持1-7b输入和灵活权重配置的存内计算架构，通过新型IMADC、BSCHA及双8T存储单元实现了计算效率与性能的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现有SRAM存内计算面临的三大关键瓶颈：模数转换器（ADC）面积开销大、多位输入处理延迟高、以及读位线（RBL）可用电压范围有限。

Method: 1. 采用了一种面积仅占3%的紧凑型存内模数转换器（IMADC）；2. 开发了位切分组合硬件加速器（BSCHA），优化了多位输入下的延迟；3. 设计了双8T存储单元，支持三元权重存储及去耦读路径，并结合字线欠驱动共源共栅（cascode）技术。

Result: 1. IMADC功耗与面积效率提升9倍；2. BSCHA相较于传统PWM和位切分模式，延迟分别降低了1.9倍和6.6倍；3. 电流线性度提升7倍，可用读位线电压范围扩大了3.5倍；支持1-7b输入、2-4b权重及1-7b输出的高度可配置性。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种面向SRAM存内计算（CIM）的集成方案，通过创新的电路架构显著提升了系统效率、延迟表现、线性度及动态范围，为实现高精度、高灵活性的存内计算提供了有效路径。

Abstract: SRAM-based analog computing-in-memory demonstrates outstanding efficiency. However, it faces three critical challenges: significant ADC overhead, high latency for multi-bit inputs, and limited read bitline voltage. To address these issues, this work proposes a multi-bit highly reconfigurable 256x128 in-memory computing array supporting 1-7b input, 2-4b weight, and 1-7b output. Three key innovations are introduced: 1) The IMADC occupies only 3% area overhead, achieving a 9x improvement compared to previous IMADC; 2) The BSCHA reduces latency by 1.9x and 6.6x compared to traditional pulse-width modulation (PWM) and bit-slicing modes, respectively; 3) A dual-8T bitcell enabling ternary weight storage through a decoupled read path, integrated with a read wordline under-driven cascode technique, improves linearity of unit discharge current by 7x and increases the usable read bitline voltage by 3.5x.

</details>


### [108] [HE^2: A Communication-Light Heterogeneous Architecture for Efficient Fully Homomorphic Encryption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31004)
*Shangyi Shi,Husheng Han,Zhaoxuan Kan,Yinghao Yang,Jianan Mu,Tenghui Hua,Ge Yu,Xinyao Zheng,Ling Liang,Zidong Du,Xing Hu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 针对 CKKS 同态加密在加速器设计中通信瓶颈严重的问题，提出了 HE^2 异构加速方案，通过计算图级优化融合算子及采用组级流水线架构，高效解决了通信延迟问题，显著提升了算力性能与能效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CKKS 方案因其计算密集型（ComOps）和内存密集型（MemOps）的双重特性，导致现有 ASIC 或 NMP 加速器面临硬件开销大、效率低的问题；而在采用混合架构时，密钥切换（Keyswitch）算子导致的频繁通信成了性能瓶颈。

Method: 1. 计算图（DFG）级优化：通过识别并融合并行的密钥切换块，利用提升算法（hoisting algorithm）减少 ModUp/ModDown 与 MemOps 之间的通信频率；2. 异构体系结构协同设计：采用组级流水线执行模式，利用分解组间的隐性并行性来隐藏通信延迟。

Result: 相比目前先进的加速器，HE^2 实现了 1.66 倍的速度提升，能效延迟面积积（EDAP）降低了 9.23 倍，且通信停顿时间仅占总延迟的 6.67%。

Conclusion: 提出了一种名为 HE^2 的异构全同态加密加速器，通过 DFG 级优化和体系结构协同设计，显著优化了密文计算中的通信瓶颈，实现了性能和能效的提升。

Abstract: CKKS, an emerging fully homomorphic encryption (FHE) scheme, has been promising in privacy-preserving applications by enabling SIMD fixed-point computations on ciphertexts. Despite its strong security guarantees, CKKS involves both compute-intensive operators (ComOps) with high computational cost and memory-intensive operators (MemOps) with large memory footprints, making existing ASIC-based or NMP-based acceleration approaches suffer from high hardware overhead and limited efficiency. This observation motivates the integration of the architectural advantages of both paradigms into a heterogeneous xPU (ASIC)-xMU (NMP) architecture. However, in such a design, frequent and long-latency heterogeneous communication caused by the dominant keyswitch operator remains a key performance bottleneck.
  In this paper, we propose $HE^2$, a communication-light xPU-xMU heterogeneous FHE accelerator with dataflow graph (DFG) optimization and architecture co-design. First, we observe that the majority of communication arises at the interface between ModUp/ModDown and neighboring MemOps. To address this, we propose a DFG-level optimization framework to fully exploit the ModUp/ModDown reduction potential of the hoisting algorithm by identifying parallel keyswitch blocks and fusing them for reduced communication frequency. Second, we design an efficient heterogeneous architecture that adopts a group-level pipelined execution to effectively hide communication latency by leveraging the inherent parallelism across decomposed groups. End-to-end evaluation results show that $HE^2$ achieves 1.66$\times$ speedup and 9.23$\times$ lower EDAP (Energy-Delay-Area Product) compared to the state-of-the-art accelerator, with communication stalls accounting for only 6.67% of the total latency.

</details>


### [109] [MixFP4: Enhancing NVFP4 with Adaptive FP4/INT4 Block Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31035)
*Jiaxiang Zou,Yonghao Chen,Ruilong Wu,Xinyu Chen*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: MixFP4提出了一种无需变动现有的块缩放GEMM路径，通过在块级别混合使用两种微格式，并利用现存缩放因子的闲置位编码，以极低硬件代价解决了单一FP4格式量化精度不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型规模扩大，块缩放低精度格式（如NVFP4）虽提升了性能，但单一的FP4微格式无法适应异构张量统计特征的差异，导致量化精度损失。

Method: 1. 引入MixFP4：在现有NVFP4框架下，每个块（block）在两种FP4微格式（E2M1和E1M2）中进行选择；2. 元数据复用：通过复用FP8 E4M3块缩放因子中的符号位来编码格式选择，实现零额外元数据开销；3. 统一解码：将两种微格式在计算单元内统一解码为E2M2格式，避免了数据路径的冗余。

Result: 在大模型任务中，MixFP4在精度上优于标准NVFP4基准，硬件实现开销极低（面积增加3.1%，功耗增加1.5%）。

Conclusion: MixFP4通过在块级别引入双微格式选择机制，在保持与现有体系结构兼容性的同时，显著提升了低精度量化的鲁棒性和准确性，且仅带来极小的硬件开销。

Abstract: As large language models continue to scale, fine-grained block-scaled low-precision formats such as NVFP4 are increasingly adopted for their substantial throughput and memory benefits. However, a single FP4 micro-format often mismatches heterogeneous block-level tensor statistics. To address this without changing the standard block-scaled MMA/GEMM execution path, we propose MixFP4, a mixed micro-format extension to NVFP4 that selects between two stored FP4 micro-formats (E2M1 and E1M2) per block. MixFP4 reuses NVFP4's scale hierarchy and encodes the format choice with zero additional metadata by repurposing the sign bit of the FP8 E4M3 block scale. By decoding both micro-formats into a unified internal E2M2 compute representation, MixFP4 avoids datapath duplication. Across representative LLM families, MixFP4 improves FP4 quantization robustness and accuracy over NVFP4 baselines with modest tensor-core overhead (3.1\% area, 1.5\% power).

</details>


### [110] [TRINE: A Token-Aware, Runtime-Adaptive FPGA Inference Engine for Multimodal AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22867)
*Hyunwoo Oh,Hanning Chen,Sanggeon Yun,Yang Ni,Suyeon Jang,Behnam Khaleghi,Fei Wen,Mohsen Imani*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: TRINE 是一款统一 FPGA 加速器与编译器，通过创新的多模式计算引擎和依赖感知任务编排，实现在单个比特流内高效处理复杂的跨模态模型，显著降低延迟并提升能效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合多模态模型（ViTs、CNNs、GNNs、NLP 等）具有迥异的计算与内存访问模式，导致其在嵌入式平台上难以满足实时性要求。现有的硬实时约束与多模态模型的多样化需求存在冲突。

Method: 1. 统一算子映射：将不同模型的层统一为 DDMM/SDDMM/SpMM 算子；2. 模式切换架构：设计可运行时切换的 PE 阵列（支持脉动阵列、SIMD、可路由加法树）；3. 硬件优化技术：集成宽度匹配的 top-k 单元（用于 token 剪枝）及依赖感知层卸载（DALO，用于任务覆盖）；4. 编译器：支持在单一比特流（Single-bitstream）下实现端到端推理，无需重配置。

Result: 在 Alveo U50 和 ZCU104 上评估显示：与 RTX 4090 相比延迟降低 22.57 倍，与 Jetson Orin Nano 相比降低 6.86 倍（功耗 20-21W）；Token 剪枝提升最高达 7.8 倍；DALO 技术提升吞吐量最高 79%；量化后精度损失低于 2.5%。

Conclusion: TRINE 通过统一的硬件架构与编译器优化，解决了多模态模型部署在嵌入式平台上的计算/内存分配差异，在保持高精度的同时，实现了超低延迟与高能效，为跨模态（视觉、语言、图模型）推理提供了一种通用的硬件方案。

Abstract: Multimodal stacks that mix ViTs, CNNs, GNNs, and transformer NLP strain embedded platforms because their compute/memory patterns diverge and hard real-time targets leave little slack. TRINE is a single-bitstream FPGA accelerator and compiler that executes end-to-end multimodal inference without reconfiguration. Layers are unified as DDMM/SDDMM/SpMM and mapped to a mode-switchable engine that toggles at runtime among weight/output-stationary systolic, 1xCS SIMD, and a routable adder tree (RADT) on a shared PE array. A width-matched, two-stage top-k unit enables in-stream token pruning, while dependency-aware layer offloading (DALO) overlaps independent kernels across reconfigurable processing units to sustain utilization. Evaluated on Alveo U50 and ZCU104, TRINE reduces latency by up to 22.57x vs. RTX 4090 and 6.86x vs. Jetson Orin Nano at 20-21 W; token pruning alone yields up to 7.8x on ViT-heavy pipelines, and DALO contributes up to 79% throughput improvement. With int8 quantization, accuracy drops remain <2.5% across representative tasks, delivering state-of-the-art latency and energy efficiency for unified vision, language, and graph workloads-in one bitstream.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [111] [FOSTER: First-order Dataset Distillation for Text-based Sequential Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30772)
*Hung Vinh Tran,Tong Chen,Xinyi Gao,Junliang Yu,Julien Monteil,Hongzhi Yin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: FOSTER是一种针对文本序列推荐的高效数据集蒸馏方法，通过一阶优化和随机采样技术，克服了传统蒸馏方法在复杂文本编码任务中的算力瓶颈，实现了小样本下的高性能模型训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于文本的序列推荐系统模型训练开销巨大，而现有的数据集蒸馏方法在处理大规模离散物品池及基于大模型的编码时面临计算效率瓶颈。

Method: 提出了一种名为FOSTER的框架，包含三个核心组件：1) 随机物品子集采样（降低编码开销）；2) 轨迹锚定参数重置的一阶优化（取代昂贵的双层梯度计算）；3) 语义相似物品共现正则化（提升合成序列质量）。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上的实验表明，FOSTER在性能上显著优于现有的数据集蒸馏和核心集选择基线方法，在极小合成数据集规模下保持了高质量的推荐准确率。
