Conclusion: FOSTER通过三种创新机制成功解决了基于文本的序列推荐中数据集蒸馏的难点，能够以极少的样本量（20条序列）实现接近全量数据集的推荐性能，同时显著降低了训练的时间与计算成本。

Abstract: Text-based sequential recommender systems, while greatly improving recommendation accuracy by incorporating item contexts, are undeniably more expensive to train. By condensing a large dataset into a compact set of synthetic samples for model training, dataset distillation offers a promising solution. However, its adoption in text-based sequential recommendation is non-trivial given the large pool of discrete items. This challenge is further compounded by language model-based item encoding, which makes bi-level optimization commonly used in dataset distillation prohibitively expensive. To this end, we propose First-order dataset distillation for Text-based Sequential Recommendation (FOSTER), which facilitates effectiveness and efficiency via three novel components: (1) stochastic item subset sampling that replaces costly full-corpus embedding extraction at each distillation step; (2) first-order optimization with trajectory-anchored parameter reset to avoid expensive bi-level gradient computation; and (3) regularization that explicitly promotes co-occurrence between semantically similar items in the synthetic sequences. Extensive experiments on three benchmarks show that FOSTER consistently outperforms existing dataset distillation and coreset selection baselines, approximating full-dataset performance using as few as 20 synthetic interaction sequences.

</details>


### [112] [On the impact of retrieved content representations in RAG Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30790)
*Jonathan J Ross,Bevan Koopman,Anton van der Vegt,Guido Zuccon*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该研究通过对比十四种文档转换方法发现，RAG提升生成质量的核心不在于复杂的文档重构或查询优化，而在于转换过程是否能够有效保留原始文档中的关键答案信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的RAG流水线多沿用针对人类阅读设计的检索组件，缺乏对“何种文档表示方式更适合LLM作为消费者”的系统性研究，且以往研究多孤立地分析单一转换方法的效果，未明确特征要素的重要性。

Method: 在固定的检索系统基础上，对检索到的文档进行了十四种不同的表现形式转换（包括选择、摘要和重构，以及查询相关与无关的变体）。在四个不同的生成器上评估问答准确率，并定量分析了每种转换后的“答案保留率”（即转换后文档是否仍包含有效答案）。

Result: 实验发现“答案保留率”是衡量生成器准确性的主要决定因素。当答案保留率高时，文档的语言风格、结构等特征对最终生成质量的提升非常有限，表明以往研究所谓的“机制优化”可能本质上是提高了答案的保留度。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了检索文档的“答案保留率（Answer Retention）”是决定RAG系统生成准确性的核心因素，而文档的具体措辞、结构、长度或查询相关性等特征在答案得到有效保留的前提下，对于生成准确性的影响相对较小。未来的研究应将“答案保留率”作为评估检索文档转换方法的基准。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) supplements a language model's input with retrieved documents, yet most RAG pipelines inherit retrieval components designed for human readers. How retrieved content should be represented when the consumer is a large language model (LLM) rather than a human is less well understood. Recent work has proposed transformations of retrieved content and identified properties that affect generation, but each examines a single transformation or property in isolation, leaving open which features of a document's representation matter most. We address this with a controlled comparison: holding retrieval fixed, we vary only the representation of retrieved documents, comparing an original baseline against thirteen transformations spanning selection, summarisation, and reformulation, in query-dependent and query-independent variants. Across these fourteen representations we measure question-answering accuracy for four generators, and for each representation we also measure answer retention: whether a known answer-bearing document still supports its answer after transformation. We find that answer retention is the primary determinant of generator accuracy; notably, when retention is high, a representation's wording, structure, length, and query-dependence have limited effect. This suggests that accuracy gains attributed to specific mechanisms in prior work may be partly explained by how well those mechanisms preserve answer-bearing content, an attribution that cannot be settled without controlling for retention.

</details>


### [113] [Inference-Free Multimodal Learned Sparse Retrieval for Production-Scale Visual Document Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30917)
*Gyu-Hwung Cho,Youngjune Lee,Kiyoon Jeong,Siyoung Lee,Sanggyu Han,Hervé Dejean,Stéphane Clinchant,Seung-won Hwang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: V-SPLADE 是首个无需推理的稀疏视觉文档检索系统，通过引入标题监督机制解决了视觉表征的词汇关联问题，实现了在保持高效检索的同时，性能超越密集检索模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉文档检索方法存在缺口：基于VLM的密集/多向量模型检索效果好但推理成本高（需神经查询编码）；基于OCR或标题的BM25方法无需神经推理，但受限于文本提取/生成的时间开销且效果一般。该研究旨在开发一种既能支持大规模检索、又无需在线神经推理的稀疏检索系统。

Method: 提出了 V-SPLADE 模型，核心技术是“标题门控令牌监督（caption-gated token supervision）”，即在训练阶段利用预训练视觉-语言模型（VLM）生成的文本标题作为词汇线索，指导视觉模型学习如何更准确地定位和激活文档图像中相关的词汇维度，实现高效的稀疏向量表示。

Result: 在六个视觉文档检索基准上，V-SPLADE 的平均 NDCG@5 比同规模的密集模型高出 13.8pp，比基于OCR/标题的BM25基准高出 6.3pp；在 18.7M 文档规模下，R@5 指标达到了同规模密集基线的两倍以上。

Conclusion: V-SPLADE 成功解决了大规模视觉文档检索中“高性能”与“无需神经推理”难以兼得的矛盾，通过引入监督信号增强了视觉文档的稀疏表示能力，在保持高效检索的同时超越了现有的密集检索模型及基于传统OCR的BM25方法。

Abstract: As large-scale visual-document corpora such as arXiv papers and enterprise PDFs continue to grow, visual-document retrieval has gained increasing attention; yet it still lacks a deployable system that lexically indexes visual documents to serve queries without neural encoding at scale. Existing methods either achieve strong retrieval quality with VLM-based dense or multi-vector models but require neural query encoding at serving time, or avoid query encoding with OCR- or caption-based BM25 at the cost of time-consuming text extraction or generation. To fill this missing serving regime, we present V-SPLADE, an inference-free sparse retriever for visual-document retrieval. However, such inference-free multimodal learned sparse retrieval systems remain underexplored and have not yet shown dense-level effectiveness under high sparsity. We attribute this limitation to a lexical grounding problem: visual sparse representations often fail to capture the lexical content embedded in document images. To address this problem, we introduce caption-gated token supervision, a training-only signal that uses VLM-generated captions as lexical cues to activate retrieval-relevant vocabulary dimensions. With this supervision, V-SPLADE improves average NDCG@5 across six visual-document retrieval benchmarks by +13.8pp over the same-scale dense baseline and by up to +6.3pp over OCR- or caption-based BM25 baselines. On an 18.7M-document corpus, it more than doubles R@5 over the same-scale dense baseline and further improves competing retrievers through score fusion by up to +2.4pp R@5. Code will be released soon at https://github.com/naver/v-splade.

</details>


### [114] [Graph-GRPO: Dependency-Aware Credit Assignment for Generative E-commerce Search Relevance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31003)
*Jiarui Che,Yifei Chen,Zhixing Tian,Chenyang Wang,Ziguang Cheng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对电商搜索相关性建模，本文提出了 Graph-GRPO，通过将推理步骤构建为依赖图并优化信用分配机制，解决了传统 RL 方法在复杂推理反馈中的痛点，显著提升了模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的强化学习方法在处理多组件推理时，往往难以区分长推理链中各步骤的正确性（信用分配偏差），导致无法有效优化结构化的相关性判断。

Method: 提出 Graph-GRPO 方法，将思维链（CoT）推理步骤建模为图结构，通过依赖边传播结果级奖励以实现细粒度的信用分配；引入主损失驱动的控制器调整传播系数；并结合 CoT 随机掩码初始化和基于节点的蒸馏技术。

Result: 在领先的电商平台的离线评估和在线 A/B 测试中，Graph-GRPO 显著提升了相关性分类指标及关键用户参与指标。

Conclusion: Graph-GRPO 通过引入基于推理依赖图的信用分配机制，有效解决了复杂推理链中细粒度反馈不足的问题，为大型语言模型在电商搜索相关性任务中的应用提供了一种高效的优化框架。

Abstract: Search relevance modeling is a core task in e-commerce search systems, assessing how well a user query matches candidate products. Rather than relying on a single holistic matching signal, relevance judgment often requires structured reasoning over query understanding, product understanding, and facet-level matching. With large language models (LLMs), this process is increasingly formulated as chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning and optimized with reinforcement learning (RL). However, existing RL methods mainly rely on outcome-level rewards and treat the entire reasoning chain as a single optimization unit. This makes it difficult to distinguish faulty reasoning steps from correct intermediate ones, leading to misaligned credit assignment. Although process-reward methods provide denser supervision, they often treat reasoning steps independently and ignore dependency-driven error propagation, making responsibility attribution difficult and limiting the optimization of structured relevance reasoning. We propose Graph-GRPO, a graph-structured extension of GRPO for multi-component relevance reasoning. Graph-GRPO constructs a relevance reasoning dependency graph, where CoT steps are modeled as nodes and their logical dependencies as edges. It propagates outcome-level rewards over the graph to derive step-level credit signals, enabling more accurate fine-grained credit assignment. We further introduce a main-loss-driven controller that adaptively adjusts edge-wise credit-propagation coefficients. Together with CoT random masking for supervised policy initialization and graph-node-based multi-head distillation, we build a trainable and deployable framework for generative relevance modeling. Extensive offline evaluations and online A/B tests on a leading e-commerce platform demonstrate that the Graph-GRPO-based framework improves relevance classification metrics and key engagement metrics.

</details>


### [115] [Fighting Numerical Hallucinations via Data-centric Compilation for Online Financial QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31064)
*Hao Chen,Xing Tang,Qirui Liu,Weijie Shi,Shiwei Li,Fuyuan Lyu,Weihong Luo,Xiku Du,Xiuqiang He*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对金融问答中数值推理幻觉问题，本文提出了一种数据驱动的“推理编译器”框架（DCRC），通过对抗训练和程序合成技术，确保了金融决策中数值推理的可审计性与准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大型语言模型在金融问答（FinQA）中存在数值推理幻觉，导致在金融等高风险领域应用不可靠；现有的检索增强生成（RAG）方法在面对噪声、计算脆弱性和可审计性方面存在不足，且孤立优化检索器或生成器的方法难以有效解决这些问题。

Method: 提出了“数据驱动推理编译器”（DCRC）框架，包含三个核心阶段：1) 对抗性数据构建，引入受控噪声以增强鲁棒性；2) 多阶段训练，培养具备证据审计与程序合成能力的“数据中心化结构智能体”（DSA）；3) 编译及执行推理，将查询和文档转化为可验证的执行程序。

Result: 在多个离线基准测试上表现优异，并在真实的在线金融问答系统中得到了验证，证明了框架能够确保数值推理的准确性和可靠性。

Conclusion: DCRC 通过以数据为中心的方法，通过显式的程序合成与执行，成功解决了金融问答中数值推理幻觉的问题，实现了兼具可信度和可审计性的金融问答系统。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have significantly advanced online data services, particularly in the domain of financial question answering (FinQA). However, such systems remain susceptible to numerical reasoning hallucinations, which critically undermine reliability in high-stakes financial applications. Although retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) has been widely adopted to ground responses in external knowledge, it introduces three persistent challenges: noise sensitivity, calculation fragility, and an auditability crisis. Existing model-centric approaches, which primarily focus on optimizing either the retriever or generator in isolation, still struggle to address these issues in an integrated manner. In this work, we pioneer a data-centric paradigm and propose a novel framework, the Data-centric Reasoning Compiler (DCRC). The framework operates through three cohesive phases: (1) adversarial data construction, which synthesizes training examples with controlled noise to teach robustness; (2) multi-stage training that cultivates a Data-centric Structuring Agent (DSA) capable of explicit evidence auditing and program synthesis; and (3) a compile-and-execute inference process, where the DSA transforms user queries and retrieved documents into verifiable, executable reasoning programs. This data-driven framework ensures faithful numerical reasoning by design. We conduct extensive experiments on established offline benchmarks and further validate our framework through deployment in a real-world online financial QA system.

</details>


### [116] [MIMO: Multilingual Information Retrieval via Monolingual Objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31171)
*Youngjoon Jang,Seongtae Hong,Heuiseok Lim*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: MIMO框架通过知识蒸馏与跨语言对比学习相结合，解决了多语言检索中对齐与均匀性的权衡问题，显著提升了跨语言信息检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有嵌入模型在多语言信息检索（MLIR）场景下表现退化；且直接应用对比学习容易导致语言聚类现象，并引起跨语言对齐与语义空间均匀性之间的权衡冲突。

Method: 提出了一个两阶段框架MIMO：（1）利用高性能教师模型的英语语义空间作为锚点，通过知识蒸馏对学生模型进行跨语言对齐初始化；（2）通过联合优化蒸馏损失与跨语言对比损失，提升检索区分度的同时保持对齐效果。

Result: 在多个MLIR及多单语基准测试中，MIMO一致优于现有的跨语言训练基线模型，并与参数规模相似或更大的现成模型相比具有竞争力。

Conclusion: MIMO通过引入蒸馏与对比学习结合的联合优化策略，成功缓解了跨语言检索中对齐与均匀性之间的权衡矛盾，为多语言信息检索（MLIR）提供了一种高效且性能优越的训练范式。

Abstract: Multilingual Information Retrieval (MLIR) reflects real-world search environments in which queries and relevant documents may appear in different languages within a mixed-language corpus. However, existing embedding models are primarily optimized for Multi-Monolingual retrieval and their performance often degrades in MLIR settings. Moreover, directly applying conventional contrastive learning to MLIR can exacerbate language clustering and expose a trade-off between cross-lingual alignment and embedding uniformity. To address these limitations, we propose MIMO: Multilingual Information Retrieval via Monolingual Objectives, a two-stage framework that uses a stable English semantic space from a high-performing teacher model as an anchor. MIMO first initializes the student model's cross-lingual alignment through knowledge distillation, and then jointly optimizes distillation and cross-lingual contrastive learning to improve retrieval discrimination while preserving alignment. Extensive experiments show that MIMO consistently outperforms existing cross-lingual training baselines across various MLIR and Multi-Monolingual benchmarks. MIMO also remains competitive with off-the-shelf models of similar or larger parameter scales. Furthermore, our cross-lingual Alignment-Uniformity analysis clarifies the distinct roles of the two loss components and shows that their combination yields a favorable trade-off between alignment and uniformity.

</details>


### [117] [Contextual Scalarisation Thompson Sampling for multi-objective decisions in public media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31291)
*Théo Maëtz,Luc Guillet,Andrea Cavallaro*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文提出了 CSTS 算法，一种能够根据实时场景动态调整多目标权重推荐的上下文老虎机方法，有效解决了公共服务媒体在复杂场景下的决策权衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的推荐系统多目标优化方法通常依赖于固定的目标组合或基于帕累托前沿的静态优化，无法根据不同情境灵活调整权重（如公共服务媒体在不同时间段对受众覆盖、文化价值和公共服务职责的优先级权衡）。

Method: 提出了一种名为 Contextual Scalarisation Thompson Sampler (CSTS) 的多目标上下文老虎机（Multi-objective Contextual Bandit）算法，该算法能够学习并根据观测到的上下文（Context）动态加权各个优化目标。

Result: 通过在瑞士国家广播公司（Radio Télévision Suisse）的真实编程数据上进行实验，结果表明 CSTS 在上下文相关性提升以及与专业策展实践的一致性方面均优于静态加权和传统上下文老虎机算法。

Conclusion: 提出了一种基于上下文的动态多目标优化方法，能够有效解决公共服务媒体在不同场景下对多个竞争目标的权重平衡问题，优于静态权重和标准的上下文老虎机算法。

Abstract: Recommender systems may operate under multiple, competing objectives. For example, audience reach, cultural values, public service mandate, and operational constraints must be balanced in editorial decisions of public service media. Existing approaches relying on fixed combinations of objectives or Pareto-based optimisation do not adapt to changing priorities across situations. In this paper, we propose Contextual Scalarisation Thompson Sampler (CSTS), a multi-objective contextual bandit method that learns to weight objectives as a function of the observed context. We evaluate CSTS on real programming data from Radio Télévision Suisse, the Swiss national broadcaster, showing improved contextual relevance and better alignment with expert curation practices compared to fixed weight and standard contextual bandit approaches.

</details>


### [118] [DynaTree: Dynamic Agentic Retrieval Tree for Time-Sensitive News Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31377)
*Siyuan Qi,Xinyuan Wang,Yingxuan Yang,Haochuan Guo,Jianghao Lin,Weiwen Liu,Yong Yu,Weinan Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: DynaTree是一种高效的新闻检索框架，通过将复杂智能体推理从在线阶段剥离至离线构建检索树，在显著降低实时计算成本的同时，提升了检索的准确性与时效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Agentic RAG方法将语义扩展与检索决策耦合，导致推理成本高昂，且难以满足时效性强的新闻检索需求。

Method: 提出DynaTree两阶段框架：1. 离线阶段：利用智能体构建可重用的语义检索树；2. 在线阶段：执行轻量级、无智能体参与的每日子树选择与检索。

Result: 在Syft新闻数据集和BEIR基准测试中表现优于标准RAG及其他Agentic基线；在线A/B测试显示，该方法显著提升了检索生存率，并优于现有的生产级检索工具。

Conclusion: DynaTree通过将智能体推理过程离线化，构建可重用的检索树，成功实现了在保证高检索性能的同时大幅降低在线推理成本。该方法验证了结构化语义扩展在提升实时新闻检索的覆盖率、时效性和相关性方面的有效性。

Abstract: Agentic Retrieval-Augmented Generation improves retrieval by integrating planning, tool use, and iterative reasoning, but existing agentic RAG methods often couple semantic expansion with retrieval decisions in short-horizon inference loops, leading to high inference cost and limited suitability for time-sensitive news retrieval. We propose DynaTree, a two-stage framework for efficient and adaptive news retrieval. In the offline stage, DynaTree uses coordinated agents to construct a reusable retrieval tree that materializes the semantic space of a query topic. In the online stage, DynaTree performs lightweight daily subtree selection over a time-localized evaluation proxy, without further agentic reasoning, tree modification, or retraining. Experiments on a multi-day Syft news benchmark and multiple BEIR datasets show that DynaTree achieves strong recall and ranking performance, consistently outperforming standard RAG and prior agentic baselines. We further deploy DynaTree in the Syft production system and evaluate it through online A/B testing from Jan. 28 to Feb. 6, 2026. The dynamically adapted variant improves survival rate from 0.32-0.53 to 0.59-0.73 over a fixed offline-selected subtree and outperforms existing production recallers on every evaluation day. These results show that persistent, structure-aware semantic expansion can translate offline agentic reasoning into practical improvements in coverage, freshness, and relevance for real-world news retrieval.

</details>


### [119] [Beyond Instance-Level Alignment and Uniformity: Semantic Factor Learning for Collaborative Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31414)
*Yajie Yu,Chenzhong Bin,Zhoubo Xu,Zhixin Zeng,Tongxin Xu,Cihan Xia,Jiafeng Wu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对传统协同过滤中负采样偏差和 GCN 性能瓶颈问题，提出 SaFeAU 框架，利用语义因子解耦与对齐来增强稀疏信号，在提升推荐精度的同时显著降低了计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 1. 现有的协同过滤（CF）范式将未交互项直接视为负样本，导致“假负样本”问题严重，影响泛化能力；2. 基于 GCN 的 CF 模型存在计算开销大、过平滑以及在稀疏数据下泛化性差等问题。

Method: 提出了 SaFeAU 框架，包含三个核心组件：1. Semantic Factor Routing (SFR)：将嵌入分层/解耦为独立的全局语义因子；2. Semantic Factor Matching (SFM)：通过语义因子匹配机制，将潜在的正样本（被误判为负样本的项目）重新识别为正信号；3. Semantic Pairs Alignment (SPA)：通过对观测到的正样本和潜在正样本进行对齐，并保持表征的均匀性（Uniformity）来优化学习过程。该方法摆脱了对显式图邻域聚合（GCN）的依赖。

Result: 在四个真实世界的稀疏数据集上，SaFeAU 在推荐准确性和计算效率方面均优于主流的 GCN-based 和 MF-based 方法。

Conclusion: SaFeAU 通过引入语义因子增强学习范式，有效解决了传统 CF 面临的负采样偏差、模型过平滑及高阶信息捕获效率低的问题，在稀疏数据集上表现出高准确性和计算效率。

Abstract: Collaborative filtering (CF) is widely used in recommender systems (RecSys) due to its simplicity and efficiency. However, existing CF methods follow an instance-level learning paradigm. During the instance learning stage, a large number of uninteracted user-item instances, of which items are potential interested by the user, are incorrectly treated as true negative samples resulting in a severe limitation to the generalization and scalability of models. Moreover, mainstream graph convolutional networks (GCNs) inherently suffer from high computational cost and over-smoothing issues, which limit the ability in capturing higher-order connectivity and lead to a poor generalization under sparse supervision signals. To address the above limitations, we propose Semantic Factor enhanced Alignment and Uniformity (SaFeAU), a novel framework that augments interacted instances with semantic factors, thereby mitigating false negative labeling and enabling matrix factorization (MF) to capture high-order CF signals without graph neighborhood aggregation. Specifically, SaFeAU consists of three tightly coupled components. First, Semantic Factor Routing (SFR) disentangles item representations into independent and global semantic factors. Building on these factors, Semantic Factor Matching (SFM) identifies uninteracted items, which share the same semantic factors with interacted ones, as potential positive pairs for enriching sparse supervision signals. Finally, Semantic Pairs Alignment (SPA) aligns both observed and potential positive pairs while promoting uniformity of user and item representations. Extensive experiments on four sparse real-world datasets show that SaFeAU consistently outperforms GCN-based and MF-based state-of-the-art CF methods in both recommendation accuracy and computational efficiency, confirming the effectiveness of the proposed semantic enhanced learning paradigm.

</details>


### [120] [Evaluating Factual Density in Multi-Source RAG: A Study in Medical AI Accuracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31506)
*Michael R. DeMarco*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对RAG系统中传统检索方法偏向词汇匹配而非事实密度的缺陷，本文提出了Factual Density (FD*) 指标及其处理流程，有效解决了“专家盲点效应”，在医疗领域测试中显著提高了事实检索的准确性与专业证据查全率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RAG检索系统主要依赖关键词匹配，往往优先选择词汇相似度高而非包含确凿事实的文本，导致“专家盲点效应”（Expert Blindness Effect），即高质量的事实证据被淹没在冗余文本中。

Method: 1. 提出Factual Density (FD*) 指标：计算经核实原子声明与总词数的比例；2. 利用NexusAgentics Ghost Audit预处理管道对原始文本进行概率事实分析评分；3. 实施基于长度区间的分层Z-score标准化，消除文本长度带来的偏差。

Result: 1. 成功消除了FD*指标与文本长度之间的强负相关性；2. 在HealthFC基准测试中，FD*优化后的检索在Top-5结果中实现了100%的系统综述饱和度，优于传统余弦相似度算法，并成功挖掘出原本排名较低的关键医学证据。

Conclusion: 事实密度（FD*）作为一种轻量级、高影响力的检索优化指标，能够有效克服传统检索中“专家盲点效应”导致的低事实密度的缺陷，显著提升RAG架构在医疗等专业领域的事实精确度。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) is the current industry standard for grounding AI in real-world facts. Traditional retrieval methods rely on keyword matching and topic proximity, ranking content based on how closely it sounds like the user's query. What they do not measure is how many verified facts the content actually contains. This structural gap, termed the Expert Blindness Effect, causes standard RAG pipelines to consistently bury high-density factual evidence in favor of lexically dominant text on the same topic. To address this gap, this paper introduces Factual Density (FD*), a novel retrieval optimization signal that measures the proportion of verified atomic claims relative to total token count. Using the NexusAgentics Ghost Audit preprocessing pipeline, raw text is scored for factual specificity using probabilistic factuality analysis to filter content before corpus ingestion. An initial formulation introduced a severe document-length confound (Pearson R = -0.8636, p = 2.27e-07). Implementing Z-score normalization within length bins resolved this bias, validating FD* as a length-independent density signal (p = 0.0749). Evaluated against the HealthFC benchmark (750 health claims labeled Supported, Refuted, or No Evidence by medical experts), FD*-optimized retrieval was the only condition to achieve 100% systematic review saturation in top-5 results, surfacing Cochrane evidence that standard cosine similarity ranked outside the top ten. Ground truth verification confirmed 25 mappings across seven HealthFC-supported claims. While full statistical validation across n=50 queries remains future work due to constraints on corpus-benchmark alignment, these findings establish factual density reranking as a low-cost, high-impact intervention for improving factual precision in health RAG architectures.

</details>


### [121] [SPECTRA: Synthetic IR Test Collections with Relevance Oracles and Controlled Distractor Diagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31575)
*Eric Liang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SPECTRA 是一种用于生成大规模、可控合成文本语料及检索测试集的框架，旨在通过模拟真实数据的统计特征，为检索系统的性能测试和诊断提供低成本的早期方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模信息检索测试需要庞大的语料库以验证索引性能、延迟和路由效率，但人工标注的测试集成本高昂，且在数据私密或系统研发初期往往不可获取。

Method: 提出了 SPECTRA 框架，该框架通过将潜在主题结构、表面文本生成、元数据控制、查询意图生成和确定性相关性预言机（relevance oracles）进行解耦，以实现对合成文本文档和关联检索测试集的生成。

Result: 原型系统实现了线性时间生成（约1.2万-1.4万文档/秒）；成功生成了包含6万文档和961万词元的语料库；ZIPF斜率保持在0.86左右；验证了随着干扰文本比例增加，BM25的检索准确率（nDCG@10）显著下降，证实了该框架能够有效模拟并揭示检索系统的失效率。

Conclusion: SPECTRA 提供了一种高效、可扩展且受控的方法来生成合成语料库和检索测试集，作为传统人工评估的有效诊断补充，有助于在早期阶段发现检索系统的性能瓶颈和故障模式。

Abstract: Scalable information retrieval testing needs corpora that are large enough to stress index construction, ranking latency, query routing, and evaluation tooling, yet human-judged test collections remain expensive and may be unavailable when documents are private or still under design. This paper introduces SPECTRA, a reproducible framework for generating synthetic text corpora and retrieval test collections through a separation of latent topical structure, surface text realization, metadata controls, query intent generation, and deterministic relevance oracles. The framework is intended as a diagnostic complement to Cranfield-style and TREC-style evaluation, not as a replacement for human assessment. A single-process Python prototype generated corpora up to 60,000 documents and 9.61 million tokens while preserving controllable long-tail vocabulary growth and producing graded relevance labels for 96 queries. In the local simulation study, generation remained close to linear at roughly 12K to 14K documents per second, estimated Zipf slopes stayed near 0.86 in absolute value, and increasing cross-topic distractor text reduced BM25 nDCG@10 from 1.00 at 2% distractors to 0.43 at 36% distractors. These results show that lightweight synthetic corpora can expose retrieval-system scaling and failure modes before costly collection construction begins.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [122] [Improved Distribution Estimation in $\ell_\infty$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30509)
*Doron Cohen,Aryeh Kontorovich,Yonatan Livshitz*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文改进了离散概率分布在 $\ell_\infty$ 范数下的估计误差界，解决了前人工作中的理论缺口，推导出了新的经验风险界及最坏分布解析，并辅以实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决离散概率分布估计中的理论界限问题，特别是针对 $\ell_\infty$ 范数下的误差估计，并回应现有文献（Kontorovich & Painsky）中未解决的开放性问题（如经验风险界的紧致性及最坏分布形式）。

Method: 主要采用了理论推导技术来建立极小极大界（minimax bounds），包括期望界和高概率尾部界，并引入了完全经验性的风险界估计方法。

Result: 建立了改进的极小极大风险界，导出了完全经验性的风险界约束，确定了最坏情况下的极值分布形式，并提供了相应的实验验证。

Conclusion: 本文改进了离散概率分布在 $\ell_\infty$ 范数下的估计界，解决了 Kontorovich 和 Painsky (2025) 提出的多个开放性问题，并为界限的紧致性和最坏情况分布提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We present improved bounds for estimating discrete probability distributions under the $\ell_\infty$ norm. These include minimax bounds in expectation and high-probability tail bounds. We resolve some of the open questions posed in Kontorovich and Painsky (JMLR, 2025) -- including a fully empirical version of the tightest risk bound they presented and identifying the form of the worst-case extremal distribution. Encouraging empirical results are reported as well.

</details>


### [123] [Batched Stochastic Linear Bandits with 1-Bit Communication Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30976)
*Ivan Lau,Daniel McMorrow,Kevin Jamieson,Jonathan Scarlett*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了在极低通信（每批次1比特反馈）下的线性赌博机问题，推导了理论下界，并提出了两种分阶段消除算法，证明即使在批次大小达 $O(\sqrt{T})$ 时，也能实现近乎无通信约束的遗憾性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在同时存在批处理（batching）和极度受限通信（每批次仅1比特反馈）约束下的随机线性赌博机问题，填补了现有每轮量化模型与总比特预算模型之间的理论空白。

Method: 提出了基于G-最优设计（G-optimal design）和1比特均值估计的两种阶段性消除算法（phased-elimination algorithms）；针对第二种算法引入了安全臂识别与热启动（warm-start）机制。

Result: 推导了该场景下的极小化极大遗憾下界 $\widetilde{\Omega}(B\min\{d,\log\lvert \mathcal{A} \rvert\} + \sqrt{dT \min\{d,\log\lvert \mathcal{A} \rvert\})$；证明了算法在特定参数范围内达到近最优遗憾上界。

Conclusion: 在大规模参数缩放设定下，每批次仅需1比特反馈即可实现接近无约束线性赌博机的极小化极大遗憾上限（minimax regret）。

Abstract: We study stochastic linear bandits under a natural combination of batching and communication constraints: the time horizon is partitioned into batches of equal size $B$, and during each batch the learner sends $B$ requested arm pulls to an agent, who then observes the corresponding $B$ rewards and responds with a single bit of feedback to the learner. For each batch, the learner specifies the 1-bit quantization rule the agent uses, which may depend on all previously received bits but not on any past rewards directly. This setting addresses a significant yet unexplored ``middle ground'' between previous models having per-round quantization only or total bit budgets only. We establish a minimax lower bound showing that $Ω(B\min\{d,\log\lvert \mathcal{A} \rvert\})$ regret is unavoidable due to the 1-bit communication bottleneck, even in the absence of noise. Combined with standard statistical limits, this yields a general lower bound of $\widetildeΩ(B\min\{d,\log\lvert \mathcal{A} \rvert\} + \sqrt{dT \min\{d,\log\lvert \mathcal{A} \rvert\}})$. We develop two phased-elimination algorithms based on $G$-optimal designs and 1-bit mean estimation. The first achieves $\widetilde{O}(dB + d\sqrt{T})$ regret, matching the lower bound up to logarithmic factors when $\lvert \mathcal{A} \rvert = \exp(Ω(d))$, and the second incorporates a safe-arm identification and warm-start procedure to obtain $\widetilde{O}(B\log\lvert \mathcal{A} \rvert + d^{3/2}\sqrt{B} + \sqrt{dT\log\lvert \mathcal{A} \rvert})$ regret, which is near-optimal in broad scaling regimes of $(\lvert \mathcal{A} \rvert, B, d, T)$. Together, our results demonstrate that a single bit of feedback per batch suffices to nearly match the minimax regret of unconstrained linear bandits in broad scaling regimes, even for batch sizes as large as $Θ(\sqrt{T})$.

</details>


### [124] [Hedging on the Frontier: Learning New Tasks with Few Samples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30997)
*Tobias Wegel,Federico Di Gennaro,Geelon So,Fanny Yang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出利用“弱单调性”假设（模型在基准任务上的相对排序与目标任务相关）来辅助小样本学习，证明了该假设下可通过对模型类进行剪枝和前沿对冲优化，从而实现理论上的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在小样本学习场景下，现有的辅助信息（如模型在公共基准测试上的表现）如果能被更科学地建模，可以显著提升目标任务的学习效率。当前缺乏一种既能真实模拟任务相关性，又能提供理论保证的方法。

Method: 1. 形式化定义“弱单调性”（Weak Monotonicity）：假设若模型在多个基准任务上优于另一模型，则在目标任务上也倾向于表现更好。
2. 统计复杂性分析：推导出在弱单调性约束下的学习复杂性界。
3. 算法设计：提出基于单调性的模型剪枝策略以及针对帕累托前沿的对冲算法。

Result: 证明了利用近似弱单调性可以有效降低模型搜索空间，并且通过在模型性能前沿上进行对冲，能够在有限的基准评估数据下获得比传统方法更优的收敛表现。

Conclusion: 在近似弱单调性假设下，可以有效地对模型类进行剪枝，并通过在帕累托前沿（Pareto frontier）上进行策略对冲（hedging），在迁移学习和模型选择聚合中通过基准测试评价实现理论上的性能增益。

Abstract: When a learner faces a new task with few samples, it must leverage any available side information. In practice, this often comes in the form of model evaluations on related tasks in public benchmarks. A key question then is how to model task relatedness such that it is both realistic and the benchmark evaluations lead to provable gains. Empirically, we observe that weak monotonicity is often approximately satisfied: if a model dominates another on many benchmarks, it also tends to outperform on the new task. We explore the statistical complexity of learning under (approximate) weak monotonicity, leveraging it within two learning paradigms: transfer learning and model selection aggregation. We show that not only can we prune the model class based on monotonicity, but we can also further adapt to the geometry of the available trade-offs by hedging on the frontier.

</details>


### [125] [Routing on the Stiefel Manifold: When Does Adaptive Subspace Selection Help for Cross-Domain EEG Decoding?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31043)
*Isabella Costa Maia,Pedro L. C. Rodrigues,Salem Said,Marco Congedo*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文针对跨域EEG解码难题，提出动态Stiefel路由方法，通过引入三种结构约束打破专家模型的退化，在SPD流形上实现了自动、稳健的域适应，显著提升了分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的黎曼深度学习方法在跨域EEG解码中面临挑战，即不同受试者的协方差矩阵占据SPD流形上的不同区域，且现有域适应方法依赖校准数据或无法跨域泛化。

Method: 提出了动态Stiefel路由框架，包含K个专家投影过滤器。为解决训练中的退化问题（坍缩为简单集成平均），引入了三项结构特性：对称锚定（Symmetric anchor）、冻结的域判别查询编码器（Frozen domain-discriminative query encoder）以及解耦的键对齐损失（Decoupled key alignment loss）。

Result: 在三个数据集上实现了性能提升，平衡精度分别从0.773提高到0.823、0.757提高到0.809、0.801提高到0.839，且无需数据集特定的超参数搜索。

Conclusion: 提出了动态Stiefel路由（Dynamic Stiefel Routing），通过解决专家过滤器退化问题，在对正定（SPD）流形上的脑电图（EEG）进行跨域解码时，实现了无需数据集特定超参数调试的高泛化性能。

Abstract: Cross-domain EEG decoding remains challenging despite advances in Riemannian deep learning: covariance matrices from different subjects occupy systematically distinct regions of the SPD manifold, yet existing domain adaptation methods either require target-domain calibration data or learn subject-specific components that cannot generalise across domains. We propose dynamic Stiefel routing: a pool of $K$ expert projection filters on the Stiefel manifold, each specialised for a different region of the SPD manifold, with each input covariance routed to the most appropriate filter via cross-attention, adapting the subspace projection per sample. A central finding is that this approach, implemented naively, provably collapses to ensemble averaging: when routing weights are uniform, the adaptive filter reduces exactly to an equal-contribution combination of experts, indistinguishable from a single fixed filter. Three structural properties break this degeneracy: a symmetric anchor $W_{\mathrm{base}} \in \mathrm{St}(n,k)$ that removes proximity bias among experts; a frozen domain-discriminative query encoder that decouples routing from task optimisation; and a decoupled key alignment loss that trains expert keys toward stable domain attractors. Together they produce the first genuinely committed and domain-structured routing on SPD manifolds, with consistent gains across three datasets: balanced accuracy improves from $0.773\to 0.823$, $0.757\to 0.809$, and $0.801\to 0.839$, with the alignment strategy determined automatically by a single data-driven rule and no dataset-specific hyperparameter search.

</details>


### [126] [Memory by Design: Probabilistic Sequence Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31163)
*Matthew Dowling,Hyungju Jeon,Cristina Savin,Il Memming Park*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了“设计模型”框架，将现代递归序列模型解释为贝叶斯滤波过程；通过引入协方差跟踪记忆不确定性，不仅统一了现有模型，还显著提升了长上下文检索的准确性和稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的线性递归模型（如Mamba、GLA等）虽高效，但缺乏对记忆更新过程中不确定性的显式建模，导致在面对复杂关联任务或长上下文检索时稳健性不足。

Method: 提出了“设计模型”（design-model）框架，通过显式贝叶斯滤波（Bayesian filtering）导出递归序列映射。具体实现为“贝叶斯层”（Bayesian Layer），在处理序列时同时维护均值和协方差，通过协方差调节写入机制（Steering writes）和增益衰减。

Result: 1. 统一了现有的线性注意力、GLA、Mamba-2/SSD及DeltaNet等亚二次方递归模型；2. 回复协方差项的“贝叶斯层”在受控碰撞研究、关联回忆及Zoology MQAR基准测试中表现出超越训练状态的稳健性；3. 将贝叶斯层蒸馏到预训练的340M Gated DeltaNet中，在保持相同计算资源的前提下提升了RULER长上下文检索性能。

Conclusion: “设计模型”框架为理解和构建高效递归序列模型提供了一种统一的贝叶斯视角。通过恢复缺失的协方差项，可以显著提升模型在推理时的稳健性及长上下文检索能力，验证了显式记忆建模在序列处理中的重要性。

Abstract: We introduce the design-model framework: a way to derive efficient recurrent sequence maps from explicit assumptions about memory. A design model writes evidence into memory by exact Bayesian filtering; a query-dependent readout produces a predictive distribution whose mean is the layer output. In our linear-Gaussian instantiation, the \emph{Bayesian Layer} propagates both a mean and a covariance: the covariance tracks uncertainty over stored associations, steering writes toward uncertain directions, attenuating gains as evidence accumulates, and preserving confident memories. The same framework unifies several sub-quadratic recurrences. Linear attention, GLA, and Mamba-2/SSD are exact filters under one design model, whereas DeltaNet and related Delta-rule models arise as covariance-reset reductions under another. Restoring the covariance yields closed-form predictions for retrieval dynamics, verified empirically, and improves robustness beyond the training regime across controlled collision studies, learned associative recall, and the Zoology MQAR benchmark; distilling Bayesian Layers into a pretrained 340M Gated DeltaNet improves RULER long-context retrieval at matched compute.

</details>


### [127] [Correcting Split Selection in Online Decision Trees via Anytime-Valid Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31239)
*Salim I. Amoukou,Saumitra Mishra,Manuela Veloso*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 针对流式学习中霍夫丁树 Split 决策的统计逻辑缺陷，提出了基于随时有效推断的替代方案，在提升模型性能的同时显著精简了模型复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的流式学习方法（如 Adaptive Random Forests）依赖霍夫丁树进行增量式学习，但其利用固定样本浓度不等式处理数据依赖的停止规则在统计学上是不严谨的，甚至可能导致错误的 Split 概率趋于 1。

Method: 将原本基于固定样本浓度不等式的 Split 决策机制，替换为基于“随时有效推断”（anytime-valid inference）的统计框架，通过该框架在任意数据流（包括非平稳环境）下实现对错误分支的有效控制。

Result: 相比传统方法，新方法在非平稳数据流下表现更优，并能显著减小决策树的规模，同时满足有限的收敛时间要求，且在平稳分布下风险单调递减。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种基于“随时有效推断”（anytime-valid inference）的决策树生长方法，解决了流式学习中霍夫丁树 split 决策的统计严谨性问题，在保证统计效力的同时有效减小了树的规模并提升了性能。

Abstract: Bagging-based ensembles, most notably Adaptive Random Forests, are among the strongest performers for learning from data streams. A common denominator across these methods is their reliance on Hoeffding Trees as base learners, which grow decision trees incrementally by testing whether a candidate split is significantly better than its alternatives using concentration inequalities. Despite their empirical success, existing variants lack valid statistical guarantees. Current analyses rely on fixed-sample concentration bounds, while split decisions are made using data-dependent stopping rules, which invalidates their guarantees and can drive the probabilty of incorrect splits to one. We introduce a principled alternative based on anytime-valid inference. Our method provides: (i) anytime-valid control of false splits under arbitrary data streams, including non-stationary settings; (ii) finite commitment time under a predictive advantage; and (iii) under stationary i.i.d. data, risk is monotone decreasing and strictly improves at every split. Empirically, we evaluate both standalone trees and their use within Adaptive Random Forests on non-stationary streams. Our method improves performance while producing substantially smaller trees.

</details>


### [128] [Entropic Projection Alignment: Estimating, Explaining, and Improving Model Performance Under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31250)
*Salim I. Amoukou,Emanuele Albini,Tom Bewley,Saumitra Mishra,Manuela Veloso*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了 Entropic Projection Alignment (EPA) 框架，通过高效时刻匹配与闭式权重推导，解决了分布偏移条件下的性能估计、特征归因及模型迁移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决分布偏移环境下的三个核心挑战：未标注目标域的性能估计、偏移特征的可解释性分析以及目标域的性能优化提升。

Method: 提出了 Entropic Projection Alignment (EPA) 框架。通过最小化源域分布到目标域分布的 KL 散度，同时进行精选时刻匹配 (moment matching)，推导出了重要性权重计算的闭式解，实现了隐式的方差控制。

Result: 在实验中表现优于当前的最优基准方法 (SOTA)，具备显著的计算效率，并提供了扎实的理论保证。

Conclusion: EPA 框架通过时刻匹配与 KL 散度最小化的结合，为解决分布偏移问题提供了一个高效、理论完备且具有鲁棒性的解决方案，证实了仅依赖时刻匹配即可实现可靠的性能估计与领域自适应。

Abstract: We propose a unified framework for addressing three key challenges of distribution shift: (1) estimating a model's performance on an unlabeled target domain, (2) explaining the shift by identifying the features responsible, and (3) improving the target domain performance. Our method, Entropic Projection Alignment (EPA), aligns the source distribution to the target by matching carefully selected moments while simultaneously minimising the KL divergence from the source. This formulation yields a unique closed-form solution for importance weights, achieving robustness through implicit variance control. Drawing on domain adaptation theory, we establish that moment matching is sufficient for reliable estimation and adaptation, avoiding the need for full density ratio recovery. Extensive experiments, together with strong theoretical guarantees, demonstrate that EPA consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines while offering substantial computational efficiency.

</details>


### [129] [Log-Ratio Propagation on the Simplex: A Theory of Cellwise Contamination for Compositional Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31345)
*Matthias Templ*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文揭示了成分数据中由于对数比变换造成的“污染传播”效应，证明了传统欧式空间的细胞级稳健估计器在单纯形空间中存在性能下降问题，并提供了基于污染诊断指纹的理论支持，为开发专门的单纯形细胞级稳健方法提供了框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成分数据分析必须通过对数比进行，但中心对数比（clr）的几何平均归一化特性导致单个成分的污染会通过对数比传播到整个向量，使得常用的欧式空间细胞级稳健方法在处理成分数据时失效。

Method: 研究了基于乘法扰动的尺度不变污染模型，建立了污染传播定理（propagation theorem），分析了包括MCD、S、τ以及坐标对齐M-估计量在内的多种欧式空间估计器在单纯形空间下细胞级崩溃点的变化，并推导了变异矩阵的细胞级影响函数（cellwise influence function）。

Result: 证明了单纯形空间下的细胞级崩溃点相较于欧式空间降低了(D-1)/D倍；证明了对数比坐标下单个成分的污染具有特定的“诊断指纹”（在变异矩阵中表现为特定的行和列膨胀），可用于识别受污染的成分。

Conclusion: 单成分污染会导致对数比（log-ratio）向量发生秩为1的偏移，使得传统的欧式空间细胞级稳健方法在单纯形空间中变得病态（ill-posed），这类估计器的崩溃点会降低。研究为单纯形空间上的细胞级稳健统计方法奠定了理论基础，指出了诊断污染源的数学依据。

Abstract: Compositional data must be analysed through log-ratios: scale invariance, the defining axiom of the field, leaves no alternative. The centred log-ratio divides by the geometric mean of every part, so a single contaminated component shifts every centred-log-ratio coordinate at once, displacing the log-ratio vector by a fixed amount that no choice of coordinates can reduce. We develop a theory of cellwise contamination on the simplex around this observation. A scale-invariant contamination model built from multiplicative perturbation combines with a propagation theorem showing that corruption of a single raw part induces a rank-one shift of the log-ratio vector, with direction determined by the contrast matrix. The resulting perturbation pattern is not equivalent to any independent cellwise contamination model in log-ratio coordinates -- so standard Euclidean cellwise methods applied to log-ratios are ill-posed under the simplex contamination mechanism. For estimators whose Euclidean cellwise breakdown is witnessed by a column-concentrated configuration -- a class including MCD, $S$-, $τ$-, and coordinate-wise $M$-estimators of location and scatter -- the cellwise breakdown value on the simplex is reduced by the factor $(D-1)/D$ relative to its Euclidean counterpart, a reduction that is tight and arises purely from the normalisation mismatch between $nD$ raw cells and $n(D-1)$ ilr cells. The cellwise influence function for the variation matrix carries a diagnostic fingerprint: contamination of a single part inflates exactly one row and column, identifying the responsible component. These results form the theoretical foundation for cellwise-robust methods on the simplex; a companion paper develops a cellwise-robust PCA estimator that exploits the propagation geometry and demonstrates it on simulated and geochemical data.

</details>


<div id='cs.PF'></div>

# cs.PF [[Back]](#toc)

### [130] [From Roofline to Ruggedness: Decomposing and Smoothing the GEMM Performance Landscape](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29752)
*Aditya Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.PF

TL;DR: 针对GEMM在GPU上表现出的非直观性能波动问题（性能崎岖），提出了一种分析与优化框架；通过动态配置优化和切片调整，成功平滑了性能曲面，将平均执行效率显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观察到相邻GEMM问题（如N仅差128）在GPU上表现出高达30%的性能差异，传统的Roofline模型无法解释这种因硬件机制（分块、缓存等）导致的“性能崎岖”现象。

Method: 1. 提出“性能崎岖度分析（Performance Ruggedness Analysis）”框架，将性能视为多维曲面而非标量；2. 通过32,768次配置扫描进行实证；3. 实现两阶段优化栈：最佳动态片（tile）选择方案 + 基于动态规划的填充与拆分优化器。

Result: 在Intel Battlemage GPU上应用该框架后，性能崎岖度降低了70%，平均吞吐量提升了30%；研究明确了剩余残差波动的来源包括内核开销、波阵面量化、DPAS几何结构及DRAM通道映射。

Conclusion: 通过引入性能崎岖度分析框架并结合动态切片选择与优化填充/切分策略，可以显著降低GPU计算性能的波动性（平滑崎岖度），并有效提升平均算力。该研究表明，性能的非连续突变主要源于软硬件交互（如片上分块、波阵面量化等），而非简单的缓存冲突。

Abstract: Adjacent GEMM problems that differ by a single 128-element step in N can show 30% different throughput on the same GPU. This pervasive performance ruggedness - invisible to roofline analysis and peak-FLOPs intuition, yet dominant for every non-peak workload - is the subject of this paper.
  We propose performance ruggedness analysis as an analytical framework complementary to roofline: rather than summarizing GPU performance with a scalar bound, treat the full multidimensional performance surface as the object of study, decompose its texture into mechanism-attributable components and separate software-removable contributions from hardware-bound ones. The framing is directly analogous to deep-learning loss landscapes - a continuous quantity (the idealized time 2MNK / compute_throughput_peak) made rugged by interaction with discrete hardware substrates (tiles, sub-groups, cache lines, DRAM channels).
  We apply the framework to BF16 NN (no transpose) GEMM on Intel Battlemage (Arc B580, sycl-tla) via a 32,768-configuration sweep (M, N, K) belongs to {128, ..., 4096}^3. The peak is 110.8 TFLOPs at the non-square shape M=3840, N=2048, K=4096 with the default tile size; the initial landscape roughness is 16.8 TFLOPs per 128-step against an ideal of 2.0. A two-stage software stack - (i) best-of-six dynamic tile selection and (ii) a novel dynamic-programming based padding-and-splitting optimizer with O(1) runtime lookup - reduces roughness by 70% and raises mean throughput by 30%. Cross-tile experiments establish that the residual sawtooth period scales exactly with software tile size, ruling out cache set conflicts and attributing the remaining variance to four hardware-bound sources (per-kernel base overhead, wave quantization, DPAS atom geometry and GDDR6 channel-hash interactions).

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [131] [Gait2Hip-60: A Unified Deep Learning Benchmark for Predicting Hip Muscle Forces and Joint Moments from Multi-Cadence Gait Kinematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30374)
*Jiaqi Zhang,Ji Hou,Qing Sun,Xianzhi Gao,Bo Huo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究开发了一个深度学习框架，通过步态运动学高效预测髋关节肌肉力和力矩。实验表明Transformer模型性能优于LSTM和Mamba，并初步验证了其在病理人群中的泛化潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的肌肉骨骼仿真方法虽然精确但计算耗时，难以应用于临床环境。因此，研究旨在开发一种高效的深度学习框架，通过步态运动学实时、准确地预测髋部肌肉力和关节力矩。

Method: 研究基于60名健康受试者在不同步频下的步态数据，利用下肢关节角度（输入）预测OpenSim计算出的髋部肌肉力和关节力矩（输出）。对比了LSTM、Transformer和Mamba三种深度学习模型，并对9名股骨头坏死（ONFH）患者进行了零样本（zero-shot）外部验证。

Result: 在健康人群基准测试中，Transformer模型表现最佳（肌肉力R2=0.819，关节力矩R2=0.862）。在ONFH患者的外部验证中，模型表现出中等的预测能力（肌肉力R2=0.537，关节力矩R2=0.569）。

Conclusion: Transformer模型在预测髋关节动力学方面表现最优，证明了利用步态运动学直接估算肌肉力和关节力矩的可行性。尽管在病理人群中展现了一定的零样本迁移能力，但临床应用仍需进一步提升泛化能力和开展更广泛的病理验证。

Abstract: Estimating hip muscle forces and joint moments during gait typically relies on musculoskeletal simulation, which is informative but time-consuming and difficult to apply in clinical settings. This study developed a deep learning framework to predict these hip dynamics parameters directly from lower-limb gait kinematics and compared three representative sequence models under a unified protocol. Gait data were collected from 60 healthy adults under three metronome-guided cadence conditions. Ten bilateral lower-limb joint angles were used as inputs, and OpenSim-derived hip muscle forces and hip joint moments were used as reference outputs. Three deep learning models of LSTM, Transformer, and Mamba were trained and evaluated using the same subject-level split, preprocessing pipeline, and metrics. The best model was then directly tested on an external cohort of 9 patients with osteonecrosis of the femoral head (ONFH) without retraining. In the healthy-subject benchmark, Transformer achieved the best subject-level mean performance for both hip muscle force prediction (RMSE = 1.33 N/kg, MAE = 0.57 N/kg, R2 = 0.819) and hip joint moment prediction (RMSE = 0.11 Nm/kg, MAE = 0.07 Nm/kg, R2 = 0.862), with similar advantages across walking cadences. In zero-shot external validation, Transformer retained moderate predictive ability in ONFH for hip muscle force prediction (RMSE = 1.51 N/kg, MAE = 0.70 N/kg, R2 = 0.537) and hip joint moment prediction (RMSE = 0.17 Nm/kg, MAE = 0.12 Nm/kg, R2 = 0.569). These findings support the feasibility of estimating hip dynamics from gait kinematics, identify Transformer as a strong baseline, and highlight the need for broader pathological validation and improved generalization before clinical application.

</details>


### [132] [Unicorn: Scaling High-Dimensional Time Series Forecasting via Universal Correlation Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30376)
*Haochen Yuan,Yichen Song,Yunbo Wang,Xiaokang Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Unicorn 通过引入潜原型码本实现通道感知的解耦，构建了一个可扩展的、支持跨域迁移的多变量时间序列基础模型框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型在通道独立（忽视依赖性）与通道依赖（难以跨数据集扩展）之间存在权衡，缺乏能够处理高维、异构及多数据集场景的通用架构。

Method: 提出 Unicorn（Universal Correlation Network）框架，核心在于引入潜原型码本（latent prototype codebook），将异构通道映射到共享潜在空间，从而学习可迁移的、与具体通道身份无关的交互模式。

Result: 实验表明，Unicorn 在多变量时间序列预测任务中显著优于现有最先进架构，特别是在少样本（few-shot）迁移场景下表现出色。

Conclusion: Unicorn 通过解耦通道身份与相关性建模，成功实现了跨异构数据集的通用时间序列表示学习，为多变量时间序列基础模型提供了一个可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Modern time series architectures face a fundamental trade-off: channel-independent models scale well with increasing data volume but ignore critical inter-channel dependencies, while channel-dependent models are expressive but remain ``dimension-bounded'', struggling to generalize across heterogeneous datasets.To bridge this gap, we introduce Unicorn (Universal Correlation Network), a framework for scalable, multi-dataset pretraining on high-dimensional time series. At the core of Unicorn is a latent prototype codebook that decouples correlation modeling from specific channel identities. By projecting heterogeneous channels into a shared latent space, UniCorN learns identity-agnostic, reusable interaction patterns that transfer across domains with diverse dimensionalities and semantics. Extensive experiments show that Unicorn significantly outperforms state-of-the-art forecasting architectures, particularly in few-shot transfer scenarios, offering a scalable path toward multivariate time series foundation models.

</details>


### [133] [When LLMs Learn to Be Consistently Wrong: A Multi-Model Study of Linear Representations of Synthetic Deception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30381)
*Vahideh Zolfaghari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过诱导模型产生合成虚假信息，发现大模型在欺骗时会在内部产生高度可探测的、领域无关的线性表征，且该表征在浅层即可被高精度识别，为AI安全监测提供了机制性依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虚假对齐（Deceptive alignment）是AI安全的重大挑战，研究者旨在通过“合成不诚实”（Synthetic dishonesty，即通过优化错误答案诱导）作为受控实验环境，探究模型虚假行为背后的表征机制。

Method: 构建了多模型范式，对五种不同架构的Transformer模型（Pythia, Gemma-2, Qwen2.5, Llama-3.1）进行LoRA微调，分别产生诚实与虚假变体；利用线性探测（Linear Probes）对隐藏状态进行解码，并结合Fisher判别比、有效秩、中心几何分析、方向稳定性及校准（ECE）等机制分析方法。

Result: 线性探测器可在模型极浅层（1-3层）以近乎完美的AUC（≥0.99）识别出虚假行为；实验验证了“线性表征假设”；探测器具有极强的跨领域泛化能力（TruthfulQA到MMLU）；Gemma-2在深层表现出极高的鲁棒性和稳定性。

Conclusion: 通过简单的监督微调可以迅速固化鲁棒且与领域无关的虚假信息表征；激活函数监控在识别模型欺骗行为方面具有高度可行性；不同模型架构在表征欺骗行为时表现出“表征坍缩”与“高维维持”两种截然不同的范式。

Abstract: Deceptive alignment, in which models maintain accurate internal representations while deliberately producing false outputs, remains a central challenge in AI safety. While strategic deception is the primary long-term concern, synthetic dishonesty - induced via direct optimization on incorrect answers - provides a controlled testbed for studying the representational basis of learned deception. We introduce a multi-model paradigm in which honest and deceptive variants of five transformer models (Pythia-1.4B, Gemma-2-2B/9B, Qwen2.5-7B, Llama-3.1-8B) are fine-tuned using LoRA on the same question distribution. Linear probes trained on mean-pooled hidden states detect synthetic dishonesty with near-perfect AUC (greater than or equal to 0.99) as early as layers 1-3 in four architectures, while Pythia-1.4B reaches a peak of 0.705. Logistic regression probes consistently match or outperform MLP probes, supporting the Linear Representation Hypothesis. Probes trained on TruthfulQA generalize with near-zero loss (Delta AUC approx. 0) to held-out MMLU subjects. Late-layer representations show strong robustness to Gaussian noise, with Gemma-2 models exhibiting exceptional stability. Mechanistic analysis of Fisher Discriminant Ratio, effective rank, centroid geometry, directional stability, cross-domain alignment, and calibration (ECE) reveals two regimes: representational collapse in Pythia/Llama/Qwen versus high-dimensional preservation in Gemma-2. Across all models, the dishonesty direction consolidates progressively in deeper layers, with optimal calibration (ECE less than 0.01 except Pythia) achievable in layers 1-4. These results demonstrate that robust, domain-invariant dishonesty representations can be rapidly entrenched via modest supervised fine-tuning, with implications for activation-based monitoring.

</details>


### [134] [Retriever Portfolios: A Principled Approach to Adaptive RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31176)
*Miltiadis Stouras,Vincent Cohen-Addad,Silvio Lattanzi,Ola Svensson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对RAG系统难以应对异构查询的问题，提出了一种能够自动选出最优检索器“组合”的方案，在提升QA性能的同时，通过并行化显著降低了计算开销和延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的RAG系统通常仅依赖单一检索器，难以应对从简单事实查询到复杂多跳推理等高度异构的查询分布需求。

Method: 提出了一种从大型候补池中自动选择小型、多样化检索器组合的算法。该方法将问题形式化为针对查询分布的“期望最佳-k”（expected best-of-k）优化目标，并利用高效算法实现组合构建及路由选择。

Result: 在多个QA基准测试中，所提出的组合方案在检索指标和回答质量上均优于单检索器和朴素多检索器基线；由于支持并行计算，该方案在实现同等精度的情况下，大幅降低了推理延迟和Token成本。

Conclusion: 所提出的自动构建检索器组合（Portfolio）的方法在保证检索效果和答案质量的同时，显著降低了推理延迟和计算成本，优于传统的单检索器系统及推理时调参方案。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems typically rely on a single retriever and a single set of hyperparameters, despite facing highly heterogeneous queries that range from simple factoid questions to complex multi-hop reasoning. We propose a method that automatically selects a small, diverse subset of retrievers (a portfolio) from a large pool of candidates, to cover different regions of the target query distribution. We formalize this setting via an expected best-of-$k$ objective over the query distribution and show that it admits an efficient portfolio construction algorithm with near-optimal guarantees. Across multiple QA benchmarks, our learned portfolios and router pipeline consistently outperform single-retriever and naive multi-retriever baselines on both retrieval metrics and answer quality. In addition, compared to inference-time hyperparameter tuning approaches, fixed portfolios enable parallel retrieval and LLM calls, achieving comparable (and sometimes better) accuracy with substantially lower latency and token cost.

</details>


### [135] [Reducing the GPU Memory Bottleneck with Lossless Compression for ML -- Extended](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30728)
*Aditya K Kamath,Arvind Krishnamurthy,Marco Canini,Simon Peter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为IBP（Invariant Bit Packing）的无损压缩算法，通过消除张量间的不变位并利用GPU特性加速解压，有效解决了ML任务中PCIe数据传输的瓶颈问题，显著提升了训练与推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习处理的大规模数据量常超出GPU内存，频繁通过PCIe进行数据传输成为性能瓶颈；虽然有损压缩可缓解该问题，但会带来难以预测的精度损失。因此，寻找一种无需顾虑精度问题的无损压缩技术成为迫切需求。

Method: 提出了“不变位填充”（Invariant Bit Packing, IBP）算法。该算法识别并消除张量组中的不变位，并结合GPU的warp并行性、低开销位操作和异步PCIe传输进行高效解压缩。

Result: 在GNN训练中性能提升74%，DLRM嵌入查询性能提升180%，LLM推理性能提升24%。

Conclusion: IBP通过一种基于消除不变位（invariant bits）的无损压缩技术，成功解决了ML任务中因PCIe带宽限制导致的传输瓶颈，在保持精度的同时显著提升了训练和推理性能。

Abstract: Machine learning (ML) training and inference often process data sets far exceeding GPU memory capacity, forcing them to rely on PCIe for on-demand tensor transfers, causing critical transfer bottlenecks. Lossy compression has been proposed to relieve bottlenecks but introduces workload-dependent accuracy loss, making it complex or even prohibitive to use in existing ML deployments. We explore lossless compression as an alternative that avoids this deployment complexity. We identify where lossless compression can be integrated into ML pipelines while minimizing interference with GPU execution. Based on our findings, we introduce Invariant Bit Packing (IBP), a novel lossless compression algorithm designed to minimize data transfer time for ML. IBP identifies and eliminates invariant bits across groups of tensors, improving throughput through GPU-optimized decompression that leverages warp parallelism, low-overhead bit operations, and asynchronous PCIe transfers. We provide easy-to-use APIs, showcasing them by adding IBP support to GNN training, as well as DLRM and LLM inference frameworks. IBP achieves, on average, 74% faster GNN training, 180% faster DLRM embedding lookup, and 24% faster LLM inference.

</details>


### [136] [A Novel Evaluation Metric for Unsupervised Learning in AIS-Based Maritime Anomaly Detection: MADQI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30388)
*Ismet Gocer,Zakirul Bhuiyan,Raza Hasan,Shakeel Ahmad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对无监督海上AIS异常检测缺乏统一评价标准的不足，提出了一种新的质量指标（MADQI），通过多维指标综合评估异常检测性能，实验证明该方法在识别异常行为方面表现鲁棒且可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有海上AIS异常检测多采用无监督学习算法（如Isolation Forest），但缺乏系统化、具有实际意义的性能评估指标。

Method: 提出了“海上异常检测质量指数（MADQI）”，该指标整合了异常率一致性（ARC）、物理合理性评分（PPS）、评分分布分离度（SDS）和极端案例证据（ECE）四个维度，并利用哈弗赛恩距离结合多块评估与自适应缩放技术进行分析。

Result: 实验结果显示，该框架的MADQI总得分为80.37%，其中ECE与ARC得分分别达到0.907和1.000，证明了其在识别极端异常和保持一致性方面的优异性能。

Conclusion: 本文提出的MADQI指标及评估框架为无监督海上AIS异常检测提供了一套系统、可靠且无需标注数据的评估方案，有效弥补了现有研究缺乏统一评估标准的缺陷。

Abstract: This paper introduces a new systematic framework for detecting anomalies in maritime Automatic Identification System (AIS) datasets. These anomalies include abnormal vessel behaviours related to speed, position jumps, time gaps, and turn angles. Although unsupervised learning algorithms such as Isolation Forest are widely used for detecting anomalous vessel movements, they often lack systematic and meaningful evaluation measures. To address this limitation, we propose a novel quality metric called Maritime Anomaly Detection Quality Index (MADQI). The prosed MADQI is a composite index designed to evaluate the anomaly detection performance of machine learning models without requiring labelled data. The proposed framework uses Haversine distance calculations to analyse AIS datasets and identify anomalies based on their spatial and behavioural characteristics. The proposed MADQI evaluation framework integrates four interconnected metrics: Anomaly Rate Consistency (ARC), Physical Plausibility Score (PPS), Score Distribution Separation (SDS), and Extreme Case Evidence (ECE). These metrics are combined through automatic normalisation using multi-chunk evaluation and adaptive scaling techniques. Experimental results on the AIS dataset show that the proposed framework achieved a MADQI score of 80.37%, demonstrating its effectiveness for unsupervised anomaly detection. In particular, the algorithm performed strongly in identifying abnormal vessel behaviour. Among the individual MADQI components, ECE and ARC achieved scores of 0.907 and 1.000, respectively, indicating excellent capability in detecting extreme anomalies and maintaining anomaly rate consistency. Overall, these results are encouraging and demonstrate that the proposed framework provides a reliable and meaningful approach for evaluating unsupervised anomaly detection in maritime AIS data.

</details>


### [137] [Federated Variational Preference Alignment with Gumbel-Softmax Prior for Personalized User Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30873)
*Jabin Koo,Hoyoung Kim,Minwoo Jang,Jungseul Ok*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了 FedVPA-GP 框架，通过联邦混合先验和正交损失优化，解决了联邦大模型对齐中变分偏好学习的后验坍塌难题，实现了更精准的个性化与偏好解耦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦大模型对齐方法常强制使用统一奖励模型，导致忽略了用户间冲突的偏好（如有用性 vs 无害性）；而直接将变分偏好学习（VPL）应用于联邦场景会面临数据稀疏和异构性带来的“后验坍塌”困境。

Method: 提出了 FedVPA-GP 框架：1. 引入“联邦混合先验”（Federated Mixture Prior）以利用群体分布缓解本地数据稀疏性；2. 引入“正交损失”（Orthogonal Loss）以在隐空间显式分离偏好原型。

Result: 在 HH-RLHF 数据集上的实验表明，FedVPA-GP 显著优于单一模型基线，能够成功解耦冲突的用户意图，并支持动态偏好切换。

Conclusion: FedVPA-GP 通过动态先验和正交损失有效解决了联邦学习中偏好建模的后验坍塌问题，证明了在保护隐私的同时实现个性化大模型对齐的可行性。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) offers a privacy-preserving pathway for aligning Large Language Models (LLMs); however, existing frameworks typically enforce a monolithic reward model, inevitably averaging out inherently conflicting user preferences (e.g., helpfulness vs. harmlessness). While Variational Preference Learning (VPL) offers a pathway to personalization, adapting it to decentralized settings presents a fundamental challenge: posterior collapse driven by severe local data scarcity and heterogeneity. In this paper, we propose Federated Variational Preference Alignment with Gumbel-Softmax Prior (FedVPA-GP), a framework designed to disentangle diverse preferences without compromising privacy. To stabilize variational inference, we introduce a Federated Mixture Prior that enables clients to leverage the aggregate population distribution as a dynamic prior. Furthermore, we incorporate an Orthogonal Loss that explicitly enforces the separation of preference prototypes in the latent space. Experiments on the HH-RLHF dataset demonstrate that FedVPA-GP significantly outperforms monolithic baselines, successfully disentangling conflicting user intents and enabling dynamic preference switching.

</details>


### [138] [NumLeak: Public Numeric Benchmarks as Latent Labels in Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30393)
*Anany Kotawala*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过NumLeak测量框架证实，顶级大模型在处理公开数值基准时存在严重的“记忆泄露”，其高性能表现更多源于对预训练数据的死记硬背而非真正的推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大模型在公开数值基准上的高性能表现可能源于预训练数据中的记忆，而非真正的样本外泛化能力。为了区分“记忆”与“推理”，需要量化评估模型对公共数值基准的记忆程度。

Method: 引入NumLeak框架，结合生产模型API边界探测（探测模型对公开数值基准的回忆能力）以及对开放因果语言模型的白盒可控验证（分析剂量反应关系及对数概率排名）。利用“留出法”（holdout）测试最新发布的数据，评估模型在未能涵盖的日期上的回答能力，并对比模型预测与真实数值的回归相关性。

Result: 顶级模型对Fama-French因子等数值的记忆相关系数高达0.97-0.99；在最新的留出测集中，虽然回答率下降，但已回答部分的相关性仍极高，证明存在明显的数值记忆。通过控制变量验证，证明模型在相关指标上的强回归能力在去除记忆成分后近乎失效（从r=0.74降至r=0.02）。

Conclusion: 现有的顶级大模型在处理特定数值数据时，表现出的并非真正的泛化能力，而是基于预训练数据中记忆的重现。当前的黑盒探测方法低估了模型对预训练数据的记忆泄露，简单的系统提示词防御在应对非对抗性攻击时虽有效，但面对记忆泄露的本质性问题，仍需更严谨的评估框架。

Abstract: Public numeric benchmarks appear in pretraining, so an evaluation that conditions on a date may be measuring memorized recall rather than out-of-sample skill. We introduce NumLeak, a measurement framework that combines API-boundary probes on production models with a white-box controlled validation on an open causal LM. Top-tier frontier LLMs recall the Fama-French market excess return at 3-seed pooled Pearson r=0.97-0.99 while staying within 0.15 within-25bps on the five sibling factors; comparable fidelity appears on U.S. unemployment, CPI inflation, and NOAA temperature. On a recent-release holdout, parse rate collapses to 21-57% but r stays at approximately 0.99 on months answered, the refuse-or-recall asymmetry a memorized channel predicts. The white-box experiment reproduces the dose-response, and logprob ranking detects memorization that open-ended generation misses, implying closed-API black-box probes understate the channel. A Sonnet "date to market-sentiment" regression that correlates with true Mkt-RF at r=0.74 collapses to r=0.02 once the model's own recall is residualized out. A one-line system-prompt defense blocks 99.8% of a non-adaptive single-turn suffix attack set at near-zero utility cost on conceptual and historical-narrative queries

</details>


### [139] [DG-CoLearn: An Efficient Collaborative Learning Framework for Dynamic Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31427)
*Ashley Hoi-Ting Au,Zikun Zhang,Ligang He,Qiang Ni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对协同动态图学习中的效率低与隐私泄露挑战，本文提出了 DG-CoLearn 框架，通过增量式图处理与隐私保护的服务器介导嵌入交换，在显著提升计算效率与降低通信开销的同时，强化了跨客户端的结构隐私保障及预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的动态图学习方法存在训练开销大（重复重训练整个快照）且不适应协同场景的问题；同时，在分布式环境中处理跨分区边时，直接共享图结构会违反隐私保护约束。

Method: 提出一种基于增量图快照处理的协作学习框架 DG-CoLearn，核心包含：1) 针对受时间更新影响的图局部区域进行增量计算；2) 引入服务器介导的嵌入交换机制，在不暴露原始跨客户端结构信息的前提下实现多跳消息传递。

Result: 相较于基线方法，DG-CoLearn 实现了最高 33.8 倍的训练加速和 27.4 倍的通信开销降低；在节点分类任务上 F1 分数提升高达 13.36%，在链路预测任务上 MAP 分数提升高达 8.27%。

Conclusion: DG-CoLearn 成功解决了协同动态图学习中计算效率低、通信开销大以及跨客户端结构性隐私泄露的问题，为大规模分布式动态图学习提供了一种高效且隐私保护的解决方案。

Abstract: Dynamic graph learning (DGL) is essential for modelling evolving graph data, but existing methods suffer from significant computational overhead due to repeated full-snapshot retraining and are not well-suited for collaborative settings with partitioned data. In realistic graph systems, cross-partition edges are unavoidable, but direct sharing of graph structure between clients may violate privacy constraints. We propose DG-CoLearn, a client-oblivious collaborative dynamic graph learning framework built on incremental graph snapshot processing, which focuses computation on graph regions affected by temporal updates while preserving historical information through temporal modelling. This incremental design is consistently applied across the entire graph processing pipeline, including a server-mediated embedding exchange mechanism to enable accurate multi-hop message passing without exposing raw cross-client structural information. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DG-CoLearn achieves up to 33.8$\times$ speedup in training time and 27.4$\times$ reduction in communication overhead, while consistently improving predictive performance on both node classification (up to 13.36% F1 improvement) and link prediction (up to 8.27% MAP improvement) tasks. These results highlight the effectiveness of DG-CoLearn in bridging efficiency, scalability, and client-to-client structural privacy in collaborative dynamic graph learning.

</details>


### [140] [LongDS-Bench: On the Failure of Long-Horizon Agentic Data Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30434)
*Kewei Xu,Xiaoben Lu,Shuofei Qiao,Zihan Ding,Haoming Xu,Lei Liang,Ningyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LongDS 是一个全新的长周期、多轮数据分析基准，揭示了当前智能体在处理长序列任务时状态维护能力不足的瓶颈，即使增加交互次数也难以弥补该缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的数据分析基准大多关注短时孤立任务，未能评估智能体在长周期内跟踪演进分析上下文的能力，导致其在实际、复杂的迭代式数据分析场景中表现不佳。

Method: 提出 LongDS 评测基准，包含 68 个基于真实 Kaggle 笔记本的任务（共 2225 轮对话），并通过反事实扰动、回滚和多状态组合等模式，重点测试智能体在长序列任务中维护、更新和恢复分析状态的能力。

Result: 最先进的智能体在 LongDS 上的平均准确率仅为 48.45%；随着任务轮次增加，性能显著下降（后期比早期下降约 47 个百分点）；长程依赖导致的错误占总失败案例的 52%--69%。

Conclusion: 现有智能体无法有效维护长周期的分析状态，增加交互步数并不能解决长程任务中的错误累积问题，研究强调了显式状态管理对于长程数据分析的重要性。

Abstract: Real-world data analysis is inherently iterative, yet existing benchmarks mostly evaluate isolated or short interactive tasks, leaving agents' ability to track evolving analytical context over long horizons untested. We introduce LongDS, a benchmark for long-horizon, multi-turn data analysis where agents must maintain, update, restore, and compose evolving analytical states. LongDS comprises 68 tasks constructed from real-world Kaggle notebooks, spanning 2,225 turns across six domains including Geoscience, Business, and Education. Tasks are designed around state-evolution patterns (e.g., counterfactual perturbation, rollback, multi-state composition), with an average dependency span of 11.3 turns. Evaluating five state-of-the-art models, we find that the best model reaches only 48.45% average accuracy, performance drops nearly 47 points from early to late turns, and long-horizon errors account for 52%--69% of failures. Further analysis shows that additional agent steps do not necessarily improve performance, suggesting that the key bottleneck is maintaining a correct analytical state rather than increasing interaction budget. We release LongDS to support research on reliable long-horizon agentic data analysis. Code and data will be released at https://github.com/zjunlp/DataMind.

</details>


### [141] [Calibrated Preference Learning: The Case of Label Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30447)
*Santo M. A. R. Thies,Viktor Bengs,Timo Kaufmann,Sebastian J. Vollmer,Eyke Hüllermeier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为概率标签排序问题引入了校准的正式框架，揭示了现有排序模型和RLHF奖励模型的校准缺陷，并证明了校准是衡量模型质量的重要度量指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的校准研究多集中于分类和回归，缺乏针对“概率标签排序”的正式校准方法。将排序视为简单类别会忽略其内在结构（如成对预测和Top-k模式）。

Method: 提出了标签排序的校准理论框架，建立了一个包含全排序、子排序和Top-k排序的校准层级体系；并通过实验评估了现有模型及RLHF奖励模型的校准性能。

Result: 证明了全排序校准能隐含其他校准级别；通过实证发现主流模型存在校准不良；发现RLHF奖励模型的校准度与基准测试准确率高度相关但并不完全一致，证明了校准度捕捉到了准确率之外的维度。

Conclusion: 本文正式定义了标签排序（Label Ranking）的校准概念，揭示了现有模型普遍存在校准不良的问题，并证明了校准质量与模型准确率相关，但评估了除了准确率之外的额外质量维度。

Abstract: Calibration, the alignment of predicted probabilities with true outcome frequencies, is essential for reliable decision-making. While extensively studied for classification and regression, calibration has not been formally addressed for probabilistic label ranking, where the goal is to predict a distribution over orderings of a label set. Naively treating rankings as classes ignores their structure and fails to capture important modalities such as pairwise and top-k predictions. We formalize calibration for label ranking and develop a hierarchy of notions covering full rankings, sub-rankings, and top-k rankings. We prove that full-rank calibration implies the others but not conversely, and sub-ranking and top-k calibration are incomparable. Empirically, we find popular label ranking models are often poorly calibrated, with substantial differences between sub-ranking and top-k metrics. Applying our framework to RLHF reward models, we find that calibration correlates strongly but not perfectly with benchmark accuracy, suggesting it captures a meaningful quality dimension beyond top-1 accuracy. These findings motivate future work on understanding the downstream effects of miscalibration and developing methods to correct it.

</details>


### [142] [Bounded Behavioral Indistinguishability for Black-Box LLM Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30448)
*Munawar Hasan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究指出大语言模型蒸馏中“语义相似”不等同于“行为一致”。论文提出了“行为不可区分性”评估框架，通过对抗性测试发现，现有的蒸馏方法（如LoRA）虽能提升语义契合度，但在模型风格、鲁棒性等深层行为特征上仍与教师模型存在可被测出的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的黑盒大语言模型蒸馏评价指标（如输出语义相似度）无法确保学生模型与教师模型在实际行为上完全一致，即“输出相似不等于行为不可分辨”。

Method: 1. 提出“有界行为不可区分性”框架（Bounded Behavioral Indistinguishability），通过四个参数($ε,q,t,A$)量化蒸馏效果。
2. 使用5,000个提示词的行为探测集，对比Qwen和Llama教师及其LoRA微调前后的学生模型。
3. 利用对抗性判别器、pairwise教师身份识别任务及A/B测试评估行为差异。

Result: 1. LoRA蒸馏确实提升了语义相似度，但仍存在显著的行为差异（判别器仍有非零优势）。
2. 模型偏差主要集中在风格/格式、鲁棒性和领域技术提示词中。
3. 对抗性评估揭示了虽然蒸馏降低了区分优势（例如Qwen从0.158降至0.081），但并未达到行为完全一致。
4. 发现差异引导的采样并不总是优于分层随机采样。

Conclusion: 语义相似度（输出匹配）不足以衡量蒸馏效果，黑盒大语言模型蒸馏需要引入有界、对抗性和类别感知的行为不可区分性评估方法。

Abstract: Black-box LLM distillation is usually evaluated as an output-matching problem: a student is considered successful when its responses are semantically similar to, or task-consistent with, those of a teacher. However, output similarity does not imply that the student is behaviorally indistinguishable from the model it imitates. We introduce bounded behavioral indistinguishability, formalized as $(ε,q,t,\mathbb{A})$-behavioral indistinguishability over an explicit prompt distribution, where $ε$ bounds distinguishing advantage, $q$ bounds oracle queries, $t$ bounds computation, and $\mathbb{A}$ denotes the adversary class.
  We instantiate this notion on Qwen and Llama teacher-student pairs using a controlled $5,000$-prompt behavioral probe suite. For each family, we compare the teacher with both the base student and the LoRA-distilled student, measuring whether distillation reduces distinguishability rather than merely improving similarity. LoRA raises semantic similarity from $0.788$ to $0.862$ for Qwen and from $0.814$ to $0.874$ for Llama. Yet adversarial evaluation reveals remaining behavioral differences: learned discriminators retain nonzero advantage, and pairwise category analysis shows artifacts concentrated in style/format, robustness, and domain-technical prompts. A pairwise teacher-identification adversary confirms this trend. With a different-family Llama judge and A/B-swap consistency filtering, Qwen distinguishing advantage drops from $0.158$ for the base student to $0.081$ after LoRA distillation. Query-budget experiments show that disagreement-guided acquisition does not consistently outperform stratified random sampling, indicating that coverage and diversity remain strong baselines. Our results show that semantic fidelity is useful but insufficient: black-box LLM distillation requires bounded, adversarial, and category-aware evaluation.

</details>


### [143] [VeriGate: Verifier-Gated Step-Level Supervision for GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30451)
*Aakriti Agrawal,Minghui Liu,Furong Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VeriGate 是一种针对 GRPO 的改进算法，通过引入“结果反馈门控”和“细粒度步骤级信用分配”，解决了推理训练中常见的奖励稀疏与学习停滞问题，显著提升了模型推理准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 GRPO 在推理训练中存在两个主要局限：1. 在结果奖励相同（即所有样本评分一致）时导致梯度归零，学习停滞；2. 缺乏步骤级的信用分配（credit assignment），限制了模型对复杂推理路径的探索和稳健性。

Method: 提出 VeriGate（Verifier-Gated Step-Level GRPO）方法，包含三个关键设计：1. 门控机制：在结果奖励失效（退化）时动态引入过程监督（PRM）；2. 未来累积奖励：将 PRM 分数转化为支持后续步骤归因的未来累积奖励；3. 组内归一化 Token 级优势：将上述奖励转化为组内归一化的 Token 级优势，实现细粒度的指导。

Result: 在 MATH 数据集上使用 Qwen2.5-Instruct (1.5B/7B) 模型进行验证，结果显示：1. 平均准确率分别提升了约 20% 和 12%；2. 大幅降低了零梯度失败率；3. 减少了奖励作弊现象，提升了整体推理质量。

Conclusion: VeriGate 通过结合结果监督和过程监督，有效缓解了传统 GRPO 在推理训练中面临的奖励稀疏和零梯度问题。该方法提高了推理模型的准确性，增强了训练稳定性，并减少了奖励作弊（reward hacking）行为，证明了细粒度奖励机制在复杂推理任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) is an effective recipe for training reasoning models with verifier-based outcome rewards, but its supervision is sparse: when all sampled trajectories for a prompt receive the same verifier reward, the group-relative advantage collapses to zero and learning stalls. Outcome-only rewards also provide no step-level credit assignment, limiting exploration and making it harder to learn robust reasoning. We present VeriGate (Verifier-Gated Step-Level GRPO), a verifier-gated extension of GRPO that addresses these limitations with three design choices. First, VeriGate keeps the verifier in charge whenever verifier rewards induce a meaningful preference among sampled trajectories, and uses process supervision only when verifier rewards are degenerate. Second, instead of collapsing Process Reward Model (PRM) step scores into a single trajectory reward, VeriGate converts them into future-cumulated rewards to assign continuation-aware credit. Third, VeriGate transforms these rewards into group-normalized token-level advantages, restoring informative gradients and fine-grained credit assignment while remaining less susceptible to reward hacking than methods that optimize aggregated PRM scores. Empirically, training on MATH with 1.5B and 7B Qwen2.5-Instruct models and evaluating on six reasoning benchmarks, VeriGate improves average accuracy by about 20% and 12% for 1.5B and 7B models respectively, substantially reduces zero-gradient failures, decreases reward-hacking behavior, and improves reasoning quality relative to outcome-only GRPO and PRM-as-outcome baselines.

</details>


### [144] [A Unified Framework for Gradient Aggregation in Multi-Objective Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30452)
*Zeou Hu,Kelvin Ho,Yaoliang Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套多目标梯度优化（MOO）的统一框架，通过对齐条件和原始优化视角解释了现有算法的收敛机制，并据此提出了鲁棒的新算法Capped MGDA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多目标优化（MOO）中各种现有算法缺乏统一的理论框架，且对收敛性的分析多为个案，导致算法间的理论联系不清，缺乏统一的收敛保证。

Method: 1. 提出梯度聚合的统一框架，分析收敛速率；
2. 建立“充分对齐条件”（Sufficient Alignment Condition）；
3. 引入对偶锥投影（Projection onto the dual cone）以保证可行性；
4. 构建原始优化视角（Primal optimization perspective）下的梯度聚合算法联系（涵盖MGDA等）；
5. 提出基于CVaR的Capped MGDA算法。

Result: 1. 建立了多目标优化中向Pareto平稳性收敛的优化速率；
2. 证明了凸包内非冲突梯度方向的选择是收敛的充分条件；
3. 将多种现有MOO算法纳入统一的原始优化框架；
4. Capped MGDA算法在对抗联邦学习任务中表现出优异的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 本文构建了梯度聚合的通用理论框架，证明了在凸包内选择非冲突方向是实现Pareto平稳性收敛的充分条件。通过对偶锥投影保证了可行性，并从原始优化视角统一了现有算法，为设计新算法（如Capped MGDA）提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: Many machine learning problems involve multiple inherent trade-offs that are best addressed by gradient-based multi-objective optimization (MOO) algorithms. Existing methods are often proposed with various motivations, analyzed case by case, and differ algorithmically in how the component gradients are aggregated at each step. In this work, we develop a unifying framework for gradient aggregation in MOO, establishing (optimal) rates of convergence to Pareto stationarity, the standard measure of performance in MOO. Central to our analysis is a sufficient alignment condition, from which we derive a theorem showing that non-conflicting directions, when chosen within the convex hull of gradients, form a fundamental sufficient condition for convergence. We further show that feasibility can be ensured through projection onto the dual cone, broadening the scope of methods that admit convergence guarantees. In parallel, we present a primal optimization perspective of gradient aggregation that encompasses established algorithms, clarifies their theoretical relationships, and enables the design of new variants. As an illustration, we introduce capped MGDA, derived from a CVaR-based formulation, and demonstrate its robustness in adversarial federated learning. Finally, we validate our theory through experiments on synthetic problems and practical benchmarks.

</details>


### [145] [Scalable Constrained Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning via State Augmentation and Consensus for Separable Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30461)
*Santiago Amaya-Corredor,Miguel Calvo-Fullana,Anders Jonsson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于对偶变量分布式共识的约束MARL算法，解决了大规模多智能体系统中全局约束难以满足且计算复杂度随规模爆炸的问题，实现了线性规模扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有独立学习（Independent RL）在处理具有全局资源约束的系统时无法有效协调个体行为，导致不可行解；而中心化训练分布式执行（CTDE）方案在智能体数量增加时复杂度呈二次增长，难以在大规模系统中扩展。

Method: 提出了一种结合状态增强策略学习（state-augmented policy learning）与对偶变量（Lagrange multipliers）分布式共识的方法。智能体在训练时通过局部通信达成对偶变量的一致性，从而在分布式训练和执行的同时实现全局约束受控。

Result: 实验表明该方法能有效协调数千个智能体，在电网需求响应场景下，相比基准方案能够在满足全局容量约束的同时保证任务履行，且在计算复杂度和扩展性上显著优于CTDE方案。

Conclusion: 分布式共识约束MARL方法证明了在多智能体系统中，通过相邻智能体间的拉格朗日乘子（dual variables）共识，能够在无需集中式架构的情况下实现全局约束满足，且具有良好的大规模扩展性。

Abstract: We present a distributed approach for constrained Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL) that combines state-augmented policy learning with distributed consensus over dual variables. Our method targets systems where agents have separable dynamics but must coordinate to satisfy global resource constraints, a setting in which, as we demonstrate empirically, independent learning fails to produce feasible solutions because agents cannot determine appropriate individual contributions toward collective constraint satisfaction. The key technical contribution is showing that lightweight neighbor-to-neighbor consensus over Lagrange multipliers suffices for globally coordinated constraint enforcement while preserving the scalability of independent training. Each agent learns a single augmented policy offline, conditioned on both its local state and a dual variable encoding constraint feedback. During execution, agents reach agreement on this dual variable through local communication alone. We prove that under mild connectivity assumptions, the consensus error among agents' multipliers is bounded, and show that this translates to a bounded constraint violation that decreases with graph connectivity and the number of consensus rounds. Unlike centralized training with decentralized execution (CTDE) approaches, whose complexity grows at least quadratically with agent count, our method scales linearly in both training and execution. Experiments on smart grid demand response demonstrate that consensus coordination is \emph{essential for feasibility}: without it, agents satisfy grid capacity constraints only by indefinitely postponing demand, a degenerate non-solution. With consensus, agents converge to a shared dual variable and satisfy both grid constraints and demand fulfillment, scaling to thousands of agents while CTDE baselines are limited to dozens.

</details>


### [146] [idSCD: Identifying Training Datasets through Semantic Correlation Descriptors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30462)
*Andrada Gobeaja,Ionut Hodoroaga,Elena Burceanu,Marius Leordeanu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于“语义关联描述符”（SCD）的白盒数据集成员推断方法。研究发现模型会在训练中内化数据集特有的伪相关性，通过分析这些“语义指纹”，攻击者或研究者可以高精度地识别模型是否曾使用过特定数据集进行训练，效果大幅优于传统黑盒方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据集成员推断方法大多依赖于黑盒的预测行为（如置信度、损失值或生成样本），缺乏对模型内部特征关联的本质探究。作者试图解决模型是否因训练数据中附带的特定伪相关性（spurious correlations）而留下可追踪的逻辑痕迹的问题。

Method: 提出了一种基于语义关联描述符（SCD, Semantic Correlation Descriptors）的白盒指纹识别方法。该方法通过捕获模型学习到的语义相关性结构，使其具备跨数据集的可比性。通过对比目标数据集的SCD与模型中提取的SCD，在无需“留一法”训练模型的情况下，计算成员关系的分数。

Result: SCD方法在自然语言推理、情感分类与医学文本分类任务中表现优异，ROC-AUC指标相较于RMIA、Attack-P、LiRA和SIF等基准方法均有显著提升，在语义特征明显的任务中相对增益超过60%。

Conclusion: 深度学习模型往往会内化训练数据中非因果的“伪相关”特征，这些特征构成了数据集特有的“语义指纹”。通过本文提出的SCD方法，研究人员可以高精度地推断特定数据集是否参与了模型的训练，证明了模型内部的语义关联结构可作为数据集归属推断的有效判据，打破了传统的黑盒评估依赖。

Abstract: Can a dataset be recognized from the spurious correlations it induces during training? We argue that datasets leave dataset-specific traces in a model's learned semantic correlation structure: incidental regularities that are predictive within a dataset, but not causal for the underlying task, can be internalized during training.
