To maximize expected return, and without further explicit prompting, the policies PromptPO outputs range from tuned proportional controllers or rule-based plans to policies that run planning algorithms like value iteration. Our results demonstrate that LLM-based policy optimization is sufficient when the LLM can leverage prior knowledge about the environment or optimization strategy. PromptPO underperforms standard RL baselines in MuJoCo domains. This demonstrates possible limitations of LLM-based policy optimization to settings that requiring fine-grained continuous control.

</details>


### [184] [Self-Certifying Transport MCMC via Dual Spectral-Gap Certificates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30722)
*Jun Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 CerT-MCMC 框架，引入两种针对归一化流的收敛性保障证书，有效解决了学习型 MCMC 难以验证收敛效率的难题，在保证维度的鲁棒性的同时，比传统评估指标具有更高的敏感度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的学习型 MCMC 方法缺乏严谨的收敛保证，难以判断模型是未能成功学习后验分布，还是现有的收敛证明技术过于保守。

Method: 提出 CerT-MCMC 框架：1. 使用归一化流（Normalizing Flow）作为独立 MH 算法的建议分布；2. 提出“覆盖证书”（Covering Certificate，针对全空间，受维度影响大）；3. 提出“分位数核心证书”（Quantile-core Certificate，基于高概率核心区域，维度鲁棒性强）。

Result: 1. 理论上证明了覆盖证书存在 O(n^{-1/D}) 的维度壁垒；2. 实证显示分位数核心证书在 D=20 的案例中仍能提供非平凡的谱间隙界；3. 该方法对流模型质量的区分度比单纯观察接受率高出 10 倍以上。

Conclusion: CerT-MCMC 是首个为学习型传输 MCMC 提供自动、维度感知收敛证书的框架。研究通过双证书机制，成功区分了流模型性能不足与证明技术局限，证明了点状 Lipschitz 认证存在维度诅咒，并验证了 quantile-core 证书在处理高维问题时的有效性。

Abstract: We propose CerT-MCMC, a framework that equips learned-transport Markov chain Monte Carlo with automatic, rigorous convergence certificates. A normalising flow maps a Gaussian reference to an approximation of the target posterior; the same flow then serves as both the independence Metropolis-Hastings proposal and the basis for a computable spectral-gap bound. We develop two complementary certificates. The covering certificate bounds the weight-ratio oscillation over the full proposal support via finite-sample covering arguments, yielding full-support spectral-gap bounds when a conservative gradient bound is available; its correction term scales as O(n^{-1/D}), making it rapidly weak and eventually vacuous as dimension increases. We prove a matching Omega(n^{-1/D}) lower bound, establishing that this barrier is intrinsic to pointwise Lipschitz certification. The quantile-core certificate restricts attention to a high-probability residual core on which the oscillation is controlled by one-dimensional empirical quantiles, with a finite-sample probability slack of O(n^{-1/2}), independent of the ambient dimension. On synthetic targets (D=2-20), structural-engineering posteriors (D=6,8), real-data logistic regression on the Heart Disease data set (D=13), and synthetic Bayesian logistic regression (D=20), the quantile-core certificate delivers non-vacuous spectral-gap bounds where the covering certificate is vacuous, and its spectral-gap proxy tracks empirical effective sample sizes within 7%. A negative control experiment confirms that the certificate discriminates flow quality by a factor exceeding 10x, whereas acceptance rates differ by only 1.15x. To our knowledge, the dual-certificate framework is the first to provide automatic, dimension-aware convergence certificates for learned-transport MCMC, distinguishing genuine transport failure from proof-technique limitations.

</details>


### [185] [OrcaRouter: A Production-Oriented LLM Router with Hybrid Offline-Online Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30736)
*Zhenghua Bao,Fengya Tian,Chris Zhang,Zhenjun Chen,Xile Ma,Yi Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OrcaRouter 是一款生产级 LLM 路由框架，通过混合离线学习与在线上下文 Bandit 算法，在保持低推理成本的同时实现了高查询处理精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型（LLM）数量增加，不同模型的能力与推理成本差异巨大，亟需一种智能路由方案来根据用户请求选择最优模型，以实现成本效益最大化。

Method: 提出了一种基于 LinUCB 的上下文多臂老虎机（Contextual Bandit）算法，利用词汇和句子嵌入特征构建模型路由策略；采用“离线训练（基于奖励矩阵拟合 Ridge 回归）+ 在线学习（根据反馈实时更新）”的混合学习协议。

Result: 在 RouterArena 公开排行榜上获得第二名（得分 72.08），以每 1,000 次查询 1 美元的成本实现了 75.54% 的路由准确率。

Conclusion: OrcaRouter 通过结合离线预训练和在线 Bandit 学习，在大规模语言模型路由任务中实现了性能与成本的平衡，证明了高效的路由机制在生产环境中的实用价值。

Abstract: The rapid development of large language models, each with distinct capabilities and inference costs, raises a practical deployment question: given an incoming request, which model should handle it? We present OrcaRouter, a production-oriented LLM router that combines a LinUCB-based contextual bandit over lexical and sentence-embedding features with a hybrid offline-online learning protocol. Offline, OrcaRouter obtains full-information feedback by evaluating each candidate model on a curated set of routing prompts, yielding a reward matrix used to fit one ridge regressor per arm. At deployment time, it initializes from these parameters and can optionally continue learning from bandit feedback, updating only the selected model's arm after observing its reward. At the time of our RouterArena submission (May 20, 2026), OrcaRouter-Adaptive ranked second on the public RouterArena leaderboard with an arena score of 72.08, achieving 75.54% accuracy at a cost of USD 1.00 per 1,000 queries.

</details>


### [186] [Chain-of-Thought and Compressed Looped Transformers: A Memory-Budget Separation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30757)
*Haozhou Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现测试时推理的效果取决于内存机制：思维链（CoT）依靠扩展上下文实现无限“存储便笺”，优于受限于固定递归状态大小的压缩循环架构。后者即便增加计算步数，也无法克服内存容量导致的复杂性瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 思维链（CoT）和循环Transformer都通过增加测试时计算来提升模型表现，但两者在“记忆”机制上存在本质区别（即上下文存储 vs. 递归隐藏状态）。作者旨在探究这种“持久可变内存”作为测试时推理核心资源的属性及其局限性。

Method: 比较了三种内存架构在测试时推理表现的差异：1. 压缩潜在循环（Compressed latent loop）；2. 全序列状态循环（Full sequence-state loop）；3. 思维链便笺（Chain-of-thought scratchpad）。利用控制性的指针追踪（pointer-chasing）和联想召回（associative-recall）任务进行实验，并结合计算复杂性理论进行理论分析。

Result: 受限于递归状态大小，压缩循环在多次迭代后依然是“小空间推理者”，无法解决需要多项式空间复杂度的复杂问题。相比之下，思维链由于可以随步数增长动态扩展其“便笺”，从而能够处理更复杂的推理任务。全序列状态循环由于记忆资源更丰富，表现更接近CoT。

Conclusion: 测试时计算的有效性取决于“持久可变内存”的容量。压缩循环模型因受限于有限的递归状态空间，无法解决P-完全问题，而拥有无限扩展上下文的思维链（CoT）则无此限制。因此，对于高空间需求的推理任务，基于上下文存储的CoT优于固定状态大小的压缩循环。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought prompting and looped Transformers both give a fixed model more test-time computation, but they differ in what they remember. Chain-of-thought stores intermediate state in generated tokens that remain in the context, whereas a looped Transformer carries state through recurrent hidden activations. We argue that this persistent mutable memory is a central resource for test-time reasoning.
  We compare three memory regimes, the compressed latent loop, the full sequence-state loop, and the chain-of-thought scratchpad. Our main result shows that a compressed loop is limited by the size of its recurrent state. Running the loop longer adds computation but does not by itself create a growing scratchpad, so a loop with a small recurrent state remains a small-space reasoner even when run for many steps. Under a standard complexity assumption, such loops cannot decide problems that are P-complete under logspace reductions, whereas polynomial-length chain-of-thought can.
  The separation is specific to compressed loops, as full sequence-state loops carry state at every input position and live in a memory-rich regime closer to explicit scratchpads. Controlled pointer-chasing and associative-recall sweeps illustrate this memory-budget view, with performance sensitive to whether the persistent-state budget matches the task's working-memory demand.

</details>


### [187] [Smaller Models are Natural Explorers for Policy-Level Diversity in GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30789)
*Yiming Ren,Yiran Xu,Zicheng Lin,Chufan Shi,Yukang Chen,Dingdong Wang,Tianhe Wu,Junjie Wang,Yujiu Yang,Yu Qiao,Ruihang Chu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对 GRPO 训练中多样性与连贯性的矛盾，该论文提出 S2L-PO 框架，通过引入较小模型提供结构化的探索信号，配合渐进式退火策略，在提升数学推理基准表现的同时降低了推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指出传统 GRPO 中通过增加 token 级随机性来增强多样性的方法容易引入噪声并破坏逻辑连贯性，而较小模型天然具备更高的一致性策略多样性，可作为更优质的探索资源。

Method: 提出了 S2L-PO（Small-to-Large Policy Optimization）框架，利用同系列中较小模型具备的高策略多样性作为“探索者”，并设计了从“离线小模型采样”向“大模型自身采样”过渡的渐进式退火策略。

Result: 在数学推理基准测试中（如 AIME 24），使用 1.7B 模型引导 8B 模型实现了 8.8% 的精度提升，同时减少了 rollout 阶段的计算资源消耗。

Conclusion: S2L-PO 证明了利用较小模型作为探索者（Explorer）优于单纯增加随机性，通过渐进式退火策略，不仅提升了数学推理任务的性能（如 AIME 24 提升 8.8%），还显著降低了 rollout 计算成本，为大模型的强化学习训练探索提供了一种高效且稳定范式。

Abstract: We identify a new dimension for enhancing rollout diversity in Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) for LLMs. While GRPO relies on diverse rollouts, prevailing strategies primarily increase diversity by injecting more token-level randomness, which may introduce step-wise noise and lead to incoherent trajectories. We uncover that smaller models within the same model family inherently exhibit higher policy-level diversity, indicated by their superior pass@k relative to larger counterparts as sample counts increase. Unlike token-level noise, this diversity is temporally correlated, preserves logical consistency, and provides structured exploration signals for gradient estimation. We thus propose S2L-PO (Small-to-Large Policy Optimization), a framework that leverages fixed small models as natural explorers to train larger models. To balance exploration and exploitation, we design a progressive annealing strategy that transitions from offline small-model rollouts to the large learner's own sampling. This shift elegantly avoids mid-training performance drops caused by the small model's capacity limits, achieving faster convergence and unlocking a higher performance ceiling. S2L-PO improves accuracy on diverse mathematical reasoning benchmarks (e.g., +8.8% on AIME 24 using a 1.7B explorer to guide the 8B model) while reducing rollout compute.

</details>


### [188] [Conformal Reliability: A New Evaluation Metric for Conditional Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30807)
*Yachen Gao,Xinwei Sun,Yikai Wang,Ye Shi,Jingya Wang,Jianfeng Feng,Yanwei Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了CReL框架，通过保形预测构建可靠性评分，旨在有效评估条件生成模型在给定置信度下的最坏情况性能，解决了现有指标难以量化生成模型不确定性与风险的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生成模型评估指标通常仅评估单一输出，无法充分捕捉模型在生成过程中的不确定性、变异性及潜在风险，缺乏衡量模型可靠性的统一标准。

Method: 提出了一种基于保形预测（Conformal Prediction）的“可靠性评分”（Reliability Score）指标，并开发了名为CReL（Conformal ReLiability）的计算框架。该框架利用保形预测构建满足覆盖率要求的预测集，并通过优化算法计算该集内的最坏性能。

Result: 理论上证明了该方法满足覆盖率保证；实验证明其能构建比现有Baseline更具信息量的预测集；在合成数据及图像-文本/文本-图像任务中验证了其指标的可解释性及计算框架的有效性。

Conclusion: CReL框架通过结合保形预测，成功量化了条件生成模型在指定置信水平下的最坏情况性能，为评估生成模型的不确定性和可靠性提供了一种有效且具可解释性的方法。

Abstract: Conditional generative models have recently achieved remarkable success in various applications. However, a suitable metric for evaluating the reliability of these models, which takes into account their inherent uncertainty, is still lacking. Existing metrics, which typically assess a single output, may fail to capture the variability or potential risks in generation. In this paper, we propose a novel evaluation metric called reliability score based on conformal prediction, which measures the worst-case performance within the prediction set at a pre-specified confidence level. However, computing this score is challenging due to the high-dimensional nature of the output space and the nonconvexity of both the metric function and the prediction set. To efficiently compute this score, we introduce Conformal ReLiability (CReL), a framework that can (i) construct the prediction set with desired coverage; and (ii) accurately optimize the reliability score within the constructed prediction set. We provide theoretical results on coverage and demonstrate empirically that our method produces more informative prediction sets than existing approaches. Experiments on synthetic data and the image-to-text and text-to-image tasks further demonstrate the interpretability of our new metric, and the validity and effectiveness of our computational framework. Source code can be found at https://ggc29.github.io/CReL/.

</details>


### [189] [IRIS: time-structured manifold projections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30810)
*Brian Ondov,Chia-Hsuan Chang,Weipeng Zhou,Xingjian Zhang,Xueqing Peng,Yutong Xie,Huan He,Qiaozhu Mei,Hua Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: IRIS 是一种能够将时间信息融入流形学习的新算法，解决了传统降维方法无法直观展示高维数据动态变化的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的流形学习算法（如 t-SNE 和 UMAP）在处理高维时序生物医学数据时无法整合时间顺序，导致难以有效展示细胞类型或类别的动态变化过程。

Method: 开发了一种新的流形学习算法 IRIS，该算法在进行降维布局时同时考虑了数据点的拓扑结构和时间顺序信息。

Result: IRIS 能够有效地对多种动态生物医学数据（如 scRNA-seq、比较元基因组学数据和文献数据）进行可视化，清晰地展示其动态演变路径。

Conclusion: IRIS 成功地将时间维度整合到流形学习中，提供了一种既能反映数据固有拓扑结构又能展示时间演变过程的新型可视化方法。

Abstract: High-dimensional biomedical data, such as cell-by-gene matrices, are increasingly generated temporally. However, Manifold Learning algorithms, like t-SNE and UMAP, cannot incorporate time-ordering in their layouts, obfuscating the dynamics of cell types or other classes. As a solution, we present IRIS, a new Manifold Learning algorithm that structures layouts both chronologically and by manifold topology. IRIS can visualize a wide range of dynamic biomedical data, including scRNA-seq, comparative metagenomics, and literature.

</details>


### [190] [Learning Permutation-invariant Macroscopic Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30812)
*Zhichao Han,Mengyi Chen,Qianxiao Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对无序微观系统的置换不变自动编码器，通过重构质量分布而非逐点重构，成功实现了对高维系统宏观动力学的有效学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多科学系统（如粒子系统）的微观状态本质上是无序的，而现有数据驱动的自编码器方法通常假设输入存在固定的顺序，无法直接有效处理此类无序数据。

Method: 提出了一种置换不变（Permutation-invariant）自动编码器框架：编码器具备置换不变性，解码器重构的是观察点处的质量分布（而非点对点重构），并联合学习了潜在状态的宏观动力学模型。

Result: 在交互粒子系统的能量动力学、Lennard-Jones流体的混合动力学以及聚合物拉伸动力学等多种实验中，验证了该方法的有效性和稳健性。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的置换不变自动编码器及其联合动态学习框架，能够有效且稳健地从无序微观系统中提取宏观动力学规律，克服了传统方法对输入顺序的依赖限制。

Abstract: Accurately modeling the macroscopic dynamics of high-dimensional microscopic systems is of broad interest across the sciences. Many data-driven approaches learn a low-dimensional latent state through an autoencoder trained for pointwise input reconstruction. These methods typically assume a fixed ordering of microscopic degrees of freedom in the input. However, in many settings, such as particle systems, the microscopic state is inherently unordered. This motivates an autoencoder framework that learns permutation-invariant latent representations. To this end, we adopt a permutation-invariant encoder and design the decoder to reconstruct the mass distribution centered at the observed points rather than per-sample reconstruction. We then jointly learn the macroscopic dynamics of the observables together with the latent states. We demonstrate the effectiveness and robustness of the proposed method across a range of microscopic settings, including learning the energy dynamics in interacting particle systems, predicting mixing dynamics in Lennard-Jones fluids, and modeling the stretching dynamics from video data of polymers moving in an elongational force field.

</details>


### [191] [Cross-Layer Subspace Coupling for LLM Compression: A Unifying Framework and Its Empirical Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30836)
*Snigdha Chandan Khilar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 大模型压缩的跨层联合优化（如SVD）虽能降低数学上的权重误差，但因违背了Transformer残差流的独立性机制，导致模型实际性能大幅下降，证明了仅关注权重重建而非激活值重建的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于SVD的大模型压缩方法（如SVD LLM和Basis Sharing）虽在数学上表现出更低的权重重建误差，但在下游任务（困惑度、准确率）中效果较差，亟待解释其背后的失败机制。

Method: 通过数学框架统一现有的SVD压缩方法，并从机制上对比分析跨层联合优化（Bundle Method）与模型前向传播机制（残差流）之间的矛盾。

Result: 实验表明，虽然联合优化可将权重重建误差降低46%，但由于Transformer残差流机制有效地解耦了相邻层，导致跨层优化并未带来实际性能提升，反而不如传统的层内SVD优化。

Conclusion: 基于“权重空间重建”的跨层压缩（如SVD LLM的变体）在实际任务中效果不佳，未来的研究重点应转向“基于激活值的层内重建”。

Abstract: Recent SVD based compression methods for large language models like SVD LLM and Basis Sharing can be unified under one optimization problem. While mathematical proofs and tests on Pythia models show this unified approach improves weight reconstruction error by up to 46% percent it fails in practical tasks. Downstream metrics like perplexity and accuracy severely degrade compared to standard per layer SVD LLM. The authors explain this failure mechanistically. Although the bundle method mathematically couples adjacent layers the transformer residual stream actually decouples them during forward passes. Thus per layer optimality matters more than joint cross layer optimization. The paper concludes that weight space reconstruction is a flawed objective for cross layer compression and future methods must focus on per layer activation reconstruction instead.

</details>


### [192] [A Lecture Note on Offline RL and IRL, Part II: Foundations of Inverse Reinforcement Learning and Dynamic Discrete Choice Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30843)
*Enoch Hyunwook Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出结构计量经济学中的动态离散选择（DDC）与机器学习中的熵正则化逆强化学习（IRL）实则为同一模型，通过数学证明其等价性，并对比分析了经典算法与现代判别式IRL方法的优劣，最后强调了基于经验风险最小化的统一估计框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决逆强化学习（IRL）的核心问题，即如何从专家离线数据中恢复其优化奖励函数。文章旨在桥接计量经济学与机器学习两个独立领域，并统一两者在面对这一问题时所使用的模型与算法。

Method: 1. 数学证明：证明了结构计量经济（DDC）与现代机器学习（熵正则化IRL）模型的等价性；2. 文献综述与比较：系统梳理了Rust、Hotz and Miller、Adusumilli and Eckardt等经典算法的局限性；3. 现代方法评估：深入分析Adversarial IRL、IQ-Learn等现代算法的识别能力与目标函数；4. 框架整合：探讨了基于经验风险最小化（ERM）的梯度估计方法。

Result: 确立了DDC与IRL的理论等价性；明确指出了经典算法在维度灾难、转移核估计和偏差等方面的局限性；阐明了现代ML领域IRL算法（如IQ-Learn等）的数学本质与识别界限。

Conclusion: 本文证实了DDC与熵正则化IRL在概率模型上的等价性，梳理了从经典计量经济学到现代机器学习在逆强化学习领域的理论演进，并指出基于经验风险最小化（ERM）的方法提供了一种统一且有效的估计框架。

Abstract: In the forward reinforcement-learning problem, the reward is fixed and known; the learner is asked to find a good policy or value function. Here we turn the question around. Given offline data generated by an expert, can we recover the reward the expert was optimizing? This is the inverse reinforcement learning problem, and remarkably, two communities, structural econometricians studying dynamic discrete choice (DDC) and machine learners studying entropy-regularized IRL, have been working on exactly the same probabilistic model under different names. We begin by proving their equivalence. We then develop the classical identification result of Magnac and Thesmar and the classical computational paradigms that grew out of it: Rust's nested fixed-point algorithm, the conditional-choice-probability approach of Hotz and Miller, and the two temporal-difference approaches of Adusumilli and Eckardt: linear semi-gradient TD and approximate value iteration. Each route has its limits: dimensionality, transition-kernel estimation, the deadly triad, or projected fixed-point bias. We then walk through the modern ML/IRL strand: adversarial IRL, occupancy matching, IQ-Learn, and offline ML-IRL, deriving each method's actual objective and stating precisely what it does and does not identify. We close with the empirical-risk-minimization framework of Kang et al., which yields a gradient-based estimator for offline IRL/DDC.

</details>


### [193] [ForecastCompass: Guiding Agentic Forecasting with Adaptive Factor Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30858)
*Yurui Chang,Yongkang Du,Yuanpu Cao,Jinghui Chen,Lu Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FoCo是一种针对智能体预测任务设计的因子导向记忆框架，通过显式存储预测因子与推理原则，并利用事后分析实现知识的持续迭代，有效改善了智能体的预测准确度与概率校准能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体记忆方法侧重于交互历史或事实存储，缺乏专门针对预测任务的“可复用预测因子”与“校准知识”的建模，导致在复杂动态环境下的预测能力受限。

Method: 提出ForecastCompass (FoCo)框架，包含：1. 分层预测任务分类法（组织经验）；2. 因子记忆（存储可复用的预测维度）；3. 推理记忆（存储概率更新、不确定性处理及校准原则）；4. 基于事后分析的语言化记忆修订机制（迭代优化记忆）。

Result: 在Prophet Arena和FutureX数据集上，通过GPT-5-mini和Gemini-2.5-Flash实验验证，FoCo在预测准确性和校准性指标上均有显著提升。

Conclusion: FoCo框架通过分层经验组织与双重记忆机制，有效提升了智能体在动态环境下的预测准确性和校准能力，证明了显式存储预测因子与推理原则对提升预测性能的价值。

Abstract: Agentic forecasting is important for decision-making in dynamic environments, but it remains challenging because agents must reason from incomplete, time-limited evidence and produce calibrated probabilities before outcomes are resolved. Memory provides a natural mechanism for transferring experience from resolved forecasts to future prediction tasks. However, existing agent-memory methods are not tailored to forecasting, as they typically store past interactions, reflections, or factual associations without explicitly representing reusable predictive factors or calibration knowledge. We propose ForecastCompass (FoCo), an adaptive factor-based memory framework for agentic forecasting. FoCo organizes forecasting experience with a hierarchical forecasting-task taxonomy, enabling retrieval task-relevant forecasting knowledge. It maintains two complementary memory components: factor memory, which captures reusable predictive dimensions, and reasoning memory, which encodes probability updating, uncertainty handling, and calibration principles. Using retrospective analyses as learning signals, FoCo iteratively revises memory through a verbalized memory-revision procedure, enabling the agent to accumulate transferable forecasting knowledge over time. Experiments on Prophet Arena and FutureX with GPT-5-mini and Gemini-2.5-Flash show that FoCo improves both probabilistic accuracy and calibration.

</details>


### [194] [DARTS: Distribution-Aware Active Rollout Trajectory Shaping for Accelerating LLM Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30859)
*Yujie Wang,Siwei Chen,Longzan Luo,Xinyi Liu,Xupeng Miao,Fangcheng Fu,Bin Cui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对大模型强化学习中长尾响应导致训练效率低的问题，提出了一种主动分布塑形方法，通过过滤冗余轨迹实现 1.77 倍的训练加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习中响应长度的长尾分布（特别是无效的冗余内容）导致了严重的 rollout 效率瓶颈，现有方案多局限于提示词层面的调度，未能从分布层面解决根本问题。

Method: 提出了主动分布塑形范式，包含“分布感知轨迹采样（distribution-aware trajectory sampling）”和“自适应冗余分配方案（adaptive redundancy allocation scheme）”，旨在将 rollout 分布引导至准确且简洁的状态。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法相比当前最先进的系统，训练速度提升高达 1.77 倍，且未对模型性能产生负面影响。

Conclusion: 主动分布塑形（Active Distribution Shaping）能有效解决强化学习中长尾响应长度分布导致的效率瓶颈，在不损失性能的前提下实现了显著的训练加速。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) has become pivotal for improving model capabilities yet suffers from rollout efficiency bottlenecks due to the long-tail response length distribution. While existing works mitigate the impact of long tails via prompt-level tail scheduling, we focus on the root source of inefficiency: the distribution itself. Specifically, we characterize the long-tail distribution at a finer granularity, identifying intra-prompt long tails, and revealing that they frequently consist of ineffective verbosity. To address this, we propose a novel paradigm of active distribution shaping to shape the rollout distribution towards conciseness and certainty, thereby fundamentally resolving tail-induced overheads. We achieve this through a distribution-aware trajectory sampling mechanism, which selects trajectories from a redundant exploration space for each prompt, and an adaptive redundancy allocation scheme to maximize both shaping effectiveness and system efficiency. Experiments demonstrate significant acceleration over state-of-the-art systems by up to 1.77x without compromising model performance.

</details>


### [195] [GlucoFM: A Dual-Stream Foundation Model for Continuous Glucose Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30865)
*Zechen Li,Keerthana Natarajan,Weizhi Zhang,Menglian Zhou,Simon A. Lee,Yuwei Zhang,Maxwell A. Xu,Zeinab Esmaeilpour,Flora D. Salim,Mark Malhotra,Lindsey Sunden,Shwetak Patel,Yuzhe Yang,Ahmed A. Metwally*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GlucoFM 是一个创新的 CGM 基础模型，通过将血糖数据解构为生理基线与瞬时事件双流处理，显著提升了血糖动力学表征的质量及其在临床预测任务中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用或 CGM 专用基础模型未能显式建模血糖动力学的独特时间结构，仅将其视为纠缠的单流时间序列，导致生理特征提取不足。

Method: 提出一种轻量级 CGM 基础模型 GlucoFM，通过将不规则数据对齐至 24 小时时间网格，并采用双流分解机制（慢速生理状态流与瞬时事件流），结合掩码上下文预测和时序动力学预测进行预训练。

Result: 在 4 个队列和 7 个临床预测任务中表现最优，平均 PR-AUC 比最佳对比模型提升 4.1 点，在糖尿病风险、β 细胞功能障碍及胰岛素抵抗等任务中处于领先，并展现出优异的跨数据集迁移能力和少样本学习能力。

Conclusion: GlucoFM 通过生理感知分解实现了有效的 CGM 表征学习，其引入的归纳偏置使其在临床代谢评估任务中表现出优异的迁移能力和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Continuous glucose monitoring (CGM) provides a dense view of daily metabolic physiology, yet existing generic time-series and CGM-specific foundation models often encode glucose traces as entangled single-stream sequences, leaving the distinct temporal structure of glycemic dynamics only implicitly modeled. We present GlucoFM, a lightweight CGM foundation model that aligns irregular recordings to a 24-hour chronological grid, preserves observation masks, and decomposes glucose dynamics into slow physiological state and transient event streams, capturing low-frequency glycemic baselines and short-term deviations that may reflect acute physiological responses or sensor artifacts. GlucoFM is pretrained on 109,066 hours of unlabeled CGM recordings from 477 subjects with two complementary objectives: masked contextual latent prediction over fused daily representations and temporal dynamics prediction over state and event streams. Across four diverse cohorts and seven clinical prediction tasks, GlucoFM achieves the strongest subject-disjoint linear-probing performance among evaluated baselines, improving average PR-AUC by 4.1 points over the best CGM-specific foundation model. Its gains are most pronounced on core metabolic outcomes, leading PR-AUC on all diabetes-risk and $β$-cell dysfunction tasks and on 3 of 4 insulin-resistance tasks. GlucoFM also achieves the best overall cross-dataset transfer performance and strong few-shot adaptation among evaluated methods, and consistent gains when aggregating multiple days for subject-level prediction, highlighting physiology-aware decomposition as an effective inductive bias for transferable CGM representation learning.

</details>


### [196] [Bandwidth Allocation with Device Partitioning for Federated Learning over Industrial IoT networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30892)
*Kangmin Kim,Jaeyoung Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对工业物联网联邦学习中的通信瓶颈，本文提出了一种基于设备计算异构性的分区带宽分配策略，通过顺序独占带宽机制显著提升了训练速度并降低了能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在工业物联网（IIoT）联邦学习中，通信延迟是制约训练效率的主要瓶颈。传统的带宽分配方式未能针对FL收敛目标进行优化，且现有方案未能有效利用设备计算能力的异构性。

Method: 提出了一种带宽分配策略，将参与设备划分为有序子集，并按顺序为每个子集授予全带宽的独占访问权，利用设备计算能力的异构性来优化训练效率。

Result: 实验表明，该策略在GC10-Det和CIFAR-10数据集上显著降低了训练时间和能耗，训练时间接近理论下界，证明了其在实际应用中的高效性。

Conclusion: 提出的基于分区（partitioning-based）的带宽分配策略在数学上被证明优于所有不进行分区的方案，并能有效减少工业物联网环境下的训练时间和能耗。

Abstract: We consider a federated learning (FL) system in which Industrial Internet-of-Things (IIoT) devices collaboratively train a global model over wireless channels without sharing local data. In such systems, communication time is a primary bottleneck that constrains overall training efficiency. Unlike conventional networks that prioritize individual quality-of-service requirements, FL systems collectively aim to converge to an optimal global model as efficiently as possible, which calls for a fundamentally different approach to bandwidth allocation. In this paper, we propose a novel bandwidth allocation policy that exploits the heterogeneity of device computing capabilities to minimize total training time. Rather than distributing bandwidth among all selected devices simultaneously, the proposed policy partitions the participating devices into ordered subsets and sequentially grants each subset exclusive access to the full bandwidth. We formally prove that this partitioning-based policy achieves a strictly lower training time than any bandwidth allocation scheme without partitioning, irrespective of the underlying scheduling algorithm. Furthermore, by reducing per-device transmission duration, the proposed policy also minimizes uplink energy consumption, which is particularly beneficial for battery-constrained IIoT devices. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets - including GC10-Det, an industrial surface defect benchmark, and CIFAR-10, a standard image classification benchmark - demonstrate that the proposed policy consistently reduces training time and energy consumption compared to existing bandwidth allocation schemes, approaching the theoretical lower bound on round time.

</details>


### [197] [Zero Collapse: A Failure Mode of Policy Gradient Methods in Discontinuous Reward Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30896)
*Nishant Kumar,Enrique Areyan Viqueira,Amy Greenwald*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了强化学习在拍卖任务中由于奖励函数的不连续性导致智能体陷入“零崩溃”（Zero Collapse）的根本原因，并提出了相应的缓解策略及形式化分析框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决强化学习在重复拍卖（如数字广告）场景中面临的挑战。现有策略梯度和基于价值的方法难以应对拍卖奖励分布中存在的“悬崖式/不连续”特征。当智能体陷入奖励为零的区域时，由于缺乏有效的梯度信息，会导致学习效率极低甚至“瘫痪”（即零崩溃现象）。

Method: 1. 理论分析：分析了不连续奖励对梯度信号的影响，以及策略随机性和步长之间的相互作用；2. 实验验证：在REINFORCE和Actor-Critic变体中展示了“零崩溃”现象；3. 改进措施：提出基于初始化和模型架构的缓解方案；4. 理论框架：构建了专门针对拍卖环境的形式化强化学习框架。

Result: 成功刻画了强化学习在拍卖场景中的“零崩溃”机制；揭示了Actor-Critic算法由于有偏的值估计更容易陷入该不稳定性区域；通过实证研究验证了该问题的普遍性，并提出了一系列提高训练稳定性的有效方法。

Conclusion: 拍卖环境中的不连续奖励机制导致了“零崩溃”（Zero Collapse）现象，该现象显著阻碍了强化学习智能体的训练。通过合理的初始化策略、架构改进以及对探索机制的优化，可以有效缓解这一问题。此外，本文提出的形式化RL框架为理解拍卖环境的独特结构性质提供了理论支持。

Abstract: Bidding in repeated auctions is a central challenge for reinforcement learning (RL), combining continuous control with the strategic complexities of digital advertising. While policy gradient and value-based methods seem well-suited for these settings, they often struggle with the discontinuous, "cliff-like" nature of auction reward landscapes. In a first-price auction, for example, a bidder receives zero reward until they cross a specific threshold, after which the reward decreases as the bid increases. This creates a landscape of flat, zero-reward regions separated by sharp boundaries.
  We identify a fundamental failure mode in this setting termed "zero collapse." We show that stochastic exploration and gradient-based updates can cause policies to overshoot optimal high-reward regions and enter flat, zero-reward regimes. Once there, the lack of an informative gradient signal makes recovery extremely sample-inefficient, effectively trapping the agent. We find that actor-critic methods are particularly susceptible, as biased value estimates can accelerate this movement toward unstable regions.
  Our contributions include: (1) a mechanistic explanation of how discontinuous rewards lead to vanishing signals and zero collapse; (2) an analysis of the interaction between policy stochasticity and step size; and (3) an empirical demonstration of this phenomenon across REINFORCE and actor-critic variants. We propose practical mitigation strategies involving initialization and architectural choices to improve stability. Finally, we introduce a formal RL framework for auction environments highlighting their unique structural properties.

</details>


### [198] [Inverse Reinforcement Learning without an Optimal Demonstrator: A Feasible Reward Set Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30903)
*Kihyun Kim,Shripad Deshmukh,Nikos Vlassis,Jiawei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种处理多个异构次优示范者奖励学习的新框架。通过将示范者的次优性转化为线性约束并求交集，实现了对奖励函数空间的有效约束，并在理论和实践上证明了该方法在高维环境（如LLM微调）下的优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的逆强化学习（IRL）通常假设存在单个最优示范者，但在实际应用中，数据往往来自多个存在异构次优性水平的、不完美的示范者。现有方法难以有效处理这种非最优且多源的数据，导致奖励学习的不确定性。

Method: 引入了一种“可行奖励集合”框架：1）将每个示范者的次优性水平编码为线性约束；2）通过求多个示范者对应可行集的交集来约束奖励函数空间；3）利用理论分析证明了集合收敛性及恢复保证；4）开发了一种结合函数逼近的离线算法以应用于高维场景。

Result: 1）理论上证明了联合可行集随数据增加单调收缩，并给出了明确的集合收敛条件；2）提供了两种奖励函数恢复的理论界限；3）在表格型网格世界及大型语言模型（LLM）微调任务的实验中，证明了该方法在奖励恢复准确性和鲁棒性上显著优于baseline。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的基于可行奖励集合（feasible-reward-set）的框架在处理多重、异构且次优的示范者数据时是有效且鲁棒的。通过将次优性建模为线性约束，该方法能在理论上保证奖励函数的恢复质量，并在高维环境（如LLM微调）中表现出优于传统IRL基准的性能。

Abstract: Inverse reinforcement learning (IRL) typically assumes demonstrations from a single optimal demonstrator, but in many applications data come from multiple imperfect demonstrators with heterogeneous suboptimality levels. We study reward learning in this setting through a feasible-reward-set framework: for each demonstrator, we encode its declared suboptimality level as a linear constraint and intersect the resulting feasible sets across demonstrators. Our theoretical analysis shows that the joint feasible set shrinks monotonically as data are added, and we give an exact characterization of when a new demonstrator strictly tightens it. We further establish two recovery guarantees for the feasible reward set of the ground-truth optimal demonstrator: one bound depends on closeness to the optimal occupancy, while the other requires only sufficient coverage and no near-optimal demonstrator. On the practical side, we introduce strategies to address the inherent reward ambiguity in the obtained reward set and provide an offline algorithm with function approximation for high-dimensional environments. Experiments in tabular grid-world and large language model (LLM) fine-tuning settings are consistent with the theoretical predictions and demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework over baselines.

</details>


### [199] [Automating Formal Verification with Reinforcement Learning and Recursive Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30914)
*Max Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了如何通过“强化学习+验证器反馈”（RLVR）和“推理时验证器指导搜索”，提升大语言模型生成形式化验证代码与证明的能力，有效改善了传统模型在Dafny和Lean环境下的生成质量及成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在形式化验证中面临训练数据匮乏且验证要求极高（需精确满足数学/逻辑规范而非仅像自然语言代码般“合理”）的挑战。

Method: 1. 在Dafny语言上利用RLVR（基于GRPO等方法）微调模型，利用编译器和验证器奖励信号训练；2. 在Lean语言中设计了基于验证器引导的推理框架，将证明生成转化为分解子目标的结构化搜索，并结合证明修订（proof reviser）机制。

Result: 1. 在Dafny任务上，通过RLVR将验证通过率从9.7%提升至31.1%；2. 在Lean任务（VeriCoding）中，验证器引导框架使成功率从46.2%提升至69.2%；3. 在更复杂的任务（VERINA）上，提出了任务分解与修订机制，并发布了面向库级验证的基准测试Dalek-Bench。

Conclusion: 利用验证器反馈（RLVR和推理时搜索）能显著提升大语言模型生成形式化验证代码及证明的能力；但该领域仍面临“规范破解”（specification hacking）和复杂仓库级验证任务的挑战，需要更强有力的基准测试与工具使用策略。

Abstract: Automated formal verification remains challenging for large language models because data for proof assistants and verification-aware languages is scarce, and correctness depends on satisfying precise machine-checkable specifications rather than producing plausible code. This thesis studies how verifier environments can improve LLM generation of verified programs and proofs through reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR) and verifier-guided inference-time search. First, we train open-source models in Dafny with RLVR using Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) and related variants, assembling generated candidates into complete programs and scoring them with compiler and verifier outcomes. Initial experiments on an APPS-derived Dafny dataset increased verified reward from 2.2% to 58.1%, but revealed specification hacking, where models exploit weak formal specifications instead of implementing the intended solutions. After filtering underspecified and vulnerable tasks, multi-turn RLVR on the refined benchmark improves the verified pass rate from 9.7% to 31.1%. Second, we develop a verifier-guided inference scaffold in Lean that treats proof generation as structured search over decomposed subgoals, verifier feedback, diagnostics, and repair. With a fixed base model, the full scaffold with proof reviser improves pass rate on an initial VeriCoding pilot set from 46.2% under direct repair to 69.2%. On the larger VERINA dataset, whole-task decomposition plus proof reviser solves 7 of 42 previously unsolved tasks. We also introduce Dalek-Bench, a repository-scale Lean benchmark derived from the Rust $\texttt{curve25519-dalek}$ verification project; preliminary results remain weak, indicating that stronger progress evaluation and task-specific tool-use policies are still needed.

</details>


### [200] [De-attribute to Forget for LLM Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30919)
*Xinyang Lu,Jiabao Pan,Rachael Hwee Ling Sim,See-Kiong Ng,Anthony Kum Hoe Tung,Bryan Kian Hsiang Low*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对大模型遗忘中常见的过度遗忘和实用性下降问题，本文提出了 DareU 框架，通过强化学习最小化模型输出的“数据归因分值”来实现受控遗忘，在有效删除数据的同时保护了模型的整体性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大模型遗忘方法（如最大化遗忘集的预测损失）往往会导致过度遗忘（over-forgetting）以及模型原有性能的下降（poor model utility），因此需要寻找一种新的优化视角。

Method: 提出了一种名为 DareU 的框架，将遗忘目标重新定义为“消除数据归因（zeroing out data attribution）”。该方法利用强化学习，通过最小化模型生成内容对特定遗忘数据集的归因分值（de-attributing）来更新模型，并采用轻量级 LLM 分类器作为归因分值的有效近似。

Result: 实验表明，DareU 在实现有效遗忘的同时，在保持模型效用和遗忘质量之间取得了更好的平衡，优于现有的基准方法。

Conclusion: DareU 通过基于数据归因的方法，有效地解决了大模型遗忘中的“过度遗忘”与“模型效用下降”的矛盾，为大模型遗忘提供了一种兼顾隐私保护与模型性能的新思路。

Abstract: The rapid development of large language models (LLMs) has raised concerns on the use of inappropriate data for training, which has led to a growing interest in LLM unlearning. Many existing LLM unlearning approaches rely on optimizing prediction loss(es), such as maximizing the loss on the forget set, but often face critical issues like over-forgetting and poor model utility. To address them, this paper novelly frames the optimization objective for LLM unlearning as one of zeroing out data attribution instead. In particular, we propose the first LLM unlearning framework based on data attribution rewards called DareU that performs reinforcement learning to update the LLM by reducing the attribution score of its generated responses (i.e., de-attributing) to the forget data owners. Empirical evaluation using an LLM classifier as an efficient approximation of attribution shows that DareU outperforms existing baselines by achieving effective unlearning while balancing forget quality and model utility well.

</details>


### [201] [Unsupervised Diffusion Solver for Combinatorial Optimization via Combinatorial Adjoint Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30920)
*Shengyu Feng,Tarun Suresh,Yiming Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对组合优化，提出了一种无需标注数据的扩散模型训练框架 CAM，通过离散伴随动力学实现高效的轨迹级优化，在无需监督的情况下取得了竞标传统求解器的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于扩散的组合优化（CO）求解器高度依赖大规模近优解的监督学习，缺乏高效、高质量的无监督训练范式。

Method: 提出“组合伴随匹配”（Combinatorial Adjoint Matching, CAM），将扩散式组合优化建模为连续时间马尔可夫链上的随机控制问题，并通过离散伴随动力学在离散生成轨迹中传播优化信号。

Result: CAM 在多个组合优化问题上均优于现有的无监督扩散基准，且在性能上与强监督学习模型及传统求解器具有竞争优势。

Conclusion: CAM 证明了基于离散伴随匹配的无监督训练框架在求解组合优化问题上的有效性，能够以无监督的方式达到甚至超越监督学习模型及传统求解器的性能。

Abstract: Diffusion-based neural solvers have shown strong promise for combinatorial optimization (CO), but existing methods typically rely on supervised training with large collections of near-optimal solutions. In this work, we extend adjoint-based trajectory optimization methods to discrete combinatorial domains. We formulate diffusion-based CO as a stochastic control problem over Continuous-Time Markov Chains and introduce discrete adjoint dynamics for propagating optimization signals through discrete generative trajectories. Building on this formulation, we propose Combinatorial Adjoint Matching (CAM), an unsupervised training framework for discrete diffusion solvers with structured and low-variance trajectory-level optimization signals. Empirically, CAM consistently outperforms existing unsupervised diffusion baselines and achieves performance competitive with strong supervised diffusion solvers and even traditional solvers across diverse combinatorial optimization problems. Our code is available at https://github.com/Shengyu-Feng/CAM.

</details>


### [202] [Spectral Anatomy of Quantum Gaussian Process Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30952)
*Jian Xu,Chao Li,Guang Lin,Yuning Qiu,Delu Zeng,John Paisley,Qibin Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了归一化谱熵 $S(K)/\log n$ 是决定量子/经典高斯过程性能的共同物理量，统一解释了近期关于量子加速失效与后验病理的争议。研究提出了一种无需错误缓解的硬件诊断指标，在真实硬件实验中表现出极高的准确性和通用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究指出量子高斯过程（QGP）在良态条件下不存在指数级加速，且高度表达的量子核会导致贝叶斯优化失效；本文旨在寻找统一的框架来解释这些现象，并为量子核模型的性能评估提供量化指导。

Method: 该研究通过理论证明和实验验证相结合的方法：理论上推导了 Nyström 近似误差的 Cauchy-Schwarz 尾界、基于 Bach 自由度 $d_\sigma(K)$ 的方差收缩恒等式以及目标依赖的最优熵刻画；实验上在不同硬件（IBM Heron）和多种核函数（HE, matchgate, IQP, RBF 等）上验证了该诊断指标的跨架构一致性。

Result: 实验结果表明，无论核函数类型如何，其在去量化、期望校准误差 (ECE) 和方差收缩方面的表现皆随 $S/\log n$ 塌缩至同一曲线。在 IBM Heron 硬件上，$S/\log n$ 指标的预测误差极低（中位数绝对误差 3.2%，规模扩展至 6 量子比特时均值误差仅 1.7%），证明了该诊断指标的鲁棒性和实际应用价值。

Conclusion: 本文证明了归一化谱熵 $S(K)/\log n$ 是量子与经典高斯过程性能的单一核心决定因素。该指标不仅解释了近期关于量子加速的负面结果和贝叶斯优化中的后验病理现象，还为评估量子核方法的有效性提供了一个通用且硬件可迁移的诊断工具。

Abstract: Two recent results have reshaped quantum Gaussian processes (QGPs). On the one hand, \citet{lowe2025assessing} rule out the exponential speedups claimed by HHL-based QGP regression in the typical, well-conditioned regime; on the other, an independent line of work shows that highly expressive quantum kernels suffer posterior pathologies that break Bayesian optimization. We show that these seemingly unrelated phenomena are governed by the same quantity: the normalized spectral entropy $S(K)/\log n$ of the kernel Gram matrix. We prove a Cauchy--Schwarz tail bound on Nyström approximation error, a finite-sample variance-contraction identity in terms of Bach's degrees of freedom $d_σ(K)$, and a characterization of the \emph{target-dependent} optimal entropy via the intrinsic dimension of the target in the kernel eigenbasis. Empirically, the diagnostic is kernel-agnostic: hardware-efficient, matchgate, IQP \emph{and} RBF/Matérn/RFF/deep-kernel families all collapse onto identical $S/\log n$ curves on dequantization, ECE, and variance-contraction panels. The NLL sweet spot lives at high entropy for smooth targets and at low entropy for band-limited quantum-data targets. The diagnostic transfers from simulator to IBM Heron hardware with median absolute error $3.2\%$ and mean $5.2\%$ in $S/\log n$ across $24$ configurations at $n_q = 4$, with matchgate and IQP within $5\%$ mean and a single HE configuration returning a $30\%$ outlier that drops to $0.5\%$ on rerun (attributed to calibration drift); the same diagnostic transfers to a second Heron backend (mean error $2.7\%$) and to a $n_q = 6$ scale-up on the original backend (mean error $1.7\%$). No error mitigation is applied throughout.

</details>


### [203] [Revisiting Zeroth-Order Hessian Approximation: A Single-Step Policy Optimization Lens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30960)
*Junbin Qiu,Zhaowei Hong,Renzhe Xu,Yao Shu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了ZoVH框架，通过将零阶Hessian近似统一至策略优化视角，利用最优基线和查询重用技术，在保证无偏性的同时显著降低了高维零阶Hessian估计的方差并提升了样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高维环境下，精确的零阶（ZO）Hessian及其逆矩阵估计在双层优化和贝叶斯推断中至关重要，但现有方法存在方差大、计算成本高和样本效率低等挑战。

Method: 1. 将零阶Hessian近似重新解释为单步策略优化（PO）的Hessian；2. 提出ZoVH框架，通过最优基线（Optimal Baseline）最小化估计方差；3. 采用查询重用（Query Reuse）策略，通过历史函数查询提升样本效率。

Result: 理论上证明了估计器的无偏性、基线的方差最优性及误差界；实验证明ZoVH在估计精度和收敛性能上优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 本文提出的ZoVH框架通过引入最优基线和查询重用策略，有效地解决了高维环境下零阶Hessian及其逆矩阵估计方差高、样本效率低的问题，并通过理论分析和实验验证了其优越性。

Abstract: Accurate Zeroth-Order (ZO) Hessian estimation is a cornerstone of derivative-free methods, essential for tasks such as bilevel optimization, Bayesian inference, and uncertainty quantification. However, obtaining a complete suite of low-variance estimators for the Hessian and its inverse in high-dimensional settings remains a significant challenge. To address this, we propose a unified framework that reinterprets ZO Hessian approximation through the lens of single-step Policy Optimization (PO). This perspective establishes a theoretical equivalence between general ZO Hessian estimators and the Hessian of a smoothed PO objective, unifying distinct classical randomized estimators as specific instances of baseline selection. Building on this foundation, we introduce ZoVH, a comprehensive suite of variance-reduced estimators for the full Hessian matrix, its regularized inverse, and the bias-corrected inverse Hessian-gradient product. ZoVH leverages two key techniques: (1) a unique optimal baseline derived to provably minimize variance, and (2) a query reuse strategy that incorporates historical function queries to enhance sample efficiency without inflating costs. Our rigorous theoretical analysis confirms the unbiasedness of the Hessian estimator, validates the variance optimality of our baseline, provides error bounds for the entire ZoVH suite, and establishes convergence guarantees for the resulting curvature-aware ZO algorithm. Extensive empirical results validate our theoretical findings, demonstrating that ZoVH achieves superior estimation accuracy and convergence performance in real-world applications. Code is available at https://github.com/Qjbtiger/ZoVH

</details>


### [204] [DEM: A Distilled Explanation Model for Interpretable Anomaly Detection in Physiological Sensor Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31007)
*Jyotirmoy Singh,Anushka Roy,Shreea Bose,Chittaranjan Hota*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DEM 是一种新型异常检测框架，通过将性能强大的黑盒模型提取为具解释性的决策树，实现了高精度、高预测速度与临床级可解释性的统一，适用于实时医疗监控场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 WBAN 生理数据异常检测面临两难：黑盒模型精度高但缺乏透明度，后置解释方法（如 SHAP/LIME）计算复杂且存在近似偏差，难以满足临床实时监控的需求。

Method: 提出了一种名为“提取解释模型”（Distilled Explanation Model, DEM）的三阶段玻璃盒框架：1. 建立线性基线；2. 将梯度增强模型（XGBoost）的非线性知识提取到决策树中；3. 基于残差生成决策树，确保解释即预测本身。此外引入了“提取保真度指标”以评估解释的可靠性。

Result: 在四大生理数据集（MIMIC-IV, WESAD, eICU, SmartNet）上表现出色，AUC 最高达 0.9964；推理速度快（0.17ms/1000样本），比 SHAP 快 1235 倍，并能提供用户可控深度的可解释规则。

Conclusion: DEM 框架通过将性能与可解释性结合，解决了 WBAN 系统中生理数据异常检测的透明度与实时性问题，证明了“内置解释”优于“事后解释”。

Abstract: Anomaly detection in physiological sensor data from Wireless Body Area Networks (WBANs) can be caused by sensor faults, network disruptions, or missing data, leading to false alarms. Hence, it demands both high predictive accuracy and clinically interpretable explanations. Existing approaches rely either on black-box models that achieve strong performance but offer no transparency, or on post-prediction explanation methods such as SHAP and LIME. In this paper, we propose the Distilled Explanation Model (DEM), a three-stage glass-box framework that distills the non-linear knowledge of a gradient boosting expert into an interpretable decision tree operating on residuals relative to a linear baseline, so that the explanation is not an approximation but the prediction itself. DEM introduces a novel distillation fidelity metric that quantifies how faithfully the explanation tree captures the expert model's non-linear contribution, providing a principled measure of explanation trustworthiness absent from prior interpretable models. Evaluated across four physiological datasets, including MIMIC-IV, WESAD, eICU, and an in-house SmartNet WBAN corpus, DEM achieves an AUC of 0.9964 on clinical contextual anomaly detection and 0.9047 on wearable stress detection while producing human-readable if-then rules at a controllable depth. Inference requires 0.17ms per 1000 samples, rendering DEM 1235x faster than SHAP-based post-hoc explanation and suitable for real-time physiological monitoring. Ablation studies confirm that the XGBoost distillation step provides measurable gains over naive residual fitting, and depth-sensitivity analysis demonstrates an explicit, user-controlled accuracy-interpretability trade-off unique to DEM among existing intrinsically interpretable models.

</details>


### [205] [Annealed Softmax Greedy in Many-Armed Bayesian Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31034)
*William Overman,Mohsen Bayati*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过数学分析解释了为何像GRPO这样不显式处理不确定性的强化学习方法依然高效：在存在大量“近最优”候选方案的假设下，退火Softmax采样本身就具备了良好的探索性能，无需额外的认知不确定性建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于强化学习的推理（RLVR）及其变体（如GRPO）通过简单的采样和奖励惩罚更新策略，缺乏显式跟踪认知不确定性的机制，但实践中却非常有效，作者旨在从理论上解释这一现象。

Method: 在多臂贝叶斯伯努利赌博机（many-armed Bayesian Bernoulli bandit）框架下，分析了退火Softmax策略的性能。通过引入“线性上尾条件”（$\beta=1$的$\beta$-正则性假设）来量化近最优动作的丰度，并证明了其悔恨界（regret bound）。

Result: 在$\beta$-正则性条件下，当动作空间规模 $m = \Theta(\sqrt{T})$ 时，退火Softmax策略能实现 $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{T})$ 的贝叶斯遗憾，这在动作空间庞大的场景下是近似最优的。对比组实验发现，在动作数量较少时，该策略可能产生线性遗憾。

Conclusion: 研究证明了对于具有“$\beta$-正则性”的任务（即存在大量近最优动作），即使策略不显式建模认知不确定性，其退火Softmax策略也能达到接近最优的贝叶斯遗憾界（$\tilde{O}(\sqrt{T})$）。该结论揭示了RLVR/GRPO等方法在缺乏显式不确定性机制的情况下依然有效的理论基础。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) and group-based policy optimization methods such as GRPO update a stochastic policy by sampling multiple completions per prompt and increasing the policy's probability on those with higher reward, regularized by a KL penalty toward a reference policy. These updates do not include explicit mechanisms that track epistemic uncertainty. This paper studies a stylized explanation for why such uncertainty-agnostic updates can nevertheless be effective. We analyze an annealed softmax (Boltzmann) policy that selects actions according to a softmax of empirical mean rewards in a many-armed Bayesian Bernoulli bandit. Under a linear upper-tail condition on the prior (the $β=1$ case of $β$-regularity), which implies an abundance of near-optimal arms, we prove that annealed softmax greedy achieves Bayes regret $\tilde{O}(m + T/m)$, and in particular $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{T})$ when the number of arms scales as $m = Θ(\sqrt{T})$. This is the near-optimal Bayes regret rate in this regime, attained also by empirical-mean greedy. Under $β$-regularity, many arms maintain empirical means close to the optimum throughout learning, so when softmax samples an arm other than the empirically best, that arm tends to be another near-optimal one rather than a clearly inferior one. By contrast, with a small number of arms, the same kind of softmax policy can suffer linear regret. The result also provides a structural analogy to RLVR, where a base policy with a non-negligible probability of producing a correct completion plays the role of $β$-regularity.

</details>


### [206] [Learning to Solve and Optimize by Evolving Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31049)
*Veronika Semmelrock,Benedetta Strizzolo,Francesco Zuccato,Gerhard Friedrich,Patrick Rodler,Konstantin Schekotihin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CHECKMATE 是一款利用代码演化自动生成算法的工具，仅需用户提供形式化规范和自然语言描述，即可自动构建超越现有工业求解器性能的解决方案，无需人工设计启发式算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统工业 AI 应用中的组合与优化问题依赖专家手动设计启发式算法，耗时且复杂；旨在消除手动指定“如何派生解（how）”的需求，仅依赖“目标解是什么（what）”。

Method: 引入了名为 CHECKMATE 的工具，结合形式化规范（确保正确性与评估）和自然语言描述（引导演化过程），通过代码演化（code evolution）自动生成算法。

Result: 在配置和调度两个工业领域的案例中，CHECKMATE 生成的算法在性能上持续优于现有的最先进求解器。

Conclusion: 通过代码演化生成算法代表了一种范式转移，即从“如何做（how）”转向“做什么（what）”，证明了形式化方法在引导自动程序生成以解决复杂工业问题方面的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Combinatorial and optimization problems are fundamental to many industrial AI applications. Solving large-scale real-world instances of such problems typically requires careful problem formalization, specialized solvers, and expert-designed heuristics. Thus, experts need to specify not only what solutions are, but also how they are derived. By introducing the tool CHECKMATE, we show that algorithm generation via code evolution represents a paradigm shift by eliminating the need to formulate the how. CHECKMATE solely relies on the what. Specifically, a formal specification ensures solutions' correctness and enables systematic performance evaluation of the generated programs, while a natural language description guides the evolutionary process. The effectiveness of our method is demonstrated on selected problems from two industrial domains: configuration and scheduling. In all cases, the evolved algorithms consistently outperform state-of-the-art solvers. This underscores the potential of formal methods in guiding code evolution for automatically solving complex real-world problems.

</details>


### [207] [STEP: Learning STructured Embeddings for Progressive Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31061)
*Lucas Thil,Jesse Read,Rim Kaddah,Guillaume Doquet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种自监督对比学习框架，将渐进式时间序列映射至具有明确几何含义的低维流形上，通过极坐标提取状态进度与操作模式，在实现高精度预测的同时具备卓越的可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对具有不可逆状态转换（如设备退化或任务完成）的渐进式时间序列，现有的黑盒模型虽然预测能力强但往往缺乏可解释性，亟需一种能自动学习可解读表示的方法。

Method: 采用自监督对比学习目标，将观察数据映射到一个低维流形空间，通过锚定两个固定的正交原型向量构建潜在空间，利用极坐标系（θ作为进度跟踪，r作为模式识别）实现对时间序列的解构与表示。

Result: 在工业退化、机器人任务和神经活动三个领域验证了该方法的有效性，在终端状态预测、多步预测和可解释相位分离方面达到或超越了现有SOTA模型，且简单的线性回归在潜在空间上即可取得竞争性结果。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种利用潜在空间几何结构来解释渐进式时间序列演变的新方法，证明了通过自监督对比学习可以无需标签地提取可解释的进度与模式信息，且在预测任务中表现优异且具有高度透明性。

Abstract: We present a novel method for learning interpretable representations of progressive time series, that is, data capturing irreversible state transitions such as degradation or task completion. Our approach uses a self-supervised contrastive objective to learn a low-dimensional latent space whose geometry is itself the interpretation: each observation becomes a point on a manifold anchored between two fixed orthogonal prototype vectors, and a trajectory becomes a path across that manifold. From this structure we read a latent compass, the polar coordinates (θ, r) of the latent vector, in which θ tracks the progression of the underlying state (e.g., from healthy to failed) and r identifies the active mode (e.g., the operating condition), without any proxy labels. We evaluate the approach against the state of the art on diverse domains, including industrial degradation, robotic tasks, and neural activity, validating three key capabilities: (1) end-state prediction, (2) multi-step forecasting, and (3) interpretable phase separation. Our method matches or improves over black-box counterparts on all of these while providing transparency about the underlying mechanisms. A simple linear regressor on top of the latent compass coordinates is competitive with deep architectures, direct quantitative evidence that the underlying state is encoded in a geometrically accessible form.

</details>


### [208] [An Efficient and Scalable Graph Condensation with Structure-Preserving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31016)
*Yulin Hu,Fuyan Ou,Ye Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为 SP-ESGC 的高效可扩展图压缩方法，通过将节点压缩与结构生成解耦，并结合热核扩散与混合聚类策略，实现了高效的图数据概括，且在不同 GNN 模型间展现出优异的泛化效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图压缩方法存在耦合优化计算开销大、对不同 GNN 架构泛化能力弱的问题，难以应用于资源受限场景。

Method: 提出了一种解耦的图压缩框架 SP-ESGC：1. 利用热核特征传播（Heat Kernel Feature Propagation）进行节点表示学习；2. 采用混合聚类策略（Hybrid Clustering Strategy）提取类内判别性中心；3. 使用预训练的边预测器（Edge Predictor）生成合成图结构。

Result: 在多个真实图数据集上的实验表明，SP-ESGC 显著提升了计算效率，生成的压缩图在不同 GNN 架构上表现出良好的泛化性能。

Conclusion: SP-ESGC 通过解耦机制有效解决了现有图压缩方法计算效率低和结构泛化性差的问题，实现了高效、高精度的图压缩，并具备跨模型架构的强泛化能力。

Abstract: Graph condensation (GC) is pivotal for enabling Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) deployment in resource-constrained scenarios by compressing large-scale graphs into compact synthetic counterparts. Existing GC methods commonly suffer from computational inefficiency due to coupled optimization as well as encountering poor generalization across GNN architectures. To address these challenges, this study proposes an Efficient and Scalable Graph Condensation with Structure-Preserving (SP-ESGC), which possesses a decoupled design that separates node condensation from graph structure generation. Specifically, it first employs heat kernel feature propagation to generate node representation via spectral graph theory-inspired diffusion. Further, a novel hybrid clustering strategy is designed to extracts discriminative intra-class centroids from the node representation. Finally, a pre-trained edge predictor infers transferable structural patterns from the original graph, ensuring accurate synthetic graph generation. Extensive experiments on real-world graph datasets demonstrate that the proposed SP-ESGC implementes a precise GC with significantly high computational efficiency. Moreover, SP-ESGC also generalizes well across diverse GNN architectures.

</details>


### [209] [UniRTL: Unifying Code and Graph for Robust RTL Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31040)
*Yi Liu,Hongji Zhang,Lei Chen,Mingxuan Yuan,Qiang Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: UniRTL 是一种新型多模态预训练框架，通过协同融合 RTL 代码语义与控制数据流图（CDFG）结构信息，有效提升了硬件设计自动化任务的表征效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RTL 表征方法多局限于单一模态（仅代码或仅图），限制了模型的表达与泛化能力。作者认为将 RTL 代码的语义信息与 CDFG 的结构信息进行模态融合，对于深度理解 RTL 设计至关重要。

Method: 提出 UniRTL 多模态预训练框架，核心技术包括：1. 联合代码与 CDFG 的建模；2. 通过互掩盖建模（mutual masked modeling）实现代码与图的细粒度对齐；3. 采用分层训练策略，整合预训练的图感知分词器（graph-aware tokenizer）及功能摘要与代码的分阶段对齐。

Result: 在性能预测（performance prediction）和代码检索（code retrieval）两类下游任务上，UniRTL 在多个实验设置中均优于现有的单模态基准方法。

Conclusion: UniRTL 通过融合代码语义与控制数据流图（CDFG）结构信息，构建了更具表达力和泛化能力的 RTL 统一表征，显著提升了硬件设计自动化（HDA）相关任务的性能，是该领域强大的基础模型。

Abstract: Developing effective representations for register transfer level (RTL) designs is crucial for accelerating the hardware design workflow. Existing approaches, however, typically rely on a single data modality, either the RTL code or its associated graph-based representation, limiting the expressiveness and generalization ability of the learned representations. For RTL, the control data flow graph (CDFG) offers a comprehensive structural representation that preserves complete information, while the code modality explicitly encodes semantic and functional information. We argue that integrating these complementary modalities is essential for a thorough understanding of RTL designs. To this end, we propose UniRTL, a multimodal pretraining framework that learns unified RTL representations by jointly leveraging code and CDFG. UniRTL achieves fine-grained alignment between code and graph through mutual masked modeling and employs a hierarchical training strategy that incorporates a pretrained graph-aware tokenizer and staged alignment of text (i.e., functional summary) and code prior to graph integration. We evaluate UniRTL on two downstream tasks, performance prediction and code retrieval, under multiple settings. Experimental results show that UniRTL consistently outperforms prior methods, establishing it as a more robust and powerful foundation for advancing hardware design automation.

</details>


### [210] [Trust-Region Behavior Blending for On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31159)
*Daniil Plyusov,Alexey Gorbatovski,Alexey Malakhov,Nikita Balagansky,Boris Shaposhnikov,Daria Korotyshova,Daniil Gavrilov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对在线策略蒸馏（OPD）初期学生策略质量差的问题，TRB通过引入动态KL约束的策略混合机制，在训练前期利用教师知识引导学生，有效提升了蒸馏的训练效率和最终性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线策略蒸馏（OPD）虽然解决了离线蒸馏的分布偏移问题，但学生模型在训练初期通常表现较差，导致后续的“教师引导”基于低质量的样本，限制了蒸馏效果。

Method: 提出Trust-Region behavior Blending (TRB) 方法。在训练初期（warmup阶段），将学生模型的采样策略替换为在KL散度信任域内最接近教师策略的行为策略，并随着训练进行将KL约束退火至零，从而平稳过渡到纯粹的策略滚动（student rollouts）。

Result: 在两个数学推理蒸馏实验设置中，TRB的表现优于所有对比方法，达到了最优平均性能。

Conclusion: TRB通过在训练初期通过KL约束平滑引导策略行为，解决了策略蒸馏中早期策略质量差导致的监督效率低的问题，在数学推理任务中表现优于现有的蒸馏方法。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student on prefixes sampled from its own policy while matching a stronger teacher. This addresses the prefix mismatch of offline distillation, but early student rollouts can still be poor, placing teacher supervision on weak or low-quality prefixes. We propose Trust-Region behavior Blending (TRB), a warmup method that replaces the early rollout policy with the closest-to-teacher behavior policy inside a student-centered KL trust region, while keeping the per-prefix reverse-KL OPD loss unchanged. The KL budget is annealed to zero, so training returns to pure student rollouts after warmup. Across two math-reasoning distillation settings, TRB attains the strongest average among the compared methods.

</details>


### [211] [The Challenges of Using Reinforcement Learning for Controlling Industrial Energy Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31044)
*Tobias Lademann,Théo Vincent,Jan Peters,Matthias Weigold*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了强化学习在真实工业热力网中的部署挑战，指出尽管算法能维持稳定性，但由于部分可观测性、设计复杂性及仿真与现实的巨大鸿沟，导致其实际性能显著低于仿真评估结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前强化学习在工业能源系统中的研究多局限于仿真环境，缺乏针对真实世界工业场景部署时的实际挑战分析。

Method: 将工业热力网控制问题建模为马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），并从部分可观测性、动作空间设计、奖励函数设计及“仿真到现实”差距四个维度系统性地分析了落地挑战。

Result: 研究表明，现有的强化学习方案在实际部署时能维持操作稳定性，但在真实环境中的性能表现远低于仿真环境的预期。

Conclusion: 在真实工业能源系统中部署强化学习面临巨大挑战，虽然可以实现运行稳定性，但与仿真环境相比存在显著的性能差距，这凸显了缩小“仿真到真实（Sim-to-Real）”差距的必要性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has shown promising results for optimizing the control of industrial energy systems, yet most existing studies remain limited to the application in simulation environments. We investigate the challenges of deploying reinforcement learning in a real-world industrial energy system, considering a thermal heating network as a use case. We formulate the task as a Markov Decision Process and systematically analyze the associated challenges along the structure of the formal description, including partial observability, action space design, reward design, and the simulation-to-reality gap. The challenges are grounded in an existing real-world deployment, where reinforcement learning achieves operational stability but shows a significant performance gap compared to simulation.

</details>


### [212] [Best-Arm Identification-Based Trust Region Selection for Bayesian Optimization on Multimodal Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31050)
*Nobuo Namura,Sho Takemori*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种将最佳臂识别（BAI）与信任域贝叶斯优化相结合的新框架，通过轨迹外推精准筛选局部搜索区域，显著提升了多模态问题的优化效率与收敛速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于高斯过程的贝叶斯优化（BO）在处理复杂多模态或高维问题时性能下降；现有的信任域BO虽有改善，但仍需更有效的区域选择机制。

Method: 提出了一种轨迹感知（trajectory-aware）框架。利用多臂老虎机（MAB）中的最佳臂识别（BAI）策略，通过外推多个局部初始化优化器的搜索轨迹，预测各优化器的最终性能，并逐步剔除次优候选者。

Result: 理论上证明了该方法比传统BO能更快收敛至全局最优；实验证明，在合成及现实世界基准测试中，该方法在处理多模态问题上具有更高的优化效率。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种基于最佳臂识别（BAI）引导的信任域贝叶斯优化框架，通过轨迹感知机制加速多模态优化，在理论上证明了其收敛速度优于传统贝叶斯优化，并在实验中验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Gaussian process-based Bayesian optimization (BO) is a popular approach for expensive black-box optimization, but its performance often degrades on complex multimodal or high-dimensional problems. Trust region-based BO mitigates this issue by focusing on local regions, and recent studies suggest that selecting an effective region can be formulated as a multi-armed bandit problem. We propose a trajectory-aware framework that integrates best-arm identification (BAI) with trust region-based BO to efficiently solve multimodal optimization problems. Our method extrapolates the optimization trajectories of multiple locally initialized optimizers to predict their final performance and progressively eliminates suboptimal candidates via BAI. We theoretically show that the proposed BAI-guided BO converges faster to the global optimum than conventional BO under mild assumptions, and demonstrate its effectiveness through extensive experiments on synthetic and real-world benchmarks.

</details>


### [213] [Learning to Bid in FCR Markets: A Best-of-Both-Worlds Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31070)
*Marius Potfer,Cheng Wan,Pierre Gruet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对欧洲FCR市场竞价不透明的挑战，本文提出一种基于组合半强盗算法的在线学习竞价策略，实现了环境自适应的悔恨界，并在历史数据回测中展现了良好的竞争性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧洲频率抑制储备（FCR）市场竞价难度大，因为竞争对手报价不透明，参与者仅能获得清算价格和中标量等有限反馈，缺乏最优报价策略。

Method: 1. 将多国FCR市场出清问题重构为针对内生对手报价向量的重复多单元统一价格拍卖问题；2. 将其转化为在线学习问题；3. 采用“博弈论最佳（Best-of-Both-Worlds）”组合半强盗（combinatorial semi-bandit）算法，仅需市场提供的常规反馈信息即可实现。

Result: 该方法在随机环境下实现了对数伪悔（logarithmic pseudo-regret），在对抗性环境下实现了O(√T)的悔恨（regret）；实验表明该算法的表现与预期缩放比例一致，且在历史数据回测中对于稳定产品表现优于基准算法。

Conclusion: 在欧洲FCR市场中，基于学习的竞价策略具有理论基础且在实践中有效。算法的性能表现取决于学习规则与产品市场稳定性之间的匹配度，针对不同稳定性产品可采取针对性策略（如在稳定产品上使用该算法，在强非平稳环境下结合EXP3类算法）。

Abstract: Bidding in the European Frequency Containment Reserve (FCR) market is challenging for flexibility providers because competing offers are hidden and bidders observe only partial feedback form the market, such as, clearing price and awarded quantity. For a participant active in a single country, we show that the multi-country FCR clearing problem can be recast as a repeated multi-unit uniform-price auction against an endogenous vector of opposing bids. This reformulation yields an online learning problem and allows us to adapt a Best-of-Both-Worlds combinatorial semi-bandit algorithm implementable from this standard market feedback. The resulting bidder achieves logarithmic pseudo-regret in stochastic environments and $\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{T})$ regret in adversarial ones. Synthetic experiments confirm the expected scaling, and backtests on historical European FCR data show competitive performance in practice: the method performs especially well on stable products, while EXP3-type baselines can be safer under stronger non-stationarity. Overall, the results show that learning-based bidding in FCR markets is theoretically grounded and practically useful when the learning rule matches product-level market stability.

</details>


### [214] [Subspace-Decomposed JEPAs: Disentangling Progression and Content in Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31111)
*Lucas Thil,Jesse Read,Rim Kaddah,Guillaume Doquet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SD-JEPA通过将潜空间分解为正交的“进展”与“内容”子空间，实现了任务进度的高效表征与解耦，提升了机器人规划能力及对语义事件的检测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的JEPA模型在学习紧凑潜空间世界模型时，缺乏明确的坐标来表征任务的进展，导致任务进度信息与环境内容纠缠，难以进行有效的规划和事件检测。

Method: 提出SD-JEPA架构，将JEPA潜空间划分为两个正交子空间：1. 进展子空间（使用余弦边际三元组损失函数驱动）；2. 内容子空间（使用LeWM的SIGReg目标函数驱动）。证明了这两种抗坍塌力作用于不相交的维度，实现了协同效应。

Result: 在大多数控制基准测试中优于LeWM基准，并在Push-T任务上超越了现有的强基线模型。1D进展坐标能精准表征任务进展，并能灵敏地响应回溯和语义突发事件，其定位语义事件的AUROC提升高达0.18，且仅用8维子空间即可解释高达95%的任务进度方差。

Conclusion: 通过引入正交子空间划分（SD-JEPA），成功将任务进展与内容表示解耦。1D角度进展坐标展现了出色的可解释性和定位任务阶段的能力，在预测任务进度和检测“惊讶”事件方面显著优于标准的潜空间预测误差标量。

Abstract: Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) learn compact latent world models by predicting future embeddings, but no single coordinate of the latent is designated to encode task progression. We carve the JEPA latent into two orthogonal subspaces with disjoint roles: a low-dimensional progression subspace shaped by a cosine-margin triplet loss, and a high-dimensional content subspace regularised by the existing SIGReg objective of LeWM. We prove that the two anti-collapse forces act on disjoint coordinates, so they compose additively rather than competing on the same dimensions. Our method, SD-JEPA improves over the LeWM baseline on the majority of its control benchmarks at matched compute, and outperforms the strongest non-LeWM JEPA baseline on Push-T; a subspace-ablation falsifier confirms the split is the load-bearing ingredient. Beyond planning, the resulting 1-D angular progression coordinate functions as a scene-aware compass on the latent. It advances with task progress, regresses when the agent backtracks, and under controlled perturbations both spikes and relocalises to a semantically appropriate new task-phase sector, separating the moment of surprise from its meaning in a way that prediction-error scalars cannot. Three quantitative tests back this up: $|Δθ_t|$ outperforms the standard latent-prediction-error surprise at localising semantic events on 40 held-out cube episodes by up to +0.18 pooled AUROC (97.5% per-episode win rate at $\pm 1$-step tolerance); a within-episode linear probe across all four environments (40 episodes per env) shows the 8-dimensional progression subspace (4.2% of the latent) explains 72-95% of task-progress variance..

</details>


### [215] [What changes after deployment? A survey on On-device Learning in TinyML](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31226)
*Massimo Pavan,Luca Pezzarossa,Fabrizio Pittorino,Manuel Roveri,Xenofon Fafoutis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过对70篇TinyML端侧学习（ODL）论文进行回顾，引入“分布变化范式”框架以解决模型部署后的分布偏移问题，揭示了算法设计与实际部署需求之间的脱节，为未来ODL的应用研究提供了分类指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘设备上的机器学习模型（TinyML）部署后常面临数据分布偏移（Distribution Change）问题，现有静态模型难以适应，而针对端侧学习（ODL）的系统性分类研究缺失，导致研究与实际应用之间存在偏差。

Method: 通过调研约70篇ODL相关文献，提出以“分布变化范式（Distribution Change Regimes）”为核心的分类框架，系统性地分析了分布偏移类型对应用场景、硬件选择以及算法结构的影响。

Result: 建立了ODL的研究分类学，揭示了不同分布变化类型与算法架构选择之间的映射关系，并指出目前学术界的方法论基准与真实世界部署需求之间存在显著的鸿沟。

Conclusion: 该综述指出，尽管TinyML在边缘设备上学习（ODL）领域已有大量研究，但现有技术在应对真实部署环境下的分布偏移时仍存在核心方法论空白，必须根据具体的分布变化模式进行针对性的架构设计。

Abstract: Machine learning models on microcontroller-class devices (TinyML) face a fundamental challenge: post-deployment distribution change undermines static models. On-device learning (ODL) addresses this by running the learning process directly on the device. The existing literature has not characterized how distribution change occurs or how different change types require different solutions. Approximately 70 ODL works are surveyed under one principle: the distribution change regime. The survey analyzes how different types of distribution change influence the applications addressable on-device, the hardware employed, and the structure of the solutions. A persistent gap between methodological benchmarks and real-world deployment scenarios is also identified.

</details>


### [216] [Scalable Bayesian Inference for Nonlinear Conservation Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31127)
*Tim Weiland,Philipp Hennig*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种将经典数值守恒律求解器与贝叶斯推断相结合的框架，利用稀疏近似技术实现了大规模非线性系统的高效、准确的不确定性量化及反演。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实系统中的非线性守恒律往往面临数据稀疏或噪声干扰，导致反问题变得病态（ill-posed），而传统数值方法和主流深度学习方法在处理上述不确定性时存在局限性。

Method: 将经典数值守恒律求解器与高斯过程先验（GP priors）结合，并通过稀疏近似技术（Sparse approximation）来处理大规模计算问题，实现物理信息约束下的不确定性量化与贝叶斯推断。

Result: 该方法在不确定性量化方面具有结构性优势；在反向问题中，能以秒级速度恢复非参数源场的后验概率，精度高于深度学习模型且省时显著。

Conclusion: 提出了一种结合经典数值方法与贝叶斯推断的非线性守恒律模拟框架。该方法在处理大规模前向模拟和反向问题的不确定性量化方面表现优异，在计算效率和准确性上均显著优于现有的深度学习基准方法。

Abstract: Nonlinear conservation laws are at the heart of many of the most important dynamical systems in science and engineering. In practical applications, such systems are often subject to various sources of uncertainty, e.g. due to sparse or noisy measurements. Inferring physical quantities and fields of interest then becomes an ill-posed problem which both classical numerical methods and modern deep learning-based methods struggle to treat appropriately. Recent work has framed classical numerical methods as Bayesian inference under Gaussian process priors, resulting in a physics-aware treatment of uncertainties. Following this line of work, we develop a novel numerically conservative method for uncertainty-aware simulations of nonlinear conservation laws. We use recent sparse approximation techniques to scale up to large-scale forward and inverse problems. For forward simulation, we inherit the accuracy of classical solvers while providing structured uncertainty quantification. On inverse problems, we recover posteriors over nonparametric source fields in seconds -- outperforming neural baselines that take minutes to produce a less accurate point estimate.

</details>


### [217] [EchoRL: Reinforcement Learning via Rollout Echoing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31228)
*Jinhe Bi,Aniri,Minglai Yang,Xingcheng Zhou,Wenke Huang,Sikuan Yan,Yujun Wang,Zixuan Cao,Michael Färber,Xun Xiao,Volker Tresp,Yunpu Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对 RLVR 训练后期因“采样全成功”导致的奖励梯度消失问题，EchoRL 通过提取采样序列中的高价值片段（EchoClip）作为辅助监督信号，成功重构训练增益并提升模型推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）在训练后期常出现“优势退化”现象：当所有采样均获奖励（全为 success）时，奖励方差变为零，导致模型因梯度消失而无法进一步优化，造成训练增益瓶颈。

Method: 提出 EchoRL 模块，通过计算采样序列的步级熵（step-level entropy）识别出其中的“EchoClip”，并将其作为辅助监督信号引入强化学习目标函数中，以利用原本会被忽略的训练信号。

Result: 在 10 个基准测试、5 种大模型架构和 4 种主流 RLVR 方法上进行了验证，结果显示 EchoRL 在保持极低额外开销的同时，能够持续提升模型的训练效果。

Conclusion: EchoRL 通过识别并利用奖励退化样本中的潜在信息（EchoClip），有效解决了强化学习中因奖励方差归零导致的梯度消失问题，显著提升了 LLM 在推理任务中的训练上限。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards is an effective route for post-training to strengthen the reasoning capability of large language models. However, as training proceeds, the learning signal can collapse thus makes the training gain become marginal and ineffective. Specifically, a growing fraction of prompts' rollouts become advantage-degenerated: all the self-generated rollouts show verified-success, making the standard deviation over their rewards be zero; accordingly each rollout's advantage becomes degenerated (zero) as well. Given such rollouts' advantages, the policy-gradient for model optimization eventually vanishes, capping the training performance. We argue that some of these rollouts still contain valuable learning signals but unfortunately omitted with the existing RLVR methods. In this paper, inspired through analyzing the entropy pattern behind golden trajectories produced by external expert models, we propose EchoRL for better exploiting the advantage-degenerated rollouts to further improve the training performance. EchoRL is a lightweight module that first identifies an EchoClip from verified-success rollouts based on their step-level entropy values, and then feeds this clip back as an auxiliary supervision signal in the RL objective. Extensive experiments across 10 benchmarks, 5 LLM backbones, and 4 popular RLVR post-training methods demonstrate that EchoRL consistently improves RLVR post-training with minimal overhead.

</details>


### [218] [Generalizing Multi-Scale Time-Series Modeling with a Single Operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31129)
*Cheonwoo Lee,Dooho Lee,Doyun Choi,Jaemin Yoo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了 SiGMA，一种基于尺度空间理论的通用多尺度时间序列预测架构，通过可学习的距离感知缩放机制，在实现高精度预测的同时，大幅提升了计算效率和资源开销表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列多尺度预测方法缺乏统一的理论基础，且大多局限于固定、离散的缩放策略，限制了模型捕捉时间动态的能力。

Method: 1. 将现有缩放方法统一为尺度算子族；2. 识别并指出现有方法依赖“固定且离散”缩放的局限性；3. 提出 SiGMA（Single Generalized Multi-scale Architecture），利用基于尺度空间理论的可学习离散高斯（LDG）核实现“距离感知”的连续缩放。

Result: 在长短期时间序列预测基准测试中表现优于所有最先进基线模型；在 16 项长期预测任务中，13 项取得最优表现；相较于最强竞争对手，训练速度提升达 5.3 倍，内存占用降低至 3.8 倍。

Conclusion: SiGMA 通过引入基于尺度空间理论的可学习离散高斯核，成功解决了现有时间序列多尺度模型依赖固定离散缩放的问题，在提升预测精度的同时，显著优化了训练速度和内存效率，为多尺度建模提供了更具理论基础和实用价值的范式。

Abstract: Multi-scale modeling has emerged as an effective design principle for time-series forecasting by capturing temporal dynamics at multiple resolutions. As no principled foundation has been established in the literature, we unify existing scaling methods into a scaling operator family, revealing a fundamental limitation of existing approaches: reliance on fixed and discrete scaling. To address this limitation, we propose SiGMA (Single Generalized Multi-scale Architecture), which enables distance-aware scaling via the learnable discrete Gaussian (LDG) kernel grounded in scale-space theory. We evaluate SiGMA comprehensively on long- and short-term forecasting benchmarks against state-of-the-art multi-scale baselines. SiGMA outperforms all competitors on both tasks, especially achieving the best performance in 13 out of 16 long-term evaluation settings. Beyond accuracy, SiGMA significantly improves training speed by up to 5.3 times and reduces memory consumption by up to 3.8 times over the strongest competitors. Code is available at https://github.com/cheonwoolee/SiGMA.

</details>


### [219] [Learning Hyperspherical Time-Frequency Representations for Time-Series Out-of-Distribution Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31155)
*Willian T. Lunardi,Samridha Shrestha,Martin Andreoni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用vMF超球面嵌入和时频联合表征学习的时间序列OOD检测框架，显著优于现有对比学习和后处理基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列数据的OOD检测研究远落后于视觉和语言领域；缺乏如何利用监督学习表征进行可靠OOD检测的理论研究。

Method: 提出了一种用于时间序列OOD检测的表征学习方法，通过von Mises-Fisher (vMF) 似然目标函数在单位超球面上诱导类条件结构；联合时域和频域编码器提取特征；使用基于距离的评分方法（k-NN和马氏距离）进行检测。

Result: 在UCR和UEA时间序列数据集的跨数据集基准协议下，该方法在k-NN和马氏距离评分指标上均优于现有的对比学习和后处理基准方法。

Conclusion: 在时间序列数据领域，利用基于vMF似然的超球面嵌入学习，能够显著增强对抗分布偏移的能力，并提供更可靠的OOD检测性能。

Abstract: Out-of-distribution (OOD) detection for time-series data remains comparatively underexplored compared to vision and language, with a limited principled understanding of how supervised time-series representations can be leveraged for reliable detection under distributional shifts. This work formulates time-series OOD detection as representation learning with hyperspherical embeddings, where class-conditional structure is induced by a von Mises-Fisher (vMF) likelihood-based objective on the unit sphere. The learned representation combines time- and frequency-domain views of the input signal via domain-specific encoders, integrating them into a joint embedding space for OOD detection. Detection uses distance-based scores over the learned embeddings, including k-nearest neighbors (k-NN) and Mahalanobis scores. We evaluate the approach at scale on the complete UCR and UEA time-series archives under a cross-dataset protocol. Empirical results show consistent improvements under both k-NN and Mahalanobis scoring over strong contrastive learning and post-hoc baselines in the same setting. Code is available at https://github.com/tiiuae/hypertf-time-series-ood.

</details>


### [220] [Learning Cardiac Latent Representations in Vectorcardiogram Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31249)
*Bosong Huang,Panzhen Zhao,Zengxiang Li,Patricia Lee,Wei Jin,Alan Wee-Chung Liew,Ming Jin,Shirui Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对十二导联ECG表征学习中的冗余与过拟合问题，本文提出LVCG框架，直接在具有物理意义的VCG空间进行自监督学习，有效提升了模型在不同任务及跨域场景下的泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的ECG表征学习方法主要基于可观测信号空间，由于十二导联ECG存在大量的空间冗余，容易导致虚假相关性（spurious correlations）和过拟合。为了利用心电活动的物理本质，研究人员试图将表征学习转移到更紧凑、具有物理意义的VCG空间。

Method: 提出了一种名为LVCG的通用自监督表征学习框架，将ECG数据映射并建模于Frank向量心电图（VCG）物理空间的统一潜表征中，通过学习视角不变（view-invariant）的潜变量来实现特征提取。

Result: LVCG在多项心电任务中均优于现有的基于ECG空间的基准方法，尤其在面临域偏移（domain shift）的场景下，展示了显著的稳健性和泛化优势。

Conclusion: 通过在VCG空间进行表征学习，LVCG能够有效减少冗余信息，克服ECG信号在不同导联间的相关性带来的过拟合问题，并在跨域评估中表现出更强的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Electrocardiography (ECG) is a cornerstone of cardiac assessment, making the learning of informative ECG representations fundamental to tasks ranging from disease diagnosis to clinical report generation. However, existing methods operate almost exclusively in the observable ECG signal space. In practice, the standard twelve-lead ECG represents multiple projections of the same underlying cardiac electrical activity from different spatial orientations. Therefore, representation learning in the ECG space inevitably introduces substantial redundancy, which may lead to spurious correlations and increased risk of overfitting. To address this and motivated by the Frank vectorcardiogram (VCG) model, we propose learning a unified latent representation of cardiac electrical activity directly in the VCG space. We introduce LVCG, the first general self-supervised representation learning framework designed to operate in this physically grounded latent space. By learning view-invariant latent VCG representations rather than lead-specific artifacts, VCG minimizes redundancy and improves generalization. LVCG generally outperforms ECG-space baselines across tasks, demonstrating enhanced robustness and generalization, especially in domain shift settings.

</details>


### [221] [TabCausal: Pretraining Across Causal Environments for Tabular Causal Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31156)
*Zi-Rong Li,Si-Yang Liu,Tian-Zuo Wang,Han-Jia Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了TabCausal，一种通过广泛因果预训练和动态任务构建来提升因果发现基础模型（CDFM）性能和迁移能力的方法，并在合成及语义基准上验证了其优越的因果结构恢复效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果发现基础模型（CDFMs）性能有限，难以匹配传统方法，核心瓶颈在于现有的因果预训练任务构造方式不够高效。

Method: 1. 采用动态任务构建策略，在多种图先验、机制、噪声模型、维度、样本量和干预条件下进行大规模预训练；2. 引入由LLM审计的语义因果环境基准（semantic causal environment benchmark），增强合成数据与现实场景的关联。

Result: 在大规模合成基准测试中，TabCausal的宏平均性能优于多种因果发现基线模型；在语义环境测试中表现出稳健的结构恢复能力，尤其是在处理干预证据时具有显著优势。

Conclusion: 提出了一种基于广泛因果预训练的因果驱动基础模型（CDFM）TabCausal，证明了广泛的因果预训练能够提升因果发现的跨数据转移能力。

Abstract: Causal discovery aims to recover directed causal relations from observational and interventional data, providing a basis for mechanistic understanding and reliable decision-making. Causal discovery foundation models (CDFMs) seek to amortize this problem by mapping a dataset directly to a causal graph in a single forward pass, avoiding per-dataset testing, search, or optimization. However, existing CDFMs remain limited, often failing to consistently match strong classical methods, and we find that a key bottleneck is how causal pretraining tasks are constructed. Based on this observation, we propose TabCausal, a data-driven CDFM trained with broad causal pretraining over diverse graph priors, structural mechanisms, noise models, dimensions, sample sizes, and intervention regimes. A dynamic task construction strategy composes these causal environments into varied discovery tasks, enabling more transferable structural learning from observational and mixed-interventional data. On large-scale synthetic benchmarks, TabCausal achieves better macro-averaged performance than a diverse set of causal discovery baselines.
