To further bridge abstract synthetic generators and realistic causal reasoning scenarios, we introduce a protocol-guided and LLM-audited semantic causal environment benchmark, where domain-grounded SCMs generate interpretable observational and interventional datasets for out-of-distribution analysis. Across both synthetic and semantic environments, TabCausal demonstrates robust structure recovery, especially under interventional evidence, highlighting broad causal pretraining as a key ingredient for transferable amortized causal discovery.

</details>


### [222] [The Terminal Representation in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31289)
*Amir Esterhuysen,Anders Jonsson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了终端表示（TR），一种无需特征分解即可捕捉奖励加权轨迹信息的轻量级表示学习方法，在多种强化学习下游任务中表现优异且计算效能更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的后继表示（SR）和默认表示（DR）通常依赖特征向量分解，这需要昂贵的计算开销且隐含要求转移动态是对称的；为了在保持奖励加权轨迹信息的同时降低维度并消除对称性假设。

Method: 提出了终端表示（Terminal Representation, TR）；开发了TR的理论基础（推导、收敛性）、零样本组合性方法以及与现有奖励公式的等价性证明。

Result: 从理论上证明了TR内嵌于DR的最高特征向量中，无需特征向量分解即可获取相同知识；实验证明了TR在下游任务中的有效性，且在学习、存储和使用方面的计算开销更小。

Conclusion: TR作为一种高效、低维的表示学习方法，能够以更低的计算成本实现与DR特征向量相当的下游任务性能，并克服了传统特征分解对对称性转移假设的依赖。

Abstract: Representation learning is a powerful tool for spatio-temporal abstraction within reinforcement learning (RL). Two well established approaches are through the successor representation (SR) and the default representation (DR). The SR encodes states by the future trajectories they induce, capturing information flow decoupled from reward. The DR builds on this by weighting trajectories with reward, integrating credit-assignment structure into the representation. Eigenvectors of both representations have been used to support a range of downstream tasks -- including option discovery, reward shaping, transfer learning, and exploration. We introduce a structurally distinct formulation: the terminal representation (TR). The TR encodes reward-weighted trajectories similarly to the DR, but can be learned as a lower-dimensionality object, and can be used directly for the mentioned applications without eigenvector computations. Eigendecomposition also imposes the assumption of symmetric transition dynamics, which the TR can bypass. In this work we develop the theoretical foundations of the TR: its derivation, convergence of two learning algorithms, its use for zero-shot compositionality, and equivalences between alternative reward formulations. We further show the TR is embedded in the top DR eigenvector, allowing it to capture the same underlying knowledge without eigendecomposition. Additionally, we provide empirical evidence of the TR as a viable alternative to existing representations in subsidiary applications, while requiring less computational overhead to learn, store, and use.

</details>


### [223] [How well does Classification Accuracy capture Concept Drift Detection Quality? An overview of Concept Drift Detection evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31186)
*Joanna Komorniczak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文深入探讨了数据流概念漂移检测的评估难题，通过实验验证了多种检测指标与分类性能的相关性，旨在建立更准确、科学的概念漂移检测评估体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然针对概念漂移（concept drift）的解决方案很多，但学术界缺乏评估漂移检测效果的统一框架，且现有研究过多依赖分类质量指标，无法准确反映检测器的真实性能。

Method: 通过7种合成数据流生成工具进行实验，研究了8种漂移检测质量指标与分类器性能之间的相关性，并引入了“漂移动态（drift dynamics）”作为变量因素进行多维度对比分析。

Result: 揭示了检测质量指标与分类性能之间的内在联系，识别出了一组最具信息量的漂移检测质量指标，并量化了漂移动态对评估结果的影响。

Conclusion: 缺乏统一评估框架阻碍了概念漂移检测的发展；通过实验分析，确定了漂移检测指标与分类性能之间的关系，旨在为该领域建立更科学的评价标准。

Abstract: Data streams are nowadays among the most frequently analyzed data structures, with the concept drift posing a major challenge encountered by processing systems. Despite the proposition of numerous solutions to counteract the accuracy degeneration due to concept drift, the scientific community has not yet established a unified framework for evaluating the concept drift detection task. Existing research often relies on classification quality metrics, but these can be affected by multiple factors and may not reliably reflect drift detection quality. In this work, we present an in-depth overview of the relationship between metrics for quantifying drift detection quality and classification performance in synthetic nonstationary data streams. The proposed research studies eight drift detection quality metrics in relation to the classifier's performance across seven synthetic data stream generation tools, additionally considering drift dynamics as a factor. The studies aim to identify the most informative set of drift detection quality metrics and provide a deep understanding of the method's evaluation.

</details>


### [224] [Inconsistency-Aware Minimization: Improving Generalization with Unlabeled Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31324)
*Hee-Sung Kim,Hyeonseong Kim,Sungyoon Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需标签的泛化性度量指标——局部不一致性，并基于此设计了IAM优化算法，有效提升了模型在各类学习任务中的泛化表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决深度学习中对泛化差距（Generalization Gap）的估计问题，并开发能够利用未标注数据提升模型泛化能力的优化方法。

Method: 从信息几何视角定义了“局部不一致性”（Local Inconsistency）；建立了该指标与费舍尔信息矩阵（FIM）及损失函数海森矩阵（Hessian）的理论联系；提出了不一致感知最小化（IAM）优化算法。

Result: 实验表明，局部不一致性与泛化差距呈显著相关；IAM在标准监督学习中性能可媲美SAM（锐度感知最小化），且在半监督和自监督场景下表现出显著优势。

Conclusion: 提出了一种基于信息几何的“局部不一致性”度量，并通过将其引入训练目标（IAM），证明了在有监督、半监督及自监督学习中均能有效提升模型的泛化性能。

Abstract: Estimating the generalization gap and developing optimization methods that improve generalization are crucial for deep learning models, for both theoretical understanding and practical applications. Leveraging unlabeled data for these purposes offers significant advantages in real-world scenarios. This paper introduces a novel generalization measure, local inconsistency, derived from an information-geometric perspective on the parameter space of neural networks. A key feature of local inconsistency is that it can be computed without explicit labels. We establish theoretical underpinnings by connecting local inconsistency to the Fisher information matrix and the loss Hessian. Empirically, we demonstrate that local inconsistency correlates with the generalization gap. Based on these findings, we propose Inconsistency-Aware Minimization (IAM), which incorporates local inconsistency into the training objective. We demonstrate that in standard supervised learning settings, IAM enhances generalization, achieving performance comparable to that of existing methods such as Sharpness-Aware Minimization. Furthermore, IAM exhibits efficacy in semi- and self-supervised learning scenarios, where the local inconsistency is computed from unlabeled data.

</details>


### [225] [FlagGAM: Rule-Based Generalized Additive Modeling for Explainable Tabular Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31189)
*Zijie Zhao,Roy E. Welsch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlagGAM 是一种新型表格数据预测框架，通过构建可解释的规则基函数，在提供高精度预测的同时，具备极强的鲁棒性与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在表格数据高风险预测场景中，现有模型难以同时兼顾高精度、高度可解释性以及对不完整/噪声输入的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出了一种基于规则基（rule-defined basis）的框架：由 Flag Core Module（将变量转化为稀疏、可读的一元基，如阈值标记、类别标记、尾部偏差基等）和加性预测头（默认）或灵活预测头（可选）构成。

Result: 在表格基准测试中，FlagGAM 在透明加性模式下表现接近 EBM；在混合类型回归任务中显著优于岭回归；在面对缺失值和噪声扰动时，AUROC 性能下降幅度小于主流基准模型；使用灵活预测头后性能可接近树模型。

Conclusion: FlagGAM 通过将特征规则构建与模型预测分离，在保证高预测精度与可解释性的同时，展现了出色的鲁棒性，是高风险领域中一个实用的模型选择。

Abstract: Tabular prediction in high-stakes domains requires models that are accurate, transparent, and robust to imperfect inputs. We propose FlagGAM, a rule-defined basis framework that separates feature-level rule construction from prediction. A Flag Core Module converts numerical and categorical variables into sparse, human-readable univariate bases, including threshold flags, category-level flags, tail-deviation bases, and categorical step functions; a default additive head then combines these bases as a restricted GAM-style predictor. Rather than reducing triggered rules to compact count summaries, FlagGAM retains a sparse rule-basis matrix that supports mixed-type classification and regression, feature-specific weighting, and optional flexible prediction heads. Across tabular benchmarks, default FlagGAM remains close to EBM in transparent additive mode, improves substantially over ridge regression on mixed-type regression, and shows smaller AUROC degradation than common baselines under missing and noisy perturbations. Flexible heads further improve accuracy and approach strong tree-based baselines, with the caveat that the resulting model should be interpreted as a rule-basis representation followed by a nonlinear predictor rather than as a fully additive GAM. Overall, FlagGAM provides a practical middle ground for tabular settings that require competitive accuracy, communicable rules, and robustness to imperfect inputs.

</details>


### [226] [Student Capacity Moderates Knowledge Distillation Effectiveness: A Systematic Study Across ResNet Teacher-Student Pairs on CIFAR-10](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31191)
*Umut Onur Yasar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了ResNet中教师-学生容量关系对知识蒸馏的影响，发现学生容量是增益的关键，同时强调了模型实现细节（如输入 stem 设计与梯度裁剪）对评估结果具有决定性影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨教师与学生模型的容量差异（capacity relationships）如何调节知识蒸馏的效果，并指出先前研究中因实现细节影响导致的结论偏差。

Method: 在CIFAR-10数据集上，对ResNet-50、ResNet-34和ResNet-18进行受控实验（3个随机种子），对比Logit-KD与Feature-KD的蒸馏效果，并排查了实现中的梯度裁剪漏洞与输入层架构设计问题。

Result: 1. 学生模型容量越大，从蒸馏中获益越多（R50->R34优于R34->R18）；2. 修复梯度裁剪 bug 后，Feature-KD 表现优于或持平 Logit-KD；3. 修正针对 32x32 输入的 ResNet stem 可提升教师性能 >5pp，远超蒸馏收益。

Conclusion: 学生容量是知识蒸馏增益的关键调节因子；特征蒸馏（Feature-KD）的效果极易受到实现细节（如梯度裁剪和架构设计）的影响；对于CIFAR-10这类任务，修正输入分辨率（ResNet stem）带来的基准性能提升远超蒸馏本身。

Abstract: We investigate how teacher-student capacity relationships modulate knowledge distillation (KD) effectiveness in ResNet-based image classification on CIFAR-10. Across three teacher-student pairs -- R50->R18, R34->R18, and R50->R34 -- we compare Logit-KD and Feature-KD under controlled, reproducible conditions (3 seeds, mean+/-std reported throughout). We report three main findings. First, student capacity is a key moderating factor in distillation gain: R34 students benefit substantially more from KD than R18 students even when teacher-student accuracy gaps are comparable, with the strongest gain of +0.30pp observed for R50->R34 Feature-KD versus +0.18pp for R34->R18 Feature-KD and +0.00pp for R34->R18 Logit-KD. Second, implementation correctness critically affects Feature-KD: a gradient clipping bug that excluded projection layers suppressed Feature-KD performance and produced misleading comparisons with Logit-KD. After correction, Feature-KD matches or outperforms Logit-KD in two of three pairs, reaching 95.55% on R50->R34 against a baseline of 95.25%. Third, input-resolution-aware architecture is a prerequisite for effective distillation: correcting the ResNet stem for 32x32 inputs raises teacher accuracy by over 5pp -- an order of magnitude larger than any KD gain. All code and results are available at github.com/umutonuryasar/kd-capacity-gap.

</details>


### [227] [dashi: A Python library for Dataset Shift Characterization to Support Trustworthy AI Development and Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31360)
*David Fernández-Narro,Pablo Ferri,Ángel Sánchez-García,Juan M. García-Gómez,Carlos Sáez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: dashi是一个开源Python库，旨在通过无监督（几何统计）和有监督（模型性能）双重方法，量化和表征数据集偏移，提升医疗AI系统的可信度和安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据集偏移（Dataset shifts）会严重降低AI模型性能并危及数据质量，特别是在医疗AI中，这种偏移可能直接影响患者安全。目前缺乏易用且全面的软件工具来分析这些偏移，因此开发了dashi。

Method: dashi 提供了一套双重分析框架：（1）无监督方法：利用信息几何和非参数统计流形技术进行数据变异性表征（如信息几何时间图、全局概率偏差等指标）；（2）有监督方法：通过定义的批次（时间或来源）量化并表征模型性能的下降。

Result: 通过三个案例研究（妊娠糖尿病、COVID-19、紧急医疗调度）验证了dashi的效用，证明其能够通过提供交互式可视化分析和变异性度量，支持AI生命周期的稳健性监测。

Conclusion: dashi是一个为医疗AI开发提供关键评估工具的开源库，通过量化数据集偏移，增强了AI系统的健壮性、安全性和可信度。

Abstract: The Artificial Intelligence (AI) life cycle requires a thorough understanding of the underlying data dynamics for robust, safe and cost-effective AI development and use. Dataset shifts are defined as changes between train and test data distributions. Whether occurring over time (temporal) or across different sites (multi-source), they can severely degrade model performance and compromise data quality. This is particularly important in health AI, where the safety and fundamental rights of patients can be severely affected by uncontrolled shifts both at training and operational stages. While the theoretical foundations of covariate, prior, and concept shifts are well established, there is a lack of accessible and comprehensive software tools to perform their analysis. We introduce dashi, an open-source Python library designed for the exploration, quantification, and characterization of dataset shifts. dashi provides a dual approach: an unsupervised approach that leverages information geometry and non-parametric statistical manifolds to data variability characterization and analysis (e.g., Information Geometric Temporal plots and Multi-Source Variability metrics like Global Probabilistic Deviation and Source Probabilistic Outlyingness), and a supervised approach that quantifies and characterizes model performance degradation. Both unsupervised and supervised approaches work across user-defined temporal and domain/source batches. We demonstrate the utility of dashi on three simulated and real-world health AI case studies on gestational diabetes mellitus, COVID-19 and emergency medical dispatch. By providing interactive visual analytics and variability metrics, dashi supports trustworthiness of AI life cycle stages enabling robust and safe machine learning pipelines through the assessment of data coherence and AI performance.

</details>


### [228] [Geometry-based Schrödinger Bridges for Trustworthy Multimodal Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31193)
*Jiayu Xiong,Jing Wang,Qi Zhang,Wanlong Wang,Jun Xue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对多模态融合中“置信度陷阱”问题，提出GMF方法，通过几何传输校正量动态评估输入质量，实现独立于模型预测的高鲁棒性判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态可信融合方法过度依赖模型自身的预测置信度，当模型“自信地出错”时会导致判定失效，存在严重的循环依赖问题，亟需一种独立于模型预测的鲁棒性判定机制。

Method: 提出几何多模态融合（GMF）框架，利用扩散薛定谔桥（Diffusion Schrödinger Bridge）与修正流（Rectified Flow）技术；通过测量输入在潜空间中所需的传输校正量（以初始速度的平方作为修正分数）来评估数据可靠性，而非依赖预测置信度。

Result: 实验结果表明，GMF在面对严重的传感器噪声及语义冲突时，其鲁棒性显著优于传统的基于置信度的基准方法，能有效识别并处理低质量数据。

Conclusion: GMF通过引入独立于分类器预测的几何度量，成功打破了传统置信度机制中的循环依赖问题，为多模态融合系统的鲁棒性提供了一种有效的新范式。

Abstract: Real-world multimodal systems must be robust against low-quality data, such as sensor noise, incomplete multimodal data and conflicting inputs. However, existing trustworthy fusion methods rely on the model's own prediction confidence to judge data quality. This creates a circular dependency: when a model is confident but wrong, these methods fail to detect the error. To break this loop, we propose Geometry-based Multimodal Fusion (GMF). Instead of relying on predictions, we evaluate reliability by measuring how much transport correction the input needs in latent space. We implement Diffusion Schrödinger Bridge transport with Rectified Flow, where the squared initial velocity gives an efficient learned correction score. Valid data has low squared velocity magnitude, while noisy, incomplete data or conflicting data requires stronger transport correction. This geometry-based reliability signal acts as an independent judge, effectively flagging unreliable inputs even when the classifier is fooled. Extensive experiments demonstrate that GMF significantly improves robustness against severe sensor noise and semantic conflicts compared to confidence-based baselines.

</details>


### [229] [Fixed-Point Masked Generative Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31215)
*Andrea Miele,Yiming Qin,Alba Carballo-Castro,Justin Deschenaux,Pascal Frossard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 CoFRe 框架，通过引入定点迭代与层参数共享，在掩码生成模型中实现了更高效的计算平衡，显著降低了参数量与训练成本，并在低预算下提升了生成效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统掩码生成模型（MGMs）在每一步去噪中都使用昂贵的双向 Transformer，导致训练成本高，且在低采样预算下生成质量受限，且现有优化方法多为固定计算分配。

Method: 引入 Fixed-Point Masked Generative Models (FP-MGMs)，通过定点求解器替代部分固定深度去噪器；设计跨步一致性损失（cross-step consistency loss）对齐相邻步的隐藏表示；采用三态重用（3SR）机制实现求解器热启动。并在预训练模型基础上支持短时间微调转换。

Result: 在文本生成（OpenWebText）中，参数减少 38.8%，训练时间缩短 11.5%，困惑度由 830.8 降至 101.8（固定预算下）；在图像生成（ImageNette）中，训练时间缩短 48.6%，VRAM 减少 50.7%，并在各预算下提升了 FID 指标。

Conclusion: CoFRe 通过引入定点迭代架构、跨步一致性损失及三态重用机制，成功提升了掩码生成模型的参数效率与推理质量，提供了一种高效的训练与推理框架。

Abstract: Masked Generative Models (MGMs) enable parallel decoding and achieve strong performance across modalities, but require full-sequence bidirectional transformers at every step, making training costly and degrading quality under low sampling budgets. Existing work improves efficiency via better samplers or cheaper fixed-depth denoisers, but they still allocate a fixed amount of denoiser computation to each refinement step. We introduce Fixed-Point Masked Generative Models (FP-MGMs), which replace part of the denoiser with a fixed-point solver over shared attention layers to enable adaptive depth with fewer parameters. To make it more effective for masked generation, we first introduce a cross-step consistency loss, which aligns hidden representations at neighboring denoising steps and, second, three-state reuse (3SR) which warm-starts the solver using the previous solution by treating differently unchanged, still-masked, and newly revealed tokens respectively. Together, these components define our complete training-to-inference framework for fixed-point masked generation, \emph{CoFRe}. We also show that pre-trained MGMs can be converted into FP-MGMs with short fine-tuning, avoiding full retraining. Across modalities, CoFRe improves the quality and cost trade-off. On OpenWebText, CoFRe reduces parameters by 38.8\%, training time by 11.5\%, and VRAM by 16.9\%, while improving generative perplexity from 830.8 to 101.8 at a budget of $96$ transformer-block forward passes, compared to MDLM. In ImageNette, CoFRe reduces training time by 48.6\% and VRAM by 50.7\%, while improving FID in all sample budgets tested. Overall, CoFRe offers a practical framework for cheaper training and stronger low-budget masked generation.

</details>


### [230] [Multivariate Distributional Reinforcement Learning Using Sliced Divergences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31222)
*Baptiste Debes,Tinne Tuytelaars*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对多维分布强化学习面临的计算与理论瓶颈，本文提出了切片分布强化学习（SDRL），通过投影技术将多维问题转化为一维问题，实现了高效且具备数学收敛保障的分布建模方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的分布强化学习（DRL）主要集中于一维回报分布，难以扩展至多维场景；多维设置面临度量指标不可通用、计算复杂以及矩阵折扣因子下缺乏收敛性证明等挑战。

Method: 提出了切片分布强化学习（SDRL），利用投影技术将多维回报分布转化为一系列一维分布；通过理论证明了在特定条件下的贝尔曼算子收敛性，并分析了Wasserstein、Cramér和MMD等多种度量方式。

Result: 在理论上证明了均匀切片下的贝尔曼收敛性，并在玩具链、网格世界及Atari游戏任务中验证了SDRL的有效性，证明其可适用于标准的单样本贝尔曼更新。

Conclusion: SDRL通过投影方法成功将一维分布强化学习扩展至多维，解决了多维分布学习中的计算效率及理论收敛性难题，为未来复杂环境下的分布强化学习研究提供了有效框架。

Abstract: Distributional reinforcement learning (DRL) models the full return distribution rather than expectations, but extending it to multivariate settings remains challenging. Many common metrics do not naturally generalize beyond one dimension or lose computational tractability, and the multivariate case introduces additional difficulties such as general matrix discounting, for which no contraction results are available. We introduce Sliced Distributional Reinforcement Learning (SDRL), which lifts tractable one-dimensional divergences to multivariate return distributions via projections. We prove Bellman contraction for uniform slicing under shared scalar discounting, and introduce a maximum-slicing variant with contraction under general dense discount matrices. SDRL supports a broad class of base divergences; we analyze Wasserstein, Cramér, and Maximum Mean Discrepancy (MMD), and characterize which SDRL variants suit the standard single-sample Bellman update used in distributional RL. We evaluate SDRL on a toy chain problem and a gridworld image-based environment as well as a subset of Atari games.

</details>


### [231] [Trading Complexity for Expressivity Through Structured Generalized Linear Token Mixing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31367)
*Erwan Fagnou,Paul Caillon,Blaise Delattre,Alexandre Allauzen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的 Token 混合器框架，通过将因果生成拆解为输入影响和递归传播，并推广递归机制以允许依赖多个过去状态，实现了推理效率与模型表达能力之间的原则性权衡与优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型中 Token 混合层在解码速度、显存占用和缓存大小之间存在折衷，需要找到一种更有效的平衡方案，以提高长距离依赖学习和生成的效率。

Method: 提出一个统一的框架，将因果生成分为“输入对输出的直接影响”和“通过过去输出的循环信息传播”两个部分，并推广了递归方程，使当前状态可以依赖于多个过去的状态（而非仅前一个），从而构建出具有特定复杂度的递归模式。

Result: 通过合成任务和语言建模任务验证，证明了新设计的递归模式能够实现预期的复杂度，并为理解和设计不同模型架构下的 Token 混合器提供了理论见解和工具包。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一个统一的框架来理解和设计高效且具有表达能力的 Token 混合器，证明了通过结构化改进递归模式可以在推理效率与模型表达能力之间达成原则性的平衡。

Abstract: Token mixing layers play a key role in how language models can learn and generate long-range dependencies. Their efficiency relies on the necessary trade-off between decoding speed and the memory requirements, along with the cache size. Considering causal generation, this paper explores new trade-offs thanks to a unified framework which separates two crucial features: (i) the direct influence of inputs on outputs in one generation step; (ii) the recurrent propagation of information through past outputs. This framework encompasses major architectures such as attention and state-space models, but also generalizes the recurrence equations by allowing each state to depend on multiple past states rather than only the immediate predecessor. By introducing structure, we design new recurrence patterns that provably achieve the desired complexity, while providing theoretical insights on their expressivity -- trading runtime for expressivity in a principled way. Empirical validation is performed on synthetic tasks, along with language modeling. Together, these results provide a unified toolkit for the understanding and design of efficient and expressive token mixers across model families.

</details>


### [232] [DRIFT: Decoupled Rollouts and Importance-Weighted Fine-Tuning for Efficient Multi-Turn Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31455)
*Jian Mu,Tianyi Lin,Chengwei Qin,Zhongxiang Dai,Yao Shu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DRIFT 是一种新型训练框架，通过将 KL 正则化的强化学习目标重构为加权监督学习，有效解决了大模型多轮交互中在线 RL 成本高与离线 SFT 效果差的矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决大模型在多轮交互任务中面临的困境：一方面，在线强化学习成本过高（频繁采样长序列轨迹）；另一方面，离线监督微调虽高效，但存在分布漂移（distribution shift）和行为崩塌（behavioral collapse）的问题。

Method: 提出了 DRIFT (Decoupled Rollouts and Importance-Weighted Fine-Tuning) 框架。该方法通过从固定参考策略中采样离线交互轨迹，计算基于回报（return）的重要性权重，并将优化过程从轨迹采样中解耦，最终通过加权监督学习（Weighted SFT）完成策略优化。

Result: 实证显示 DRIFT 在多轮交互任务中达到了与强化学习方法相当或更好的性能，同时保持了接近标准监督微调的训练效率。

Conclusion: DRIFT 成功地将多轮交互的优化任务转化为加权的监督学习问题，在保持监督微调（SFT）的高效性和简洁性的同时，显著优于或持平现有的多轮强化学习基线模型。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed in multi-turn interactive settings where users or environments can iteratively provide lightweight feedback. Unfortunately, optimizing such behavior presents a sharp dilemma in practice: online reinforcement learning is able to effectively address multi-turn dynamics but is prohibitively expensive due to the cost of generating full correction trajectories at every update, whereas offline supervised fine-tuning (SFT) is efficient but suffers from distribution shift and behavioral collapse. To this end, we novelly propose DRIFT (Decoupled Rollouts and Importance-Weighted Fine-Tuning), a framework that operationalizes the theoretical insight that the KL-regularized RL objective is equivalent to importance-weighted supervised learning. DRIFT decouples rollout from optimization by sampling offline interaction trajectories from a fixed reference policy, deriving return-based importance weights, and optimizing the policy via weighted SFT on the resulting dataset. Empirically, we demonstrate that DRIFT matches or exceeds the performance of multi-turn reinforcement learning baselines while maintaining the training efficiency and simplicity of standard supervised fine-tuning. Code is available at https://github.com/2020-qqtcg/DRIFT.

</details>


### [233] [Spectral Reach: Understanding Neural Scaling as Progress into the Spectral Tail](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31244)
*Konstantin Nikolaou,Jonas Scheunemann,Sven Krippendorf,Samuel Tovey,Christian Holm*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“谱位置”这一工具揭示了神经缩放定律的内在机制：大模型通过特征学习深入挖掘数据中的微弱信号（谱尾部），从而实现了比小模型更强的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管神经缩放定律（Neural scaling laws）被广泛应用，但其背后的机制尚不明确，主要原因是缺乏可扩展的分析工具来理解模型规模、计算量与性能之间的深层关系。

Method: 提出了“谱位置”（spectral position）这一衡量指标，用于量化经验神经正切核（eNTK）中哪些特征值（eigenvalues）在驱动损失下降；通过在不同规模的模型上进行对比实验，观察训练过程中谱位置的演变，并分析特征学习在其中的作用。

Result: 实验发现：1. 训练过程中学习重点会从主导模式向谱尾部漂移；2. 大模型具有更大的“谱触及”能力，能处理小模型无法利用的微弱信号；3. 特征学习能够动态放大梯度，是实现谱触及的关键机制。

Conclusion: 大规模模型能够通过特征学习实现更深层的“谱触及”（spectral reach），即利用更弱的信号进行学习，从而在训练后期超越小模型的性能。模型性能的提升与特征学习对梯度幅值的动态放大直接相关。

Abstract: Neural scaling laws describe predictable power-law relationships between model size, dataset size, compute, and performance. While these laws guide the development of modern foundation models, the mechanisms underpinning them remain poorly understood, in part due to the absence of scalable analysis tools. To close this gap, we introduce "spectral position": a scalable measure of which eigenvalues of the empirical neural tangent kernel (eNTK) currently drive loss reduction. Applying this measure to scaling experiments, we find that spectral position decreases throughout training: learning shifts from dominant eigenmodes into the spectral tail. Larger models reach further into the tail than smaller models, revealing a size-dependent capacity we call "spectral reach". This suggests why larger models achieve lower losses: they sustain learning on weak spectral signals inaccessible to smaller models. We further identify feature learning as a key enabler of spectral reach. It adaptively amplifies gradient magnitudes as learning advances, sustaining progress where frozen representations stall. This points to concrete interventions through architecture and optimizer design.

</details>


### [234] [Lightweight CNN-Based Anomaly Detection for High Voltage Converter Modulators in the Spallation Neutron Source](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31259)
*Alberto D. Cencillo,Leonardo Concepción,Julián Luengo,Isaac Triguero*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究针对高功率脉冲转换器（HVCM）故障检测，通过优化深度学习模型中“时间处理”与“通道交互”的操作顺序，并引入自适应通道加权，显著提升了故障前兆的识别能力，并在SNS数据集上取得了超越现有最优方法的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型加速器设施（如SNS）中HVCM的故障是导致停机的主要原因。现有的深度学习方法在处理多通道传感器数据时，往往过早地混合了时间和通道维度，忽略了不同通道间的独立性或特定的结构化关联，导致未能精准捕捉各类故障的前兆。

Method: 研究者提出了调整时间滤波与跨通道混合操作顺序的新架构，并引入了“每脉冲自适应通道重加权”机制。通过对比不同结构顺序对检测性能的影响，并结合消融实验分析关键输入通道，验证了模型对于不同故障家族的敏感度。

Result: 在SNS的四个子系统（RFQ, DTL, CCL, SCL）公开数据集上的评估结果显示，最优模型达到了0.816的AUC-PR和0.934的AUC-ROC。该方法在绝大多数子系统和6个故障类别中的5个上均超过了当前的最优性能（SOTA）。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了在深度学习架构中分离时间特征提取与通道间交互（即架构归纳偏置）对于检测高功率脉冲转换器故障至关重要。通过调整这两种操作的顺序并引入自适应通道加权，模型能够更有效地捕捉复杂故障的前兆，在SNS HVCM数据集上表现优于现有最优方法。

Abstract: Unscheduled trips of high-power pulsed converters are a leading source of downtime at large accelerator facilities. At the Spallation Neutron Source (SNS), the High Voltage Converter Modulators (HVCMs) are consistently the second-largest contributor to lost beam time. Each HVCM pulse is recorded across sensor channels spanning currents, voltages, and magnetic fluxes, whose mutual interactions encode the operating state of the system. Fault precursors do not manifest uniformly across these channels: depending on fault type, they may alter the temporal structure of individual signals, change the statistical dependencies among channels, or both. Existing deep-learning approaches typically process multi-channel signals with standard convolutional pipelines that entangle temporal and cross-channel operations from the first layer, giving the model no explicit mechanism to represent channel independence or structured inter-channel interaction. We hypothesise that architectural inductive bias, specifically the ordering of temporal filtering and cross-channel mixing, plays a central role in detection performance on this class of data. To test this, we vary the order in which these two operations are applied, and examine whether per-pulse adaptive channel reweighting further improves sensitivity. Evaluated on the public HVCM dataset across all four SNS subsystems (RFQ, DTL, CCL, SCL), our best variant achieves a pooled AUC-PR of 0.816 and AUC-ROC of 0.934, outperforming the state of the art on most subsystems and five of the six fault families. Ablations identify three dominant input channels and link per-fault-family performance to whether precursors manifest as amplitude shifts in individual channels or as subtler patterns requiring joint channel representations to surface.

</details>


### [235] [Skill Reuse as Compression in Agentic RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31509)
*Zhikun Xu,Yu Feng,Jacob Dineen,Taiwei Shi,Jieyu Zhao,Ben Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ReuseRL 通过利用 MDL 原则强制大语言模型智能体学习可重用的抽象技能模式，有效抑制了强化学习中的过拟合，显著提升了智能体的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型智能体在强化学习训练中容易产生难以泛化的、碎片化的“捷径”行为；作者假设当智能体的成功轨迹具备结构化可压缩性（即能被分解为一组可重用的抽象模式）时，其泛化性能更好。

Method: 提出 ReuseRL 框架：基于最小描述长度（MDL）原则，从成功的轨迹中提取共享的技能字典（Skill Dictionary），并在强化学习目标中加入分段压缩成本（Segmentation Cost）进行惩罚，滤除冗余的特异性行为；同时从理论上推导了 PAC-Bayes 泛化界。

Result: 在 ALFWorld、TextWorld-Cooking 和 Countdown-Stepwise 环境中，ReuseRL 相比于基础的 GRPO 和其他强基线模型，无论是在分布内（in-distribution）还是分布外（out-of-distribution）任务中，均取得了更高的成功率。

Conclusion: 通过最小描述长度原则引入压缩惩罚，能够显著提升大语言模型智能体在RL训练中的泛化能力，证明了结构化可压缩性与策略鲁棒性之间的正相关关系。

Abstract: Large language model agents trained with reinforcement learning (RL) often learn brittle, task-specific shortcuts. We hypothesize that agents generalize better when their successful trajectories are structurally compressible, decomposed into a small set of reusable abstract patterns. To formalize this, we introduce ReuseRL, which grounds agentic RL in the Minimum Description Length (MDL) principle. ReuseRL extracts a shared skill dictionary from successful trajectories and augments the RL objective with a segmentation cost, explicitly penalizing idiosyncratic behaviors that encode poorly. We prove a PAC-Bayes generalization bound for this compression penalty. Across ALFWorld, TextWorld-Cooking, and Countdown-Stepwise, ReuseRL improves in- and out-of-distribution success over vanilla GRPO and strong round-length baselines.

</details>


### [236] [Algorithmic Recourse of In-Context Learning for Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31272)
*Wenshuo Dong,Jiaming Zhang,Shaopneg Fu,Hongbin Lin,Di Wang,Lijie Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次研究了ICL模型的算法回溯问题，提出了ASR-ICL框架以生成高效、稀疏的回溯方案，并从理论和实验上证明了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型在表格数据预测中的应用（ICL），针对高风险决策（如信贷审批）提供可解释的算法回溯机制变得迫切，但目前针对ICL模型的回溯研究尚属空白。

Method: 1. 开展理论分析，推导回溯的定义、界限，并论证随上下文窗口增大，ICL的回溯结果向传统基准方法收敛。 
2. 提出名为“Adaptive Subspace Recourse for In-Context Learning (ASR-ICL)”的零阶回溯框架，通过自适应子空间搜索实现对黑盒ICL模型的有效归因建议。

Result: 在多个真实数据集上的实验表明，ASR-ICL在保持高质量回溯的同时，显著减少了查询次数，并实验验证了理论中预测的收敛行为。

Conclusion: 本文提出的ASR-ICL框架首次实现了针对上下文学习（ICL）模型的高效、可操作且稀疏的算法回溯，并通过理论与实证分析验证了其有效性及收敛性。

Abstract: As predictive models are increasingly deployed in high-stakes settings such as credit approval, there is a growing need for post-hoc methods that provide recourse to affected individuals. Many such models operate on tabular data, where features correspond to real-world attributes. Recently, in-context learning (ICL) has enabled large language models to perform tabular prediction by conditioning on labeled examples at inference time, without explicit training. However, algorithmic recourse for tabular decision-making under ICL remains largely unexplored. In this work, we present the first study of algorithmic recourse for tabular data under ICL. We carry out a theoretical analysis, showing that recourse remains well-defined and bounded, and we characterize how recourse converges toward classical solutions as the context size increases. In practice, we propose a novel zeroth-order recourse framework, Adaptive Subspace Recourse for In-Context Learning (ASR-ICL), that efficiently generates actionable and sparse recourse for black-box ICL models. The proposed framework naturally extends to multi-class tabular tasks. Experiments across multiple real-world datasets and models demonstrate that ASR-ICL achieves recourse quality comparable to existing methods with fewer queries and empirically confirm the predicted convergence behavior, supporting our theoretical analysis.

</details>


### [237] [Positional versus Symbolic Attention Heads: Learning Dynamics, RoPE Geometry, and Length Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31558)
*Felipe Urrutia,Juan José Alegría,Cinthia Sanchez Macias,Jorge Salas,Cristian B. Calderon,Cristobal Rojas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了Transformer注意力头在多跳推理中的学习机制，量化了位置与符号机制在长序列外推上的性能差异，发现符号化机制比位置机制更具鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了理解Transformer模型如何解决结构化任务，并预测其在处理新任务及长序列时的行为，以确保模型的安全部署。

Method: 通过在两种结构等价但逻辑需求不同的多跳推理任务（数字位置推理与字母符号推理）上训练GPT-J模型；利用特定的注意力头分类指标，结合RoPE旋转位置编码的几何特性分析注意力头的计算角色。

Result: 成功训练出的模型主要依赖“纯头”（pure heads），即表现出单一位置性或符号性的注意力头；研究定量界定了位置机制与符号机制，并定义了“差异性”（discrepancy）概念以度量二者对外推长度的鲁棒性差异。

Conclusion: 符号化机制比位置机制在处理更长序列时具有更强的外推鲁棒性，位置机制在长序列上的表现更受局限。

Abstract: Transformer-based language models are widespread in today's society. As such, understanding the mechanisms by which they solve structured tasks and predicting how they may behave in novel scenarios is of great importance for safe deployment. We study the learning dynamics of attention heads in a controlled setting by training a decoder-only Transformer (GPT-J) on two structurally equivalent multi-hop reasoning tasks: a number task requiring positional reasoning and a letter task requiring symbolic reasoning. Using a recently introduced metric that classifies attention-head behavior as positional or symbolic for a given prompt, we show that successful learning is associated with the emergence of pure heads, i.e., heads that express themselves as either positional or symbolic. Despite the tasks' structural equivalence, they impose different mechanistic demands: the number task requires both positional and symbolic heads, whereas the letter task requires only symbolic heads. We then identify the computational roles of these heads, characterize the basic functions they implement, and give theoretical constructions showing how single-layer RoPE-based attention can realize these functions through geometrically interpretable query, key, and value operations. This analysis yields a quantitative separation between positional and symbolic mechanisms in their robustness to longer sequences, formalized through a novel notion of discrepancy. We empirically validate the resulting predictions in both controlled and real-world models, showing that symbolic mechanisms extrapolate more reliably to longer sequences while positional mechanisms face sharper limitations.

</details>


### [238] [Learning Parametric Nitrogen Fertilizer Response Curves Using Neuro Symbolic Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31276)
*Giorgio Morales,John Sheppard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种神经符号回归方法，无需预设函数形式即可自动发现作物氮肥响应曲线的数学模型，实现了田间不同管理区域的精准、可解释性建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有氮肥响应模型在预定义的参数形式（灵活性受限）与不透明的机器学习模型（可解释性差）之间难以平衡，且缺乏从数据中发现区域特异性功能关系的能力。

Method: 提出了一种基于变换器（Transformer）的“多集符号骨架预测”策略，通过构建多样化的输入子集并强制执行一致性约束，结合遗传算法实现闭式函数（符号骨架）的自动发现与参数拟合。

Result: 在合成数据上验证了其在稀疏数据下的稳健性；在冬小麦实证研究中，该方法不仅在拟合误差上优于传统的二次平台函数和指数函数，还成功识别了不同管理区域内独特的响应曲线特征。

Conclusion: 神经符号回归（Neuro-SR）成功克服了传统参数化模型和黑箱机器学习的局限，能够从数据中自动学习并揭示田间不同管理区域（MZs）特有的氮素响应函数，为精准农业提供了可解释且高效的决策支持工具。

Abstract: Accurately modeling crop response to Nitrogen (N) fertilization is a fundamental challenge in precision agriculture, as it impacts both economic returns and environmental sustainability. Existing approaches either rely on predefined parametric forms or opaque machine learning models, limiting their ability to interpret or discover site-specific functional relationships from data. In this work, we propose a neuro symbolic regression (SR) approach to learn parametric N-response curves without assuming a predefined functional form. Our approach integrates a transformer-based Multi-Set Symbolic Skeleton Prediction strategy, enabling the discovery of shared functional structures across multiple subdomains or management zones (MZs). By constructing diverse input subsets and enforcing consistency across them, the method recovers robust symbolic skeletons that are subsequently fitted to observed data using a genetic algorithm. This framework was first evaluated on synthetic one-dimensional problems to assess its robustness under varying levels of epistemic uncertainty. The results demonstrate the ability of the proposed SR approach to recover correct expressions even in data-scarce regimes. In this work, we present the results of applying our method to real-world winter wheat data, learning distinct parametric N-response curves for different MZs within a field. The results show that the discovered expressions not only achieve lower fitting errors than traditional models such as quadratic-plateau and exponential functions, but also capture diverse functional behaviors across spatial regions. This demonstrates the potential that neuro SR has to enable the discovery of site-specific agronomic relationships and support informed decision-making in precision agriculture.

</details>


### [239] [Graph Neural Networks Are Not Continuous Across Graph Resolutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31315)
*Christian Koke,Yuesong Shen,Abhishek Saroha,Marvin Eisenberger,Bastian Rieck,Michael Bronstein,Daniel Cremers*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了 GNN 在处理跨尺度图数据时因信息传播机制缺陷而缺乏连续性，并提出了一种改进架构以实现跨尺度的表示一致性与可靠泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有共识认为 GNN 是连续的，但研究发现 GNN 在面对相同底层对象但分辨率不同的图时，会产生差异巨大的潜在表示，导致模型缺乏跨尺度的稳定性和一致性。

Method: 1. 理论分析：指出传统 GNN 在图收敛模式下的不连续性及其结构根源（信息传播机制缺陷）；2. 架构改进：针对该结构缺陷提出了一种具备跨尺度连续性的 GNN 模型修改方案。

Result: 通过理论证明与一系列数值实验验证，证实了改进后的架构能够有效解决跨尺度表示不一致问题，并提升了模型在该场景下的泛化可靠性。

Conclusion: GNN 的不连续性源于信息传播机制的结构性障碍。通过引入提出的模型改进方案，可以有效实现跨尺度连续性，从而提升模型在不同分辨率图数据间的表示一致性和泛化能力。

Abstract: We show that contrary to conventional wisdom in the community, graph neural networks (GNNs) are not continuous with respect to all natural modes of graph convergence. As a result, GNNs may generate substantially different latent representations for graphs that are very similar. In particular they assign vastly different latent embeddings to graphs that represent the same underlying object at different resolution scales. We trace this failure of continuity back to a structural obstruction arising from commonly used information-propagation schemes. Building on this insight we then derive a principled modification to standard GNN architectures which equips models with continuity across scales. The proposed modification enables consistent integration of distinct resolutions and reliable generalization between them. We systematically validate our theoretical findings in a wide range of numerical experiments.

</details>


### [240] [Generalized Intention Modeling in Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31318)
*Mateusz Odrowaz-Sypniewski,Jasmine Bayrooti,Ajay Shankar,Amanda Prorok*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对现有对手建模方法在任务适应性上的不足，本文提出了一种通过最大化互信息学习性能驱动意图组合的自适应框架，显著提升了在非合作多智能体强化学习中的适应能力与决策质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的对手建模方法依赖于预定义的启发式信息（如动作或状态）来编码意图，但这在不同任务和环境中往往不够全面，缺乏通用性。

Method: 提出了一种任务自适应的对手建模框架，该框架：1. 学习多个意图表示的性能驱动混合权重；2. 引入了一种最大化与自我智能体未来收益间互信息的全新意图表示方法，以确保捕获对性能最相关的对手特征。

Result: 在多种环境中始终达到或超过现有最先进（SOTA）基线算法的性能，并提供了对模型在不同场景下为何有效的深刻见解。

Conclusion: 通过学习性能驱动的意图表示混合模型，该方法能自适应地提取对手信息，在多智能体强化学习中实现了比固定基准更优且更具解释性的决策效果。

Abstract: Modeling an opponent's intent is critical for effective decision-making in non-cooperative, competitive, and general-sum multi-agent reinforcement learning. Existing opponent modeling methods encode intent using an embedding derived from episode information chosen a priori, such as the opponent's next action or a future environment state, and use this to guide the ego-agent's behavior. These approaches assume that the chosen information is universally representative of intent; however, we show empirically that this is not the case as intentions are often task- and environment-dependent. To address this, we introduce a task-adaptive opponent modeling framework that learns a performance-driven mixture of multiple intent representations. We further introduce a new intention representation that maximizes mutual information with the ego-agent's future returns, thereby capturing opponent information that is most directly relevant to performance. Our approach consistently matches or exceeds the performance of state-of-the-art baselines across diverse tasks and yields insights into when and why different opponent modeling strategies succeed.

</details>


### [241] [A Unifying View of Variational Generative Wasserstein Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31369)
*Paul Caucheteux,Clément Bonet,Anna Korba*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Generative Wasserstein Flows (GWF)框架，利用Wasserstein梯度流和JKO离散化方案，统一了现有的生成建模方法，并扩展了其在不同散度度量下的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决现代生成模型虽然都基于最小化概率分布间的散度，但在算法和几何原理上缺乏统一性的问题，试图通过Wasserstein梯度流视角将不同方法整合至同一理论框架下。

Method: 引入了基于Wasserstein梯度流的统一框架（GWF），利用Jordan-Kinderlehrer-Otto (JKO) 方案对分布优化进行离散化，并将其扩展至f-散度、积分概率度量（IPM）及最大均值差异（MMD）。

Result: 证明了多种生成模型（如GAN及相关算法）可被视为对f-散度目标的参数化JKO方案；导出了基于JKO的新型生成算法；分析了参数化Wasserstein流并实证评估了JKO正则化的影响。

Conclusion: 研究提出了生成式Wasserstein流（GWF）框架，统一了现有的多种生成模型，并探索了正则化与参数化映射在Wasserstein动力学中的作用，为理解生成建模的几何与最优传输视角提供了深刻的理论基础。

Abstract: Many modern generative models can be viewed as minimizing divergences between probability distributions, yet they rely on different algorithmic and geometric principles. Wasserstein gradient flows provide a continuous-time formulation for optimizing over distributions, and can be approximated through their implicit discretization via the Jordan-Kinderlehrer-Otto (JKO) scheme. In this work, we present a unified theoretical framework for generative modeling based on Wasserstein gradient flows, which we refer to as Generative Wasserstein Flows (GWF). We show that a broad class of existing methods can be derived as instances of parametric JKO schemes for $f$-divergence objectives, and we establish equivalences between several recently proposed algorithms. We extend this framework beyond f-divergence to Integral Probability Metrics and squared Maximum Mean Discrepancy, deriving new JKO-based generative algorithms, and clarifying their connections with GANs. We study empirically the impact of the JKO regularization for a wide set of objectives. Finally, we analyze parametric Wasserstein flows, where the dynamics are restricted to distributions induced by parametrized maps.

</details>


### [242] [Softsign: Smooth Sign in Your Optimizer For Better Parameter Heterogeneity Handling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31371)
*Dmitrii Feoktistov,Timofey Belinsky,Andrey Veprikov,Amir Zainullin,Aleksandr Beznosikov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 SoftSignum 优化器，通过将硬符号更新平滑化为温度可控的数值更新，克服了传统符号优化器的收敛局限性，提升了深度学习模型训练的性能与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于符号（sign-based）和 LMO 的优化器虽然具有内存效率高的优点，但其固定幅值的更新策略与梯度幅值解耦，忽略了参数间的异质性，容易导致终端收敛时的震荡，阻碍了模型的精确收敛。

Method: 1. 提出 SoftSignum：将硬符号映射替换为温度可控的软符号变换，允许梯度更新在符号化与数值化之间平滑过渡；2. 开发了基于分位数自适应的温度调度机制；3. 提出 SoftMuon：将该原则扩展至矩阵值优化器；4. 构建了基于强凸正则化器和 Fenchel 共轭的广义几何松弛框架。

Result: 实验证明，SoftSignum 和 SoftMuon 在包括大语言模型（LLM）预训练在内的多种深度学习任务中，性能均显著优于传统的硬符号优化器及标准 AdamW 优化器，且在随机非凸设置下得到了理论收敛性保证。

Conclusion: SoftSignum 和 SoftMuon 通过引入对温度可控的平滑松弛机制，解决了硬符号更新导致的收敛困难和震荡问题，在保持低内存优势的同时，实现了比传统符号优化器和 AdamW 更优的收敛性能。

Abstract: Sign-based and LMO-inspired optimizers have recently attracted substantial attention in deep learning due to their strong performance and low memory footprint. However, their fixed-magnitude updates can hurt terminal convergence: they decouple update mechanisms from gradient magnitudes and fail to account for parameter heterogeneity, often leading to oscillation rather than convergence. We propose SoftSignum, a smooth relaxation of sign-based optimization that replaces the hard sign map with a temperature-controlled soft-sign transformation, enabling a parameter-wise transition from sign-like updates to magnitude-sensitive SGD-like steps. We complement it with an adaptive quantile-based temperature schedule and extend the same principle to matrix-valued optimizers, obtaining SoftMuon. We also develop a generalized geometry-relaxation framework based on strongly convex regularizers and Fenchel conjugates, proving convergence in stochastic non-convex setting. Experiments on diverse deep learning tasks, including LLM pretraining, show that SoftSignum and SoftMuon consistently improve over their hard sign-based counterparts and standard AdamW.

</details>


### [243] [Constrained Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning with Max-Min Criterion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31388)
*Giseung Park,Hyunyoung Nam,Woohyeon Byeon,Amir Leshem,Youngchul Sung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合 Max-Min 优化准则与显式约束满足的新型多目标强化学习框架，解决了公平性与约束条件难以兼顾的难题，并在理论与多个实际应用场景中证实了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 Max-Min 多目标强化学习虽然能提升公平性，但在处理现实场景中必要的约束条件时能力不足，缺乏有效的框架来兼顾公平与约束满足。

Method: 将 Max-Min 准则与显式约束满足相结合，构建了新的多目标强化学习框架，通过理论收敛性分析及在网格环境、建筑热控、运动控制和交通管理中的实验进行验证。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法在建筑热控、多目标运动控制和温室气体排放感知交通管理等领域，能够成功平衡公平性目标与安全/约束限制。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种具备约束满足能力的 Max-Min 多目标强化学习框架，理论上验证了其收敛性，并实证了其在复杂多目标决策场景下的有效性和公平性。

Abstract: Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning (MORL) extends standard RL by optimizing policies with respect to multiple, often conflicting, objectives. While max-min MORL has emerged as an effective approach for promoting fairness, its applicability remains limited, particularly when constraints must be incorporated. In this paper, we propose a MORL framework that integrates the max-min criterion with explicit constraint satisfaction. We establish a theoretical foundation for the proposed framework and validate the resulting algorithm through convergence analysis and experiments in tabular settings. We further demonstrate the practical relevance of our approach in simulated building thermal control, multi-objective locomotion control, and greenhouse-gas-emission-aware traffic management. Across these domains, our method effectively balances fairness and constraint satisfaction in multi-objective decision-making.

</details>


### [244] [Fixed Universal Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31423)
*Jingwen Liu,Alexandr Andoni,Daniel Hsu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了“通用Transformer”概念，证明了固定参数的Transformer在足够大的嵌入维度下，仅通过改变输入编码即可模拟任意Transformer，揭示了输入表示在模型表达能力中的核心作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨Transformer架构的表达能力来源，研究固定参数的Transformer是否能够通过合适的输入编码模拟特定类别的目标模型，从而揭示模型权重与输入表示在表示能力中的相对贡献。

Method: 1. 定义并引入“通用Transformer”（通用图灵机概念的类比）；2. 提出基于大嵌入维度（embedding dimension）的显式稀疏构造方法；3. 从理论上证明随机初始化的Transformer几乎确定性地具有通用性；4. 在括号匹配和多跳推理算法任务上进行实证验证。

Result: 1. 证明了当嵌入维度足够大时，存在可实现通用性的稀疏构造方案；2. 理论验证了通用性是随机初始化Transformer的一个泛化特性（generic property）；3. 实验数据支持了理论发现，即Transformer可以通过其输入表征实现复杂的算法逻辑。

Conclusion: Transformer的表达能力在很大程度上可能源于输入表示（input representation）而非模型权重本身。

Abstract: We introduce \emph{universal transformers}: fixed transformers that can simulate any transformer in a given class via a suitable input embedding. Analogous to a universal Turing machine, the input embedding encodes a description of the target model while all internal parameters remain fixed. We provide explicit sparse constructions achieving universality when the embedding dimension is sufficiently large, and further show that universality is generic: randomly initialized transformers are universal almost surely, which aligns with recent empirical results of Zhong and Andreas (2024). We empirically validate our theory on the algorithmic tasks of parenthesis balancing and multi-hop reasoning. Our results suggest that much of a transformer's expressive power may reside in its input representation rather than its learned weights.

</details>


### [245] [Flow map learning in nonlinear vector autoregressive models: influence of the feature-library structure on the training error](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31438)
*Markus Gross*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析了非线性向量自回归（NVAR/NG-RC）模型的学习可辨识性，揭示了训练误差随时间分辨率变化的缩放规律，并指出仅增加延迟项而不进行特征库匹配无法保障模型的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对时间序列预测中非线性与时间延迟依赖的学习问题，探讨非线性向量自回归（NVAR）/下一代储层计算（NG-RC）模型的可辨识性问题，并揭示训练误差与时间分辨率之间的渐近扩展规律。

Method: 将NVAR/NG-RC模型视为Koopman算符的近似，理论分析了训练误差随时间分辨率、非线性度及延迟项数量变化的扩展律（Scaling laws），并针对多项式和傅里叶特征库进行了数学推导与数值实验验证。

Result: 揭示了训练误差表现出的典型（预）渐近扩展律，证明了该扩展律取决于特征库对流映射的早期李级数系数的逼近能力；明确了训练误差与时间分辨率、非线性阶数及延迟项数之间的依赖关系。

Conclusion: 研究强调了模型类与数据生成过程匹配的重要性。虽然增加延迟项可以减小单步训练误差，但只有在特征库具备足够的非线性表示能力时，才能提升长程预测性能。训练误差的减小并不一定等同于泛化能力的提升。

Abstract: Time series forecasting often requires learning nonlinear and time-delayed dependencies. A paradigmatic class of forecasting models are nonlinear vector autoregressive processes (NVAR), also known as next-generation reservoir computers (NG-RCs). These models approximate the Koopman operator on the space spanned by their explicit feature library. We consider the identifiability problem for learning Markovian nonlinear dynamical systems and show that the training error as a function of time resolution follows characteristic (pre-)asymptotic scaling laws. These laws depend on whether the feature library can represent the early Lie-series coefficients of the flow map (propagator) exactly or merely approximately. For dynamical systems governed by polynomial vector fields, we demonstrate the mechanism for NVAR/NG-RC models with monomial and Fourier feature libraries. We determine the dependence of the training error on the temporal resolution, the involved nonlinear degree, and the number of delay terms. While delay terms reduce the optimal one-step training error, they improve long-horizon forecasts only when the library provides sufficient nonlinearity. Thus, small training error coexists with weak generalization as the model class is mismatched to the true data-generating process. Numerical experiments on various chaotic dynamical systems confirm the theoretical predictions.

</details>


### [246] [Balanced LoRA: Removing Parameter Invariance to Accelerate Convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31484)
*Valérie Castin,Kimia Nadjahi,Pierre Ablin,Gabriel Peyré*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对 LoRA 训练收敛效率不佳的问题，论文提出了 BaLoRA，通过引入平衡流形投影技术改善损失函数条件数，实现了更快、更优的模型微调效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA 存在固有的超参数化现象（即不同的低秩因子对可得到相同的权重矩阵），且不同参数组合导致的条件数差异巨大，从而影响训练收敛速度。

Method: 提出 Balanced Low-Rank Adaptation (BaLoRA)，通过在每次迭代中将权重投影到“平衡流形（balanced manifold）”上，优化损失平面的条件数，且该方法计算成本低，可无缝集成到现有工作流中。

Result: BaLoRA 在数学证明和实证研究中均优于标准 LoRA，具备更快的收敛速度，并在多项微调任务中表现出更好的性能。

Conclusion: BaLoRA 通过在优化过程中保持低秩因子的平衡性，解决了 LoRA 中因超参数化导致的条件数变差问题，从而显著提升了收敛速度和模型最终性能。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) is the most widely adopted method for fine-tuning large language models. Notably, LoRA is inherently overparameterized: multiple pairs of low-rank factors can yield the same adapted weight matrix. We show--both theoretically and empirically--that these pairs exhibit significantly different condition numbers. As a result, converging to different loss minimizers directly impacts the convergence rate of LoRA. Building on this observation, we introduce Balanced Low-Rank Adaptation (BaLoRA), a variant of LoRA that projects iterates onto a balanced manifold. This manifold improves the conditioning of the loss landscape while preserving the adapted matrix. The projection step is computationally lightweight and integrates seamlessly into existing fine-tuning pipelines. Empirically, BaLoRA converges faster than standard LoRA and achieves superior performance across a range of fine-tuning tasks.

</details>


### [247] [Graphical einops: bridging tensor networks and computation graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31485)
*Vincent Wang-Maścianica,Nikhil Khatri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将深度学习架构图转化为正式图形演算的方法，通过嵌套管状图表示张量轴，实现了张量运算的简化证明与优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的深度学习架构图仅起示意作用，深层逻辑仍依赖繁琐的数学推导；需要一种能将图形表示与程序等价性（tensor-program identities）直接挂钩的正式方法。

Method: 引入了一种基于张量轴嵌套分级管（nested graded tubes）的正式图形演算，核心重写规则为等级自然性（grade-naturality）。

Result: 提供了一套短小精悍的图论推导方法，支持自动化的等变性证明，并成功将注意力掩码转化为预处理操作，实现了稀疏注意力块的高效化。

Conclusion: 通过将复杂的张量运算转化为直观的图形计算，该研究展示了一种正式形式化深学习计算图的方法，使等变性证明和重构优化变得更加简单高效。

Abstract: Architecture diagrams are ubiquitous in deep learning, but they are usually only representational: the tensor-program identities they suggest are still proved by prose and tensor-axis manipulation. We introduce a formal graphical calculus for the structural fragment of tensor programming underlying einops, making such diagrams proof-enabling. Our calculus represents tensor axes as nested graded tubes around a base type. The tube boundary recovers the undirected tensor-network view of axes, while the directed interior retains the operational reading of computation graphs. The key rewrite is grade-naturality: sliding spectacles over tubes. Standard equivariance proofs become short diagrammatic derivations. We additionally demonstrate how our rewrite system may be applied to convert attention masks into pre-processing operations, recovering efficient implementations of sparse attention blocks.

</details>


### [248] [On the Relationship Between Activation Outliers and Feature Death in Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31518)
*Elana Simon,Etowah Adams,James Zou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究揭示了稀疏自编码器中“特征死亡”现象是由激活值分布中的异常值引起的，并提出了用均值中心化（减去激活均值）作为解决该问题的统一且有效的预处理方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏自编码器（SAE）在训练中普遍存在“特征死亡”（feature death）问题，导致字典容量浪费并导致叠加态（superposition）重现。研究旨在揭示特征死亡的原因，特别是解释为何不同模型间的死亡率存在巨大差异（如AlphaFold3异常高）。

Method: 1. 形式化定义异常值严重程度度量指标 γ = \|μ\|/\|σ\|；2. 在跨越语言、视觉、蛋白质和基因组模型的 454 个模型层组合上进行实验验证；3. 对比分析偏置（bias）学习过程与特征死亡率，并测试均值中心化（mean-centering）的可行性。

Result: 1. 发现维度级激活异常值是导致特征死亡的根本原因：异常值导致预激活偏移，导致部分特征始终无法触发；2. 指标 γ 与初始死亡率呈高度相关（Spearman 相关系数 0.82-0.89）；3. 证实了均值中心化能彻底消除由异常值引起的特征死亡。

Conclusion: 通过均值中心化（减去激活平均值）可以有效消除激活异常导致的特征死亡现象。这为稀疏自编码器（SAE）的预处理提供了理论依据，并证明了在处理具有高异常值的模型时均值中心化的必要性。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) decompose neural network activations into interpretable features, but many learned features never activate, a problem called feature death that wastes dictionary capacity and can reintroduce superposition. Death rates vary dramatically between models: near-zero on GPT-2, over 70% on AlphaFold3 with identical configurations. We find that dimension-level activation outliers (dimensions whose mean magnitude is large relative to per-token variation) cause this by shifting pre-activations at initialization based on each feature's alignment with the activation mean. Features anti-aligned with the mean receive permanently negative pre-activations and never fire. We formalize outlier severity as $γ= \|μ\|/\|σ\|$; it predicts initial death rates (Spearman $ρ= 0.89$ for dead-by-TopK, $0.82$ for dead-by-ReLU) across 454 model-layer combinations spanning language, vision, protein, and genomic models. Dead features can revive during training, but recovery requires the SAE bias to learn the activation mean, a process that is prohibitively slow at high $γ$. Mean-centering (subtracting the activation mean) sidesteps this and eliminates outlier-induced death across all tested models, confirming the mechanism and providing a principled basis for when and why this preprocessing step is necessary.

</details>


### [249] [Chem-PerturBridge: a harmonized compendium of small molecule perturbation transcriptomic effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31522)
*Artur Szałata,Olga Novitskaia,Maiia Shulman,Matthew Mella,Altynbek Zhubanchaliyev,Fabian J. Theis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Chem-PerturBridge 是一个整合了1.25M标准化小分子扰动转录组样本的资源，旨在解决数据碎片化问题，并被证明是提升化合物表征学习效果的有效预训练数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的用于小分子建模的转录组资源高度碎片化（跨技术、元数据、控制组和预处理流程），缺乏统一性，限制了大规模扰动模型的训练与评估。

Method: 整合了来自不同来源的8种检测类型、136种细胞环境共37k化合物的1.25M转录组样本，进行了统一的标识符标准化、元数据对齐及复制感知（replicate-aware）的条件效应处理。并利用该资源进行了跨数据集匹配一致性评估，以及基于OP3（化合物留一）标准的表征学习预训练评估。

Result: 1. 揭示了跨数据集的细粒度 logFC 排名一致性较弱；2. logFC 方向的一致性较为稳定；3. Chem-PerturBridge 作为预训练资源能显著提升模型在各类化合物表征评估任务（如 OP3 评估）中的表现，优于传统的 L1000 或指纹方法。

Conclusion: Chem-PerturBridge 提供了一个标准化的多源转录组数据集，验证了跨数据集特征的一致性规律，并证明了其作为预训练资源在化合物表征学习中的卓越性能。

Abstract: Large perturbation models require training data encompassing chemical, cellular, and assay diversity. Current transcriptomic resources for small-molecule modeling, however, are fragmented across technologies, metadata conventions, controls, doses, and preprocessing pipelines. We introduce Chem-PerturBridge, a harmonized multi-dataset resource comprising over 37k compounds, 136 cellular contexts, and 1.25M transcriptomic samples across eight assay types, with standardized identifiers, metadata, and replicate-aware condition-level effects. We use the resource to evaluate matched-condition agreement across datasets and replicate agreement within datasets. Matched same-compound conditions generally show weak agreement in fine-grained logFC rankings and magnitudes across most dataset pairs, often falling below same-context different-compound baselines. In contrast, logFC direction agreement is substantially more stable and usually exceeds these baselines. We further evaluate Chem-PerturBridge as a pretraining resource for compound representation learning. Under a compound-held-out OP3 evaluation split, embeddings pretrained on Chem-PerturBridge improve over L1000-only embeddings, Morgan fingerprints, and the descriptor-free OP3 baseline across metrics. An extensive molecule-holdout evaluation across 11 datasets further shows that models trained on Chem-PerturBridge outperform or match those that are not. Chem-PerturBridge therefore supports both diagnostic evaluation of cross-dataset signature agreement and model-oriented reuse of heterogeneous perturbation transcriptomic data.

</details>


### [250] [Value Functions as Supermartingale Certificates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31524)
*Alessandro Abate,Daniel Contro,Mirco Giacobbe,Agustín Martínez-Suñé,Diptarko Roy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过证明强化学习的值函数可以编码随机系统的$\omega$-正则规范的超鞅认证，架起了形式化验证与强化学习之间的桥梁，为在连续状态空间中实现具备形式化保证的强化学习策略提供了新路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的随机系统认证方法具有良好的形式化保证但主要用于验证；而强化学习虽然擅长解决$\omega$-正则任务，但在一般状态空间下缺乏满足规范的形式化保证。本文旨在通过建立二者之间的理论桥梁，实现带有形式化保证的强化学习。

Method: 建立理论联系：证明了在特定奖励函数设置下，几乎确定性满足$\omega$-正则属性的策略所对应的值函数，编码了该规范的Streett超鞅认证。并利用强化学习算法进行认证合成和验证。

Result: 在理论上证明了值函数与超鞅认证的一致性；实验验证显示该方法能够有效地在有限Markov决策过程中进行认证合成，且理论框架适用于有限、可数无限及连续状态空间。

Conclusion: 本文确立了强化学习（RL）值函数与形式化验证中超鞅（supermartingale）证明之间的理论联系，证明了可以通过强化学习来合成几乎确定性满足$\omega$-正则属性的策略的认证，为在一般状态空间（包括连续空间）中实现有保障的RL提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Certification methods for stochastic systems provide sufficient proof rules, based on real-valued supermartingale certificates, to determine the almost-sure satisfaction of $ω$-regular properties (and therefore of linear temporal logic) over general state spaces, encompassing both countably infinite and continuous state spaces. Conversely, reinforcement learning (RL) methods for $ω$-regular tasks have received considerable attention, but they typically lack formal guarantees that the learned policy satisfies the specification, except possibly for finite state and action spaces. We bridge these two lines of research by establishing a novel theoretical connection: under an appropriate reward, the value function associated to a policy that almost surely satisfies an $ω$-regular property encodes a Streett supermartingale certificate for that specification. Our results, validated experimentally on finite Markov decision processes, hold for finite, countably infinite, and continuous state spaces, suggesting a principled route to certificate synthesis via RL.

</details>


### [251] [Functional Attention: From Pairwise Affinities to Functional Correspondences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31559)
*Jiefang Xiao,Maolin Gao,Simon Weber,Guandao Yang,Daniel Cremers*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为“函数式注意力”的算子学习方法，通过将注意力机制重新解释为基函数间的线性映射，实现了更紧凑、具备分辨率不变性的全局函数建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Transformer算子学习方法通常将连续域离散化为Token，未能充分利用连续函数的全局函数结构，导致算子学习的泛化能力和分辨率不变性受限。

Method: 提出“函数式注意力”（Functional Attention），基于几何函数映射（Functional Maps）理论，用结构化线性算子替代传统的Softmax注意力机制，并在自适应基函数空间中建模函数间的对应关系。

Result: 在偏微分方程求解、3D分割及回归任务中达到或超越了当前SOTA水平，且对不同分辨率的离散化具有极强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: “函数式注意力”机制成功将注意力重构为基函数间的函数映射，在算子学习任务中实现了高精度、分辨率不变性及良好的泛化性能。

Abstract: Learning mappings between infinite-dimensional function spaces, or operator learning, is essential for many machine learning applications. Although transformer-based operators are popular, they often rely on token-wise attention. These methods treat continuous fields as discrete tokens and usually ignore the global functional structure. We introduce \emph{Functional Attention}, which reinterprets attention as a functional correspondence between adaptive bases. Inspired by geometric functional maps, our method replaces softmax affinities with structured linear operators. This yields a compact, generalizable, resolution-invariant representation that explicitly captures global dependencies. Experiments demonstrate that \emph{Functional Attention} can match state-of-the-art performance in many operator learning tasks, including solving PDEs, 3D segmentation, and regression, while remaining robust to varying discretizations. Project page is available at https://github.com/xjffff/FUNCATTN.

</details>


### [252] [Effective Biological Representation Learning by Masking Gene Expression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31562)
*Kian Kenyon-Dean,Alina Selega,Ihab Bendidi,Jordan M. Sorokin,Luca Bertinetto,David Errington,Hayley Donnella,Oren Kraus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TxFM 证明了通过精细的架构设计和高质量的数据集策划，归纳式自监督学习模型可以在处理 RNA-seq 数据时显著优于现有的大规模转录组基础模型和线性基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的转录组基础模型（FMs）在处理 RNA-seq 数据时，受限于技术噪声和批次效应，往往表现逊于简单的线性基线模型，导致深度表征学习的临床/药物发现价值受到质疑。

Method: 1. 提出 TxFM 模型，采用针对 RNA-seq 计数数据优化的掩码自编码器（Masked Autoencoding）架构；
2. 构建并使用 DiverseRNA-1.4M 数据集作为训练语料；
3. 进行归纳表征学习（Inductive representation learning）评估及关键架构配置的消融实验。

Result: TxFM 在 DiverseRNA-1.4M 上训练后，生成了高保真的基因表征，其性能超过了规模大 100 倍以上的地图集（Atlas-scale）规模基础模型。

Conclusion: 归纳式自监督学习是转录组表征的有效建模方法，但前提是模型架构和训练数据策划必须经过精心设计与权衡，而非盲目追求数据规模。

Abstract: RNA sequencing produces rich and diverse datasets of gene expression, offering compelling insights into cellular state and function that have many applications in drug discovery. Modeling such data is challenging due to inherent technical noise and experimental batch effects, as evidenced by many existing transcriptomic foundation models (FMs) underperforming relative to linear baselines. Such results raise the question of whether deep representation learning provides a distinct advantage over the direct use of raw transcript counts. Our work explores this by developing a new self-supervised model, TxFM, with a focus on inductive representation learning evaluations. TxFM employs a masked autoencoding approach tailored to diverse RNA-seq count data, and our ablation study empirically identifies crucial architecture configurations required for strong transfer performance. Additionally, we curate a public training corpus, DiverseRNA-1.4M, and find that TxFM trained on this curated dataset yields high-fidelity gene representations that outperform FMs trained on atlas-scale corpora over 100x larger. Overall, our results indicate that inductive self-supervised learning is a viable modeling approach for transcriptomics representation, provided a careful synthesis of model architecture and training data curation.

</details>


### [253] [Giving Sensors a Voice: Multimodal JEPA for Semantic Time-Series Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31580)
*Utsav Dutta,Gerardo Pastrana,Sina Khoshfetrat Pakazad,Henrik Ohlsson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CHARM 是一种新型多变量时间序列表征学习模型，通过融合传感器文本描述与 JEPA 训练框架，实现了高性能、鲁棒且具备可解释性的跨数据集时间序列建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管 Transformer 在语言和视觉领域表现出色，但针对异构多变量时间序列的通用表征学习仍研究不足。现有模型难以有效应对传感器噪声、缺乏跨不同数据集的泛化能力及可解释性。

Method: 提出 CHARM 模型：1. 基于 Transformer 编码器并引入通道级文本描述；2. 设计通道顺序等变（equivariant）架构；3. 采用 JEPA（联合嵌入预测架构）训练目标，并引入新型损失函数以增强时序稳定性；4. 使用描述感知门控机制（description-aware gating）实现可解释性。

Result: 在异常检测、分类以及短长期预测任务上，仅使用线性探测（linear probe）即可达到优异性能；文本信息有效提升了跨数据集的泛化能力。

Conclusion: CHARM 通过结合通道文本描述、JEPA 目标和通道顺序等变性，为异构多变量时间序列提供了一种鲁棒、可解释且具有跨数据集泛化能力的通用表征学习方案。

Abstract: Transformer-based architectures have advanced sequence modeling in language and vision, yet general-purpose representation learning for heterogeneous multivariate time series remains underexplored. We introduce CHARM (Channel-Aware Representation Model), which incorporates channel-level textual descriptions into a Transformer encoder equivariant to channel order. CHARM is trained with a Joint Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA) and a novel loss promoting informative, temporally stable embeddings; latent-space prediction encourages robustness to sensor noise while description-aware gating provides interpretability through learned inter-channel relationships. Across anomaly detection, classification, and short- and long-term forecasting, the learned embeddings achieve strong performance using only a linear probe. Performance is driven primarily by the JEPA objective and conditioning architecture, with text descriptions serving as channel identifiers for cross-dataset generalization.

</details>


### [254] [A Tight Theory of Error Feedback Algorithms in Distributed Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31594)
*Daniel Berg Thomsen,Adrien Taylor,Aymeric Dieuleveut*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对分布式学习中的梯度压缩问题，对EF和EF21两种主流误差反馈算法进行了严谨的收敛性分析，确定了最优参数配置，并证明了其在多智能体系统中的理论性能达到了单机优化水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式学习中通信成本是主要瓶颈，梯度压缩技术虽能缓解此问题但会损害收敛性；现有的误差反馈机制变体众多，缺乏对其相对性能的深入理解与分析。

Method: 对经典的误差反馈（EF）和EF21算法进行理论收敛性分析，通过构建针对性的最优Lyapunov函数，推导并确定了使得算法收敛的最优步长选择策略。

Result: 得出了EF和EF21算法的紧致收敛性分析，证明了在分布式学习场景下，这些算法能保持优秀的收敛性能，且分析结果在多节点环境下依然稳健。

Conclusion: 通过为EF和EF21算法构建最优Lyapunov函数并确定最佳步长，证明了这两类方法在分布式环境下的收敛性，且结果与节点数量无关，并达到了单智能体环境下的理论最优界。

Abstract: Communication costs are a major bottleneck in distributed learning and first-order optimization. A common approach to alleviate this issue is to compress the gradient information exchanged between agents. However, such compression typically degrades the convergence guarantees of gradient-based methods. Error feedback mechanisms provide a simple and computationally cheap remedy for this issue, but numerous variants have been proposed, and their relative performance remains poorly understood. This paper provides tight convergence analyses for two of the main error-feedback algorithms from the literature, the classic Error Feedback method (EF) and Error Feedback 21 (EF21), by identifying optimal step-size choices and constructing optimal Lyapunov functions tailored to each method. The results hold independently of the number of agents and recover the known best guarantees possible in the single-agent regime.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [255] [Polynomial Histograms for Memory-Efficient Representation of Long-tailed System Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30360)
*Murray Stokely,Tim Hesterberg,Arif Merchant,Nate Coehlo*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多项式直方图技术，通过在直方图箱体中存储统计矩来更高效地表示分布式性能数据，在同等存储开销下显著降低了信息损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分布式系统中，需要以最小的系统开销高效地收集、聚合和存储跨多个维度的海量性能指标（如延迟分布），并支持下游的分析应用。

Method: 1. 提出了一种针对分箱数据（binned data）的信息损失度量方法。 2. 引入了多项式直方图技术，即在直方图的每个箱体中存储底层的统计矩（moments）。 3. 将此方法与等存储开销下的传统直方图进行对比评估。

Result: 实验表明，多项式直方图在同等存储成本下能减少信息损失，并在生产环境的文件系统指标分析中表现出比传统直方图更优的准确性。

Conclusion: 多项式直方图（Polynomial Histograms）在存储开销受限的情况下，比传统直方图能更准确地表征分布特征，尤其是在高阶矩对下游分析至关重要时，具有显著的性能优势。

Abstract: Distributed systems must frequently keep track of many different types of performance metrics across many different computers. For example, the latency distribution of certain operations may be computed for a large combination of computers, users, and operations. These empirical distributions need to be collected at minimal expense on the individual software components, efficiently aggregated across multiple dimensions, and stored in a compact representation for a variety of downstream data analysis applications.
  We describe an information loss metric for binned data that allows us to optimize cost of information loss from different histogram representations. We explore the use of polynomial histograms where each bin of a histogram is annotated with moments of the underlying distribution in that bin. These polynomial histograms are compared to traditional histograms using the same storage cost for additional bins instead of annotations in each bin. We describe an application of these techniques for file system metrics for a large production system, and analytically characterize when polynomial histograms offer more information at lower cost.

</details>


### [256] [A Datalog Framework for Conflict-Free Replicated Data Types](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31569)
*Elena Yanakieva,Annette Bieniusa,Stefania Dumbrava*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个使用Datalog构建的声明式框架，通过将CRDT语义转换为可执行的逻辑程序，实现了对复杂并发协作应用的高效建模、测试与自动化分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式应用中协作数据（如CRDT）的并发交互语义复杂，且缺乏系统性的自动化推理和验证机制，难以保证多用户协同更新的正确性。

Method: 提出一个基于Datalog的声明式框架，将CRDT语义模型化为操作上下文上的可执行逻辑程序，从而使并发语义变得明确、可组合，并支持基于属性的测试（Property-based Testing）。

Result: 通过协作图数据编辑案例研究证明了该方法的可行性，实验结果显示该框架能有效验证CRDT的正确性，并具备一定的可扩展性。

Conclusion: Datalog是一种用于建模、原型设计及自动化验证CRDT语义及其组合的有效工具，为分析分布式一致性模型提供了系统化的逻辑基础。

Abstract: Distributed applications increasingly support local-first collaboration over shared data, allowing multiple users to perform updates concurrently without global coordination. Such collaboration requires careful design to capture the intended semantics of the concurrent interactions.
  We introduce a declarative framework for specifying and reasoning about the semantics of conflict-free replicated data types (CRDTs) and CRDT-based applications in Datalog. The framework models CRDT semantics as executable logic programs over operation contexts, making concurrency explicit and compositional, and thus amenable to automated analysis. As one application, we use property-based testing to compare implementations. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to systematically use Datalog as a foundation for prototyping and analyzing complex CRDTs and their compositions.
  We evaluate our methodology using a collaborative graph data editing case study and report experimentation results assessing correctness validation and scalability with an increasing number of operations and replicas.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [257] [Physics-informed Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning under Hybrid Contact Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30503)
*Vittorio Giammarino,Anastasios Manganaris,Ahmed H. Qureshi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对现有物理启发式目标条件强化学习（Pi-GCRL）在接触丰富操作任务中表现不佳的问题，提出了通过接触感知和分层设计的改进框架，成功将物理归纳偏置转化为鲁棒的操作能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 Pi-GCRL 虽然在导航等领域效果显著，但在接触丰富的任务中面临混合动力学、模态依赖的可控性和非光滑价值景观等挑战，这导致 naive Pi-GCRL 在此类环境下表现不佳，需要更具适应性的建模策略。

Method: 分析了现有 Pi-GCRL 在接触丰富环境下的局限性，并提出了“接触感知（contact-aware）”和“分层（hierarchical）”的 reformulated 方法，旨在将物理启发的归纳偏置有选择性地应用于操作任务的不同阶段或层面。

Result: 实验验证了新提出的接触感知分层框架有效地解决了接触丰富操作任务中的性能退化问题，相较于传统方法在操作任务上表现更稳健、更具扩展性。

Conclusion: 在接触丰富的操作任务中，简单应用现有的物理启发式目标条件强化学习（Pi-GCRL）方法会导致性能下降。通过引入接触感知和分层公式，可以有效地在操作问题中选择性地应用物理启发式归纳偏置，从而提升在复杂接触环境下的目标达成能力。

Abstract: Learning to reach arbitrary goals from sparse feedback requires agents to infer a rich notion of reachability across state--goal pairs. Goal-conditioned reinforcement learning (GCRL) tackles this challenge by learning policies that generalize across goals, but this generalization becomes increasingly difficult as the underlying dynamics become high-dimensional, hybrid, or contact-dependent. To address this issue, physics-informed GCRL (Pi-GCRL) introduces optimal-control-inspired inductive biases into goal-conditioned value learning. While Pi-GCRL methods have proven effective in navigation and object-free goal-reaching domains, their reliability in contact-rich tasks remains unclear, where contact interactions induce hybrid dynamics, mode-dependent controllability, and nonsmooth value landscapes. In this work, we show that these structural properties can cause existing Pi-GCRL methods to degrade when applied naively to contact-rich manipulation. Motivated by this analysis, we introduce contact-aware and hierarchical formulations that apply physics-informed inductive biases selectively across the manipulation problem. Our results provide a principled step toward extending Pi-GCRL to contact-rich manipulation.

</details>


### [258] [VLM-GLoc: Vision-Language Model Enhanced Monte Carlo Localization for Robust Semantic Global Localization in Cluttered Quasi-Static Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30506)
*Shivendra Agrawal,Bradley Hayes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了VLM-GLoc，利用视觉语言模型（VLM）的语义感知能力进行蒙特卡洛定位，成功解决了重复室内环境中的全局定位歧义难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在超市、办公室等具有高度几何相似性（即“几何别名”）和长尾语义分布的室内环境中，传统几何定位方法表现不佳，且特定领域的视觉方案难以应对视觉杂乱和动态变化。

Method: 提出了VLM-GLoc，这是一种分层语义蒙特卡洛定位（MCL）方法：1. 将VLM作为统一的语义观测前端；2. 利用VLM提取文本特征、过滤噪声并进行永久性推理；3. 设计了一种通过文本到地图检索来播种粒子的逆向语义提案机制。

Result: 在3,500平方英尺的超市和3,700平方英尺的实验室中进行了实验，分别达到了70%和74%的全局定位成功率，大幅优于传统的纯几何方法及领域特定基准。

Conclusion: VLM-GLoc通过引入视觉语言模型（VLM）作为语义观测前端，有效解决了复杂环境中的几何外观相似性问题，验证了多模态大模型在提升机器人全局定位鲁棒性方面的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Global localization in geometrically aliased, quasi-static environments such as grocery stores, offices, schools, and hospitals poses a significant challenge for mobile robots. Grocery stores with parallel aisles and a long tailed distribution of products, as well as offices and labs with repetitive furniture such as chairs, desks, monitors, and doors, exemplify common indoor environments that present geometric and even semantic ambiguity. Traditional approaches rely either on distinct geometric features or on domain-specific vision pipelines that struggle with long-tail semantic distributions and transient visual clutter. We present VLM-GLoc, a method for hierarchical semantic Monte Carlo Localization (MCL) that leverages open-vocabulary Vision-Language Models (VLMs) as a unified semantic observation front-end. We hypothesize a three-fold benefit from VLMs: (1) extracting highly discriminative rich text features, (2) implicit quality filtering of blurry or dynamic objects, and (3) permanence reasoning for targeted data augmentation. We introduce an inverse semantic proposal mechanism that seeds particles via text-to-map retrieval. Evaluated across two real-world environments with different characteristics and two different platforms: a 3,500 sq. ft. grocery store with a cellphone and a 3,700 sq. ft. lab space with a quadruped, VLM-GLoc achieves 70% and 74% global localization success respectively, substantially outperforming traditional geometry-only and domain-specific baselines.

</details>


### [259] [ARISTO Hand: Sensing-Driven Distal Hyperextension for Fine-Grained Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30508)
*Aaron Kim,Dong Ho Kang,Mark Helwig,Mingyo Seo,Kazuto Yokoyama,Tetsuya Narita,Luis Sentis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 为了解决机械手抓取薄物体的痛点，ARISTO Hand通过引入主动远端过伸展机构与混合触觉传感器架构，大幅提升了薄物体的抓取力度与操作感知精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的拟人化机械手在处理薄物体时，往往因缺乏精准的接触几何控制和可靠的力感知能力，难以完成精密操作任务。

Method: 1. 硬件设计：研发具备主动远端过伸展（Active Distal Hyperextension）功能的腱驱动机械手；2. 传感器集成：采用混合触觉感知架构（硬质指甲端力矩传感器+软质电容式触觉阵列）；3. 验证实验：通过力学性能定量表征及SD卡拔插任务进行验证。

Result: 1. 实现超过标准屈曲运动学的指尖接触控制；2. 针对1-20mm厚度的物体，拔出力提升了2.76倍；3. 解决在边缘接触时本体感受力估计失效的问题，提高了感知可靠性。

Conclusion: ARISTO Hand通过集成主动远端过伸展和混合触觉感知，显著提升了机械手在处理极薄物体时的抓取能力和感知精度，为精密操作任务提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Manipulating thin objects requires precise contact geometry and reliable force perception, yet many anthropomorphic robotic hands lack the mechanical and sensing capabilities needed for such interactions. We present the ARISTO Hand, a tendon-driven robotic hand that integrates active distal hyperextension with a hybrid fingertip-sensing architecture that combines a rigid, nail-mounted force-torque sensor and a soft capacitive tactile array. Active hyperextension enables controlled fingertip engagement beyond the kinematic limits of standard flexion, increasing pull-out force by 2.76x for object thicknesses of 1-20 mm while preserving the nominal grasp capability. The rigid nail-mounted sensor provides reliable force measurements during edge contacts, where the sensitivity of proprioceptive force estimation degrades as the contact geometry approaches kinematic singularities. We validate the proposed architecture through quantitative force characterization and a multi-stage SD card extraction and insertion task. Video and supplementary materials are available at: https://aristohand.github.io

</details>


### [260] [Any-ttach: Quick End-effector Swapping Enables Manipulation Dexterity with Simplicity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30569)
*Weizhe Ni,Jinzhou Li,Haoyu Li,Cody Andres Alessio-Bunnell,Wenjing Pan,Xianyi Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Any-ttach框架通过引入快速自动更换末端执行器机制，证明了机器人可以通过更换专用工具而非复杂机械手来以更简单的方式实现复杂的操作灵巧性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究人员认为机器人操作灵巧性的传统研究方向（构建高自由度多指手）存在局限，强调了人类手部复杂性更多是为了工具使用和制造。受此启发，提出了Any-ttach框架，旨在通过快速更换末端执行器的方式实现简单的灵巧操作。

Method: 1. 设计了一种低成本的自动更换末端执行器接口；2. 开发了一套用于采集人类演示的手持设备；3. 构建了一个任务规划框架，用于组合学习到的、参数化的和规划好的工具使用技能。

Result: 1. 提高了工具更换的可靠性，提升了演示效率，降低了工具位姿偏差；2. 成功支持多种工具和末端模块；3. 在制作三明治和处理黄瓜的长程任务中，通过切换执行器连续执行了六种工具使用子技能。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种通过“工具中心化”范式替代“高自由度复杂手部设计”的新思路。证明了快速更换末端执行器（Any-ttach）能以更低成本和更简单的结构显著提升机器人的操作多样性和任务完成能力，为复杂长程操作任务提供了一种可行的替代方案。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation dexterity is often pursued by building increasingly complex high-DoF multifingered hands. While many robotic hands are designed to replicate human morphology, the functional role of human hands suggests a different perspective: much of their complexity may exist to enable tool use and tool making. This observation motivates Any-ttach, a tool-centric manipulation framework that treats quick end-effector swapping as a mechanism for dexterity with simplicity. Any-ttach combines a low-cost automatic swapping mechanism for an open-close robot interface, a handheld device for collecting human demonstrations, and a task planning framework that composes learned, parameterized, and planned tool-use skills. The system supports diverse tools and end-effector modules, including daily tools, articulated tools such as scissors, Fin Ray fingers, and a low-cost anthropomorphic hand, through the same shared interface. Our experiments show that Any-ttach improves tool-swapping reliability, increases demonstration efficiency, reduces tool-pose variability, and supports diverse tool-use skills. In two long-horizon tasks, making a sandwich and preparing a cucumber, Any-ttach executes six tool-use subskills through end-effector switching and execution monitoring. These results suggest that robots can expand manipulation capability not only through more complex end-effectors, but also through rapidly exchangeable tools and end-effector modules. More details and videos are available at https://any-ttach.github.io/.

</details>


### [261] [Caspar: CUDA Accelerator for Symbolic Programming with Adaptive Reordering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30583)
*Emil Martens,Aaron Miller,Matias Varnum,Annette Stahl*

Main category: cs.RO
