TL;DR: Caspar是一个链接Python符号化编程与C++ GPU高性能计算的库，通过自动生成CUDA内核简化了高性能非线性优化在机器人领域的应用，并在束调整任务中验证了其显著的速度和效率优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器人系统中，虽然GPU功能强大，但其开发门槛高且编程复杂。旨在解决高性能GPU并行计算与易用的符号化编程开发模式之间的脱节问题，实现优化算法的高效部署。

Method: 基于SymForce库，提出了一种将Python符号表达式自动映射并编译为高性能C++ CUDA内核的框架。通过符号微分自动生成非线性优化所需的雅可比矩阵计算，并提供了一个高效的非线性GPU求解器。

Result: 在BAL数据集上的束调整实验表明，Caspar的性能优于市面上的先进求解器，速度提升5至20倍，且内存占用更低，同时保持了相当的求解精度。

Conclusion: 通过符号化建模与高性能CUDA内核生成的自动化结合，Caspar证明了在机器人及计算机视觉优化任务（如束调整）中，能够显著提升计算效率。该方法不仅简化了GPU加速开发，且在保持精度的同时，在速度和内存利用率上超越了现有的先进求解器。

Abstract: We present Caspar, a library that makes the power of modern GPUs more accessible in robotics and provides a state-of-the-art nonlinear GPU solver that can be applied to a wide range of different optimization problems. Caspar bridges the gap between expressive symbolic programming in Python and high-performance GPU runtimes in C++ by automatically generating optimized CUDA kernels from symbolic expressions. Building on the SymForce library, users can easily define and combine symbolic expressions, including Lie group operations, to generate custom CUDA kernels. To use Caspar as a solver, users need only define the symbolic residual functions; Caspar then uses symbolic differentiation to generate the necessary GPU kernels and interfaces to perform nonlinear optimization.
  In this paper, we present the core components of Caspar and showcase its performance by performing bundle adjustment on the Bundle Adjustment in the Large (BAL) dataset. We benchmark Caspar against other state-of-the-art bundle adjusters and show that it is 5 to 20 times faster than the best alternative, requires less memory, and achieves similar accuracy. This illustrates the benefit of our symbolic GPU programming approach. Caspar is released as part of SymForce and is freely available at https://github.com/symforce-org/symforce

</details>


### [262] [Exploiting Chordal Sparsity for Globally Optimal Estimation with Factor Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30617)
*Avinash Subramanian,Connor Holmes,Timothy D. Barfoot,Frank Dellaert,Frederike Dümbgen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种将凸松弛（SDP）自动集成进GTSAM的方法，并通过贝叶斯树分解实现对复杂状态估计问题的快速全局求解，解决了传统求解器易陷入局部最优的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人状态估计依赖于因子图，但主流局部求解器易陷入局部极小值；现有全局求解器（凸松弛）虽然性能稳健，但面临建模复杂度高和计算开销大的问题。

Method: 1. 在GTSAM框架内开发了一种自动生成凸SDP松弛的程序，适用于常见因子和变量类型。 2. 利用GTSAM原生的贝叶斯树（Bayes tree）结构对SDP问题进行分解，利用弦图稀疏性显著加速求解过程。

Result: 在3D位姿图SLAM（环形拓扑）和2D定位（链式拓扑）任务中，该方法表现出优秀的计算扩展性，实现了兼顾全局最优性与计算效率的平衡。

Conclusion: 该研究成功地将凸松弛（SDP）集成到现有的GTSAM框架中，通过利用贝叶斯树分解技术，在保持全局最优性保证的同时，显著降低了计算复杂度，为解决机器人领域状态估计中的局部极小值问题提供了高效的实用工具。

Abstract: Robust and efficient state estimation is crucial for perception, navigation, and control in robotics. State estimation problems are conveniently modeled using the factor-graph framework as enabled by modern software packages such as GTSAM or g2o. However, the standard solvers included in such frameworks are local and may converge to poor local minima, posing significant safety concerns. Conversely, techniques based on convex relaxations have been shown to provide a means of globally solving or certifying many state estimation problems. However, these relaxations 1) often require substantial effort to formulate, and 2) may incur significantly higher cost compared to efficient local solvers, as they require solving a large semidefinite program (SDP). In this work, we address both shortcomings by 1) creating a new procedure within the GTSAM framework for automatically constructing convex SDP relaxations for any factor graphs with common factor and variable types, and by 2) exploiting the Bayes tree constructions native to GTSAM to decompose the SDP problem, leading to significant speedup in solver time for chordally sparse problems. We demonstrate the favorable scaling of this structure-exploiting global estimator compared to standard local solvers for two case studies: A 3D pose-graph SLAM problem with a ring factor graph and a 2D localization problem with a chain factor graph. The software framework is available at https://github.com/borglab/gtsam.

</details>


### [263] [Bidirectional Incremental Generalized Hybrid A*](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30647)
*Sidharth Talia,Oren Salzman,Siddhartha Srinivasa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对高维动力学系统，通过将双向搜索引入增量广义混合 A* (IGHA*)，Bi-IGHA* 有效解决了多分辨率搜索中节点冻结导致的解被掩盖问题，大幅降低了计算扩展需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对复杂动力学系统在非结构化环境中运动规划的“维度灾难”问题；现有方法（如 IGHA*）在多分辨率搜索中存在“冻结节点可能会遮蔽潜在最优解”的缺陷，需要通过双向搜索来规避该限制。

Method: 提出了双向增量广义混合 A*（Bi-IGHA*）算法。它将 Bi-Directional Search 引入 IGHA* 框架中，在多分辨率层次结构下通过双向扩展来减少搜索深度，并配合节点冻结机制实现任意时刻（anytime）规划。

Result: 实验证明 Bi-IGHA* 在 R3、R4 和 R6 规划问题维度上大幅减少了节点扩展次数，在高机动越野自动驾驶场景下，在保持闭环控制性能的同时显著降低了计算开销。

Conclusion: Bi-IGHA* 成功在保持渐近最优特性的同时，通过双向搜索机制缓解了 IGHA* 中冻结节点导致解空间被遮蔽的问题，显著提升了高维 kinodynamic 规划的效率。

Abstract: We focus on the problem of efficient anytime kinodynamic planning for systems with complex dynamics in unstructured environments that make precomputing motion primitives infeasible. Directly applying A* to such problems is computationally infeasible due to the curse of dimensionality. Methods such as Hybrid A* addressed this burden by discretizing the state space, but in turn creating a coupling between tree discovery and the discretization resolution. The Incremental Generalized Hybrid A* (IGHA*) performs search over a hierarchy of resolutions in an anytime fashion to break this coupling, by freezing vertices to use in later search iterations rather than pruning them. However, the frozen vertices can hide solution-supporting vertices from the search at a particular iteration. While classical bidirectional search is motivated by the reduction of search depth, extending IGHA* into the bidirectional setting (termed Bi-IGHA*) obtains additional benefit by fundamentally mitigating the behaviour induced by frozen vertices hiding solutions. We show that Bi-IGHA* preserves IGHA*'s guarantees on monotonic cost improvement and termination. We empirically show that Bi-IGHA* substantially reduces expansions on R3, R4, and R6 planning problems, and achieves equivalent closed-loop performance with kinodynamic planning for high-speed off-road autonomy while requiring significantly fewer expansions. Website: https://personalrobotics.github.io/IGHAStar/biighastar.html

</details>


### [264] [Primitive Subspaces Mediate Few-Shot Transfer in VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30695)
*Anya Singh,Cabrel Happi,Jai Relan,Varun Nair,Vidyut Baradwaj*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究提出通过基元感知训练构建可迁移的子技能库，显著提升了VLA模型在工业任务中的少样本迁移效率，并从因果角度验证了基元表现对泛化的决定性作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在工业环境下部署成本高昂，因每个新任务均需进行微调。研究旨在通过基元感知训练（primitive-aware training）让模型习得可组合的子技能库，以实现无需微调的低成本、高效任务泛化。

Method: 1. 架构选择：在REASSEMBLE与LIBERO-Long数据集上对比OpenVLA和π0.5两种架构。
2. 训练策略：对比“扁平化训练（flat trajectories）”与“基元分割训练（primitive-segmented episodes）”及对应的基元特定语言提示。
3. 评估范式：通过5种不同数量（m=0, 1, 3, 5, 10）的示范样本进行少样本迁移测试，且不进行权重更新。
4. 因果验证：对隐藏状态的“基元可解码子空间”进行消融实验。
5. 纠偏：修正了分块策略（chunked policies）在单步动作范围门控（action-range gates）评估中的统计偏差。

Result: 1. 高效性：基元训练模型仅需3条示范即可达到基准训练模型10条示范的性能（3倍效率提升）。
2. 可迁移性：在持有任务（held-out tasks）上，模型展示了强劲的少样本迁移能力。
3. 机理证明：消融实验显示，移除基元表征子空间导致少样本迁移能力下降32个百分点，证实了其因果必要性。
4. 方法论贡献：识别并解决了分块策略评估中因“簇内家族误差膨胀”导致的错误失败率虚高问题。

Conclusion: 基于基元（primitive）的训练能够诱导出可迁移的子技能库，从而显著提升VLA策略的少样本泛化性能。该方法在无需微调的情况下，实现了比传统扁平化训练高出3倍的样本效率，并证明了基元表征在实现任务迁移中的因果必要性。此外，研究还纠正了分块策略评估中的方法论偏差。

Abstract: Deploying vision-language-action (VLA) policies in industrial environments requires the ability to teach new tasks at low cost, a property current VLAs lack, since each new task requires fine-tuning. We investigate whether primitive-aware training produces a transferable artifact: a learned library of sub-skills that can be composed at inference time, conditioned on a small number of demonstrations, to perform tasks the policy was never trained on. We train two VLA architectures with different inductive biases, OpenVLA and $π_{0.5}$, on the REASSEMBLE contact-rich assembly dataset under matched LoRA fine-tuning recipes and locked hyperparameters, varying training between flat trajectories and primitive-segmented episodes with primitive-specific language prompts. We hold out 6 object-task combinations from training and evaluate few-shot transfer: models receive $m \in \{0, 1, 3, 5, 10\}$ demonstrations of a held-out task and attempt execution without weight updates. We replicate across three training seeds and validate on a second dataset (LIBERO-Long). Primitive-trained models reach 78% of fine-tuned upper-bound performance with only m=3 demonstrations, while flat-trained models require m=10 demonstrations to reach the same level -- a $3\times$ sample efficiency gap that replicates across seeds, architectures, and datasets. To establish causation, we ablate the primitive-decodable subspace of hidden states and show few-shot transfer degrades by 32 percentage points while ablating a random subspace of equal dimensionality has no effect, indicating primitive representations are causally necessary rather than incidentally correlated with transfer. We identify and correct a methodological pitfall in evaluating chunked policies: family-wise inflation of single-step action-range gates produces order-of-magnitude higher false-failure rates against ground-truth human demonstrations.

</details>


### [265] [GSAM: A Generalizable and Safe Robotic Framework for Articulated Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30740)
*Beichen Shao,Mengying Xie,Heng Su,Wanyi Zhang,Mingyan Li,Yan Ding,Fausto Giunchiglia,Chao Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为 GSAM 的机器人框架，通过视觉感知+VLM常识推理修正，结合交互约束规划，解决了机器人操作铰接物体时的泛化性差及安全性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理多样的铰接物体时泛化能力差，且末端执行器与把手交互过程中易产生破坏性碰撞，缺乏安全性。

Method: 1. 视觉感知器：获取物体的运动学参数。
2. VLM 精调重构器：利用思维链（CoT）常识推理修正原始感知结果。
3. 交互约束生成器：结合模型知识与避障策略，转化为规划约束。
4. 运动学感知规划器：对轨迹和姿态进行可达性验证。

Result: 在 5 个类别、50 个铰接任务中表现优异，相比最优基线，标准差降低 3.1%，操作成功率提升 36.0%。

Conclusion: GSAM 通过多模态推理与受限运动规划相结合，显著提升了机器人在多样化铰接物体操作中的泛化能力与安全性。

Abstract: Articulated object manipulation is a unique challenge for service robots. Existing methods employ end-to-end policy learning, visionmotion planning, and large-language/visual-language model (LLM/VLM), but often overlook the diversity of articulated objects and the complexity of interactions between end-effector and handle, leading to limited generalization and destructive collisions. To address this, we propose GSAM, a generalizable and safe robotic framework for articulated object manipulation. Specifically, a vision-based perceiver generates the kinematic parameters. Considering that pre-trained markers in perceiver yield raw estimations that may deviate from commonsense, we present a f ine-tuned VLM-based refiner, using chain-of-thought (COT) commonsense reasoning to refine perception. To prevent destructive collisions, we design an interaction constraint function generator, integrating articulated object, interaction pose, and obstacle avoidance knowledge into a base. LLM then functionalize these constraints and apply them to trajectory and posture planning. A kinematic-aware manipulation planner verifies reachability for trajectory and posture. Experiments on 50 hinge tasks across 5 object categories and 50 randomly initialized end-effectorhandle configurations show that GSAM reduces standard deviation by 3.1% and improves manipulation success rate by 36.0% compared to the best baseline, respectively demonstrating the superior object generalization and interaction safety of GSAM in practical scenarios.

</details>


### [266] [SSR: Scaling Surefooted and Symmetric Humanoid Traversal to the Open World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30770)
*Ruiqi Yu,Yiwen Wang,Yuan Hao,Jun WU,Qiuguo Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SSR框架，通过想象立足点引导、等变对称增强和多判别器运动先验，实现了人形机器人在非均匀地形下的端到端、稳健、拟人化移动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人在人类环境中的实际部署受限于复杂、非均匀地形上的视觉稳定落脚点选择与协调全身运动控制的挑战。

Method: 提出SSR框架：1. 想象立足点引导（Imagined foothold guidance）：建模摆动足接触并评估支持稳定性以优化落脚点；2. 等变潜在空间对称增强（Equivariant latent-space symmetry augmentation）：促进高维视觉下的双侧协同；3. 地形特异性多判别器运动先验（Terrain-specific multi-discriminator motion priors）：确保运动的人性化。

Result: 实验表明，SSR在多样的现实世界地形（包括台阶、缝隙、高平台）中表现出安全、稳定且高质量的移动能力，并支持长距离户外导航。

Conclusion: SSR框架成功实现了人形机器人在开放世界中基于视觉的稳健、自然且长距离的移动能力，证明了端到端学习在处理复杂地形（如台阶、缝隙、高平台）时的有效性。

Abstract: Extending humanoid traversal to the open world is key to practical deployment in human environments, but remains challenging. The robot must use vision to ensure safe and reliable foot placement on heterogeneous terrain under highly dynamic motion, while producing coordinated, natural whole-body behaviors. We propose SSR, an efficient end-to-end framework for egocentric vision-based humanoid traversal that jointly learns these capabilities. SSR introduces imagined foothold guidance, which learns to model forthcoming swing-foot contacts and evaluates their support to guide pre-touchdown swings toward stable regions, reducing edge slips. It further employs equivariant latent-space symmetry augmentation to efficiently induce bilateral coordination under high-dimensional visual observations, and uses terrain-specific multi-discriminator motion priors to encourage human-like behavior across scenes. Extensive experiments show that SSR achieves safe, stable, and high-quality locomotion on diverse real-world terrains, including stairs with varied structures and extreme challenges such as wide gaps and high platforms, while enabling reliable long-horizon traversal in open outdoor environments.

</details>


### [267] [Object-Informed Model Predictive Path Integral Control for Non-Prehensile Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30778)
*Nikola Raicevic,Bharath Raam Radhakrishnan,Chenbin Yu,Ki Myung Brian Lee,Nikolay Atanasov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分层的MPPI控制策略，通过预先规划对象轨迹来引导机器人进行长跨度、避障式非抓取操作，显著提升了操作成功率与响应速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长跨度非抓取式机器人操作存在欠驱动和接触不连续性带来的规划难题，传统MPPI难以在复杂的长跨度任务中高效求解。

Method: 提出了一种分层模型预测路径积分（MPPI）控制方法：首先解决一个简化的假设对象可直接驱动的问题以获取参考轨迹，随后在联合机器人-对象状态空间中求解控制问题，并以该参考轨迹作为指导。

Result: 在仿真实验中，任务成功率提升了40%，控制频率提升了26%；在真实硬件实验中，成功率提升了20%，且计算开销与标准MPPI相当。

Conclusion: 提出了一种分层MPPI控制框架，证明了利用简化的对象级规划来引导机器人级规划能显著提高非抓取操作在长跨度、避障任务中的成功率与计算效率。

Abstract: Long-horizon planning for non-prehensile robot manipulation is challenging due to underactuated and discontinuous interactions. We propose a hierarchical formulation of model predictive path integral (MPPI) control that guides robot-level planning with a separately computed object-level plan to achieve efficient long-horizon prediction. We first solve a simplified object-only problem, assuming the object can be actuated directly, and use the planned object trajectory as a reference in solving the joint robot-object planning problem. We evaluate our method in both simulation and hardware using a 6-DoF xArm6 manipulator to perform object pushing tasks in which the target object must reach a goal while avoiding static obstacles, necessitating non-myopic reasoning. Our object-informed MPPI increases task success by 40\% with a 26\% faster control frequency in simulation, and by 20\% in real experiments with similar computation as regular MPPI.

</details>


### [268] [Two Degree-of-Freedom Vibratory Transport in a Grasp](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30780)
*C. L. Yako,Shenli Yuan,Kenneth Salisbury*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用受控非对称振动实现机器人夹爪内物体2-DoF操控的方法，并通过实验验证了通过调节震动波形可实现物体在夹持状态下的平移与旋转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决传统机器人抓取系统中，实现被抓取物体在夹爪内部进行细微位置和姿态调整的难题，利用振动动力学通过非对称波形实现对工件的操控。

Method: 1. 提出利用闭环伺服控制移动表面产生非对称“粘-滑”震动波形；2. 建立动力学模型分析粘附与滑动加速度对平均运动速度的影响；3. 设计双自由度振动表面（平移+平面内旋转），结合平行夹爪实验系统，通过实验验证理论趋势并实现物体的多自由度操控。

Result: 解析模型准确地描述了振动参数与物体平均运动速度之间的趋势；实验证明了该方案在平行夹爪配置下能有效对多种物体进行双向移动与旋转，且平移的物理规律同样适用于平面内旋转。

Conclusion: 非对称振动可作为实现受控、多自由度（2-DoF）抓取物体操控的有效手段，通过调节粘附与滑动加速度参数，可精确控制物体在重力场下的移动与旋转。

Abstract: In this paper, we use asymmetric vibrations to demonstrate two degree-of-freedom (DoF) in-hand manipulation of grasped parts. The asymmetric vibrations are achieved through closed-loop position control of a moving surface, which applies a periodic stick-slip waveform to the part to be manipulated. We show analytically how two vibratory waveform parameters, the sticking acceleration and the slipping acceleration, affect average part velocity when moving against gravity. The theoretical trends are then validated using an experimental setup where the squeeze force is controlled and part motion is recorded by a high-resolution encoder. We also develop a 2-DoF vibratory surface capable of translation in one direction and rotation about the surface normal. Using two of these 2-DoF surfaces in a parallel jaw gripper configuration, we bidirectionally translate and rotate a variety of grasped parts, as well as demonstrate that the same waveform trends for translation also persist for in-plane rotation.

</details>


### [269] [Feat2Go: Visual Feature-Grounded Value Estimation for Embodied Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30795)
*Junyang Shu,Zhiwei Lin,Bingqing Wei,Yongtao Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Feat2Go 引入了一种细粒度价值估计框架，利用视觉世界模型自动生成结构化的进度奖励，解决了 VLA 模型在长周期具身任务中奖励稀疏和设计困难的问题，显著提升了任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决具身强化学习中长程操作任务存在的稀疏监督问题，以及传统手工设计奖励函数过于困难的问题，旨在通过更细粒度的方法提升视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的性能。

Method: 1. 从预训练视觉世界模型中提取逐块（patch-level）相似度，通过基于趋势的聚类将片段划分为语义阶段，从而生成连续的进度目标；2. 训练 embodied value model（具身价值模型）根据当前观察和指令预测该结构化进度；3. 利用预测的价值函数重塑策略优化过程中的终端奖励。

Result: 在 ManiSkill3 数据集上，将 OpenVLAOFT 的分布外成功率从 17.5% 提升至 82.9%，并保持了 96.9% 的分布内表现；在 RoboTwin 2.0 的域随机化任务中达到 88.8% 的成功率，优于现有方法。

Conclusion: Feat2Go 是一种用于具身强化学习的细粒度价值估计框架，通过利用预训练视觉世界模型提取的结构化进度作为引导，显著提升了 VLA 模型在长程操作任务中的性能，且无需繁琐的手动奖励工程。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning is a promising approach for improving the capabilities of vision-language-action (VLA) models while avoiding the heavy data requirements of imitation learning. However, its effectiveness for VLA models is often constrained by sparse supervision and the difficulty of designing informative reward signals for long-horizon manipulation. In this work, we present Feat2Go, a fine-grained value estimation framework for embodied reinforcement learning. Specifically, Feat2Go first derives a continuous progress target from a pretrained visual world model by measuring patch-level similarity to subgoal states and partitioning episodes into semantic stages with trend-based clustering. We then train an embodied value model to predict this structural progress from the current observation and task instruction, and use the predicted value to reshape terminal rewards during policy optimization. The proposed framework is compatible with existing VLA policy reinforcement learning pipelines, including PPO and GRPO, and does not rely on manual reward engineering. Extensive experiments on ManiSkill3 and RoboTwin 2.0 demonstrate that Feat2Go consistently improves the performance of existing VLA models under both single-arm and bimanual manipulation settings. More specifically, on ManiSkill3, Feat2Go improves OpenVLAOFT from 17.5% to 82.9% average out-of-distribution success while retaining 96.9% in-distribution performance. On RoboTwin 2.0, Feat2Go achieves an average success rate of 88.8% in domain-randomized task settings, outperforming prior reinforcement learning methods.

</details>


### [270] [Hide-and-Seek in Trajectories: Discovering Failure Signals for VLA Runtime Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30834)
*Seongheon Park,Wendi Li,Changdae Oh,Samuel Yeh,Zsolt Kira,Michael Hagenow,Sharon Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对 VLA 机器人模型存在执行故障的问题，Hide-and-Seek 框架通过轨迹级的粗监督对比学习，在无需人工逐帧标注的情况下，有效实现了故障动作的精准检测与定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的视觉-语言-动作 (VLA) 模型在复杂环境下易出现执行故障，而现有检测方法要么计算昂贵，要么因简单的标签传播机制导致缺乏时序局部性，难以精准定位故障时刻。

Method: 提出“Hide-and-Seek”学习框架，将故障检测建模为粗监督学习问题；结合轨迹间和轨迹内的对比学习目标，从轨迹级标签中显式地挖掘并定位具有故障特征的动作序列。

Result: 在 LIBERO、VLABench 及真实机器人平台上显著优于现有方法，在保持高故障检测准确率的同时，实现了更优的响应时效性，且具备良好的任务泛化能力。

Conclusion: Hide-and-Seek 框架证明了无需逐帧标注，仅依靠轨迹级监督即可实现高效、精准的机器人执行故障检测，为具身智能系统的稳健部署提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models enable robots to follow natural language instructions and generalize across diverse tasks, but they remain vulnerable to execution failures that compromise reliability in real-world deployment. Detecting such failures during execution is therefore critical for the robust deployment of embodied systems. Existing failure detection methods either rely on expensive action resampling or external models, while alternatives propagate trajectory-level labels uniformly across every timestep, obscuring localized failure signals. In this paper, we propose \textbf{Hide-and-Seek}, a framework that formulates VLA failure detection as a coarsely supervised learning problem. By combining inter-trajectory and intra-trajectory contrastive objectives, Hide-and-Seek localizes failure-indicative actions and induces temporally structured failure signals from trajectory-level supervision alone, without any step-level annotation. We evaluate Hide-and-Seek on LIBERO, VLABench, and a real-world robotic platform across three representative VLA policies: OpenVLA, $π_0$, and $π_{0.5}$.Our method achieves state-of-the-art multi-task failure detection performance with a practical accuracy--timeliness trade-off under conformal prediction, and generalizes well to both seen and unseen tasks.

</details>


### [271] [High-Load-Density Electro-Permanent Magnetic Foot with Controllable Adhesion for Quadruped Wall-Climbing Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30849)
*An Li,Bo Tao,I-Ming Chen,Han Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于环形Halbach阵列的高载荷密度电永磁足端，通过优化磁路与闭环控制，实现了四足机器人在各类铁磁表面上的可靠吸附与稳定攀爬。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决四足机器人在铁磁表面攀爬时，对空气间隙敏感、吸附力不足及复杂表面适应性差的问题，旨在提高攀爬的安全性和高载荷能力。

Method: 设计了环形Halbach阵列电永磁（CHN-EPM）单元，利用三维磁路与磁通集中效应；开发了含两级脉冲电流控制的磁化驱动系统；结合柔性压力传感器实现力反馈与状态监测。

Result: CHN-EPM最大吸附力超过1000N，负载自重比超过200:1；在Unitree GO2机器人上验证了其在天花板、垂直墙面及涂漆、穿孔、弯曲等复杂表面上的稳定攀爬能力。

Conclusion: 该新型电永磁足端结构通过优化磁路设计与电流控制策略，实现了高载荷密度、可靠的磁吸附与脱附，显著提升了四足机器人在复杂铁磁环境下的攀爬适应能力。

Abstract: To enable reliable climbing locomotion of quadruped robots on ferromagnetic surfaces, this paper presents a high-load-density electro-permanent magnetic foot with controllable adhesion, featuring force-feedback circular Halbach-net electro-permanent magnet (CHN-EPM) adhesion units and a magnetization control system. Due to its three-dimensional magnetic circuit structure and flux-concentration effect, the CHN-EPM enables a distributed parallel magnetic flux path with enhanced flux utilization, resulting in reduced sensitivity to air-gap variations and allowing effective adhesion to be maintained even under partial contact conditions. The proposed CHN-EPM generates a maximum adhesion force exceeding 1000 N with a load-to-weight ratio over 200:1. A magnetization driver and a two-stage pulse current control strategy are developed to regulate the excitation current amplitude and duration, enabling accurate and reliable magnetization. By incorporating a flexible pressure sensor for contact force feedback, the system can effectively monitor attachment and detachment states, ensuring robust adhesion switching under uncertain contact conditions. The proposed system is integrated into a commercial quadruped robot (Unitree GO2), demonstrating high-load adhesion on ceiling and vertical-wall surfaces and stable locomotion on painted, perforated, and curved ferromagnetic surfaces.

</details>


### [272] [Wall-OSS-0.5 Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30877)
*Ryan Yu,Pushi Zhang,Starrick Liu,Brae Liu,Miracle Kang,Shalfun Li,Lights Shi,Ellie Ma,Ping Yang,Chris Pan,Jerry Chen,Dongxiu Liu,Rain Sun,Miles Guo,Byron Zhang,Hugo Zhou,Zach Xu,Vincent Chen,Harrison Huang,James Wang,Dance Kuzi,Andy Zhai,Hang Su,Roy Gan,Lucy Liang,Hao Wang,Qian Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了开源VLA模型Wall-OSS-0.5，证实了通过特定预训练策略获得的VLA模型在无需微调时即具备零样本机器人操作能力，且在微调后性能优于现有先进模型，从而重新定义了VLA预训练的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA研究通常只报告微调后的指标，导致一个核心问题悬而未决：VLA预训练本身是否直接赋予了模型机器人操作能力，还是仅仅提供了一个更好的初始化权重？研究旨在证实并衡量预训练即具备的通用机器人操作能力。

Method: 提出了4B参数的开源模型Wall-OSS-0.5，基于3B VLM骨干网并增加动作生成组件；采用“梯度桥接联合训练”（gradient-bridged co-training）策略，结合离散动作预测（利用VLM固有梯度）、多模态预测（维持语言理解）和连续流匹配（用于部署接口）。预训练涵盖20多种机器人形态、百万级轨迹及多模态数据。

Result: 在零样本（Zero-shot）阶段，模型在17个任务套件中展现出非凡的物理真实机器人行为。在微调后，在15个真实机器人任务上平均任务进度达到60.5%，比π_0.5提升了17.5%，且证实了动作训练未损耗模型原有的多模态视觉语言能力。

Conclusion: VLA预训练不仅仅是下游策略学习的初始化手段，更是一种可以直接部署、具备可观测机器人能力的通用方法。预训练模型在无需微调的情况下即展现出非凡的零样本执行能力，并能作为更优的基座提升微调后的性能。

Abstract: Large-scale Vision-Language-Action (VLA) pretraining is increasingly adopted as the foundation for robot policies, yet the evidence for pretrained VLAs is almost invariably reported after task-specific fine-tuning.This leaves a foundational question unanswered: does VLA pretraining itself yield executable robot behavior, or does it merely furnish a better initialization for downstream policy learning? We present Wall-OSS-0.5, an open-source 4B VLA built upon a 3B VLM backbone augmented with action-generation components, designed so that pretrained robotic capability is directly measurable on physical hardware.The model is pretrained across more than 20 embodiments, processing over one million robot trajectories per epoch alongside a grounded multimodal corpus. We adopt a gradient-bridged co-training recipe in which three objectives play distinct and complementary roles: discrete action prediction routes strong VLM-native gradients into the backbone, multimodal prediction preserves grounded vision-language understanding, and continuous flow matching serves as the deployment-time action interface. Before task-specific fine-tuning, the pretrained checkpoint achieves non-trivial zero-shot real-robot behavior, completing several tasks, including a held-out deformable manipulation task, at high task progress on a 17-task suite. After fine-tuning, the same checkpoint serves as a stronger adaptation prior, reaching 60.5% average task progress on 15 real-robot tasks and outperforming π_0.5 by 17.5%. Multimodal evaluations further confirm that action training does not erode grounded vision-language competence: the model preserves broad vision-language ability while strengthening embodied grounding. Together, these results reposition VLA pretraining from an initialization strategy to a directly testable, already useful source of robot capability.

</details>


### [273] [Trajectory Planning for Non-Communicating Mobile Robots using Inverse Optimal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30906)
*Nina Majer,Yannick Epple,Xin Ye,Stefan Schwab,Sören Hohmann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合逆最优控制和联合预测的移动机器人避障算法，通过准确推测非通信机器人的行为以提升协作效率，实验表明其比传统方法速度更快且解的稳定性极高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决非通信移动机器人在碰撞避免场景下互动效率低的问题，即如何准确推测对方意图并进行协同避障。

Method: 提出了一种结合轨迹规划与预测的新型算法。核心包括：利用逆最优控制（Inverse Optimal Control）根据观测轨迹估计其他机器人的目标状态；采用“换位思考”策略，使每个机器人在考虑自身预测的同时，通过联合预测问题来推演其他机器人的行为；最终将这些预测结果整合进避障规划中。

Result: 在包含2至8个机器人的仿真场景中，该算法使所有机器人到达目标点的中位时间缩短了9.8%；同时，算法具有极高的可靠性，在所有实验中均能顺利完成规划与预测任务，没有出现求解失败的情况。

Conclusion: 该算法在多机器人避障场景中表现出了高效性和鲁棒性，相比于基于恒定加速度的预测方法，在时间效率上提升了9.8%，且在所有测试场景下均能成功求解，未出现无解情况。

Abstract: To enable an efficient interaction of non-communicating mobile robots in collision avoidance scenarios, we present a novel combined trajectory planning and prediction algorithm. Inverse optimal control is used to estimate unknown goal states of all robots based on observed past trajectories. Each robot also takes the perspective of other robots in considering self-prediction and solves a joint prediction problem using the estimated goal states. The resulting predictions are then considered for planning. Simulation results of scenarios with 2-8 robots show that the median of the durations until all vehicles reach their goals is 9.8 % faster compared to planning with constant acceleration based estimated goal states. Moreover, the proposed approach never leads to the solver being unable to find a solution to the planning or prediction problem.

</details>


### [274] [Enhancing Human-Likeness in Reinforcement Learning Agents via Hierarchical Macro Action Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30928)
*Usman Nizamani,M. Shaheer Luqman,Fawad Javed Fateh,Ali Shah Ali,Murad Popattia,M. Zeeshan Zia,Quoc-Huy Tran*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出的HiMAQ框架通过两层分层矢量量化策略，将人类行为演示编码进强化学习智能体，在保证任务成功率的基础上，实现了更具可解释性和行为人类化的决策模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的强化学习智能体往往过于追求奖励最大化，导致其行为模式与人类表现差异较大，从而限制了模型的可解释性和可靠性。因此，需要一种既能保持高性能，又能产生类人行为的框架。

Method: 提出了一种层级宏动作量化方法（HiMAQ），包含两级连续的矢量量化：底层将输入动作映射为细粒度的子动作簇，高层将这些子动作簇进一步聚合成宏动作簇，从而对人类演示数据进行编码以约束智能体行为。

Result: 在D4RL基准测试中，HiMAQ的表现优于非层级基线（MAQ），在保持与主流RL算法（IQL, SAC, RLPD）兼容并维持高成功率的同时，显著提升了人类相似度评分。

Conclusion: HiMAQ 框架通过分层矢量量化成功地在保持高任务奖励的同时，赋予了强化学习智能体更接近人类的行为模式，且具有良好的算法通用性。

Abstract: Human-like agents are a long-standing goal of artificial intelligence. Despite strong performance, most reinforcement learning (RL) agents remain reward-driven and often exhibit behaviors that differ from humans, limiting interpretability and reliability. In this work, we introduce a novel human-like RL framework that predicts action sequences closely aligned with human behaviors while maximizing rewards. Specifically, we encode human demonstrations into macro actions using a hierarchical macro action quantization approach (termed HiMAQ) consisting of two successive levels of vector quantization. The lower quantization level maps input actions to fine-grained subaction clusters, while the higher quantization level aggregates these subaction clusters into action clusters. Extensive evaluations on the D4RL benchmarks show that our hierarchical approach outperforms the non-hierarchical baseline (MAQ), achieving better human-likeness scores while maintaining comparable or better success rates than previous RL agents. The improvements generalize across integrations with various RL algorithms, namely IQL, SAC, and RLPD.

</details>


### [275] [RDGen: Demonstration Generation for High-Quality Robot Learning via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30957)
*Zijian Zhu,Menglin Zou,Zhuang Li,Yaojie Tu,Xinhai Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RDGen通过使用强化学习生成的自动化、高质量轨迹来替代昂贵的人类遥操作数据，从而突破了VLA模型数据供给的瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLA模型受限于高质量机器人轨迹数据的匮乏，而基于人类遥操作的数据采集方式高昂且难以扩展。

Method: 提出了RDGen框架：利用基于VLM的任务解析器识别任务对象，结合Grounding DINO进行目标定位，并利用从模拟环境迁移至真实世界的RL策略生成结构化轨迹，过滤并收集高质量的演示数据用于VLA模型训练。

Result: 在抓取任务中，RDGen实现了高成功率，且生成的轨迹比人类遥操作更平滑，显著提升了下游VLA模型的性能。

Conclusion: RL生成的演示数据可以作为机器人策略学习更可靠、更一致的监督信号，有效克服了传统人类遥操作的成本和扩展性瓶颈。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have emerged as a promising paradigm for general-purpose robot control. However, their performance remains fundamentally constrained by the availability of high-quality robot trajectory data. In current robot learning practice, such data are primarily collected through human teleoperation, which is labor-intensive, costly, and difficult to scale. In this paper, we propose RDGen, a sim-to-real reinforcement learning framework for generating high-quality robot demonstrations. Rather than employing reinforcement learning solely as the final control policy, RDGen leverages trained RL policies as a structured trajectory generator. The system consists of a VLM-based task parser that identifies task-relevant objects, a Grounding DINO-based object localizer, and an RL policy transferred from simulation to the real robot. Successful rollouts are then harvested as clean, high-quality demonstrations for downstream VLA training, while the simulation stage further provides a scalable source of additional trajectories at little marginal cost. Experiments on a pick-and-place task demonstrate that the transferred RL policy achieves a high task success rate. Compared with human teleoperation, RDGen produces significantly smoother trajectories and yields superior downstream VLA performance. These results indicate that RL-generated demonstrations can serve as more reliable and consistent supervisory signals for robot policy learning.

</details>


### [276] [A study on a Real-Time VR-Based Teleoperation Framework for Manipulator in Dynamic Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30989)
*InGyu Choi,GeonYeong Go,SunWoo Ahn,HyoJae Kang,Min-Sung Kang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文针对VR遥操作在复杂环境下的安全性缺陷，提出了一种集成实时避障与运动优化的框架，通过GPU加速实现了动态、实时且对非专家友好的机器人安全遥操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VR遥操作研究多侧重于收集模仿学习数据，缺乏对机器人实际部署中动态环境适应性、安全性以及防范操作失误的深入考量。

Method: 1. 基于VR接口的实时控制；2. 采用GPU加速的逆运动学（IK）求解；3. 结合轨迹优化算法，在机器人运动约束下生成合法的关节路径；4. 实现对静态和动态障碍物的避障处理。

Result: 通过7自由度机械臂实验验证，该方法在无障碍、静态障碍及动态障碍三种场景下均表现出稳定的在线操作性能，实现了操作者指令与碰撞感知路径生成的良好平衡。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种集成了实时避障功能的VR遥操作框架，通过GPU加速的运动学与轨迹优化技术，能够有效地在动态环境中保障操作安全，并能处理非专业操作带来的风险。

Abstract: Robot teleoperation enables safe, non-contact task execution in hazardous environments where direct human access is difficult, and its application has expanded with recent VR technologies. Many VR teleoperation studies, however, have primarily served as data-collection tools for robot imitation learning, so they often do not explicitly address dynamic obstacles, workspace changes, or collision risks during operation. For real deployment aimed at operator safety, teleoperation must react to dynamic situations with low latency and remain robust to mistakes made by inexperienced operators. This paper presents a VR teleoperation framework that supports real-time manipulation while handling collisions with both static and moving obstacles. The framework integrates GPU-accelerated inverse kinematics and trajectory optimization within a VR interface to generate feasible joint commands at each control cycle under robot constraints. Experiments with a 7-DoF manipulator demonstrate stable online behavior and collision-aware motion generation across three scenarios: obstacle-free, static-obstacle, and moving-obstacle environments. The results indicate that the proposed approach generates motion consistent with the operator's command while producing safe detours when obstacles interfere with the commanded path.

</details>


### [277] [Can Aerial VLA Models Cooperate? Evaluating Closed-Loop Air-Ground Coordination with CARLA-Air](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31066)
*Tianle Zeng,Yanci Wen,Xueang Yu,Hong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了空地协同评估环境 CARLA-Air，研究发现当前 aerial VLA 模型难以直接实现高效的空地协同，并指出实现该目标需要克服在伙伴状态理解、延迟控制与协同目标对齐方面的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 aerial VLA 模型在单机任务（如目标跟踪、导航）上表现出色，但其在需要紧密闭环交互的空地协同场景下的迁移能力尚不明确。

Method: 构建了CARLA-Air评估环境，通过统一CARLA与AirSim的运行时、物理时钟和传感流水线，实现了物理一致的空地交互；并在移动平台降落与遮挡恢复护送两个任务上，对现有的 aerial VLA 和规划基线模型进行了评估。

Result: 现有的 aerial VLA 模型可以将单机能力转化为协同行为的能力较弱；基于文本的提示（State prompting）效果有限，且简单的双向交互往往无法提升性能，甚至会放大误差。

Conclusion: 现有的视觉语言动作（VLA）模型虽然具备单机能力，但在零样本协同工作中表现不佳。要实现高质量的空地协同，未来研究需重点解决显式的伙伴状态建模、低延迟动作协调以及团队级目标对齐问题。

Abstract: Recent aerial vision-language-action (VLA) models show promising single-UAV capabilities, such as tracking moving objects and navigating to language-specified landmarks. However, it remains unclear whether these capabilities can transfer to air-ground cooperation, where a UAV and a UGV must act jointly in a shared, closed-loop physical world.
  We study this question with CARLA-Air, a single-process air-ground evaluation environment that unifies CARLA and AirSim inside one Unreal Engine runtime. By sharing the same world state, physics tick, and sensing pipeline, CARLA-Air enables physically consistent UAV--UGV interaction and precise measurement of simulation-timestamp alignment and effective coordination latency.
  Using CARLA-Air, we evaluate representative aerial VLA and planning baselines on two complementary diagnostic tasks: moving-platform landing and occlusion-recovery escort. The results show that current aerial VLA models can often track or follow a ground partner, but struggle to convert this single-agent competence into stable cooperative behavior. State prompting provides limited benefit, and naive bidirectional interaction fails to consistently improve performance and can amplify errors for most baselines. These findings suggest that, under the tested text-based cue interfaces, zero-shot cooperative air-ground VLA requires three components beyond the current paradigm: explicit partner-state grounding, low-latency action coordination, and team-level objective alignment. Our code is available at https://github.com/louiszengCN/CarlaAir.

</details>


### [278] [Seeing Fast and Slow: Bimodal 3D Scene Graphs for Open-set Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31067)
*Marcel Bartholomeus Prasetyo,Shrutika Vishal Thengane,A Manicka Praveen,Yi Loo,Malika Meghjani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了 BiMoSG，一种双模态 3D 场景图生成框架。它通过动态切换粗/细粒度表示，在保障开放集任务感知精度的同时，实现了比现有先进方法更快的推理速度，满足机器人实时部署需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人执行开放集任务时，需要根据任务需求和环境探索进程动态切换场景表示的粒度，以平衡计算效率与环境感知的详尽程度。

Method: 提出了 BiMoSG，一种双模态 3D 场景图生成方法：默认使用“快速”模式生成粗粒度场景图，并在识别到任务相关区域时切换至“慢速”模式生成精细的开放词汇 3D 场景图。

Result: 实验表明，BiMoSG 的生成速度显著优于现有的先进开源方法，并支持与机器人任务执行过程的实时集成。

Conclusion: BiMoSG 通过双模态架构在保持高性能的同时实现了实时任务执行，证明了动态调整场景图粒度对开放集环境下的机器人任务执行至关重要。

Abstract: Open-set task execution can significantly benefit from seamlessly switching between coarse and fine scene representations depending on the context and the evolving information as the robot explores the environment. For example, it is often sufficient to start with a coarse scene representation initially and only employ a finer, more granular scene representation when the robot encounters regions which are likely to contain the task relevant objects. Hence, in this work, we propose BiMoSG, a bimodal 3D scene graph generation approach for open-set tasks. BiMoSG employs a "fast" mode by default to efficiently generate a coarse 3D scene graph and can switch to a "slow" mode for generating a finer open vocabulary 3D scene graph of task relevant objects. We demonstrate that our proposed 3D scene graph generation approach is significantly faster than the open-source state-of-the-art approaches. This allows us to integrate the scene graph generation process with task execution for real-time deployment.

</details>


### [279] [Don't Fool Me Twice: Adapting to Adversity in the Wild with Experience-Driven Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31119)
*Navin Sriram Ravie,Andrew Jong,Krrish Jain,John Liu,Omar Alama,Bijo Sebastian,Sebastian Scherer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为“Don't Fool Me Twice”的框架，让移动机器人能够通过在线持续学习，结合语义分析与内核回归技术，主动识别并适应环境中那些未预见的、与具身交互相关的物理干扰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉语言模型虽能预测通用常识危险，但难以捕捉机器人与环境交互过程中特定于“具身（Embodiment）”的未知干扰。研究旨在通过在线学习，使自主移动机器人在“野外”非结构化环境中面对突发干扰时，能够进行因果归因并优化未来的规划能力。

Method: 提出一种名为“Don't Fool Me Twice”的持续学习框架。具体方法包括：使用视觉语言模型（VLM）结合视觉上下文对干扰进行语义因果推断；利用核回归（Kernel Regression）进行瞬态异常的少样本建模；通过语义体素建模评估认知不确定性，将干扰转化为可学习的空间行为以支撑后续的决策规划。

Result: 在模拟环境和真实硬件平台上进行了多机器人载体与多模式干扰实验。结果验证了框架在处理未知交互式风险时的有效性，显著提升了机器人对环境异常的适应能力和规划鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了基于语义的持续学习框架能够使移动机器人有效地识别、建模并适应未知的、依赖于具身交互的干扰，从而在非结构化环境中实现更鲁棒的自主操作。

Abstract: In robotics, dangers and adversity modes are often embodiment-specific and relative to each agent. A frontier of autonomous mobile robotics is to enable agents to operate effectively in the wild in unseen unstructured environments. A significant challenge in unseen unstructured environments is that it may not be possible to predict all the dangers to the specific robot. Although recent work has used large foundation vision-language models (VLMs) to preemptively predict an exhaustive list of common-sense dangers, it remains difficult to capture possible interaction and embodiment-dependent adversities. We propose a continual learning framework for a mobile embodied agent to learn online from disturbances and attribute anomalous behaviours to causes through semantics, enabling better prediction and planning of the world in the future. Our framework, "Don't Fool Me Twice", first observes disturbances and describes their effects on the robot; this description is augmented with visual context to query a VLM to predict possible causes; the local disturbance is characterized using kernel regression, which allows for efficient, few-shot modeling of transient anomalies. We leverage semantic voxel-centric modeling to estimate epistemic uncertainty, enabling richer downstream recovery by treating interaction-driven disturbances as learnable spatial behaviors. We present four hypotheses and validate them in simulation and on hardware across embodiments and adversity modes.

</details>


### [280] [TARIC: Memory-Augmented Traversability-Aware Outdoor VLN under Interrupted Semantic Cues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31121)
*Tianle Zeng,Hanjing Ye,Jianwei Peng,Jingwen Yu,Hanxuan Chen,Hong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合可通行性感知与3D语义记忆的室外VLN框架，通过在语义线索缺失期间维持稳定的可执行导航，显著提升了机器人在长距离复杂环境下的导航成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在长距离开放环境的视觉语言导航（VLN）中，一旦目标语义线索丢失，传统方法常陷入回溯、振荡或盲目探索的困境，且现有的记忆方法难以应对由于可通行性约束导致的绕行，导致导航失效。

Method: 提出了一种统一的室外VLN框架，核心包括：1. 利用实时近场可通行性分析，将语义方向转化为可执行的航向；2. 构建世界坐标对齐的3D语义记忆库，配合不确定性感知读取机制，以缓解在绕行过程中的记忆模糊与过时问题。

Result: 在仿真中成功率提升超过10个百分点；在真实世界长距离（600-1000m）测试中，成功率达到40%（基准仅为17.5%），并在长期的线索缺失阶段展现出极高的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该框架通过整合近场可通行性与全局三维语义记忆，有效解决了长期室外VLN在语义线索缺失期间的导航失效问题，实现了更具鲁棒性的目标导向导航。

Abstract: Outdoor vision-language navigation (VLN) in long-range, open-world environments is frequently disrupted by semantic-cue interruptions, where informative goal cues become sparse, occluded, or leave the field of view. Once such cues disappear, agents enter a cue-free phase and often degrade into backtracking, oscillatory headings, or aimless exploration. While memory-based methods attempt to bridge these gaps, they often fail under traversability-driven detours: the remembered cue direction may be infeasible, forcing detours that prolong cue-free phases and gradually render robot-centric cues stale and implicit histories blurred. This makes traversability a stability condition for maintaining goal-directed guidance, rather than merely a local safety concern.
  We propose a unified outdoor VLN framework that survives semantic-cue interruptions by maintaining traversability-consistent executable guidance throughout prolonged cue-free phases. Specifically, our method extracts semantic bearings from visibility-gated goal or exploration cues and grounds them into executable headings using a real-time near-field traversability profile, providing goal-consistent feasible guidance beyond reject-only safety filtering. To prevent guidance degradation during detours, we lift intermittent 2D evidence into a world-aligned 3D cue memory with an uncertainty-aware readout mechanism, ensuring guidance remains continuously reachable and stable as the robot moves.
  We evaluate the framework on quadrupedal and wheeled platforms over 600--1000 m routes. Our method improves simulation success rate by over 10 percentage points over the strongest baseline and achieves a real-world success rate of 40%, compared to 17.5% for the strongest baseline, with substantially higher robustness during prolonged cue-free intervals.

</details>


### [281] [HARP-VLA: Human-Robot Aligned Representation Learning for Vision-Language-Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31234)
*Xiang Zhu,Puzhen Yuan,Yichen Liu,Jianyu Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HARP框架通过解耦并重新对齐人类与机器人的视觉表现，使VLA模型能够利用海量人类视频有效预训练，突破了跨具身操控学习的瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在VLA（视觉-语言-动作）模型预训练中，人类视频与机器人动作之间的跨具身差异（视觉表示不一致、动作空间不匹配）导致模型难以有效利用大规模人类视频进行通用策略学习。

Method: 1. 利用极少量成对的人机演示数据作为跨具身桥梁，大量非成对视频作为动态监督数据。
2. 引入源相关对判别对齐损失（source-relative pair-discriminative alignment loss）和操作中心辅助线索（manipulation-centric auxiliary cues），训练机器人适应的视觉编码器。
3. 利用对齐后的视觉编码器训练潜在动作模型（latent action model），并通过轻量级机器人动作头将潜在动作转换为可执行命令。

Result: 在CALVIN基准测试中平均任务长度达到4.481；在真实场景操纵任务中成功率比最强基线提升了7.1%。

Conclusion: HARP通过学习统一的视觉和动作表示，成功解决了多具身（cross-embodiment）视觉与动作不一致问题，显著提升了VLA模型从人类视频学习的泛化能力与终端操作性能。

Abstract: Learning generalizable vision-language-action (VLA) models from large-scale human videos is promising but challenging due to cross-embodiment discrepancies in both visual observations and executable actions. While latent action models reduce the action execution gap by learning action abstractions, they still rely on visual features. Thus, misaligned human and robot visual representations can lead to inconsistencies in policy inputs and induce domain-dependent latent actions, hindering effective co-training with human videos. To address this, we propose HARP, a human-robot aligned representation learning framework for more effective VLA pretraining from human videos. Specifically, HARP uses limited paired human-robot demonstrations as cross-embodiment bridges and abundant unpaired human and robot videos as a scalable dynamics supervision data source. It trains a robot-adapted visual encoder and a latent action model with manipulation-centric auxiliary cues and a source-relative pair-discriminative alignment loss, which adapts robot representations toward human semantics while preserving pair-level discrimination. The learned aligned vision encoder and latent action model provide a unified vision and action representation for VLA-style policy learning, where human and robot videos provide vision-language-to-latent-action supervision and a lightweight robot action head grounds latent actions into executable commands. Experiments on feature visualization, simulation, and realworld manipulation show improved human-robot alignment and downstream policy performance, achieving 4.481 average length on CALVIN ABC$\rightarrow$D and a 7.1\% realworld success rate gain over the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [282] [Before Parc Fermé: RL-Time Pruning for Efficient Embodied LLMs in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31256)
*Luca Benfenati,Ali Azimi,Matteo Risso,Fabio Carapellese,Daniele Jahier Pagliari,Alessio Burrello*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 为解决具身 LLM 在机器人控制中的实时部署难题，本文提出 BPF 剪枝策略，通过在强化学习训练过程中同步进行剪枝，实现了模型轻量化与任务控制性能的最优平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身大模型（LLMs）作为机器人控制核心时，其巨大的内存需求和生成时延限制了实时部署。现有的剪枝方案多局限于训练后，未充分利用强化学习的闭环反馈和针对性监督，导致性能折损。

Method: 提出“Before Parc Fermé (BPF)”策略：1. BPF-RL：在强化学习过程中进行迭代剪枝；2. BPF-SFT/RL：先在 SFT 阶段部分剪枝，再在 RL 阶段持续压缩，直至达到目标比率。通过在 RobotxR1 自动驾驶控制流程中集成 LLM-Pruner 进行验证。

Result: BPF 实现了最佳的任务性能与内存/吞吐量权衡。在 RobotxR1 模型中，BPF-SFT/RL 相比同系列的较小稠密模型，单位性能损失下的压缩效率提升了 1.69 倍；在 Jetson AGX Orin 平台上，解码吞吐量最高提升了 27%。

Conclusion: BPF 是一种在具身智能模型强化学习（RL）阶段进行剪枝的有效策略。相比传统的事后修剪或直接使用小模型，BPF 能更好地平衡任务性能、模型大小和推理时延，是优化具身 LLM 控制器的优选方案。

Abstract: Embodied Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used as reasoning modules in robotic control pipelines to improve human-robot interaction, but their memory and generation latency make real-time deployment difficult. Pruning can reduce these costs, but for controllers that undergo multiple pre- and post-training phases, the crucial question is not only how much to prune, but when pruning should occur.
  In this work, we propose Before Parc Fermé (BPF), a pruning strategy performed during RL that compresses embodied LLM controllers while they are still being optimized for closed-loop behavior. This allows pruning decisions to account for the task-specific supervision and closed-loop feedback that shape the final controller. We propose two variants: BPF-RL, which performs iterative pruning during RL by removing part of the model at predefined training intervals, and BPF-SFT/RL, which first prunes part of the model structure during SFT and then further compresses it during RL using the same iterative strategy as BPF-RL until the target pruning ratio is reached. We evaluate BPF on RobotxR1, an LLM-based autonomous-driving control pipeline, using an established LLM pruning framework (LLM-Pruner), and compare it against post-training pruning, post-training pruning with RL recovery, SFT-stage pruning, and smaller dense models from the same family.
  Our results show that BPF provides the best task-performance vs. memory and throughput trade-off among the considered pruning strategies. When compressing the larger RobotxR1 models, BPF-SFT/RL achieves a $1.69\times$ better size-end-to-end performance trade-off than directly selecting a smaller dense model from the same family, measured as removed parameters per lost percentage point of control adaptability. On the Jetson AGX Orin mounted on the target robotic platform, the compact models improve decode throughput by up to $27\%$.

</details>


### [283] [DeMaVLA: A Vision-Language-Action Foundation Model for Generalizable Deformable Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31286)
*Taiyi Su,Jian Zhu,Tianjian Wang,Youzhang He,Zitai Huang,Jianjun Zhang,Chong Ma,Hanyang Wang,Tianjiao Zhang,Munan Yin,Weihao Ding,Yi Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为 DeMaVLA 的通用变形操纵 VLA 模型，通过高效的动作生成架构和大规模混合真实世界数据训练，解决了多任务变形物体（如衣物）折叠中的通用性与任务干扰问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在处理变形物体（如衣物）时通常依赖于特定类别的策略，而混合多任务训练又常导致任务干扰和性能下降。需要一种能够跨不同类别、几何形状和场景的通用变形操作模型。

Method: 1. 架构：采用 VLM 主干网并引入轻量级动作专家（通过剪枝层数实现，同时保持层级对齐）；2. 动作生成：使用流匹配（Flow Matching）进行连续动作预测；3. 数据训练：首先进行 5000 小时的真实世界双臂演示预训练，随后通过人机协同的 DAgger 流水线在混合折叠数据上进行后训练。

Result: 在 RoboTwin 仿真平台和真实世界的家庭折叠基准测试中均表现出优秀的性能，验证了该方法在处理多样化变形物体操作任务上的通用性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: DeMaVLA 通过结合大规模真实世界预训练、高效的动作专家架构以及人机协同的数据增强策略，证明了构建支持多种变形物体折叠任务的通用 VLA 模型的有效性。

Abstract: Real-world household robots require Vision-Language-Action (VLA) foundation models that can acquire reusable manipulation skills across diverse objects, task conditions, and household environments. Deformable-object folding is a representative challenge, requiring robots to handle clothing items from random initial states across varying categories, geometries, materials, and scenes. However, existing VLA systems commonly train separate policies for different object categories, while naively mixed multi-task training often suffers from task interference and degraded performance. To move beyond category-specific folding policies, we introduce DeMaVLA, a VLA foundation model for generalizable Deformable Manipulation. DeMaVLA adopts a VLM backbone with an action expert and formulates continuous action generation using flow matching. To improve efficiency, the action expert is constructed by pruning every other transformer layer while preserving layer-wise alignment with the VLM backbone, reducing training and inference cost. DeMaVLA is first pre-trained on approximately 5,000 hours of selected real-world dual-arm demonstrations to acquire general manipulation priors. It is then post-trained on mixed folding data that aggregates self-collected demonstrations and corrective trajectories from real-robot failures across multiple folding tasks through a human-in-the-loop Data Aggregation~(DAgger) pipeline. Experiments show that DeMaVLA achieves competitive performance on RoboTwin and strong real-world results on our household folding benchmark. These results highlight the value of scalable real-world data, efficient action generation, and corrective learning for general-purpose VLA policies in deformable-object manipulation.

</details>


### [284] [AR Forcing: Towards Long-Horizon Robot Navigation World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31314)
*Yifei Yang,Zehua Fan,Huan Li,Aoqi Wang,Lida Huang,Haibao Yu,Haiyan Liu,Xuanyao Mao,Jason Bao,Liang Xu,Bingchuan Sun,Yan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对扩散模型在机器人导航中因训练与推理不一致（分布偏移）导致的长程稳定性差问题，本文提出了 AR Forcing 训练策略，通过在训练中引入自回归更新，有效增强了复杂环境下的预测鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决基于扩散的机器人导航模型因“并行训练 vs. 自回归推理”造成的分布偏移（distribution shift）问题，该问题导致模型在长程预测时性能不稳定。

Method: 提出一种名为“AR Forcing”的自回归训练策略，将标准扩散损失整合进自回归训练循环中，使模型在训练时利用自身的产出更新上下文，从而模拟推理过程中的状态分布。

Result: 在 RECON、SCAND、HuRoN 和 TartanDrive 等多个导航数据集上的实验表明，相比强基准模型，AR Forcing 显著提高了长程导航生成的图像一致性和轨迹预测精度。

Conclusion: AR Forcing 弥合了扩散模型在机器人导航任务中训练与推理阶段的分布偏移，在无需复杂判别器或额外匹配损失的情况下，显著提升了长程预测的一致性和轨迹准确性。

Abstract: The diffusion based robot navigation world models are typically trained using parallel supervision, while autoregressive inference is employed during path planning. This results in a distribution shift between training and inference, which destabilizes the performance over long-horizon prediction. We propose AR Forcing, an autoregressive training strategy, which integrates the standard diffusion loss into the autoregressive training loop. At each step, the model uses its own predictions to update the context and optimize the single step noise prediction objective, thereby explicitly exposing the model to the inference state distribution during training. Our method does not require additional discriminators or distribution-matching losses, retains the original diffusion framework and sampler, and is easy to integrate. Experiments on multi-domain navigation datasets (RECON, SCAND, HuRoN, TartanDrive) show that compared with strong baselines, AR Forcing improved the consistency of generated images during long-horizon navigation and the accuracy of predicted trajectories, enhancing robustness of the model in complex known and unknown environments. We will release the code soon.

</details>


### [285] [Surface Constraint Policy for Learning Surface-Constrained and Dynamically Feasible Robot Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31321)
*Shuai Ke,Jiexin Zhang,Huan Zhao,Zhiao Wei,Yikun Guo,Jie Pan,Han Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对机器人曲面操作中几何约束缺失和动作不稳定问题，本论文提出了一种曲面约束策略（SCP），结合高斯核几何建模与动态运动基元，实现了高效稳定的曲面操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于扩散模型的模仿学习在处理复杂曲面约束任务时，缺乏显式的几何约束建模，导致生成的动作随机性影响了曲面紧密贴合及接触稳定性。

Method: 1. 利用权重高斯核函数对示范数据中的曲面几何约束进行显式建模；2. 结合扩散策略（Diffusion-based policy）从多模态输入推断任务级意图；3. 通过基于相似度的动作映射方法，将意图转化为曲面约束下的动态运动基元（DMPs），以确保动作的平滑和合规性。

Result: 实验在多个曲面操作任务中表现优异，在任务成功率、曲面贴合精度及接触稳定性方面均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的SCP方法有效地解决了基于扩散模型的模仿学习在复杂曲面约束任务中存在的几何建模缺失和动力学可行性差的问题，实现了高成功率及稳定的接触操作。

Abstract: Diffusion-based imitation learning methods have driven rapid progress in robot dexterous manipulation tasks. However, they have limitations when applied to tasks that involve complex free-form surface constraints because of their lack of explicit surface geometry constraint modeling and the dynamic feasibility issue, resulting in stochastic action generation that fails to achieve reliable surface alignment and maintain stable contact. To address these limitations, we propose a novel surface constraint policy (SCP) for generating robot actions that satisfy free-form surface constraints on the basis of human demonstrations and real-time visual observations. First, the surface geometry constraint is encoded using a two-dimensional weighted Gaussian kernel function that is derived from demonstrations. Building on the encoded surface geometry constraints, the diffusion-based policy is used to infer task-level action intentions from multimodal sensory inputs, including visual observations and robot state feedback. These intentions are further transformed into surface-constrained dynamic movement primitives (DMPs) through a similarity-based action mapping method, thereby enabling smooth and compliant motion execution. The SCP achieves generation of structured surface geometric intent and dynamically admissible actions. The proposed method is validated on multiple surface manipulation tasks and compared with existing techniques. The experimental results demonstrate superior task success rates and contact stability under surface constraints.

</details>


### [286] [Learning Terrain-Aware Whole-Body Control for Perceptive Legged Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31343)
*Sikai Guo,Yudong Zhong,Guoyang Zhao,Botao Dang,Zhihai Bi,Jun Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种地形感知全身控制框架（TA-WBC），通过结合外感知特征提取、基于接触平面的末端解耦采样以及双策略蒸馏技术，显著提升了足式操作机器人在复杂地形下的移动操作能力与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的足式操作机器人全身控制器多依赖本体感知，缺乏对外部环境地形拓扑的感知，导致其在复杂地形下的作业范围受限及运动稳定性不足。

Method: 1. 提出一种基于强化学习（RL）的统一控制策略；2. 设计混合外感知编码器（Hybrid exteroception encoder）以提取地形特征；3. 提出一种基于足部接触平面的末端执行器采样方法（End-effector sampling method），实现底盘波动与操作目标解耦；4. 引入双策略蒸馏模块（Dual-policy distillation），在保持地形适应性的同时实现复杂的全身运动。

Result: 仿真和真实环境实验表明，该控制器能实现更广的作业空间、更小的跟踪误差，并显著减少了行走时的意外跌倒（stumbles）。

Conclusion: 本文提出的 TA-WBC 框架通过将外感知能力融入全身控制，有效地提升了足式操作机器人在复杂地形下的作业范围、稳定性和轨迹跟踪精度，验证了统一策略处理复杂地形下移动操作任务的可行性。

Abstract: Legged manipulators integrate exceptional terrain adaptability along with mobile manipulation capabilities, which make them highly promising for deployment in human-centric environments. By coordinating the control of both legs and arms, a whole-body controller can significantly expand the operational workspace of legged manipulators. However, many existing whole-body controllers primarily depend on proprioception and do not incorporate the critical exteroception required for effective terrain topology perception. This limitation can hinder their ability to adapt to varying environmental conditions and navigate complex terrains effectively. In this paper, we introduce TA-WBC, a terrain-aware whole-body control framework for legged manipulators, which features a novel RL-based unified policy tailored to whole-body loco-manipulation tasks in various terrains. Specifically, we employ a hybrid exteroception encoder to extract terrain features, providing an essential basis for the robot to proactively adapt posture and footholds. Furthermore, to facilitate stable cross-terrain loco-manipulation, we propose a novel end-effector sampling method based on the foot contact plane, decoupling manipulation target from base fluctuations. Moreover, a dual-policy distillation module is introduced to integrate expansive whole-body motion with terrain adaptability without catastrophic forgetting. The simulation and real-world experiments validate the robustness of our proposed controller, which leads to a larger reachable space, less tracking error, and reduced unexpected stumbles. This unified policy highlights the promising capabilities of legged manipulators in performing loco-manipulation tasks across complex terrains.

</details>


### [287] [Haptic Sorter: A Unified Planning Framework for Online Shape Estimation and Real-Time Pose Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31352)
*Zhuoyi Lu,Lin Yang,Sri Harsha Turlapati,Domenico Campolo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合触觉感知与操作规划的几何计算框架，通过贝叶斯优化进行形状推断、利用自适应势函数进行交互建模，并借助在线ODE实现实时姿态估计，有效解决了在遮挡和传感器噪声条件下的机器人操作难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作通常依赖于已知的物体形状和姿态，但在实际环境中，精确的几何信息往往难以获取，且姿态推断常受限于传感器不确定性和视线遮挡。因此需要一个能整合触觉感知、建模与规划的框架来处理这类问题。

Method: 1.利用贝叶斯优化（BO）引导触觉探索以推断物体形状（使用超椭圆近似几何边界）；2.构建基于物体几何信息的自适应操作势函数（Manipulation Potential），用于准静态机器人-物体交互；3.提出一种在线常微分方程（ODE）方法，结合模型预测和触觉反馈进行实时姿态推断。

Result: 在2D机器人分拣任务中进行了部署，并通过改变物体几何形状，在模拟环境和真实多臂机器人系统中验证了该框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的统一框架成功实现了在缺乏先验几何信息和存在传感器不确定性的环境下，通过触觉感知进行形状估计、姿态推断与抓取规划，在模拟和真实世界机器人任务中验证了其鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Robotics manipulation usually assumes that the shape and pose of the object are known to the robot prior to motion planning. However, precise geometric information is not always available in practice, and pose inference suffers from sensor uncertainties and view occlusion. In this work, we propose a unified model-based geometric framework integrating robotic haptic perception, modeling, and manipulation planning. Our novelties involve: \textit{i)} Introducing Bayesian Optimization (BO) to guide the haptic exploration for object shape inference, where superellipses are used to approximate geometric boundary; \textit{ii)} Adaptive formulation of manipulation potential encoding object geometry for quasi-static robot-object interaction; \textit{iii)} Proposing an online Ordinary Differential Equation (ODE) for real-time pose inference based on model prediction and tactile feedback. We deploy our system on a 2D robotic sorting task, and vary object geometries to validate the robustness and generalizability of our framework in both simulation and a real-world multi-arm setup.

</details>


### [288] [LiftNav: Path Planning via Semantic Lifting in TSDF-Guided Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31376)
*Hannah Schieber,Dominik Frischmann,Victor Schaack,Angela P. Schoellig,Daniel Roth*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LiftNav 是一种新型混合机器人导航框架，通过结合 TSDF 几何地图与 Gaussian Splatting 视觉地图，辅以 YOLO 语义感知和 B-样条轨迹优化，在确保安全避障的同时实现了高效的室内语义导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法存在缺陷：经典表示（如 TSDF）缺乏语义信息，而基于 Gaussian Splatting 的方法虽然视觉效果逼真但几何结构偏“软”，难以满足机器人精确避障的需求。因此，需要一个既能支持安全规避又能理解环境语义的混合框架。

Method: 1. 基础架构：采用基于 GSFusion 的混合地图，结合了 TSDF（用于几何）与 3D 高斯泼溅（用于视觉）。
2. 感知模块：集成 YOLO 实时目标检测，通过 TSDF 将 2D 检测结果提升至 3D 空间。
3. 规划模块：使用基于 B-样条（B-spline）的轨迹优化，并引入铰链损失函数（hinge-loss-based collision penalty）以增强轨迹的平滑性与安全性。

Result: 在 Replica 数据集的模拟测试中，LiftNav 相较于现有的辐射场（radiance field）基准方法，实现了 100% 的路径规划可行性，并且生成的导航轨迹更短。

Conclusion: LiftNav 证明了通过结合经典的 TSDF 几何表征与新兴的 Gaussian Splatting（GS）模型，可以在未知室内环境中有效平衡高精度的碰撞规避与语义理解能力。该框架为机器人实现鲁棒的语义导航提供了一种高效的混合方案。

Abstract: Autonomous robots in unknown indoor environments require both reliable collision avoidance and object-level understanding. Classical representations such as TSDF support safe planning but lack semantics, while photorealistic methods like Gaussian Splatting (GS) provide rich appearance yet suffer from soft geometry, limiting precise obstacle avoidance. We present LiftNav, a hybrid navigation framework built on GSFusion's TSDF+GS dual map, augmented with a real-time pipeline of YOLO-based detection, TSDF-based 3D lifting, and B-spline trajectory optimization. This design enables flexible semantic navigation without dense 3D embeddings. We further introduce a hinge-loss-based collision penalty that improves trajectory smoothness and safety. We evaluate our approach in a simulation using the Replica dataset. Compared against a state-of-the-art radiance field baseline we show a 100% feasibility rate and shorter trajectories.

</details>


### [289] [Adaptive Artificial Time-Delay Control with Barrier Lyapunov Constraints for Euler-Lagrange Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31405)
*Saksham Gupta,Rishabh Dev Yadav,Sarthak Mishra,Amitabh Sharma,Sourish Ganguly,Wei Pan,Spandan Roy,Simone Baldi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对欧拉-拉格朗日系统的控制难题，提出了一种结合时间延迟估计与障碍李雅普诺夫函数的自适应框架，实现了在无需模型知识下的不确定性补偿与严格的状态约束满足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有控制设计难以同时满足欧拉-拉格朗日系统（如机器人）中状态相关不确定性的补偿和随时间变化的状态约束要求。

Method: 提出了一种自适应控制框架：结合基于人工时间延迟的估计（TDE）来处理状态相关的不确定性，并利用障碍李雅普诺夫函数（BLF）来处理随时间变化的状态限制；设计了一种自适应律在线估计TDE近似误差的上限。

Result: 实现了无需先验模型知识的实时不确定性补偿，同时成功将位置和速度限制在设定的时变边界内。

Conclusion: 该方法通过理论分析证明了闭环系统的稳定性，并通过五自由度机械臂实验验证了其在抑制动态不确定性的同时强制满足安全约束的卓越性能，优于现有技术。

Abstract: This paper addresses the challenge of simultaneously compensating for state-dependent uncertainties and enforcing time-varying state constraints in Euler-Lagrange systems, a common requirement in robotics that remains underserved by existing control designs. A novel adaptive control framework is developed that combines an artificial time-delay-based uncertainty estimation strategy, also known as time-delay estimation, with a barrier Lyapunov function to enforce constraint-aware control design. Specifically, a state-dependent upper bound on the time-delay estimation approximation error is analytically formulated, and an adaptive law is constructed to estimate its parameters online, enabling real-time state-dependent uncertainty compensation without relying on prior model knowledge. To ensure constraint compliance, the barrier Lyapunov function-based controller enforces time-varying bounds on both position and velocity. The resulting architecture is provably stable via Lyapunov analysis. Experimental results on a five-degree-of-freedom robotic manipulator validate the framework's capability, compared with the state of the art, in maintaining strict adherence to safety-critical constraints under dynamic uncertainties.

</details>


### [290] [Actuator-Aware Inverse Kinematics with Joint-Limit Admissibility for Torque-Controlled Redundant Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31436)
*Mohammad Dastranj,Mahdi Hejrati,Jouni Mattila*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种考虑执行器转矩限制与联合限位的逆运动学（IK）求解方案，通过凸优化方法在运动学与底层转矩控制层之间建立有效桥梁，解决了冗余机器人在受限条件下的运动指令兼容性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的运动学层面逆运动学（IK）未考虑下位转矩控制器的特性，导致在转矩控制的冗余机器人中，纯运动学指令可能无法转化为最优的实际运动效果，特别是在存在关节限位约束时。

Method: 提出了一种基于凸二次规划（QP）的执行器感知逆运动学求解器。其核心设计包括：利用控制障碍函数（CBF）处理关节限位约束；通过惩罚松弛变量处理任务方程；引入控制器兼容性目标（考虑历史指令一致性及执行器转矩能力权重）来解决冗余度问题。

Result: 通过在七自由度上肢外骨骼上的虚拟分解控制实验，证明该方法降低了关节限位的触发频率，确保了所输出的速度指令在底层控制器允许的范围内，并显著提升了任务末端的实际轨迹跟踪性能。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种独立于底层转矩控制器的运动学中间层，通过联合考虑执行器特性的逆运动学方案，有效改善了多冗余度机器人在受限环境下的跟踪性能。

Abstract: This paper proposes actuator-aware inverse kinematics for torque-controlled redundant robots under joint-limit constraints. In the considered architecture, the inverse-kinematic output is not merely a purely kinematic joint-velocity command; it is the required joint velocity supplied to a downstream torque-level controller. Therefore, a small commanded task residual may not necessarily improve realized motion. The proposed method formulates a convex quadratic programming problem whose decision variable is the joint-level required velocity. Control barrier function style bounds impose reference-level joint-limit admissibility, while the task equation is handled through a penalized slack variable. Redundancy is resolved using a controller-compatibility objective that accounts for previous-command consistency and actuator torque-capacity weighting. The method is independent of the particular torque-level controller and can serve as an intermediate IK layer between an endpoint trajectory and a redundant robot controller. Experiments on a virtual-decomposition-controlled seven-degree-of-freedom upper-limb exoskeleton compare the method with standard inverse-kinematic baselines and a constrained task-preserving quadratic programming baseline. The results indicate lower limit-pushing commands, bounded admissible required velocities, and improved realized task behavior in the tested trajectory, without modifying the downstream controller.

</details>


### [291] [On-Device Robotic Planning: Eliminating Inference Redundancy for Efficient Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31460)
*Joonhee Lee,Hyunseung Shin,Hyunmi Kim,Pei Zhang,Jeonggil Ko*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: REIS 是一套能够利用任务的时间冗余性，通过减少重复推理来显著加速具身机器人控制的决策框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于 LLM/VLM 的机器人推理策略虽然性能优异，但推理延迟极高，难以满足实际机器人实时控制的需求；研究发现机器人任务中存在显著的时间冗余。

Method: 提出了一种名为 REIS 的机器人决策框架，核心包括：1. 轻量级场景门控（lightweight scene gating）；2. KV 缓存引导的启发式路由（KV-steered affordance routing）；3. 慎思推理（deliberative reasoning），通过这些技术消除冗余推理。

Result: 在 ALFRED 基准测试和真实世界机器人任务中，REIS 有效降低了推理开销，同时保持了与原有高性能方法相当的任务完成率。

Conclusion: REIS 通过引入一种类似于人类认知机制的决策框架，既保留了大型模型的高级语义理解能力，又通过大幅降低推理延迟，成功实现了在具身智能场景下的实时性需求。

Abstract: Reasoning-based robotic policies using large language and vision-language models achieve strong semantic planning capabilities but mostly suffer from a high inference latency that limits practical real-time deployment. In this work, we observe that robotic reasoning workloads contain substantial temporal redundancy, where consecutive observations frequently produce identical actions and subgoals. Based on this insight, we present REIS, a human cognition inspired robotic decision-making framework that minimizes unnecessary reasoning while preserving semantic adaptability. REIS combines lightweight scene gating, KV-steered affordance routing, and deliberative reasoning to accelerate robotic control under embodied constraints. Experiments on ALFRED, and real-world robotic tasks demonstrate that REIS significantly suppresses reasoning overhead while maintaining competitive task performance.

</details>


### [292] [IDOL: Inverse-Dynamics-Guided Future Prediction for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31476)
*Chenghao Zhang,Timin Li,Dongmei Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出IDOL框架，通过在潜在空间引入逆动力学，将世界模型的未来预测直接转化为可执行的轨迹优化，有效解决了预测与规划脱节的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前端到端自动驾驶中的世界模型虽能预测未来场景，但预测结果与轨迹规划之间的关联较弱，未能有效将其转化为具体的运动指令及轨迹更新。

Method: 提出了一种基于潜在鸟瞰图（Latent BEV）空间的逆动力学引导未来预测框架。主要包括三个核心步骤：1. 使用BEV世界模型预测多个未来潜在场景状态；2. 利用逆动力学模型解码相邻未来状态，提取过渡感知（transition-aware）的轨迹特征和运动增量（motion deltas）；3. 将运动增量用于优化规划轨迹，并引入轻量级闭环细化模块以提升长程一致性。

Result: 在NAVSIM v1和v2基准测试中，IDOL框架表现优于同类方法，实现了最先进（SOTA）的性能。

Conclusion: IDOL通过引入逆动力学，成功将世界模型中的“未来场景预测”转化为“可执行的规划引导”，增强了世界模型与运动规划之间的耦合，在NAVSIM基准测试中达到了SOTA水平。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving has emerged as a compelling paradigm for learning planning directly from sensor observations, while recent world-model-based approaches further enrich this paradigm by enabling explicit reasoning about how the scene may evolve in the future. Yet future prediction alone does not guarantee better planning unless the predicted evolution can be converted into planning-relevant trajectory updates. Many current methods still forecast future scene states without explicitly decoding the motion implications hidden in state transitions. As a result, future reasoning often remains descriptively useful but only weakly coupled to executable motion generation. To address this limitation, we propose \mathbf{IDOL}, an inverse-dynamics-guided future prediction framework for world-model-based end-to-end planning in latent BEV space, where inverse dynamics serves as the key bridge between future prediction and trajectory optimization. IDOL first predicts multiple future latent scene states with a BEV world model, then applies an inverse dynamics model to adjacent latent futures to decode transition-aware trajectory features and recover planning-relevant motion deltas that explain how the latent world evolves over time. These inverse-dynamics-derived signals are used to optimize the planned trajectory, turning future forecasting from passive scene anticipation into actionable planning guidance. A lightweight closed-loop refinement module further improves long-horizon consistency by reusing the optimized trajectory for another round of future-aware reasoning. By introducing inverse dynamics into latent future reasoning, IDOL tightens the coupling between world modeling and planning. Extensive experiments on the NAVSIM v1 and NAVSIM v2 benchmarks show that IDOL achieves state-of-the-art performance among comparable methods.

</details>


### [293] [Batched Differentiable Rigid Body Dynamics in PyTorch for GPU-Accelerated Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31481)
*Yue Wang,Yanran Xu,Wenbo Wu,Chuanhang Qiu,Zhaoxing Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BARD是一个全PyTorch实现的、针对GPU优化的批处理刚体动力学库，通过创新的架构设计显著提升了机器人大规模强化学习的计算吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人大规模强化学习中，现有的CPU绑定动力学库（如Pinocchio）限制了训练流程的吞吐量，成为了GPU计算瓶颈。

Method: 1. 采用分层惰性评估缓存（tiered lazy-evaluation cache）减少冗余树遍历；
2. 利用预计算的Rodrigues常数实现无矩阵乘法（matmul-free）的关节变换；
3. 使用层级并行传播（level-parallel propagation）将序列操作降维为树深度批处理步骤。

Result: 在NVIDIA H200上，BARD的运动学和雅可比矩阵计算吞吐量比Pinocchio高出约64倍；在系统辨识实验中，质量参数恢复误差仅为1.24%；在Isaac Lab quadruped训练任务中，比Pinocchio快8.5倍，比ADAM快2.0倍。

Conclusion: BARD通过提供一个全GPU加速、可自动微分的动力学库，有效解决了机器人强化学习中CPU计算瓶颈问题，在吞吐量上显著优于传统库（如Pinocchio和ADAM）。

Abstract: As robot control shifts toward large-scale reinforcement learning with in-loop dynamics computation, the community's reliance on CPU-bound libraries such as Pinocchio creates a throughput bottleneck in GPU-based training pipelines. We present BARD (Batched Articulated Rigid-body Dynamics), a self-contained PyTorch implementation of Featherstone's rigid-body dynamics algorithms, optimized for batched GPU evaluation and automatic differentiation. Three design choices make this efficient: a tiered lazy-evaluation cache that avoids redundant tree traversals, matmul-free joint transforms via pre-computed Rodrigues constants, and level-parallel propagation that reduces sequential operations to tree-depth batched steps. On five robot models (7-23 DOFs), BARD matches Pinocchio numerically while reaching up to 64x higher throughput for Forward Kinematics and 63x for Jacobians at batch size 4096 on an NVIDIA H200. We validate differentiability through gradient-based system identification on a 7-DOF manipulator, recovering link masses to 1.24% mean error under 5% torque noise, and integrate BARD into an Isaac Lab AMP training pipeline for an 11-DOF spined quadruped with 4096 parallel environments, where it is 8.5x faster than Pinocchio and 2.0x faster than ADAM for in-loop dynamics. BARD is open-sourced at: https://github.com/YueWang996/bard-pytorch-dynamics.

</details>


### [294] [Learning Controlled Separation of Small Objects Between Two Fingers with a Tactile Skin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31486)
*Ulf Kasolowsky,Berthold Bäuml*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种利用多指机器人手皮肤的触觉反馈，通过强化学习实现小物体（6mm颗粒）数量分离的控制方法，无需视觉辅助，并验证了该方法在真实硬件上的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决机器人从容器中抓取多个小物体后，如何通过两指操作精确分离并保留目标数量物体的问题，特别是在无视觉辅助或环境受限的情况下。

Method: 1. 提出一种基于强化学习（RL）的控制策略，通过触觉皮肤获取反馈；2. 在仿真环境中训练模型，使用稀疏奖励机制；3. 训练位置估计器以辅助接触位置预测；4. 在真实DLR-Hand II机器人上进行实物实验验证。

Result: 1. 证明了纯触觉（无视觉）反馈足以完成该任务；2. 对比发现，高空间分辨率触觉传感器能实现近乎完美的任务表现，低分辨率传感器（4x4 taxels）相较于仅使用关节传感器，性能提升可达20%；3. 成功实现了在真实机器人硬件上的Sim-to-Real迁移。

Conclusion: 在多指灵巧手操作中，空间分辨的触觉信息对于精细化操作（如小物体数量分离）至关重要。研究成功展示了纯触觉闭环控制方法，并验证了从仿真到现实（Sim-to-Real）的迁移可行性。

Abstract: We introduce and solve the novel task of controlled separation of small objects with two fingers of a multi-purpose robotic hand: after grasping into a box of small objects, the task is to drop as many of them until a desired number remains between the fingers. The objects are small compared to the width of the fingers but also in absolute terms. In our case little pellets with a diameter of only 6mm are handled. We show that the task can be performed purely tactile (no vision) using a spatially-resolved tactile skin on a fingertip. The separation policy is trained in simulation via reinforcement learning using a straightforward sparse reward, which basically checks if the desired number of objects is reached. In simulation experiments, we provide an exhaustive analysis of the benefits of using spatially-resolved tactile feedback: while an ideal (high-resolution) tactile sensor allows solving the task almost perfectly, a sensor with lower spatial resolution (here 4x4 taxels) still leads to an improvement of up to 20% compared to using only the fingers' joint sensors. For this analysis, we further train an estimator alongside the policy that predicts the ground truth contact positions. Finally, we demonstrate the successful sim-to-real transfer for the DLR-Hand II equipped with a tactile skin.

</details>


<div id='cs.DS'></div>

# cs.DS [[Back]](#toc)

### [295] [An Optimal Algorithm for Binary Closest String](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31417)
*Nick Fischer,Mursalin Habib*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文针对二进制最近字符串问题，提出了一种简单且条件最优的随机算法，其运行时间为 $O^*(4^d)$，刷新了此前的世界纪录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对“二进制最近字符串问题”（Binary Closest String），旨在寻求以最优距离 $d$ 为参数的更高效算法，以突破此前 $O^*(5^d)$ 的极限，并验证算法在理论上的最优性。

Method: 采用随机化算法设计技术，相较于以前基于搜索或特定的组合策略，该方法更为简洁高效，实现了更快的运行时间。

Result: 实现了 $O^*(4^d)$ 的运行时间，打败了此前该领域领先的 $O^*(5^d)$ 算法，并匹配了由 ESA 2023 提出的细粒度下界，证明了其条件最优性。

Conclusion: 作者提出了一种运行时间为 $O^*(4^d)$ 的随机算法，该算法在参数 $d$（最优汉明距离）下达到了已知的最优复杂度，且该复杂度与现有的细粒度理论下界一致，是条件最优的。

Abstract: We revisit the Binary Closest String problem, which asks, given a set of binary strings $X \subseteq \{0, 1\}^n$, to compute a string minimizing the maximum Hamming distance to $X$. A long line of work has focused on parameterized algorithms with respect to the optimal distance $d$, yielding a sequence of improvements from $O^*(d^d)$ through $O^*(16^d)$, $O^*(9.513^d)$, $O^*(8^d)$, $O^*(6.731^d)$ to the current best-known running time of $O^*(5^d)$ [Chen, Ma, Wang; Algorithmica '16].
  We present a faster randomized algorithm running in time $O^*(4^d)$. Our result matches a recent fine-grained lower bound [Abboud, Fischer, Goldenberg, Karthik C.S., Safier; ESA '23], and is therefore conditionally optimal. As an extra benefit, our algorithm is remarkably simple.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [296] [PhyDrawGen: Physically Grounded Diagram Generation from Natural Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30512)
*Nafiul Haque,Syed Nazmus Sakib,Shifat E Arman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PhyDrawGen 是一种旨在解决物理绘图“幻觉”的神经符号系统，通过解耦物理约束求解与视觉生成，确保生成的物理示意图符合真实的自然定律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生成式 AI 绘图模型在生成物理图表时常出现“幻觉”，如忽视力矢量、违反能量/动量守恒定律及几何配置错误，无法满足物理学严谨性的要求。

Method: 采用神经符号流水线：1. 利用大语言模型将文本转化为类型化的场景图；2. 使用确定性约束求解器将场景图转换为满足物理定律（如受力平衡、光学路径约束）的平面直线图（PSLG）；3. 引入基于 Qwen-VL 的“提出-验证”反馈环进行视觉校准。

Result: 在包含 1,449 个力学、光学及电磁学问题的基准测试中，PhyDrawGen 的物理准确性显著优于 GPT-5-image、Gemini 2.5 Flash 和 Gemini 3 Pro 等顶级大模型。

Conclusion: PhyDrawGen 证明了神经符号方案（将 LLM 的语义理解与确定性物理约束求解器相结合）能有效解决大模型绘图中的“物理幻觉”问题，为生成具备严谨逻辑的科学可视化图表提供了新范式。

Abstract: Generating physics diagrams from text requires strict adherence to physical laws. While current generative models produce visually plausible outputs, they systematically hallucinate force vectors, ignore conservation laws, and violate geometric constraints. We present PhyDrawGen, a neuro-symbolic pipeline that decouples semantic scene understanding from physical constraint satisfaction. First, a large language model extracts a typed scene graph from the problem text. A deterministic solver then converts this graph into a Planar Straight-Line Graph (PSLG), encoding force balance, optical paths, and field topologies as exact geometric primitives. Finally, a fine-tuned Qwen-VL model implements a visually grounded propose-verify loop to iteratively correct any constraint violations. Evaluated on a benchmark of 1,449 problems spanning mechanics, optics, and electromagnetism, PhyDrawGen significantly outperforms GPT-5-image, Gemini 2.5 Flash, and Gemini 3 Pro, demonstrating robust physical accuracy even on unusual-object problems.

</details>


### [297] [Physically Viable World Models: A Case for Query-Conditioned Embodied AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30542)
*Adam J. Thorpe,Stepan Tretiakov,Cheng-Hsi Hsiao,Su Ann Low,Xingjian Li,Hassan Iqbal,Neel P. Bhatt,Ufuk Topcu,Krishna Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对现有世界模型视觉预测强但物理推理弱的问题，本文提出应构建基于“查询相关最简物理抽象”的模块化世界模型，以确保具身智能体在面对干预时能够进行物理上真实可行的决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的观测预测型世界模型虽然能生成视觉上合理的预测，但在处理干预请求时往往因无法捕捉潜在物理结构而导致决策错误、交互预测失败或产生不安全行为。亟需一种物理上可行且结构化的建模方法。

Method: 提出了一套模块化的建模框架，包括环境表征、潜在状态与参数估计、动作规范、干预动态及查询级响应。通过“自主编排器（Orchestrator）”识别相关物理抽象，并整合分析、模拟或学习的混合式动态模型以回答特定干预查询。

Result: 证明了在视觉场景相同但物理属性不同的受控基准测试中，现有模型表现出的物理失效问题。验证了该文提出的模块化抽象方法能够成功解决现有模型无法回答的干预查询，并为构建更稳健的具身智能体提供了设计原则。

Conclusion:  embodied AI 的世界模型应优先考虑“物理可行性”而非单纯的观测预测。通过基于查询构建满足干预需求的最简物理抽象，可以提升模型的安全性、可解释性及审计能力，这是解决现有世界模型在处理交互任务时表现不一致的关键。

Abstract: World models for embodied AI must be physically viable: constructed to answer intervention queries by representing the physical structure governing action outcomes, rather than merely predicting future observations. Existing observation-predictive world models can produce visually plausible but physically wrong rollouts. This failure is structural; distinct physical systems can look identical yet diverge under intervention. We expose this problem with controlled benchmarks that fix the visible scene while varying latent physics. We show that such models may recommend infeasible actions, mispredict interaction outcomes, or certify unsafe behavior. We argue that embodied AI requires world models that identify the simplest physical abstraction sufficient to answer an intervention query.
